### [29] [X-Reset: Scaling Object-Centric Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Embodiment Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35715)
*Prithwish Dan,Chenyang Ma,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 X-Reset：利用人类手-物演示生成重置状态，解决从零强化学习训练通用灵巧操作策略时的探索难题，无需模仿演示或逐任务奖励塑形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真 RL 可无机器人演示训练灵巧操作，但任务无关奖励下的通用策略探索极难：接近、抓取并重定向多样高自由度物体难以从头发现；现有方法依赖高质量机器人演示、逐任务奖励塑形或限制行为模式。

Method: X-Reset 将手-物状态运动学重定向为含噪机器人状态，过滤仿真中不稳定状态，并将其余状态作为 RL 训练重置；采用通用物体中心奖励。策略仅依赖物体状态与目标，演示通过重置分布进入训练。

Result: 在 20 个物体、三种 embodiment（22-DoF 手搭配两种机械臂及平行夹爪）上训练通用策略，解决从零 RL 探索问题；随训练物体数量扩展，泛化到未见物体，可从含噪手姿态估计学习，并实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: X-Reset 表明人类手-物演示可作为重置分布有效引导通用灵巧操作 RL，避免模仿/跟踪与手工奖励塑形，实现可扩展、可泛化、可迁移的策略学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation can train dexterous manipulation policies without robot demonstrations, but training a single generalist policy with task-agnostic rewards faces a severe exploration problem: approaching, grasping, and reorienting diverse objects with many degrees of freedom is difficult to discover from scratch. Prior works make exploration tractable with high-quality robot demonstrations, per-task reward shaping, or by restricting policies to narrow modes of behavior. We propose X-Reset, a framework that instead resolves exploration with human hand-object demonstrations. Rather than imitating or tracking retargeted human motion, X-Reset kinematically retargets hand-object states to noisy robot states, filters out states that are unstable in simulation, and samples the remainder as resets during RL training with general-purpose object-centric rewards. The resulting policy depends only on object state and goal, with demonstrations entering training through the reset distribution. We show that X-Reset trains generalist policies on 20 objects across three embodiments---a 22-DoF hand on two different arms and a parallel-jaw gripper---and resolves the exploration challenges of RL from scratch. X-Reset scales with the number of training objects, generalizes to unseen objects, can learn from imperfect hand-pose estimates, and transfers behaviors zero-shot from sim-to-real.

</details>


### [30] [Manifold-Stable Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35454)
*Amirhossein Nazerian,Ali Pezeshki,Jianguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流匹配 MSFM，利用收缩理论从任意环境先验出发，保证样本流形不变并横向收敛到数据流形，从而提升几何保持与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何流匹配通常假设先验支撑在数据流形上，需要往往不可得的几何知识；缺少该知识时，低回归误差并不保证流形保持，而流形保持与任务性能经验相关。因此需要一种能从任意先验出发并保证流形稳定性的方法。

Method: 引入 manifold-stable flow matching (MSFM)。基于非线性动力学中的收缩理论，将学习到的切向传输与规定的法向收缩结合。对已知流形使用解析投影，对未知数据几何使用 PCA 估计局部仿射代理。在已知与未知流形两种情形下实现收缩，保证流形不变性和在指定时间窗（如 1 秒）内横向收敛到流形。推导兼容的概率路径，并将训练损失分解为可学习的切向项与法向残差。

Result: 椭圆实验达到约 10^{-6} 的平均终端离流形误差。Push-T 机器人任务成功率从 74% 提升到 82%。Robomimic Square 任务成功率从 60% 提升到 72%，同时旋转流形偏差从 10^{-2} 量级降至 10^{-7}。MSFM 的终端几何误差由所选数值容差控制。

Conclusion: 结果表明 MSFM 具有更强的几何保持能力和更高的任务性能，支持将规定法向收缩作为学习生成传输的补充。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) learns generative dynamics through velocity regression. Geometric FM variants commonly assume a prior supported on the data manifold, requiring geometric knowledge that is often unavailable. Without such knowledge, low regression error alone does not guarantee manifold adherence. Adherence keeps generated samples within valid configurations and is empirically associated with better task performance. We introduce manifold-stable flow matching (MSFM), which can start from an arbitrary ambient prior, not necessarily supported on the manifold. Using tools from nonlinear dynamics, namely contraction theory, MSFM combines learned tangential transport with prescribed normal contraction. The construction uses analytical projectors for known manifolds and local affine proxies estimated by principal component analysis for unknown data geometry. By implementing contraction theory in both cases of known and unknown manifolds, we guarantee manifold invariance and transverse convergence to the manifold within a desired time window (e.g., one second). We derive a family of compatible probability paths and decompose the training loss into a learnable tangential term and a normal residual. An ellipse experiment attains a mean terminal off-manifold error of order $10^{-6}$. In Push-T robotic experiments, MSFM raises success from $74\%$ to $82\%$. In the Robomimic Square task, success increases from $60\%$ to $72\%$, while rotation-manifold deviation decreases from order $10^{-2}$ to $10^{-7}$. The MSFM terminal geometric errors are controlled by the chosen numerical tolerance. These results demonstrate stronger geometric adherence and higher observed task performance, supporting prescribed normal contraction as a complement to learned generative transport.

</details>


### [31] [Adjoint Guidance Flow: Amortized Critic Guidance for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34944)
*Jeongsol Kim,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Jangho Park,Kwanyoung Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Adjoint Guidance Flow (AGF)，将轨迹感知的 critic 引导摊销为轻量引导网络，在冻结预训练 VLA 与 critic 的情况下提升流式 VLA 策略的长期回报，同时显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 VLA 策略通常通过行为克隆训练，未显式优化长期任务回报；现有 critic 引导方法依赖采样器的一步代理来微分 critic，并在每个流步骤反传 critic ensemble，导致计算和内存开销较大。

Method: 将 critic 引导的流生成建模为确定性最优控制问题，其最优引导为协态，携带终端 critic 梯度沿剩余流反向传播；冻结 VLA 和 critic，仅训练轻量引导网络回归该协态。推理时每步仅需一次引导网络前向，无需 critic ensemble、反向传播或伴随计算。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa、LIBERO-Pro 上，AGF 稳定提升预训练 VLA，与 critic 引导和策略微调基线相当，并在跨任务部署单一引导强度时最鲁棒。相比 QGF，AGF 每引导步快 3.6 倍，参数少 7.0 倍，性能相当甚至更优。

Conclusion: 结果表明 critic 引导可以兼具轨迹感知能力与轻量化特性，为流式 VLA 策略提供高效、可扩展的测试时引导方案。

Abstract: Flow-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are typically trained by behavior cloning and thus do not explicitly optimize long-term task return. Critic guidance steers generation toward higher-value actions, but existing methods differentiate the critic through a one-step surrogate of the sampler and back-propagate a critic ensemble at every flow step. In contrast, here we propose Adjoint Guidance Flow (AGF), which amortizes trajectory-aware critic guidance into a lightweight guidance network while preserving the pretrained VLA policy. Specifically, we formulate critic-guided flow generation as a deterministic optimal control problem, whose optimal guidance is a costate that carries the terminal critic gradient back through the remaining flow, and regress the guidance network onto this costate while keeping both the VLA and critic frozen. This design provides favorable memory and throughput scaling during training, and inference needs one guidance-network forward pass per step, without the critic ensemble, back-propagation, or adjoint computation. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and LIBERO-Pro, AGF consistently improves pretrained VLAs, remains competitive with critic-guidance and policy-fine-tuning baselines, and is the most robust method when a single guidance strength is deployed across tasks. Compared with QGF, AGF runs $3.6\times$ faster per guidance step with $7.0\times$ fewer parameters, with comparable and even better performance, showing that critic guidance can be trajectory-aware and lightweight.

</details>


### [32] [Learning High-Risk High-Precision Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34851)
*Nam Hee Kim,Markus Kirjonen,Perttu Hämäläinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCOOT：一种基于优势加权回归（AWR）的深度强化学习算法，面向动作不可逆的高风险、高精度运动控制，并在计算台球击球任务中实现高精度动作与多策略发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL运动控制算法多在顺序决策任务上评测，不精确动作可被后续动作纠正，因而噪声动作仍可能获得高回报；但高风险、高精度任务中动作结果不可逆，奖励景观呈尖锐峰值和脊线，现有方法难以应对。作者以计算台球作为此类问题的代表。

Method: SCOOT基于AWR，包含三项关键改进：1）仅使用精英样本进行策略优化，使策略更好捕捉稀有高奖励动作样本；2）采用专家混合（MoE）策略，根据状态在奖励景观的不同模式间切换；3）加入距离正则项和学习课程，鼓励先探索多样策略，再适配最优样本。

Result: 在基于物理的台球击球学习中验证了各改进的效果，能够学习到高动作精度的击球，并针对给定球型发现多种击球策略。

Conclusion: SCOOT适用于动作不可逆的高风险、高精度运动控制，可缓解尖锐奖励景观带来的优化困难，并支持多模态策略学习与策略发现。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms for movement control are typically evaluated and benchmarked on sequential decision tasks where imprecise actions may be corrected with later actions, thus allowing high returns with noisy actions. In contrast, we focus on an under-researched class of high-risk, high-precision motion control problems where actions carry irreversible outcomes, driving sharp peaks and ridges to plague the state-action reward landscape. Using computational pool as a representative example of such problems, we propose and evaluate State-Conditioned Shooting (SCOOT), a novel DRL algorithm that builds on advantage-weighted regression (AWR) with three key modifications: 1) Performing policy optimization only using elite samples, allowing the policy to better latch on to the rare high-reward action samples; 2) Utilizing a mixture-of-experts (MoE) policy, to allow switching between reward landscape modes depending on the state; 3) Adding a distance regularization term and a learning curriculum to encourage exploring diverse strategies before adapting to the most advantageous samples. We showcase our features' performance in learning physically-based billiard shots demonstrating high action precision and discovering multiple shot strategies for a given ball configuration.

</details>


### [33] [LRC-JEPA: Disentangling Dynamics and Residual Context for Efficient World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34375)
*Luzhe Huang,Lei Chu,Jingyi Liang,Yuhuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LRC-JEPA 通过将世界模型信息解耦为用于规划的紧凑预测潜变量 z 和用于重建的残差上下文嵌入 u，在保持轻量化的同时提升复杂场景中的规划成功率与表征解耦能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑 JEPA 世界模型可实现高效潜空间规划，但在无奖励自监督下，低维表征必须同时编码动作条件动力学与可预测视觉上下文。这种竞争会使可控状态与高秩干扰外观纠缠，随场景复杂化而降低规划性能，因此需要显式解耦动态可控信息与持久但非规划的视觉上下文。

Method: 提出 LRC-JEPA：轻量端到端世界模型，将信息路由到紧凑预测潜变量 z 与 learned-query residual-context embeddings u。仅 z 被动力学模型传播并用于规划；u 捕获时间持久信息以支持 cross-attention reconstruction；可微残差连接鼓励 z 保留互补动态内容。理论分析表明，在明确假设下，所得表征满足充分性、最小性、干扰不变性与解耦性。

Result: 在四个仿真控制环境中，LRC-JEPA 相比参数量匹配的 JEPA 基线平均规划成功率提升 9 个百分点，并可匹配或超过显著更大的预训练模型。在真实世界 Bridge-v2 数据集上，其 5.5M 参数 active encoder 优于 DINO-WM（22.1M）与 V-JEPA2（303.9M）编码器，同时实现更快规划。物理状态探针、重建干预与消融实验验证了其表征解耦的有效性。

Conclusion: LRC-JEPA 通过分离可控动态潜变量与残差上下文嵌入，有效缓解 nuisance appearance 对规划的干扰，以轻量编码器取得更强规划性能与更快推理，并验证了理论上的解耦、最小与干扰不变表征。

Abstract: Compact JEPA world models enable efficient latent-space planning, but low-dimensional representation trained under reward-free self-supervision must encode both action-conditioned dynamics and predictable visual context. This competition can entangle controllable state with high-rank nuisance appearance and degrade planning as scenes become more complex. We introduce LRC-JEPA, a lightweight end-to-end world model that routes information into a compact predictive latent $\mathbf{z}$ and learned-query residual-context embeddings $\mathbf{u}$. Only $\mathbf{z}$ is propagated by the dynamics model and used for planning, while $\mathbf{u}$ captures temporally persistent information for cross-attention reconstruction; a differentiable residual connection encourages the latent to retain complementary dynamic content. Under explicit assumptions, we show that the resulting representation is sufficient, minimal, nuisance-invariant, and disentangled. Across four simulated control environments, LRC-JEPA improves average planning success over a parameter-matched JEPA baseline by 9 percentage points and matches or exceeds substantially larger pretrained models. On the real-world Bridge-v2 set, its 5.5M-parameter active encoder outperforms DINO-WM (22.1M) and V-JEPA2 (303.9M) encoders while also enabling faster planning. Physical-state probes, reconstruction interventions, and ablations confirm the effectiveness of LRC-JEPA's representation disentanglement.

</details>


### [34] [Emergence, Not Bandwidth: Physical Coupling and the Limits of Learned Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34373)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文为限速Dec-POMDP给出最优通信消息、压缩代价与协议可读性的理论刻画，并在MuJoCo耦合任务中量化强化学习与最优2比特发送器之间的差距，发现RL主要失败于协议发现而非表示或维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涌现通信研究对限速团队中消息应编码什么、跨时间压缩代价如何、以及学得协议何时可被队友解读多依赖经验观察，缺乏与学习器无关的最优基准；作者希望区分信息论极限与优化失败。

Method: 在限速Dec-POMDP中证明可达通信性能边界；在三个MuJoCo场景（零、部分、刚性物理耦合）中每决策严格2比特，并通过关闭同一场景中的物理侧信道构造判别性条件，固定身体、任务与奖励；使用25种子、7个匹配速率基线，并做热启动与跨play/协议对齐实验。

Result: 通信价值由耦合决定：刚性耦合下任何信道不优于沉默（+0.001±0.001，p=0.982，n=25）；无耦合时各条件均能解决任务；部分耦合下工程2比特发送器中位数1.000，而学习发送器仅0.482，与沉默不可区分（p=0.400，n=25）。共享字母表下带宽无法解释差距；从工程接收器热启动可达0.857，冷启动0.562（p<0.001），表明非表示或维持问题；跨play中自博弈0.980降至跨种子0.144，最佳构造对齐仍留下至少77%差距。

Conclusion: 在限速多智能体通信中，最优性能可由理论界定，RL的不足主要是未能发现可互操作协议；学习到的协议可自用但难以互相理解，因此评估涌现通信需以最优基准和跨play可读性为核心。

Abstract: Rate-limited multi-agent teams raise three questions the emergent-communication literature has answered only empirically: what an optimal message should encode, what compression costs over a horizon, and when a learned protocol is unique enough for a teammate to read. We answer them for rate-limited Dec-POMDPs, then measure how far reinforcement learning falls short of the optimum. Our theorems fix what is achievable independently of any learner, so a gap between an engineered and a learned sender at the same bit budget is an optimization fact, not an information-theoretic one. We instantiate this on three MuJoCo arenas spanning zero, partial and rigid physical coupling, charging every condition exactly 2 bits per decision, and create the discriminating regime by closing a physical side channel within one arena, holding bodies, task and reward fixed. Communication value is governed by coupling: under rigid coupling through a shared object, no channel beats silence (+0.001 +/- 0.001, p = 0.982, n = 25), since proprioception already carries that information; without coupling, every condition solves the task; under partial coupling, the engineered 2-bit sender reaches an interquartile mean of 1.000 but the learned one reaches 0.482, indistinguishable from silence (p = 0.400, n = 25). With a shared alphabet, bandwidth cannot explain the gap. Warm-starting from an engineered receiver localizes the failure: the same channel reaches 0.857 versus 0.562 cold-started (p < 0.001), so it is neither representational nor one of maintenance; reinforcement learning fails to discover the protocol. Cross-play shows learned protocols are individually meaningful but mutually unintelligible: self-play 0.980 collapses to 0.144 across seeds, and our best constructed alignment leaves at least 77% of that gap. All headline results use 25 seeds per arena and seven published baselines at matched rate.

</details>


### [35] [Safe Score Matching: Diffusion Policies with Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability for Online Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33337)
*Boyang Li,Matthew Kim,Sylvia Lee Herbert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Safe Score Matching (SSM)，将Q-score matching与Hamilton-Jacobi可达性结合用于硬约束安全强化学习，通过门控双分支分数目标训练扩散策略，在多个安全控制基准上取得更优的安全-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线安全RL需最大化奖励同时满足安全约束。既有方法多将安全松弛为软期望代价约束，并用原始-对偶拉格朗日更新，仅保证平均安全。硬状态级约束常借助HJ可达性施加，但可行域与不可行域需优化不同目标：前者奖励最大化，后者恢复至可行域。这导致目标动作分布本质多模态，而现有HJ安全RL中的高斯或确定性actor难以表达，易塌缩到次优模态。扩散策略具备表达此类分布的能力，Q-score matching可通过分数回归在线训练扩散策略，但此前仅用于奖励最大化。

Method: SSM是一种离策略actor-critic方法，将Q-score matching适配到硬约束安全RL。其核心是用HJ可达性门控双分支分数目标：在可行集内，去噪过程退化为对HJ critic判定为可行动作的Q-score matching；在可行集外，恢复分支将去噪偏置到最坏违反更低的区域。由此扩散策略可同时表示奖励最大化与恢复行为的多模态动作分布。

Result: 在四旋翼和固定翼轨迹跟踪及稳定-避障基准上，SSM取得最佳或接近最佳的任务性能，并保持低假安全率；原始-对偶基线表现出更多不安全行为，基于可达性的基线则更保守。在Safety-Gymnasium速度任务上，SSM取得最低成本，同时奖励具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SSM表明，将扩散策略的Q-score matching与HJ可达性结合，可有效应对硬约束安全RL中的多模态动作分布问题，并改善安全性与任务性能之间的权衡。

Abstract: Online safe reinforcement learning (RL) seeks policies that maximize reward while satisfying safety constraints. A popular line of research in safe RL relaxes safety to a soft expected-cost constraint and solves the resulting Constrained Markov Decision Process via primal-dual Lagrangian updates that only enforce safety on average. To address this limitation, hard, state-wise constraints are introduced and often imposed through Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability. Yet such constraints require solving different objectives in the feasible and infeasible regions: reward maximization in the former, recovery toward the feasible regions in the latter. The resulting target action distributions are inherently multimodal, and this structure poses a fundamental challenge for the Gaussian or deterministic actors used in existing HJ-based safe RL, which often collapse onto suboptimal modes. Diffusion policies provide the expressiveness needed to represent such distributions, and recent work on Q-score matching offers a route to training them for online RL by score regression -- but has been applied only to reward maximization. We propose Safe Score Matching (SSM), an off-policy actor-critic method that adapts Q-score matching to hard-constrained safe RL by gating a two-branch score target with HJ reachability: inside the feasible set, the denoising process degenerates to Q-score matching on actions classified as viable by the HJ critic; outside, a recovery branch biases denoising toward regions with lower worst-case violation. On quadrotor and fixed-wing trajectory-tracking and stabilize-and-avoid benchmarks, SSM attains the best or near-best task performance with low false-safe rates, whereas the primal-dual baseline admits more unsafe behavior and reachability-based baselines tend to be more conservative; on Safety-Gymnasium velocity tasks, SSM attains the lowest cost with competitive reward.

</details>


### [36] [When an Evaluation Rule Writes Training Labels: Measuring Human-Reference Forgiveness in NAVSIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33189)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Jingxin Yang,Jiaqi Ye,Youran Sun,Shuo Xin,Kejia Zhang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文量化 NAVSIM 中 GTRS-Dense 标签生成的“人类参考宽容”规则：当人类参考在某一指标上得分为 0 时，生成器会把该场景 16,384 个候选轨迹全部标为通过。作者禁用该覆盖后比较标签，发现其在大量场景中抹除了监督所需的候选区分度，并评估其对 lane keeping 等指标训练与评测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAVSIM 作者为单轨迹打分引入人类参考宽容，以避免惩罚语境合理的机动，但警告这可能掩盖重要失败。在标签生成阶段，该规则会把整列候选目标置为通过，可能系统性污染训练监督；因此需要测量其实际后果。

Method: 重新运行 GTRS-Dense 标签生成器并禁用覆盖，将覆盖前目标与发布标签在全部 103,288 个 navtrain 场景上比较；统计被抹除的候选区分度、按损失指标分布及 lane keeping 列中失败候选数。在 navtest 上评估发布 lane-keeping 头、随 GTRS 发布的 Hydra-MDP 检查点，以及继续训练 300 步（3 个配对种子）的 GTRS-Dense 检查点，以覆盖前结果为真值计算 AUC，并与失败候选数匹配的未宽容场景对比。另以禁用宽容方式评分，比较 lane keeping 与 EPDMS。

Result: 该规则在 11,237/103,288 个 navtrain 场景（10.8793%）抹除了训练损失读取的候选区分度；lane keeping 占 13,042 个被宽容损失列中的 9,982 个，其中位被宽容列在覆盖前有 14,391/16,384 个候选失败。navtest 上被 lane keeping 宽容的场景中，发布 lane-keeping 头对覆盖前结果的中位 AUC 为 0.7095，而匹配的未宽容场景为 0.9807；Hydra-MDP 对应为 0.6627 与 0.9761。继续训练 300 步后，使用覆盖前目标在宽容场景上的 AUC 比发布目标高 0.1086–0.1251，匹配组差距缩小但仍大于零。禁用宽容评分时，在任一五个损失指标上被宽容的 navtest 场景中 lane keeping 高 2.478–3.524 分，但相邻帧规划一致性更低；这些变化大于其余场景。EPDMS 同样评分不能区分两套目标。

Conclusion: NAVSIM 的 human-reference forgiveness 在标签生成中并非无害：它大规模抹除候选监督信号，使模型在被宽容场景上的判别能力显著下降。改用覆盖前标签可改善该短板并缩小与未宽容场景的差距，但差距仍存在；评测时禁用宽容会提高 lane keeping 分数却牺牲规划一致性，且效果依指标而异，EPDMS 对目标集不敏感。

Abstract: When the human reference scores zero on a metric, the released GTRS-Dense label generator for NAVSIM marks every candidate trajectory in the scene as passing it. NAVSIM's authors introduced this human-reference forgiveness to avoid penalizing contextually justified maneuvers when scoring one trajectory, and warned that it could overlook important failures. In label generation it sets a whole column of 16,384 candidate targets to passing. To measure the consequences for supervision, we re-run the generator with the overwrite disabled and compare the pre-overwrite targets with the released labels on all 103,288 navtrain scenes. The rule erases a candidate distinction that the training loss reads on 11,237 of them (10.8793%). Firing usually changes most of a column: lane keeping carries 9,982 of the 13,042 forgiven loss columns, and its median forgiven column had 14,391 of 16,384 candidates failing before the overwrite. On held-out navtest scenes forgiven on lane keeping, the released lane-keeping head's median AUC against the pre-overwrite outcome is 0.7095; on unforgiven scenes matched on failing-candidate count it is 0.9807. For the Hydra-MDP checkpoint released with GTRS, whose configuration takes the same label file, the two values are 0.6627 and 0.9761. Continuing the released GTRS-Dense checkpoint for 300 optimizer steps with three paired seeds, we observe the forgiven-scene AUC 0.1086-0.1251 higher with pre-overwrite than with published targets, and a narrower gap between matched groups, still above zero. Scoring with forgiveness disabled, we observe lane keeping higher by 2.478-3.524 points on navtest scenes forgiven on any of five loss metrics, with lower adjacent-frame plan consistency. Both changes are larger there than on the rest. EPDMS, scored the same way, does not separate the two target sets.

</details>


### [37] [When Does Backpropagating Through Policy Memory Matter? Physical Credit, Optimizer Updates, and Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33169)
*Xingjian Li,Yi Han,Jianhua Z. Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究带记忆策略中切断存储表示反向路径何时真正有代价：固定前向计算、仅改变导数边，考察梯度、优化器更新和继续训练。结果表明，该切断的影响取决于物理信用跨度、裁剪方式和优化器，且应以从初始化训练的表现及优化器更新来评估，而非只看原始梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带记忆策略可通过两条反向路径学习：动作产生的物理状态，以及存储的表示。Transformer-XL和截断时间反向传播（TBPTT）会保留存储历史的值，但切断其梯度路径。作者追问：这种切断在何时会显著影响学习？

Method: 在保持前向计算不变的前提下，仅改变反向图中的导数边，测量参数梯度、优化器实际施加的更新以及继续训练效果。实验包括一个Transformer船舶轨迹模型和一个四旋翼跟踪策略，比较分离键值缓存、不同截断长度以及在不同训练阶段启用记忆梯度切断。

Result: 在船舶模型中，当梯度流经所有更早物理状态时，分离键值缓存使梯度范数降至约十分之一且方向变化很小；但在单步物理信用下影响微弱。强裁剪时优化器主导更新差异：全局范数裁剪消除了大部分梯度差异，而AdamW将两种切断位置间2%的梯度差异在切换步放大为最高31%的更新差异。四旋翼从初始化训练、0.20 m/s速度噪声下，移除记忆使跟踪误差增加43%，切断记忆梯度使其增加32%；低噪声下切断的平均代价甚至超过记忆的价值。两步截断无显著增益，但隐藏速度时两步窗口可捕获大部分记忆价值；八步截断消除一半至四分之三代价。仅在训练最后五分之一启用切断，在0.20–0.30 m/s下将代价低估约三倍，但在低噪声或隐藏速度时不成立。

Conclusion: 评估记忆切断的代价时，应从一开始训练就启用该切断；比较不同反向图时，应关注优化器施加的更新，而非原始梯度。

Abstract: Policies with memory can learn along two backward paths: through the physical states their actions produce and through the representations they store. Transformer-XL and truncated backpropagation through time cut the second path at stored history while keeping its values. We ask when this cut matters. Holding the forward computation fixed and varying only derivative edges, we measure parameter gradients, the updates the optimizer applies, and continued training in a Transformer vessel-trajectory model and a quadrotor tracking policy. In the vessel model, detaching the key-value cache shrank the gradient to about a tenth of its norm, with little rotation, when gradients flowed through all earlier physical states, but barely changed it under one-step physical credit. In this strongly clipped regime the optimizer, not the gradient, set how far updates differed: global-norm clipping removed most of the gradient difference between memory-cut graphs, whereas AdamW turned a 2% gradient difference between two placements of the cut into update differences of up to 31% at the step where the placement was switched. In a quadrotor trained from initialization with 0.20 m/s velocity noise, removing memory raised tracking error by 43% and cutting memory gradients raised it by 32%; at low noise the cut's mean cost exceeded the value of memory. Two-step truncation segments gave no measurable gain, although with hidden velocity a two-step window captured most of the value of memory; eight-step segments removed half to three quarters of the cost. Switching the cut on only for the last fifth of training understated its cost about threefold at 0.20-0.30 m/s, but not at low noise or with hidden velocity. These results suggest measuring the cost of a memory cut by training with it from initialization, and comparing backward graphs by the updates the optimizer applies rather than by raw gradients.

</details>


### [38] [What Must a World Model Distinguish for Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33030)
*Rongzhe Wei,Hans Hao-Hsun Hsu,Peizhi Niu,Yifan Li,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出规划所需的世界模型不必保留所有物理细节；所需保真度取决于规划查询、候选集与规划器。作者用机制/响应/决策充分性层级形式化该差距，并在碰撞、非线性动力学和机器人规划中验证。查询条件联合生成模型在已见目标上遗憾更低，但泛化到未见目标时优势消失；因此提出模块化设计：查询决定“看哪里”，动作条件模型预测“会发生什么”，以复用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型通常追求准确预测物理后果，但规划只需在候选动作中做出足够好的选择。不同查询对物理变化及其精度的要求不同：粗决策可丢弃大量预测所需信息，细决策可能要求接近完整分辨率。此外，自适应规划中最终选择不需要的信息仍可能用于发现好候选。因此需明确世界模型应保留什么，以及查询信息应注入规划系统何处。

Method: 形式化机制充分性、响应充分性和决策充分性层级，分析查询、候选集与规划器如何共同决定哪些物理变化重要及其精度。在碰撞系统、非线性动力学和机器人规划中研究这些效应。比较查询条件联合生成动作与结果的模型、动作条件世界模型，并提出模块化设计：由查询决定搜索或关注位置，由动作条件模型预测结果，使同一预测可跨目标复用。

Result: 查询条件联合模型在已见目标上比动作条件世界模型取得更低遗憾，但该优势在泛化到未见目标时大幅消失。说明将查询信息直接耦合进结果预测不利于跨目标复用；模块化设计则能保留动作条件预测的复用性，并由查询控制候选生成或搜索。

Conclusion: 世界模型所需的充分性不是固定物理精度，而是相对于规划查询、候选集与规划器而言。设计上宜将查询信息用于“何处看/如何搜索”，将“会发生什么”留给动作条件预测，从而兼顾目标适应、跨目标泛化与预测复用。

Abstract: World models simulate the consequences of action candidates, but good planning need not preserve every physical distinction required for accurate prediction. We formalize this gap through a hierarchy of mechanism, response, and decision sufficiency. Given a candidate set, the planning query determines which physical variations matter and how precisely they must be preserved: coarse decisions can discard much of the information needed for prediction, whereas fine decisions may require nearly the same resolution. In practice, planners often adaptively search to construct candidates, and information unnecessary for final selection may still be needed to discover good candidates. What a world model must preserve therefore depends on the query, the candidate set, and the planner. We study these effects in a collision system, nonlinear dynamics, and robotic planning. These varying requirements raise a design question: where should query information enter the planning system? A model that jointly generates actions and outcomes conditioned on the query achieves lower regret than an action-conditioned world model on seen objectives, but this advantage largely disappears when generalizing to unseen objectives. Motivated by this, we propose a modular design in which the query determines where to look and an action-conditioned model predicts what will happen, allowing the same predictions to be reused across objectives.

</details>


### [39] [Constrained Flow Policy Updates: A Generalized Schrödinger Bridge View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32952)
*Boyang Li,Matthew Kim,Sylvia Herbert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RAFALE：一种基于流策略的离策略 actor-critic 安全强化学习方法，直接在流生成路径上对增广拉格朗日目标求导，避免估计 score，并在七个 Safety-Gymnasium 任务上取得竞争性奖励且满足成本预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线安全 RL 需在最大化奖励的同时满足安全约束。奖励与安全可导致多模态动作分布，传统 primal-dual 方法使用高斯 actor 易坍缩到单一子优模态，非凸拉格朗日景观优化不稳定；扩散/流策略虽可表达多模态，但已有扩散 actor 依赖估计并匹配增广拉格朗日目标策略的 score，且流策略缺少动作对数密度用于熵正则。

Method: 提出 RAFALE（Reparameterized Augmented-Lagrangian Flow Actor with Least Energy），一种离策略 actor-critic 安全 RL 方法。其核心是直接通过流策略的生成路径对增广目标求导，无需估计 score；针对流策略无动作对数密度，采用 FLAC 的无密度动能正则项。将更新表述为受约束的单端广义 Schrödinger bridge，并证明对每个源样本，该路径空间问题等价于动作空间中的熵正则问题。正噪声时，其解仅在估计成本超过由拉格朗日乘子设定的阈值处对纯奖励动作分布重新加权；噪声消失时，最优值收敛到流策略直接优化的最小能量映射目标。

Result: 在七个 Safety-Gymnasium 任务上，RAFALE 取得竞争性奖励，且每个任务的平均最终成本均在预算内；强基线则往往在奖励与成本之间取舍。消融实验支持增广目标与流 actor 二者的必要性。

Conclusion: RAFALE 通过流策略与增广拉格朗日目标的直接路径求导，为安全 RL 提供了一种无需 score 估计、可表达多模态动作分布且满足约束的离策略方法，并在多任务上验证了其有效性与关键组件必要性。

Abstract: Online safe reinforcement learning (RL) seeks policies that maximize reward while satisfying safety constraints. Reward and safety can induce multimodal action distributions, challenging the prevailing primal-dual methods: Gaussian actors may collapse onto a single suboptimal mode, and optimization over the nonconvex Lagrangian landscape can be unstable. Diffusion and flow policies can represent such distributions, but recent work with a diffusion actor relies on estimating and matching the score of an augmented-Lagrangian target policy. Instead, we differentiate the augmented objective directly through the generation path of a flow policy, so no score needs to be estimated. Because a flow policy lacks a readily available action log-density for entropy regularization, we build on the density-free kinetic-energy regularizer of FLAC, a recent reward-only method, and propose Reparameterized Augmented-Lagrangian Flow Actor with Least Energy (RAFALE), an off-policy actor-critic method for safe RL. We formulate its update as a constrained one-ended generalized Schrödinger bridge and show that, for each source draw, this path-space problem is exactly an entropy-regularized problem in action space. At positive noise, its solution reweights the reward-only action distribution only where the estimated cost exceeds a threshold set by the Lagrange multiplier. As the noise vanishes, the optimal value converges to that of a least-energy map objective that the flow policy optimizes directly. Across seven Safety-Gymnasium tasks, RAFALE achieves competitive reward with mean final cost within budget on every task, whereas strong baselines trade one for the other; ablations support the necessity of both its augmented objective and its flow actor.

</details>


### [40] [Optimizing H-Graph Hybridization for Diffusion-Guided RRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32897)
*Omer Talmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对固定的预训练 DiTree 扩散运动规划器，提出因子多样性与仅精修多样性两种推理时多样化策略，并通过 H-Graph 混合，在 15 个 AntMaze 场景中显著缩短路径长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的采样运动规划器单次运行即可生成高质量轨迹，但推理阶段的随机多样性尚未被充分利用。

Method: 对固定的预训练 DiTree 模型提出两种推理时多样化策略：因子多样性扫描随机种子与 Diffusion Goal Bias（DGB）参数；仅精修多样性扫描扩散精修强度（RS），控制 RRT 生成轨迹被编辑的幅度。两者通过 H-Graph 混合，并在 holonomic AntMaze 机器人的 15 个迷宫场景中评估。由于单次运行基线仅部分成功，还将 H-Graph 结果与基于池的统计进行对比。

Result: H-Graph 使因子多样性与仅精修多样性的平均池长度分别提升 18.8% 与 14.5%，最佳个体候选长度分别提升 9.7% 与 6.8%；与成功基线的轨迹长度相比，分别提升 18.2% 与 19.9%。

Conclusion: 推理时参数变化是可靠且免训练的路径多样性来源，H-Graph 混合能可靠地将该多样性转化为更短、更高质量的轨迹。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planners guided by diffusion models produce high-quality trajectories in a single run, yet the stochastic diversity available at inference time is left largely unexploited. We present two inference-time diversification strategies for a fixed, pretrained DiTree model, combined via H-Graph hybridization, and evaluate them on a holonomic AntMaze robot across 15 maze scenarios. The first, factorial diversity, sweeps the random seed and Diffusion Goal Bias (DGB) parameter, the second, refinement-only diversity, sweeps the diffusion refinement strength (RS) that controls how much an RRT-generated trajectory is edited. Because a single-run baseline only partially succeeds, we additionally compare H-Graph results with pool-based statistics. H-Graph improves the mean pool length of the factorial and refinement-only diversities by 18.8% and 14.5%, respectively. In addition, it also improves the best individual candidate's lengths by 9.7% and 6.8%, respectively. And last, compared with the successful baseline's trajectory length, it improves the results by 18.2% and 19.9%, respectively. These results show that inference-time parameter variation is a reliable, training-free source of path diversity, and that H-Graph hybridization reliably converts this diversity into shorter, higher quality trajectories.

</details>


### [41] [SAMBAR: Selective Anchoring via Method of Multipliers for Balanced Knowledge Acquisition and Retention in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32108)
*Aayushi Shrivastava,Xunlan Zhou,Hongrui Zhao,Ziyu Chen,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAMBAR 是一种无需旧任务演示数据的 VLA 持续学习算法，通过约束优化与方法乘子缓解微调中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略需持续学习新任务，但朴素微调会导致灾难性遗忘并损害通用能力；现有持续学习方法多依赖重放旧任务数据，而旧演示数据并非总是可用。

Method: 将持续学习建模为约束优化问题，并用方法乘子求解：利用对偶变量随约束违反累积而增大惩罚，避免参数远离旧值；同时选择性锚定对旧任务关键的参数，保留旧知识并释放其他参数用于新任务学习，从而平衡知识获取与保持。

Result: 在 LIBERO 仿真基准和真实硬件上评估；顺序微调 VLA 时，所有无重放基线均完全遗忘第一个任务，而 SAMBAR 能保留已学习的每个任务。

Conclusion: SAMBAR 可在不访问任何旧任务演示的情况下实现 VLA 持续微调，并有效防止灾难性遗忘，兼顾新任务学习与旧任务保留。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale pretraining to ultimately achieve generalist manipulation. Deployed VLA policies must support continual learning to acquire new tasks over time. Teaching a VLA a new task generally requires finetuning it on demonstrations of that task. However, naively finetuning on downstream tasks causes the policy to forget earlier tasks and degrades generalist capabilities. This failure is known as catastrophic forgetting. Most continual learning methods counter it by replaying data from earlier tasks. However, the old task demonstrations are not always readily available. In this paper, we introduce SAMBAR, a continual learning algorithm that prevents catastrophic forgetting during VLA finetuning without requiring access to the demonstrations of any previously learned task. We propose to cast continual learning as a constrained optimization problem and solve it with the method of multipliers. In our approach, the method of multipliers drives the policy to learn the new task without the model parameters drifting far away from their previous values. In contrast to a standard regularization penalty, the method of multipliers raises the penalty as the constraint violation accumulates by using a dual variable. We also selectively anchor the parameters critical to previous tasks to preserve past knowledge, leaving other parameters free for new task acquisition. The combination of dual variable and selective anchoring, therefore, balances knowledge acquisition with knowledge retention. We evaluate our method, SAMBAR, on the LIBERO simulation benchmark and on hardware. When sequentially finetuning on a VLA, every replay-free baseline we compare against completely forgets the first task it learned, whereas SAMBAR retains every task it has learned.

</details>


### [42] [Grounding Vision-Language Models in Driving Semantics: A Multi-Dataset Predicate Framework for Explainable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31636)
*Mohamed Chouai,Fazli Faruk Okumus,Stefan Kugele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种确定性的多数据集谓词框架，从可测量的几何、运动学、时序、地图与交通控制证据中推导驾驶场景语义，并构建统一的谓词知识图谱，以提升视觉语言模型驾驶场景理解的语义可验证性、一致性与可追踪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型越来越多用于驾驶场景理解，但其输出中的语义关系往往难以与底层交通情境进行核验，缺乏确定性、跨数据集一致性和可追溯的语义表示。

Method: 该框架以确定性谓词定义驾驶场景语义，输入证据包括几何、运动学、时序、地图和交通控制信息；仅通过数据集特定接口恢复所需场景信息，而谓词定义在 nuPlan 与 nuScenes 间保持不变，并物化为统一的 Predicate Knowledge Graph。使用人工标注的谓词关系进行定量语义验证，每数据集抽取 200 个场景；并在 NuPlanQA 的九个子任务上使用冻结的 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 模型评估 Predicate KG。

Result: 语义验证宏 F1 分别为 nuPlan 0.94、nuScenes 0.93，共享谓词的平均跨数据集差异为 0.02。在 NuPlanQA 九个子任务中，谓词 grounding 在七个子任务上取得所评估视觉输入条件中的最高准确率，包括 Traffic Light（53.2% 提升至 71.5%）、Situation Assessment（76.2% 提升至 86.1%）和 Action Recommendation（82.9% 提升至 89.0%）；Weather/Lighting 基本不变（89.4% vs. 88.8%），与缺少对应谓词一致；Predicate KG 仅输入在九个子任务中的八个优于 metadata-only 输入。

Conclusion: 确定性谓词能够提供一致且可追踪的语义表示，并且在 oracle grounding 条件下，可降低谓词词汇表所覆盖推理任务对视觉输入的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for driving-scene understanding, yet the semantic relations expressed in their outputs are often difficult to verify against the underlying traffic situation. This paper introduces a deterministic multi-dataset predicate framework that derives driving-scene semantics from measurable geometric, kinematic, temporal, map, and traffic-control evidence. Dataset-specific interfaces are used only to recover the required scene information, while predicate definitions remain unchanged across nuPlan and nuScenes and are materialised in a common Predicate Knowledge Graph. Quantitative semantic validation against manually annotated predicate relations on 200 scenarios from each dataset yields macro F1 scores of 0.94 on nuPlan and 0.93 on nuScenes, with an average cross-dataset difference of 0.02 across the shared predicates. The Predicate KG is further evaluated using a frozen LLaVA-OneVision-7B model on the nine NuPlanQA subtasks. Predicate grounding achieves the highest accuracy among the evaluated visual-input conditions in seven of nine NuPlanQA subtasks, including Traffic Light (53.2% to 71.5%), Situation Assessment (76.2% to 86.1%), and Action Recommendation (82.9% to 89.0%). Weather/Lighting remains essentially unchanged (89.4% vs. 88.8%), consistent with the absence of corresponding predicates, while Predicate KG only input outperforms metadata-only input in eight of nine subtasks. The results show that deterministic predicates provide a consistent and traceable semantic representation and, under oracle grounding, can reduce visual dependence for reasoning tasks covered by the predicate vocabulary.

</details>

### [43] [Task-Oriented Communications for Edge-Assisted Multi-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35173)
*Zhengru Fang,Huanhuan Lou,Senkang Hu,Yihang Tao,Zongdian Li,Yiqin Deng,Jingjing Wang,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种网络自适应的任务导向通信框架，用于UAV/UGV在无卫星定位环境下，通过按需卸载视觉语义匹配实现绝对定位，兼顾定位精度与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV/UGV在城市峡谷、室内、干扰或欺骗环境中易丢失卫星定位，需依赖与地理标签数据库的视觉匹配；但机载算力与能量受限，且无线吞吐时变，因此需决定何时卸载、传哪些视图与语义速率、服务哪个客户端。

Method: 框架联合优化卸载决策、视图/语义速率选择与客户端调度，融合可扩展正交正则化变分信息瓶颈（O-VIB）编码、基于信息价值（VOI）的请求控制、VOI加权Lyapunov调度；O-VIB支持重要性排序的潜前缀和不同视图子集，仅当预测定位风险降低超过通信与服务成本时才请求边缘协助。

Result: 在CARLA多视图UAV数据、匹配每路线流量预算下，VOI控制相比预算化周期卸载将平均与p95路线误差降低24.8%和31.0%；真实室内UAV/UGV实验中，相比未压缩全视图CLIP检索，平均位置误差降低28.0%和14.4%，描述符流量减少98.6%和98.2%；高拥塞下，价值感知整形将Top-10%高价值请求的边缘p95延迟从137.7 ms降至32.8 ms，降低76.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，任务导向、价值感知的通信与计算协同可在显著压缩语义传输的同时提升定位鲁棒性，并在高拥塞下优先保障高价值请求的时延性能。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) often lose satellite positioning in urban canyons, indoor facilities, and jammed or spoofed environments, making vision-based matching with geo-tagged databases important for absolute positioning. However, limited onboard computation and energy often require localization to be offloaded over wireless links with time-varying throughput. We present a network-adaptive task-oriented communication framework that jointly determines when to offload, which views and semantic rate to transmit, and which client to serve. The framework combines scalable orthogonality-regularized variational information bottleneck (O-VIB) encoding, value-of-information (VOI)-guided request control, and VOI-weighted Lyapunov scheduling. O-VIB supports importance-ordered latent prefixes and different view subsets, while edge assistance is requested only when its predicted reduction in localization risk exceeds the communication and service cost. Under a matched per-route traffic budget, VOI-guided control reduces mean and p95 route errors by 24.8% and 31.0% over budgeted periodic offloading on CARLA multi-view UAV data. In real-world indoor UAV and UGV experiments, the pipeline reduces mean position error by 28.0% and 14.4% over uncompressed all-view CLIP retrieval while cutting descriptor traffic by 98.6% and 98.2%, respectively. Under high congestion, value-aware shaping reduces edge-side p95 latency for the top-10% high-value requests by 76.2%, from 137.7 ms to 32.8 ms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [44] [JRDB-AVR: An Active Visual Reasoning Benchmark for Embodied Agents in Real-World Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35032)
*Zhixi Cai,Fucai Ke,Sukai Huang,Maria Garcia de la Banda,Peter J. Stuckey,Gholamreza Haffari,Hamid Rezatofighi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 JRDB-AVR，一个面向具身主动视觉推理的基准，要求智能体按时间戳和视角主动请求有限观测，并同时评估最终答案与所依赖的视觉证据；同时提出参考方法 JRDB-AVR-Agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身视觉推理中，智能体视野有限，回答所需证据可能分布在时间、视角和交互对象之间。现有视觉推理基准多评估被动观测和最终答案，忽略主动推理与证据获取，导致模型可能未观察到关键对象、时间或视角就给出看似合理的答案。

Method: 基于真实 JRDB 机器人数据，通过结构化问题生成引擎构建 JRDB-AVR 基准。智能体接收视觉推理问题后，可按时间戳和视角请求有界观测；评估同时考察最终答案与支撑答案的 grounded visual evidence。基准包含多真实环境中的问题，涉及时间搜索、视角选择和以人为中心的组合推理。作者还提出 JRDB-AVR-Agent，作为参考主动推理智能体方法，维护显式、观测接地的图式世界模型，并通过求解完成回答。

Result: 实验显示，当前基线的答案准确率与证据准确率之间存在显著差距；当前 VLM 能产生缺乏证据支持的正确答案，说明仅看最终答案会高估具身视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: 对于具身视觉推理，必须采用主动且证据感知的评估方式，以衡量模型是否真正获取并利用支撑答案的视觉证据。

Abstract: In complex embodied visual reasoning scenarios, an agent often has only a limited field of view, and the evidence needed to answer a question may be distributed across time, viewpoint, and interacting objects. A model may therefore give a plausible answer without ever observing the relevant object, time, or view that supports it. Current visual reasoning benchmarks largely evaluate passive observations and final answers, overlooking settings that require active reasoning and evidence acquisition. We introduce JRDB-AVR, a benchmark derived from existing real-world JRDB robotics data through a structured question-generation engine that turns this gap into an explicit evaluation: an embodied agentic system receives a visual reasoning question, requests bounded observations by timestamp and viewing angle, and is evaluated on both the final answer and the grounded visual evidence supporting it. The benchmark contains diverse questions over multiple real-world environments involving temporal search, viewpoint selection, and human-oriented compositional reasoning. We also introduce JRDB-AVR-Agent, a reference active reasoning agentic method that maintains an explicit observation-grounded graph-based world model and answers through solving. Experiments reveal a substantial gap between answer accuracy and evidence accuracy in current baselines, showing that current VLMs can produce unsupported correct answers and that active evidence-aware evaluation is necessary for embodied visual reasoning. Code and benchmark are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Does Adversarial Training Improve Generalization in Multi-View VLAs? Revealing and Mitigating View Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33707)
*Futa Waseda,Shuhei Kurita,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗训练（AT）对多视图视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在自然分布偏移下泛化能力的影响，揭示“视角崩溃”与“鲁棒性捷径”，并表明AT带来的是选择性而非通用鲁棒性收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽在分布内表现良好，但在部署偏移下常退化。对抗训练可在不显式预知具体偏移的情况下提升模型自适应鲁棒性，但其对多视图VLA的自然分布偏移泛化究竟有何影响尚不清楚，尤其是跨视图依赖与鲁棒感知/融合之间的区别未被充分研究。

Method: 使用一个直接由预训练VLM适配而来的多视图VLA，在LIBERO-Plus的七个偏移轴上评估泛化；先进行直接对抗训练，再通过受控视角干预分析跨视图依赖，并引入简单的View Swap干预以降低固定视角依赖，随后重新评估AT效果。

Result: 直接AT显著改善Camera Viewpoint和Sensor Noise这两个仅影响第三人称视图的偏移，但对其他偏移效果不一甚至为负；受控视角干预暴露出“视角崩溃”：AT可能使策略过度依赖腕部视图，形成“鲁棒性捷径”，即对偏移视图的表观鲁棒性来自减少使用该视图而非更鲁棒的感知。采用View Swap后，AT进一步改善Camera Viewpoint、Sensor Noise和Robot Initial State，但在其他偏移上效果仍混合。

Conclusion: 多视图鲁棒性需要区分“鲁棒感知”（在视图内偏移下提取可靠信息）与“鲁棒融合”（根据各视图可靠性调整依赖）；AT提供的分布偏移收益是选择性的而非通用的，因此评估多视图VLA鲁棒性时必须分离感知改善与跨视图依赖变化。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for closed-loop robot control, transferring their perceptual and semantic capabilities to action prediction. Despite strong in-distribution performance, however, VLAs often degrade under deployment shifts. Adversarial training (AT) offers a model-adaptive approach to robustness without explicitly anticipating individual shifts, but its effect on natural distribution-shift generalization in multi-view VLAs remains unclear. We study this question using a multi-view VLA directly adapted from a pretrained VLM and evaluate generalization across seven LIBERO-Plus shift axes. Direct AT substantially improves Camera Viewpoint and Sensor Noise, the two shifts affecting only the third-person view, yet produces mixed or negative effects on other shifts. Controlled view interventions reveal a surprising failure mode that we term view collapse: Direct AT can shift cross-view reliance so strongly that the policy becomes dominated by the wrist view. This exposes a \textit{robustness shortcut}: apparent robustness to a shifted view can arise from reduced use of that view rather than more robust perception of it. This motivates a distinction between robust perception, extracting reliable information under within-view shifts, and robust fusion, adapting reliance across views according to their reliability. To reduce fixed view reliance, we use a simple View Swap intervention and then re-evaluate AT. With View Swap, AT further improves Camera Viewpoint, Sensor Noise, and Robot Initial State, while its effects remain mixed on other shifts. Our results show that multi-view robustness requires separating improved perception from changes in cross-view reliance, and that AT provides selective rather than generic distribution-shift benefits.

</details>


### [46] [Feedback Makes Perfect: A Closed-Loop Framework for NL-to-STL Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33287)
*Bowen Ye,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作将自然语言到信号时序逻辑（NL-to-STL）的翻译重构为闭环反馈过程：先生成STL，再回译为自然语言供用户检查，并用自然语言修正迭代修订，直到用户接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次、开环的NL-to-STL翻译存在准确率上限。自然语言本身有歧义，且用户写下的内容未必完全反映其真实意图，因此目标规约并不完全包含在输入文本中。

Method: 提出闭环NL-to-STL框架：每个生成的STL公式被反向翻译为自然语言供用户核对；用户通过自然语言纠错驱动公式修订，直至接受规约，用户无需读写形式语法。框架基于反馈控制中的不对称性：从歧义语言到形式逻辑的前向路径困难且易错，而从结构化STL回到语言的反馈路径可做到高度精确，从而以精确反馈路径使不精确前向路径获得精确闭环行为。

Result: 在500条专家编写的需求和7个LLM上实验。回译解释与专家判断一致率达99.5%。闭环精化将强模型从约89%的开环准确率提升至98.0--99.2%，对弱模型提升超过30个百分点（如17.6%提升至48.0%）。消融表明增益来自反馈的语义内容，而非重复尝试。专家审计和280次会话的用户研究进一步证实闭环可靠性。同时识别出能力阈值，超过该阈值后反馈不再带来帮助。

Conclusion: 将NL-to-STL翻译视为闭环反馈可有效突破开环准确率上限，但反馈收益受模型能力阈值限制；该框架让用户无需掌握形式语法即可获得更可靠的STL规约。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) enables rigorous verification and control of cyber-physical systems, but writing correct specifications requires expertise that most requirement holders lack. Large language models can translate natural-language (NL) requirements into STL, yet stronger translators alone approach an accuracy ceiling. We argue that this ceiling stems from how the task is posed: one-shot, open-loop translation is somewhat ill-defined. Natural language is ambiguous, and, more fundamentally, what a person writes may not always be what they intend, so the target specification is not fully contained in the input text. We therefore reformulate NL-to-STL translation as a closed-loop feedback process. Each generated formula is translated back into natural language for the user to check, and natural-language corrections drive revision until the user accepts the specification. Users never read or write formal syntax. This framework rests on an asymmetry familiar from feedback control theory. The forward path, from ambiguous language to formal logic, is hard and error-prone. The feedback path, from structured STL back to language, can be made highly precise, and a precise feedback path lets an imprecise forward path achieve precise closed-loop behavior. Experiments on 500 expert-authored requirements and seven LLMs support this view. Back-translated explanations agree with expert judgments in 99.5\% of cases. Closed-loop refinement raises strong models from about 89\% open-loop accuracy to 98.0--99.2\%, and yields gains of over 30 percentage points for weaker models (e.g., 17.6\%$\rightarrow$48.0\%). Ablations show these gains come from the semantic content of the feedback rather than from repeated attempts. An expert audit and a 280-session user study further confirm the reliability of the loop. We also identify a capability threshold above which feedback no longer helps.

</details>


### [47] [PlanGuard: A Guardrail for Multi-Step Plan Safety in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32801)
*Junchi Chen,Changtao Miao,Yuxiao Xiang,Zhenchao Jin,Haojie Yuan,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,He Liu,Bo Zhang,Jiansheng Cai,Zhe Li,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PlanGuard，首个在执行前评估完整多步具身任务计划在 current environment 中物理安全性的检测器，并构建 MSP-Safe 数据集与 STAC-OPD 蒸馏方法，使 2B 小模型实现高效整计划物理风险检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防护忽视多步计划中由子任务依赖与环境交互引发的组合式物理风险：通用 guardrails 关注语义伤害，具身安全检测器则孤立评估子任务。因此需要一种能在执行前评估完整多步计划物理安全性的方法。

Method: 提出 PlanGuard，执行前评估完整多步计划在当前环境中的物理安全性。构建 MSP-Safe 数据集：配对任务构造、多种 planner 生成计划、三位 judges 安全标注。先用任务导向 SFT 建立基础计划安全评估能力；再提出 STAC-OPD（Strong-Teacher Adaptive Compensation for On-Policy Distillation），在学生 on-policy 轨迹上提供自适应强教师监督，结合来自微调强教师的 token-level 分布迁移与 probability-routed sequence-level compensation：学生倾向参考安全决策时保留学生目标，否则使用教师重构目标。

Result: PlanGuard-2B 在所有测试子集上平均达到 87.15% ACC 与 87.21% F1，证明紧凑模型规模下也能有效进行整计划物理风险检测。

Conclusion: PlanGuard 弥补了多步具身计划组合式物理安全检测的空白，并通过 MSP-Safe 与 STAC-OPD 让小模型具备接近强教师的安全评估能力。代码与数据集将公开释放。

Abstract: Embodied task planners may produce multi-step plans whose subtask dependencies and interactions with the environment create physical risks during execution. Yet existing safeguards overlook such compositional risks, as general-purpose guardrails focus on semantic harm and embodied safety detectors assess subtasks in isolation. To address this gap, we introduce PlanGuard, the first pre-execution detector that evaluates the physical safety of a complete multi-step plan in its current environment. For training and evaluation, we construct a Multi-Step Plan Safety (MSP-Safe) dataset through paired task construction, plan generation using diverse planners, and safety annotation by three judges. Task-oriented SFT on MSP-Safe establishes fundamental plan-safety assessment capabilities, yet a substantial gap remains between compact models suitable for real-time deployment and stronger but costlier large models. Accordingly, we propose Strong-Teacher Adaptive Compensation for On-Policy Distillation (STAC-OPD), which provides compact models with adaptive strong-teacher supervision along their on-policy trajectories. It combines token-level distribution transfer from a fine-tuned strong teacher with probability-routed sequence-level compensation, retaining student-generated targets when the student favors the reference safety decision and using teacher-reconstructed targets otherwise. Across all test subsets, PlanGuard-2B achieves average 87.15% ACC and 87.21% F1, demonstrating effective whole-plan physical-risk detection at compact model scale. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [48] [Are Vision-Language-Action Models Robust to One-Step Observation Perturbations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32550)
*Shojiro Yamabe,Jun Sakuma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究VLA模型对单步瞬时观测扰动的鲁棒性，发现此类扰动会显著降低性能且影响与动作块执行长度相关，并提出CARE动态选择执行长度以提升鲁棒性并保持干净性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA安全研究主要关注贯穿整个回合的持续扰动，而回合内仅短暂出现的瞬时观测损坏仍未被充分探索，这对VLA在物理世界部署的安全风险评估至关重要。

Method: 在每回合单个时间步施加一步扰动，评估VLA鲁棒性，并分析动作块执行长度对扰动影响的作用；据此提出CARE，依据与先前预测动作块的一致性动态选择执行长度，仅在扰动下选择更短执行长度。

Result: 实验表明，单步扰动会显著降低VLA性能，且其影响取决于动作块执行长度；CARE以较低计算开销提升鲁棒性，同时通过仅在受扰动时缩短执行长度来保持干净环境下的性能。

Conclusion: 瞬时观测损坏是VLA部署中不可忽视的安全威胁，动作块执行长度是影响鲁棒性的关键因素，CARE为高效动态调整执行长度、兼顾鲁棒性与干净性能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Understanding the safety risks of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for their deployment in the physical world. Existing safety research has mainly considered persistent perturbations that are applied continuously to observations throughout an episode. However, momentary observation corruption, in which observations are severely perturbed only briefly within an episode, remains an underexplored safety threat. To address this gap, this work investigates robustness to one-step perturbations applied at a single time step per episode. Our experiments reveal that these perturbations substantially degrade VLA performance and that their impact depends on the action chunk execution length. Based on them, we propose CARE, which dynamically selects the execution length based on consistency with the previously predicted action chunk. CARE improves robustness with low computational overhead while preserving clean performance by selecting shorter execution lengths only under perturbations.

</details>


### [49] [Metro-WM: Long-Horizon Latent Planning with Realisable Sub-Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31868)
*Royson Lee,Fady Rezk,Titouan Parcollet,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JEPA 模型预测控制擅长短时域零样本目标到达，但长时域规划受限；分层方法通过宏规划器预测中间隐子目标，而无约束隐子目标预测存在根本缺陷。Metro-WM 改为从离线经验中检索真实状态作为子目标，并在全图上规划与重规划，从而显著提升长时域性能、速度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 JEPA 的分层规划器通常让宏规划器生成未落地的隐向量子目标，但这些子目标可能物理不可实现，导致长时域规划不可靠。因此需要让子目标 grounded 于真实可观测状态，并增强对执行误差的恢复能力。

Method: 提出 Metro-WM：构建以离线专家演示或随机动作轨迹中观测帧为顶点的图，连接不同 episode 的帧并拼接为通向目标的路线；子目标通过检索图中真实状态获得，而非生成隐向量。规划在全图上进行，当微规划器偏离时，可立即从当前状态重新寻找最优路径。

Result: 实验表明，Metro-WM 相比次优分层方法将长时域成功率提升最多 37.33 个百分点，速度提升最多 10.9 倍，离线计算减少 13–56 倍，且调参更少。额外分析显示其能找到比离线演示更短的路径，优于依赖查询自身演示的 oracle，并在极度稀疏数据下仍保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 将子目标约束为先前经验中的真实状态，可解决无约束隐子目标预测的根本缺陷；Metro-WM 通过图检索与全图重规划，在长时域目标到达中实现了更高成功率、更高效率、更少计算与更强鲁棒性。

Abstract: Model-predictive control with Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) provides a strong zero-shot goal-reaching planner, but it is only effective over short planning horizons. Hierarchical extensions attempt to bridge this gap by learning a macro planner to predict intermediate latent sub-goals to guide the micro planner. In this work, we demonstrate that unconstrained latent sub-goal prediction is fundamentally flawed. A rigorous evaluation reveals that a leading state-of-the-art macro planner routinely emits physically unrealisable sub-goals. To resolve this, we introduce Metro-WM, a hierarchical framework that issues sub-goals by retrieving genuine states from prior experience rather than generating ungrounded latent vectors. Specifically, Metro-WM constructs a graph whose vertices are observed frames from offline expert demonstrations or random-action trajectories, allowing frames from different episodes to be connected and stitched into routes to the goal. Planning over the full graph also makes the system highly robust to execution errors: if the micro planner drifts off course, Metro-WM instantly finds a new optimal path from the current state. Our experiments show that Metro-WM achieves superior long-horizon success rates of up to 37.33 percentage points over the next best hierarchical approach while being up to 10.9 times faster, requiring both 13-56 times less offline compute and fewer tuned hyperparameters. Additional analysis reveals that Metro-WM finds shorter paths than the offline demonstrations, outperforms an oracle relying on the query's own demonstration, and maintains robust performance under extremely sparse dataset conditions.

</details>


### [50] [ConflictVLA-Bench: Benchmarking Behavioral Responses of Vision-Language-Action Models to Premise Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31792)
*Liyu Hou,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ConflictVLA-Bench，研究VLA对无效任务前提的响应，揭示“失败坚持”（Failed Persistence）现象：模型失败并非停止追求，而是继续接近原目标后出错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA评估多关注终局任务结果，但任务失败本身无法区分行为脱离与继续追求原目标后执行失败，因此需要同时评估结果与执行过程。

Method: 基于LIBERO构建ConflictVLA-Bench，包含2,826个提示条件冲突任务，覆盖四类冲突、四种结构配置和两种提示条件；为冲突rollout配对前提一致的参考rollout，并评估任务结果与执行过程。

Result: 在八个VLA上，无效前提使原目标完成率至少下降17.3个百分点，OpenVLA下降达56.2个百分点；即使前提一致任务成功、匹配冲突任务失败，模型仍常继续接近原目标、保留早期轨迹结构，且动作幅度抑制有限。显式前提检查也未稳定产生选择性和协调的行为变化。

Conclusion: 终局失败既不能证明行为脱离，也不能证明拒绝；仅凭结果不足以评估VLA，需结合执行过程与配对参照来分析其对无效前提的响应。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models perform strongly on manipulation tasks, their responses to invalid task premises remain underexplored. Existing evaluations of premise conflicts often focus on terminal task outcomes, yet task failure alone cannot distinguish behavioral disengagement from continued pursuit followed by an execution error. We call the latter pattern Failed Persistence. To study this phenomenon, we introduce ConflictVLA-Bench, which pairs conflict rollouts with premise-consistent reference rollouts and evaluates both outcomes and execution processes. Built on LIBERO, the benchmark contains 2,826 prompt-conditioned conflict tasks spanning four conflict families, four structural configurations, and two prompt conditions. Across all eight VLAs, invalid premises reduce original goal completion by at least 17.3 percentage points, with the reduction reaching 56.2 percentage points for OpenVLA. Crucially, even when models succeed on premise-consistent tasks and fail on their matched conflict tasks, they often continue to approach the original targets, retain early trajectory structure, and show limited action magnitude suppression. Failed Persistence therefore recurs across the evaluated models. Explicit premise checking does not consistently produce selective and coordinated behavioral changes. These findings show that terminal failure alone establishes neither behavioral disengagement nor refusal and that outcomes alone are insufficient for VLA evaluation. Experimental data and additional details are available on the project page: this https URL

</details>

### [51] [Beyond Isolated Entities: Relation-Aware Multi-Entity Modeling for Unsupervised Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31753)
*Zhongpeng Pan,Xina Cheng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出INTERACT，一个面向巡逻机器人与监控系统的无监督视频异常检测框架，通过人物-物体交互建模、关系预测与一致性检验，解决实体单独正常但关系异常的问题，在ShanghaiTech上达到84.5%帧级AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素重建和孤立实体方法在个体实体看似正常、但其空间或运动关系异常时容易失效，难以识别熟悉实体间的异常交互。

Method: INTERACT包含三个核心模块：POAMI联合建模每帧中人和物体的外观、运动与空间配置，捕获个体状态及跨实体上下文；TGRIP基于历史关系记忆和目标帧几何预测目标实体交互状态，无需显式身份跟踪；MIRA通过条件流重建和语义一致性检查评估预测，提供超越预测误差的互补异常证据。

Result: INTERACT在ShanghaiTech基准上取得84.5%帧级AUC的SOTA性能；消融研究表明移除跨实体注意力导致最大性能下降；该方法对人与物体关系变化引起的异常尤其有效，并在一般场景中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 关系建模对视频异常检测至关重要；INTERACT通过交互中心的关系分析与一致性检验有效检测关系型异常，源代码将公开。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection for patrol robots and surveillance systems must recognize abnormal interactions among familiar entities. Existing pixel-reconstruction and isolated-entity methods may fail when individual entities appear normal but their spatial or motion relations are abnormal. This work presents Interaction-Centric Network for Temporal Entity-Relation Analysis and Consistency Testing (INTERACT), a framework for unsupervised video anomaly detection. Person-Object Appearance-Motion Interaction (POAMI) moves beyond isolated-entity encoding by jointly modeling the appearance, motion, and spatial configurations of persons and objects in each frame. The resulting representations capture both individual entity states and their cross-entity context. Based on these representations, Target Geometry-Guided Relational Interaction Prediction (TGRIP) predicts target entity interaction states from historical relational memory and target-frame geometry without explicit identity tracking. Motion-Interaction Reconstruction and Alignment (MIRA) then evaluates these predictions through conditional flow reconstruction and semantic consistency checking, providing complementary anomaly evidence beyond prediction error alone. INTERACT achieves state-of-the-art performance, obtaining a frame-level AUC of 84.5% on the ShanghaiTech benchmark. Ablation studies show that removing cross-entity attention causes the largest performance drop, demonstrating the necessity of relational modeling. INTERACT is particularly effective for anomalies caused by changes in relations among people and objects, while maintaining competitive performance in general scenarios. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>

### [52] [Vision-Language Agents for Active Perception in Optics Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32918)
*Ryan Lopez,Sachin Vaidya,Seou Choi,Serena Landers,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究以光学实验为测试平台，检验通用视觉语言模型能否仅凭视觉反馈和交互历史，在无工程化标量目标的情况下闭环控制实验室实验；结果表明，在任务特定自然语言引导下，VLM可估计执行器响应、通过干预消歧并主动获取稀疏信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型正日益用于科学工作流，但其作为智能体直接从视觉反馈控制实验室实验的能力仍未被充分探索。许多实验任务天然缺乏密集、预定义的数值目标，反馈信号可能稀疏、间歇或视觉上模糊；因此，通用实验智能体应能解释视觉观察、采取行动获取有用反馈，并根据行动后果调整行为。

Method: 以实验光学为测试平台，在三个隔离不同能力的系统上评估智能体：迈克尔逊干涉仪、双镜腔和四镜光学中继。智能体观察相机图像，直接发出执行器与测量命令，并保留交互历史；控制过程中不接收工程化标量目标。研究结合实验与匹配仿真，并给予任务特定自然语言引导。

Result: 在各项实验与匹配仿真中，VLM能够估计执行器-响应关系，通过干预解决模糊观察，并在信号稀疏时主动创建有信息量的视觉反馈。

Conclusion: 预训练多模态模型可作为实验闭环中的重要决策智能体；该工作也确立了光学作为科学智能体视觉推理与主动感知的物理基础测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being used in scientific workflows, but their ability as agents to directly control laboratory experiments from visual feedback remains underexplored. This capability is important because many laboratory tasks do not naturally provide dense, pre-defined numerical objectives: informative signals can be sparse, intermittent, or visually ambiguous. A more general laboratory agent should instead be able to interpret visual observations, take actions to acquire useful feedback, and adapt its behavior based on the consequences of those actions. We study whether general-purpose VLMs can perform this kind of closed-loop scientific control using experimental optics as a testbed. We evaluate agents on three experimental systems that isolate distinct capabilities: a Michelson interferometer, a two-mirror cavity, and a four-mirror optical relay. The agents observe camera images, directly issue actuator and measurement commands, and retain their interaction history without receiving an engineered scalar objective during control. Across these experiments and matched simulations, we find that, given task-specific natural-language guidance, VLMs can estimate actuator-response relationships, resolve ambiguous observations through intervention, and actively create informative visual feedback when signals are sparse. These results suggest that pretrained multimodal models can serve as important decision-making agents within the experimental loop. Our work also establishes optics as a physically grounded testbed for visual reasoning and active perception in scientific agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [53] [What Stops Recursive Self-Improvement in Robotics? Lessons from 123 Rounds of Agentic Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31760)
*Jiaming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个可自我改进的机器人智能体，并在家务操作任务上运行123轮改进，发现智能体能自主发现并部署新能力，但改进无法累积，目标任务始终失败；主要瓶颈不在智能体本身，而在关系感知、技能链学习和评测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究机器人能否像当前的编码智能体改进软件那样改进自身，即在无需人类编写机器人代码的情况下，通过失败驱动自主发现缺失能力、编写技能或引入外部模型，并持续迭代提升。

Method: 构建一个智能体系统：观察机器人失败，推断缺失能力，编写新技能或寻找并安装外部模型，在仿真中测试每次改动，然后重复。在家庭操作任务上运行123轮改进，目标任务是“将调料放到冰箱顶层货架”。

Result: 智能体能自主发现能力：发现目标不可见后，主动请求主动观察模型，调试并部署可用的搜索技能。但改进没有累积：改动虽通过测试，目标任务始终未成功。智能体很少是瓶颈，三个外部因素主导：1）链式感知模块不理解关系，SAM 3等分割器能找到货架却找不到“顶层货架”，智能体用几何规则补洞但无法收敛；2）技能链将学习锁定在第一步，长任务多在早期失败，证据和修复集中于早期，后续技能难以被触达、测试和改进；3）评测框架决定智能体学到什么，智能体优化评测指标（包括错误之处），弱测试和误导性记忆使活动变成停滞。

Conclusion: 将上述教训提炼为构建可自我改进机器人系统的具体建议，并为每条建议配以可证伪实验，以检验其有效性。

Abstract: Can a robot improve itself the way coding agents now improve software? We built an agentic system to find out. It watches a robot fail, works out which capability is missing, writes new skills or finds and installs external models, tests every change in simulation, and repeats, with no human writing robot code. We ran it for 123 improvement rounds on household manipulation tasks. This report describes what we learned. The good news is that the agent can discover capabilities on its own: noticing that its targets were out of view, it asked for an active-viewing model, debugged it, and deployed a working search skill. The bad news is that its improvements did not add up. Changes kept passing their tests, yet the target task, putting condiments on the top shelf of a fridge, never succeeded. We found that the agent was rarely the bottleneck. Three things around it were. First, chained perception modules do not understand relations. Segmenters such as SAM 3 find shelves but not "the top shelf", so the agent filled the gap with ever more geometric rules that never converged, when what it needed was a different kind of model. Second, skill chains lock learning onto the first step. Long tasks mostly fail early, so evidence and fixes pile up there, and later skills are rarely reached, tested, or improved. Third, what the agent learns is decided by the harness. The agent optimized exactly what the evaluator measured, including where it was wrong, and weak tests and misleading memory turned activity into a standstill. We distill these lessons into concrete recommendations for building robot systems that improve themselves, each paired with an experiment that could prove it wrong.

</details>


### [54] [ArticulateArena: A Metric for Articulated Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33931)
*Yumeng He,Yongfei She,Huanyu Chen,Chun Yuan,Peihao Li,Joseph Masterjohn,Yin Yang,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ArticulateArena：一种面向articulation的表示不变度量，类似Chamfer距离，但比较单自由度关节诱导的运动而非参数；并用运动感知树编辑距离扩展到完整运动学树，同时发布ArticulateArena-20K并重新评估已发表重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有articulation评估通常分别评分关节类型、轴方向、原点与运动限位，但这些参数共同描述同一物理运动，且同一运动可有多种等价参数化。因此，等价编码可能被判为最大错误，且部分分量误差在预测准确处病态或未定义；核心问题是缺乏对“运动”本身的合理距离定义。

Method: 将每个单自由度关节表示为其李代数端点twist的无序对，比较关节诱导的运动而非编码参数，并证明所得商距离为度量。该度量统一fixed、revolute、prismatic、helical关节，通过紧化纳入continuous关节，并按质量分布加权后可解释为运动部件以米为单位的RMS运动。进一步提出运动感知树编辑距离，将度量提升到完整运动学树，用同一运动单位惩罚虚假或缺失关节等结构错误；在固定内积下，该距离对树重标记仍为度量。

Result: 提出ArticulateArena度量与ArticulateArena-20K数据集；该数据集包含19,977个经校验运动学的articulated objects，并在新度量下重新评估已发表重建方法。摘要未给出具体性能提升数值。

Conclusion: ArticulateArena为articulation预测提供表示不变、可度量且统一多种关节类型的评估框架，并将关节误差与运动学树结构误差统一到运动单位中，可作为现有评估协议的替代或补充，以更可靠地比较articulated object重建方法。

Abstract: Modern methods reconstruct or generate simulation-ready articulated objects, predicting not only their geometry but also how their parts are connected and allowed to move. Evaluating the geometry is straightforward, but evaluating the predicted articulation is not, because articulation specifies a motion rather than a shape, and there is no agreed distance between two motions. More specifically, existing protocols score joint type, axis direction, origin, and motion limits separately, although these parameters jointly describe a single physical motion, and the same motion can be written as different parameter values. As a result, a joint can score maximally wrong against an equivalent encoding of itself, and several component errors are ill-conditioned or undefined exactly where predictions become accurate. We propose ArticulateArena, a representation-invariant counterpart of Chamfer distance for articulation that compares the motions one-DOF joints induce rather than the parameters that encode them. It represents each joint by the unordered pair of its Lie-algebra endpoint twists, and we prove that the resulting quotient distance is a metric. It unifies fixed, revolute, prismatic, and helical joints, brings continuous joints into the same score through a compactification, and reads as the RMS motion of the moving part in meters when weighted by its mass distribution. A motion-aware tree edit distance lifts the metric to full kinematic trees, pricing structural errors such as spurious or missing joints in the same motion units as joint errors, and for a fixed inner product it remains a metric on trees up to relabeling. Alongside the metric we release ArticulateArena-20K, a new suite of 19,977 articulated objects with verified kinematics, and we re-evaluate published reconstruction methods on it under the new metric. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [55] [Residual Learning-Based Control of Vehicle Platoons with $\ell_2$ Stability Guarantees via Recurrent Equilibrium Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33892)
*Brian Delgado,Anh-Tu Nguyen,Hamid Taghavifar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于残差学习的异构车队控制框架，用REN增强LMI标称控制器与扰动观测器，并在增益约束下保证稳定性与扰动弦稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构车辆队列面临参数不确定性和外部扰动，传统标称控制器难以同时保证鲁棒性、闭环稳定性和队列弦稳定性。

Method: 先通过线性矩阵不等式设计标称控制器，并加入扰动观测器补偿；再离线训练循环平衡网络REN，利用存储轨迹和标称模型预测误差学习残差；REN被约束满足给定ℓ₂增益上界，从而利用小增益条件证明局部闭环稳定和扰动弦稳定。

Result: 实验表明，与标称控制器相比，所提框架降低了车间距误差和速度误差。

Conclusion: 残差学习增强的控制器在保持可证明稳定性和弦稳定性的同时，提升了异构车队在参数不确定性与外部扰动下的跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a residual learning-based control framework for heterogeneous vehicle platoons subject to parametric uncertainty and external disturbances. A nominal controller designed via Linear Matrix Inequalities (LMIs), along with disturbance-observer compensation, is enhanced by a Recurrent Equilibrium Network (REN) trained offline using stored trajectories and nominal-model prediction errors. The REN is constrained to satisfy a prescribed $\ell_2$-gain bound, enabling sufficient small-gain conditions for local closed-loop stability and disturbance string stability. Experiments demonstrate reduced spacing and velocity errors relative to the nominal controller.

</details>


### [56] [DexTaG: Tactile-as-Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33882)
*Han Yang,Yian Wang,Yunlong Song,Zhenjia Xu,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexTaG：一种触觉引导的强化学习框架，用于从手套动捕数据学习接触丰富的灵巧操作，并通过联合重定向与无触觉学生控制器实现真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手套式动捕可扩展地采集灵巧手演示数据，但人机手存在运动学差异，记录的人类动作无法直接在机器人上执行，尤其在涉及手内重定向的接触丰富工具使用任务中。已有工作用强化学习或轨迹优化在仿真中弥合差距，但难以保留人类接触模式，常产生不自然操作和不稳定功能抓取；且每条参考轨迹需单独训练策略或求解优化，效率低。

Method: DexTaG在训练中利用手套采集的触觉信号引导策略搜索，使其趋向测得的人类接触模式，从而减少对精确参考几何进行接触监督的依赖。为提升效率，对同一物体的所有训练轨迹联合训练一个可泛化的重定向器；再将其蒸馏为无触觉、以目标物体轨迹为条件的学生控制器，用于真实世界部署。

Result: 在记号笔和锤子操作任务上，DexTaG学到自然且接触丰富的行为，而采用基于距离的接触启发式基线无法学到；方法能泛化到同一物体和任务的留出轨迹，并在OakInk2上优于单轨迹基线。

Conclusion: DexTaG通过触觉引导和共享重定向，有效提升了从手套动捕数据到机器人灵巧操作的迁移，尤其适用于接触丰富任务，并支持真实世界部署。

Abstract: Glove-based motion capture is emerging as a scalable approach to collecting dexterous-hand demonstration data. However, due to the kinematic gap between the human and robot hand, the recorded human motions cannot be executed directly on the robot, especially for contact-rich tool-use tasks involving in-hand reorientation. Prior work bridges this gap in simulation through reinforcement learning (RL) or trajectory optimization, but the human contact pattern is hard to preserve under such formulations, often producing unnatural manipulation and unstable functional grasps. These methods also train a separate policy or solve a separate optimization for each reference trajectory, which is inefficient. To solve these problems, we propose DexTaG, a tactile-guided RL framework for dexterous manipulation. During training, tactile signals captured by the glove guide policy search toward the measured human contact pattern, reducing reliance on precise reference geometry for contact supervision. To improve efficiency, we train a single generalizable retargeter jointly on all training trajectories of the same object. The retargeter is further distilled into a tactile-free student controller conditioned on the target object trajectory for real-world deployment. On marker-pen and hammer manipulation tasks, DexTaG learns natural, contact-rich behaviors that baselines with distance-based contact heuristics fail to learn, generalizes to held-out trajectories of the same object and task, and outperforms single-trajectory baselines on OakInk2.

</details>


### [57] [Robot-GST: geometry-aware spatial-temporal robot policy representation and evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33872)
*Sichao Liu,Zekun Wang,Lixuan Tang,Yiming Li,Xiaohan Wang,Hanzhi Zhang,Daqiang Guo,Peng Zhou,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Robot-GST，基于 3D 高斯溅射与 SAM3D 构建 Gaussian-SAM 真实到仿真环境，实现“先仿真评估后执行”的几何感知时空行为表征与验证框架，以提升长时程操作部署可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型驱动的端到端操作策略发展迅速，但部署仍不可靠：许多策略缺乏显式预测任务结果、评估生成动作能否达到期望最终状态的机制，导致长时程操作中执行误差不断累积。

Method: 该方法利用 RGB-D 观测，通过 3D 高斯溅射与 SAM3D 重建高保真 Gaussian-SAM 机器人环境，支持真实到仿真的策略验证；融合视觉观测与语言指令，借助大视觉语言模型进行时空推理以实现长时程任务规划；引入基于几何采样的高斯感知最终状态估计与基于状态的轨迹规划；执行前在 Gaussian-SAM 环境中模拟并评估候选动作序列，过滤不可行行为。

Result: 在涉及刚体、软体与可变形物体的代表性操作任务（方块放置、玩具打包、鸭子重排）上验证，表明几何感知时空推理与状态感知执行能提升不同物体类别下的操作可靠性。

Conclusion: 将几何感知重建与高质量渲染和仿真相结合，为评估机器人操作行为提供了一种可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies are advancing rapidly with increasing reliance on vision-language models for end-to-end decision making. However, reliable deployment remains challenging because many policies lack explicit mechanisms for predicting task outcomes and evaluating whether generated actions will achieve desired final states, causing execution errors to accumulate during long-horizon manipulation. We present Robot-GST, a geometry-aware spatio-temporal behaviour representation and evaluation framework that constructs a Gaussian-SAM robotic environment for real-to-sim policy verification and improves the reliability of real-world manipulation deployment. Our approach constructs a high-fidelity robotic environment from RGB-D observations using 3D Gaussian Splatting and SAM3D, enabling ``simulation and evaluation before acting''. It integrates visual observations and language instructions with spatio-temporal reasoning for long-horizon task planning using large vision-language models. To bridge high-level planning and real-world execution, we introduce Gaussian-aware final-state estimation through geometric sampling and state-based trajectory planning. Before execution, candidate action sequences are simulated and evaluated in the Gaussian-SAM environment to filter infeasible behaviours. We validate our approach on representative manipulation tasks involving rigid, soft, and deformable objects, including cube placing, toy packing, and duck rearrangement, demonstrating that geometry-aware spatio-temporal reasoning and state-aware execution improve manipulation reliability across different object categories. Our results suggest that combining geometry-aware reconstruction with high-quality rendering and simulation provides a scalable approach for evaluating robotic manipulation behaviours. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [58] [DeltaSeek: Toward Active Perception in Evolving Construction Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33836)
*Sanjay Acharjee,Md Nazmus Sakib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeltaSeek，一个面向演化建筑环境的主动感知初始框架，形式化感知具身的可允许观测集，并在Husky A300+UR5e上比较底盘与腕部RGB-D相机的可见性与移动成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑/施工环境持续变化，导致显著几何改变，使静态建图与配准性能下降，因此机器人需要主动选择传感配置以解析环境当前状态。本文聚焦一个关键前提：机器人的感知具身如何约束其可获取的观测。

Method: 形式化具身的可允许观测集；基于IFC构建基准；使用Husky A300移动底盘搭载UR5e，比较底盘安装与腕部安装的RGB-D配置；以几何可见性评分观测，以可行驶距离衡量代价；在房间尺度场景中设置8个受控变化，覆盖4种可观测性条件；穷举评估240个可行基座位姿和5种机械臂姿态。

Result: 两个变化没有任何底盘视角可观测，而腕部相机可解决二者；对于两种具身均可观测的变化，腕部相机的中位基座移动距离为6.0 m，底盘相机为15.2 m。

Conclusion: 结果区分了传感限制与获取成本，可判断某观测是不可能获得还是仅需更多移动；该工作为演化建筑环境中的主动感知提供基础，表明具身配置显著影响可观测性与感知代价。

Abstract: Construction environments evolve continuously, causing large geometric changes that degrade static mapping and registration performance. This necessitates active perception, where robots deliberately select sensing configurations to resolve the environment's current state. We present DeltaSeek, an initial framework toward active perception in evolving built environments. While our broader objective is a system that reasons about where, how, and when to observe, this paper addresses a critical prerequisite: how a robot's sensing embodiment constrains the observations it can acquire. We formalize an embodiment's permissible observation set and evaluate with a Husky A300 equipped with a UR5e on an IFC-derived benchmark under chassis-mounted and wrist-mounted RGB-D configurations, scoring observations by geometric visibility and effort by drivable distance. In a room-scale scene with eight controlled changes spanning four observability conditions, exhaustive evaluation over 240 permissible base poses and five arm postures shows that two changes admit no chassis viewpoint whatsoever, while the wrist camera resolves both. For changes observed by both embodiments, the median base travel is $6.0$~m for the wrist camera and $15.2$~m for the chassis camera. These results distinguish sensing limitations from acquisition costs, clarifying whether an observation is impossible or simply requires more travel.

</details>


### [59] [Achieve What You Imagined: Learning to Align Actions with Visual Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33832)
*Yuheng Qiao,Ziran Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Daqiang Guo,Yichen Luo,Zhibo Pang,Peng Zhou,Sichao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将世界-动作模型（WAM）生成的视觉预测视为目标条件视觉提议，而非可直接执行计划；利用冻结的动作条件世界模型预测动作后果，并基于两个未来预测的一致性及终端目标对齐构建反馈，通过流策略优化（FPO）优化WAM动作头，在真实UR5操作任务上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM可联合预测未来视觉观测与机器人动作，但其视觉预测与生成动作所隐含后果之间存在不一致：WAM常先给出视觉上合理的任务完成结果，却不能产生可靠实现该结果的动作序列。因此，需要利用这种跨模型预测差异作为反馈来改进机器人策略，同时避免在线机器人交互和额外训练任务特定奖励模型。

Method: 将WAM生成的视觉预测作为目标条件视觉提议；使用冻结的动作条件世界模型预测动作条件后果；根据两个未来预测之间的一致性以及与终端目标的对齐程度构造反馈；利用该反馈，采用Flow Policy Optimization（FPO）优化WAM的动作头。该框架无需在线机器人交互，也无需额外训练任务特定奖励模型。

Result: 在四个真实世界UR5操作任务中，该方法将平均成功率从43.4%提升至75.1%，而π0.5为61.4%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在所评估的操作任务中，跨模型预测差异可为改进机器人策略提供有效反馈，所提框架能够提升WAM动作生成的任务完成可靠性。

Abstract: World-action models can jointly predict future visual observations and robot actions. However, discrepancies may exist between their visual predictions and the consequences implied by generated actions. We observe that WAMs can often generate visually plausible task-completion outcomes before producing action sequences that reliably achieve them. Consequently, we treat the WAM-generated visual prediction as a goal-conditioned visual proposal rather than a directly executable plan. We use a frozen action-conditioned world model to predict action-conditioned consequences and construct feedback based on consistency between the two future predictions and alignment with the terminal goal. Leveraging this feedback, we employ Flow Policy Optimization (FPO) to optimize the action head of the WAM. This framework avoids online robot interaction and additional training of task-specific reward models. Across four real-world UR5 manipulation tasks, our method increases the mean success rate from 43.4% to 75.1%, compared with 61.4% for $\pi_{0.5}$. These results show that cross-model prediction discrepancy can provide useful feedback for improving robot policies under the evaluated manipulation tasks. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [60] [CodeActionBench: Evaluating Agentic Code-as-Policy for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33807)
*Yiheng Lyu,Xueying Jiang,Wenhao Li,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 CodeActionBench，用 25 个操作任务和智能体式 Code-as-Policy 范式，评估通用多模态模型能否将视觉理解与推理转化为可执行代码驱动的具身操作，并在统一机器人 API 与隐藏物理结果验证器下进行受控比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身操作评估常依赖任务特定微调、演示、外部专用感知/抓取模块、特权场景状态或预定义策略，难以公平衡量通用多模态模型自身将视觉理解与推理转化为操作行为的能力，因此需要一个受控、可复现且不依赖任务特化组件的基准。

Method: 构建 CodeActionBench，包含 25 个操作任务；智能体需在无任务特定微调、无演示、无外部专用感知或抓取模块、无特权场景状态、无预定义任务策略的条件下，自主选择视觉证据、形成任务相关 3D 估计、构建操作目标，并迭代执行与修正策略。共享机器人 API 提供 RGB 观测、标定几何操作、机器人反馈和有界运动；固定任务实例、资源预算和隐藏物理结果验证器支持跨模型与框架配置的受控比较。

Result: 在 9 种配置、675 次尝试中，成功率范围为 2.7% 至 73.3%。最佳配置 GPT-6 Astra 搭配 Codex CLI 在三次尝试内至少成功解决 25 个任务中的 22 个，表现最好但仍留有很大提升空间。轨迹分析显示主要困难包括空间对齐、物体保持和完成判断，并出现动作已成功完成但任务仍失败的情况。

Conclusion: CodeActionBench 提供了一个受控测试平台，用于衡量通用模型如何将其能力转化为操作行为，并系统考察该过程中的典型失败场景，表明当前通用多模态模型在具身操作中仍有显著改进空间。

Abstract: How well can general-purpose multimodal models turn visual understanding and reasoning into embodied manipulation via executable code? We introduce CodeActionBench, a benchmark of 25 manipulation tasks that evaluates this capability through agentic Code-as-Policy. Without task-specific fine-tuning, demonstrations, external specialist perception or grasp modules, privileged scene state, or predefined task policies, agents should select visual evidence, form task-relevant 3D estimates, construct manipulation targets, and iteratively execute and revise their policies. A shared robot API provides RGB observations, calibrated geometric operations, robot feedback, and bounded motion, leaving task-dependent decisions to the evaluated agent. Fixed task instances, resource budgets, and a hidden physical-outcome verifier support controlled comparisons across models and harness configurations. Extensive evaluations across nine configurations and 675 attempts achieve success rates ranging from 2.7% to 73.3%. The strongest configuration, GPT-6 Astra with Codex CLI, solves 22 of 25 tasks at least once in three attempts, demonstrating the best performance while still leaving substantial room for improvement.
