Trajectory analyses reveal difficulties in spatial alignment, object retention, and completion judgment, including task failures despite successfully completed motions. CodeActionBench provides a controlled testbed for measuring how general-purpose models translate their capabilities into manipulation behavior and for examining typical failure scenarios in that process.

</details>


### [61] [Principal Steering Subspaces for Online Adaptation of Frozen Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33765)
*Jialeng Ni,Nathan Zhao,Kunpeng Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出主导向子空间（PSS），一种基于解码器响应几何的低维前向查询控制接口，用SAC在线适配冻结的扩散/流匹配机器人策略，并在RoboMimic及人形VLA系统上验证其更快收敛、更稳定训练和更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器人策略具有表达性强的行为先验，但通过在线交互更新大型扩散或流匹配模型代价高昂。潜空间强化学习虽可通过控制初始采样噪声避免更新预训练生成器，但高维噪声对解码动作的影响呈强各向异性，直接全潜空间控制可能低效且不稳定。

Method: PSS通过有限差分解码器响应构建固定的低维控制基；Soft Actor-Critic仅控制主导响应方向，而正交补空间在每次查询时独立地从高斯先验重采样。该方法还集成到冻结的闭源30亿参数视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中，用于人形学习系统，并包含同步转移收集、重置时优化和延迟感知异步部署。

Result: 在RoboMimic的3个任务、扩散与流匹配策略上，响应谱显示显著集中。在5个匹配的任务-生成器对中，PSS通常比全潜空间控制收敛更快、后期训练更稳定，且最终性能更强。受控Diffusion-Square消融进一步表明，同等维度下主导响应方向优于随机和最低响应子空间。在探索性螺丝刀放置评估中，冻结VLA成功率为2/10，经SAC+PSS适配后提升至6/10。

Conclusion: 结果表明，解码器响应几何可作为在线适配冻结生成式机器人策略的实用控制基，PSS在低维控制、训练稳定性和实际VLA任务成功率方面均具优势。

Abstract: Generative robot policies provide expressive behavior priors, but updating a large diffusion or flow-matching model through online interaction is costly. Latent-space reinforcement learning avoids updating the pretrained generator by controlling its initial sampling noise, yet high-dimensional noise can have strongly anisotropic effects on decoded actions. We introduce Principal Steering Subspaces (PSS), a forward-query interface that constructs a fixed low-dimensional control basis from finite-difference decoder responses. Soft Actor-Critic controls the leading response directions, while the orthogonal complement is independently resampled from the Gaussian prior at each query. On three RoboMimic tasks with diffusion and flow-matching policies, response spectra reveal substantial concentration. Across five matched task-generator pairs, the training curves indicate that PSS generally converges faster and exhibits more stable late-training behavior than full-latent control, while achieving stronger final performance overall. Controlled Diffusion-Square ablations further show that leading-response directions outperform random and least-responsive subspaces of equal dimension. We further integrate PSS with a frozen, closed-source 3B-parameter vision-language-action (VLA) policy in a humanoid learning system with synchronous transition collection, reset-time optimization, and latency-aware asynchronous deployment. In an exploratory screwdriver-placement evaluation, success is observed in 2/10 trials for the frozen VLA policy and 6/10 after SAC+PSS adaptation. These results support decoder-response geometry as a practical basis for online adaptation of frozen generative robot policies.

</details>


### [62] [AnyStep-WAM: Budget-Aligned Distillation and Adaptive Inference for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33748)
*Rui Wang,Xiangyu Wang,Donglin Yang,Yibo Li,Canyang Chen,Zhongrui Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AnyStep World Action Model，一个支持可调去噪预算与场景自适应计算分配的 WAM 框架。它通过预算对齐的教师轨迹蒸馏训练区间条件流映射，并用轻量风险-收益调度器选择最小去噪预算，在保持任务成功率的同时大幅减少去噪步数，并提升一步去噪预算下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 world-action models 通常依赖固定去噪步数的迭代生成，但机器人操作任务中的动作块对生成误差敏感度不同：关键动作需要高精度，非敏感动作可少步快速生成。因此需要一种可根据场景动态分配计算、支持可变去噪预算的通用方法。

Method: 提出 AnyStep World Action Model。采用 budget-aligned teacher-trajectory distillation，利用显式冻结教师转移和共享低秩适配器，训练 interval-conditioned flow maps，从而支持从一步预测到多步细化的动作生成。在此基础上，设计轻量 risk-benefit scheduler，从单次一步预览中预测基于教师曲率的难度和特定预算下的学生-教师保真度，选择满足风险自适应保真度要求的最小预算。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上评估 Motus、FastWAM 和 LingBotVA 三个 WAM，平均去噪步数分别减少 60.2%、49.8% 和 85.28%，同时保持基线任务成功率。AnyStep 训练在一步去噪预算下显著提升性能，三个模型成功率分别提高 7.07%、12.08% 和 8.94%。六个真实世界操作任务进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: AnyStep WAM 实现了可调预算预测与场景自适应计算分配，在保持任务成功率的同时显著降低去噪开销，并改善极低预算下的一步生成性能，适用于真实机器人操作任务。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple predictive visual modeling with action generation, typically relying on iterative denoising with a fixed denoising steps. However, manipulation tasks contain actions chunks with varying sensitivity to generation errors: critical actions require precision, while less sensitive actions allow faster generation with fewer denoising steps. Here we introduce AnyStep World Action Model, a general framework for tunable-budget prediction and scene-dependent computation allocation. Our budget-aligned teacher-trajectory distillation trains interval-conditioned flow maps using explicit frozen-teacher transitions and shared low-rank adapters, supporting action generation from one-step prediction to multi-step refinement. Building on this capability, a lightweight risk-benefit scheduler predicts teacher-curvature-based difficulty and budget-specific student-teacher fidelity from a single one-step preview, selecting the smallest budget predicted to satisfy risk-adaptive fidelity requirements. We evaluate our framework on three widely used WAMs Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA using RoboTwin 2.0. Our method reduces average denoising steps by 60.2%, 49.8%, and 85.28%, respectively, while maintaining baseline task success rates. In particular, our AnyStep training substantially improves model performance under a one-step denoising budget, increasing task success rates by 7.07%, 12.08%, and 8.94% on Motus, FastWAM, and LingBotVA, respectively. Experiments on six real-world manipulation tasks further validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [63] [MomWorld: Momentum-Aware Latent World Model for Long-Horizon Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33737)
*Ziying Song,Shengkai Zhang,Lei Yang,Haozhuang Chi,Yuchen Liu,Jiangtao Su,Lin Liu,Ziyang Liu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动量感知潜在世界模型MomWorld与动量条件流匹配模块MoFlow，用于自动驾驶长时域规划，在多个基准上提升规划一致性并将6秒时域平均碰撞率较MomAD降低12.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域规划需预测场景演化与潜在风险，但现有方法难以将观测历史中的运动趋势传播到未来；基于单一潜在状态的长 rollout 会衰减有用动态、保留过时运动模式，并破坏可靠的近期规划。

Method: MomWorld从历史到当前观测中提取场景运动趋势，并将潜在动量传播到未来时域，联合预测未来构型与动量状态；采用可学习动量持久机制保持稳定趋势，场景条件动量更新适应未来动态，场景自适应重置门在突变时抑制过时动量。MoFlow为动量条件流匹配模块，可在少量积分步内将基础轨迹细化以对齐预测的未来场景演化，并通过时域感知残差融合在保留近期规划稳定性的同时允许更强的远距离修正。

Result: 在NAVSIM、nuScenes和Bench2Drive上的大量实验表明，MomWorld提升了长时域规划一致性，并在6秒规划时域上相较MomAD将平均碰撞率降低12.2%。

Conclusion: 动量感知的潜在世界建模与动量条件流匹配能有效缓解长时域规划中的趋势传播与动态衰减问题，在复杂交互场景中提升自动驾驶规划的安全性与稳定性。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning enables autonomous vehicles to anticipate scene evolution and potential risks, supporting safe and stable decisions in complex interactions. However, existing methods struggle to propagate motion trends from observed history into the future. Long rollouts based on a single latent state may further attenuate useful dynamics, retain stale motion patterns, and disrupt reliable near-term plans. We introduce MomWorld, a momentum-aware latent world model for long-horizon planning. MomWorld extracts scene motion trends from historical-to-current observations and propagates latent momentum into future horizons, jointly predicting future configuration and momentum states. A learnable momentum persistence mechanism preserves stable trends, scene-conditioned momentum updates adapt future dynamics, and a scene-adaptive reset gate suppresses stale momentum under abrupt changes. We further propose MoFlow, a momentum-conditioned flow-matching module that refines a base trajectory to align with the predicted future scene evolution in only a few integration steps, with a horizon-aware residual fusion that preserves near-term planning stability while permitting stronger long-range corrections. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM, nuScenes and Bench2Drive demonstrate that MomWorld improves long-horizon planning consistency and reduces the average collision rate by 12.2% relative to MomAD over a 6-second planning horizon.

</details>


### [64] [Observability-Informed Optimal Sensor Placement for Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33681)
*Samuel Smocot,James Richard Forbes,Audrey Sedal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文应用并实验评估了一种软体机器人最优传感器布置的系统性方法，该方法通过凸优化最大化基于可观测性Gramian的指标，并在软连续体臂上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人传感器布置方法要么缺乏跨不同形态的通用性，要么未考虑系统动力学。

Method: 采用凸优化求解使可观测性Gramian指标最大化的最优传感器配置，并在软连续体臂上利用位置和应变测量进行实验评估。

Result: 使用最优传感器布置的Kalman滤波状态估计在所有正弦输入试验中均比基线获得更低的重建误差，改进量级为毫米。

Conclusion: 该案例表明线性控制理论工具可指导软体机器人最优传感器布置，提供了一种可解释的传感器布置方法，并可能推广到其他形态。

Abstract: This paper presents the application and experimental evaluation of a systematic method for optimal sensor placement in soft robots. Existing methods either lack generalizability across different soft robot morphologies or do not account for system dynamics. The applied method uses convex optimization to find the optimal sensor configuration that maximizes an observability Gramian-based metric. The framework is experimentally evaluated using position and strain measurements on a soft continuum arm. Kalman filter state estimates using optimal sensor placements yield lower reconstruction error than a baseline across all sinusoidal input trials, with improvements on the order of millimeters. This case study shows that linear control theory tools can guide optimal sensor placement in soft robots, suggesting an interpretable approach to sensor placement that may extend to other morphologies.

</details>


### [65] [Demonstration-Free Success-Probability Reward Learning for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33653)
*Duo Wu,Haifeng Wang,Rongwei Lu,Jinghe Wang,Tianyi Xiong,Zhimin Wang,Chao Yu,Shuai Ma,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无专家示范的奖励学习范式：eVTA0 直接从稀疏任务结果与策略经验中，通过时序差分式自举学习稠密成功概率奖励；RLER 进一步在策略演化中闭环自适应奖励，在 LIBERO 仿真与真实机器人操作中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可让通用机器人策略通过试错改进，但效果受稀疏任务奖励限制。现有通用奖励模型多依赖专家示范学习任务进度，会与策略优化时遇到的混合质量 rollout 产生分布不匹配，导致其对次优和失败行为的估计不可靠，而这些行为正是策略需要学习的关键。

Method: 理论上证明终端任务结果隐含定义了中间时间步的稠密成功概率反馈，并可通过自举递归学习。基于此提出 eVTA0：无需专家示范或中间标注，从混合质量策略 rollout 中用时序差分式自举学习成功概率。进一步提出 RL with Evolving Rewards (RLER)，一个闭环框架，利用策略演化过程中新收集的 rollout 自适应更新 eVTA0。

Result: 实验表明 eVTA0 比最先进奖励模型提供更有信息量的奖励；在相同 RL 训练预算下，在 LIBERO 所有任务套件上取得最佳平均策略性能，成功率较初始策略提升 5.4%-13.8%。真实世界操作中，RLER 将总体成功率提升 20%-26%，在分布外条件下提升 35%-36%。

Conclusion: 结果证明无示范奖励学习以及随策略演化自适应调整奖励的有效性。项目网页：this https URL。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables generalist robot policies to improve through trial-and-error interaction, yet its effectiveness is fundamentally constrained by sparse task rewards. Existing general-purpose reward models typically alleviate this issue by learning task progress from expert demonstrations, but introduce a distribution mismatch with the mixed-quality rollouts encountered during policy optimization, making their estimates unreliable on suboptimal and failed behaviors from which the policy must learn. In this work, we introduce a demonstration-free reward learning paradigm where dense reward feedback can be learned directly from sparse task outcomes and policy experience. We theoretically show that terminal task outcomes implicitly define dense success-probability feedback at intermediate timesteps, which can be recursively learned through bootstrapping. Based on this insight, we introduce eVTA$_0$, which learns success probabilities from mixed-quality policy rollouts through temporal-difference-style bootstrapping, without expert demonstrations or intermediate annotations. We further introduce RL with Evolving Rewards (RLER), a closed-loop framework that adapts eVTA$_0$ using newly collected rollouts as the policy evolves. Experiments show that eVTA$_0$ provides more informative rewards than state-of-the-art reward models and achieves the best average policy performance across all LIBERO task suites under the same RL training budget, improving success rates by 5.4%-13.8% over the initial policy. In real-world manipulation, RLER further improves overall success rates by 20%-26%, with 35%-36% gains under out-of-distribution conditions. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of demonstration-free reward learning and adapting rewards as the policy evolves. Project webpage: this https URL .

</details>


### [66] [InfraVLA: Extending Vision-Language-Action Navigation with Infrastructure Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33647)
*Lukas Vierling,Benjamin Ramtoula,Luke Robinson,Ronald Clark,Daniele De Martini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InfraVLA，将预训练导航 VLA 适配到室内已有 CCTV 等静态基础设施视角，通过两阶段训练使策略在关键决策点利用外部摄像头信息，显著提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库、办公室、医院等室内环境通常已安装摄像头，可观测机器人自身视角看不到的区域；但现有导航策略（包括 VLA 模型）未利用这些基础设施视角。外部视角仅在少数关键决策点提供决定性信息，仅靠微调难以让策略学会使用它们。

Method: InfraVLA 是一种端到端方法：用 CCTV 编码器将每个外部视角编码为输入序列 token，并与预训练导航 VLA 融合。针对外部视角稀疏关键的问题，采用两阶段训练：先在带过采样反事实数据的示范上训练，再在恢复数据上训练。

Result: 在两个模拟仓库任务（寻找指令指定物体、绕行被阻塞通道）上，分布内成功率均为 100%，而无 CCTV 基线分别为 34.0% 和 73.6%；分布外测试集成功率为 88.2% 和 88.9%。在真实四足机器人上，用不到 10 分钟示范微调后成功率达 83.3%，仅机载视角基线为 29.2%。

Conclusion: 利用室内已有基础设施摄像头可显著增强机器人导航 VLA 在关键决策点的感知与决策能力，并在仿真和真实四足平台上大幅优于仅使用机载视角的基线。

Abstract: Many indoor environments in which robots operate, such as warehouses, offices, and hospitals, already have cameras installed. They observe parts of the building that the robot cannot see from where it stands, yet navigation policies, including recent vision-language-action (VLA) models, do not use them. We propose InfraVLA, an end-to-end method that adapts a pretrained navigation VLA to such static infrastructure views: a closed-circuit television (CCTV) encoder turns each external view into tokens of the input sequence. Because the views matter only at rare decision points, fine-tuning alone did not make the policy use them in our experiments; we therefore train in two stages, on demonstrations with upsampled counterfactual data and then on recovery data. We evaluate on two simulated warehouse tasks, finding an object named in the instruction and rerouting around blocked aisles, where the deciding information is often visible only to the infrastructure cameras. Tested in distribution, InfraVLA reached a success rate of 100% on both, against 34.0% and 73.6% for a baseline without CCTV input. On out-of-distribution test sets it reached 88.2% and 88.9%. On a real quadruped fine-tuned with under 10 minutes of demonstrations, the policy reached 83.3% against 29.2% for the on-board-only baseline.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond One-Step Accuracy: State-Affine Latent Transition for Reliable Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33595)
*Boyuan Zhang,Yingjun Du,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出联合嵌入世界模型仅优化单步预测不足以反映递归规划中的误差传播，提出状态仿射隐转移 SALT，并用递归多步展开训练以匹配规划时的自回归使用方式，从而显著提升视觉规划闭环表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合嵌入世界模型通常在编码状态上做单步预测训练，但规划会递归地把学习到的转移作用于自身预测；因此单步精度无法刻画多步展开中的误差传播。作者将多步展开误差分解为各步新引入误差及其后续传播，并寻求能简化该传播的转移形式。

Method: 作者证明状态仿射动力学正是雅可比与状态无关的可微转移，可消除非线性传播残差，使误差传播算子仅依赖动作序列。据此提出 SALT（State-Affine Latent Transition）：一种动作条件状态仿射动力学模型，动作同时调制状态变换与加性更新；训练采用递归多步展开监督，将每个预测隐状态反馈到转移中，使训练与规划时的使用方式一致。

Result: 在四个视觉规划环境中，SALT 的单步预测误差比匹配的 LeWM 基线高 1.48–2.19 倍，但在所有环境中都将闭环成功率平均提升 10.0 个百分点。在 OGBench-Cube 上，执行后模型预测代价急剧上升而失败的回合比例从 23.3% 降至 2.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，世界模型规划性能不能仅由单步预测精度衡量；采用状态无关雅可比的状态仿射转移并结合递归多步展开监督，可减少多步误差传播，使模型在闭环规划中更鲁棒，即使单步精度较低也能获得更高成功率。

Abstract: Joint-embedding world models enable visual planning by learning action-conditioned dynamics in latent space. Yet they are commonly trained for one-step prediction on encoded states, while planning recursively applies the learned transition to its own predictions. One-step accuracy therefore does not capture how prediction errors propagate under recursive rollout. We decompose multi-step rollout error into the errors introduced at individual steps and their propagation through subsequent transitions. We show that state-affine dynamics are precisely the differentiable transitions with state-independent Jacobians, eliminating the nonlinear propagation residual and making the error propagation operators depend only on the action sequence. Guided by this result, we introduce SALT (State-Affine Latent Transition), an action-conditioned state-affine dynamics model in which the action modulates both the state transformation and the additive update. We train SALT through recursive multi-step rollout supervision, feeding each predicted latent state back into the transition so that training matches how the model is used during planning. Across four visual planning environments, SALT exhibits $1.48$--$2.19\times$ higher one-step prediction error than the matched LeWM baseline, yet improves closed-loop success in every environment by $10.0$ percentage points on average. On OGBench-Cube, the fraction of episodes that fail with a sharp rise in model-predicted cost after execution decreases from $23.3%$ to $2.0%$.

</details>


### [68] [SLIP-VLA: Single-Step Latent Imagination for Policy Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33575)
*Tianfu Li,Haoxuan Xu,Wenbo Chen,Haitian Li,Changchuan Yang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Yuan Liu,Lujia Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLIP-VLA 提出单步潜想象框架，以单次去噪更新获得时间稠密的未来潜表示，并通过几何/语义对齐和动作条件世界模型/逆动力学增强感知与控制充分性，实现高效且 SOTA 的 VLA 操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 多直接从当前观测预测动作，缺乏对未来场景演化的显式建模；引入未来预测的方法中，稠密未来建模常依赖昂贵的迭代去噪，而一步替代方案性能不及多步方法。

Method: SLIP-VLA 为 VLA 配备 Single-Step Latent Imagination：用单次去噪更新得到时间稠密的未来潜表示；将中间潜表示与未来几何和语义特征对齐以提升感知充分性；并通过动作条件潜世界建模和逆动力学建模，将潜状态转移与机器人动作显式耦合以提升控制充分性。

Result: 在多种仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务上达到 SOTA 性能，单步潜想象仅需 12 ms。

Conclusion: SLIP-VLA 在高效未来建模与强动作性能之间取得平衡，验证了单步潜想象用于未来感知动作预测的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are increasingly effective for robotic manipulation, yet most predict actions directly from current observations without explicitly modeling future scene evolution. Recent methods introduce future prediction to improve action generation, but dense future modeling often requires expensive iterative denoising, while one-step alternatives can underperform their multi-step counterparts. To reconcile efficient future modeling with strong action performance, we present SLIP-VLA, a policy learning framework that equips VLA models with a Single-Step Latent Imagination for future-aware action prediction. SLIP-VLA obtains temporally dense future latent representations with a single denoising update, and we improve the perceptual sufficiency of these representations by aligning intermediate latents with future geometric and semantic features. We further improve their control sufficiency through action-conditioned latent world modeling and inverse dynamics modeling, explicitly coupling latent transitions with robot actions. SLIP-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks, while its single-step latent imagination takes only 12 ms.

</details>


### [69] [DexRoam: Learning Mobile Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation from Egocentric Whole-Body Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35761)
*Rui Zhou,Yibo Yuan,Junkai Zhao,Fangyuan Zhao,Xiaoguang Zhao,Shanghang Zhang,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexRoam 是一套从人类全身演示中学习移动双臂灵巧操作的系统，通过无追踪器VR采集与三阶段对齐，将连续耦合的全身运动迁移至机器人，并提升VLA策略学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动双臂灵巧操作要求在同一轨迹中持续协调移动、全身运动与手指级灵巧性，导致机器人演示数据瓶颈严重。第一人称人类演示可扩展，但既有方法常简化人类运动，丢弃了任务依赖的细粒度耦合结构。

Method: 开发仅用消费级VR头显与头戴立体相机的无追踪器全身操作演示采集系统；随后进行具身、动作语义、时间三阶段显式对齐，将捕获运动映射到机器人动作空间，保留细粒度全身运动，使人类与机器人演示可被标准VLA策略联合学习。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，人类演示在不同VLA骨干和训练范式下均持续提升策略学习：GR00T N1.7平均成功率从29%升至56%，pi0.5从32%升至57%；仅用一半机器人演示即可匹配纯机器人训练。消融实验确认每个对齐阶段均必要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在保留细粒度运动结构的前提下，人类演示可有效支持可扩展的全身移动操作学习，为缓解机器人演示瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of locomotion, whole-body motion, and finger-level dexterity within a single trajectory, creating a severe robot demonstration bottleneck. Egocentric human demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, but prior approaches ease the transfer by simplifying human motion, discarding exactly the fine-grained, coupled structure such tasks depend on. We present DexRoam, a complete system for learning mobile bimanual dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations, in which whole-body motion remains continuous and coupled throughout the human-to-robot transfer process. To enable scalable collection of whole-body human manipulation demonstrations, we develop a tracker-free capture system using only a consumer VR headset and a head-mounted stereo camera, without external cameras or motion trackers. We then perform three explicit alignment stages---embodiment, action-semantic, and temporal---to map captured motion into the robot action space, preserving fine-grained whole-body motion and allowing human and robot demonstrations to be jointly learned by standard VLA policies. Real-world experiments with different VLA backbones show that human demonstrations consistently improve policy learning across training paradigms, raising average success from 29% to 56% on GR00T N1.7 and from 32% to 57% on pi0.5, while matching robot-only training with half the robot demonstrations. Ablations confirm that each alignment stage is necessary. These results highlight the potential of human demonstrations for scalable whole-body mobile manipulation with preserved fine-grained motion structure.

</details>


### [70] [RoboCompiler: Graph-Native Compilation of Closed-Chain Robots for Consistent Modeling, Control, and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35717)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Joongheon Kim,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboCompiler 是一个图原生的编译框架，将统一机构图编译为共享机械接口，以一致处理闭链、耦合驱动与接触变化下的配置、运动、力与动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有运动学闭环、耦合执行器和变化接触的机器人，其配置、运动、力与动力学模型需要保持一致；但控制与仿真通常各自重建接口，导致闭环约束和执行器一致性难以维护。

Method: 该框架从刚体、关节、坐标系、惯量和执行器端口构建规范机构图，生成闭环路径与解析残差雅可比，并通过秩检查延拓与校正组装可行配置；用切空间提升将独立速度映射为完整机器人及任务运动，用成对执行器端口映射保持虚功；用约束曲率校正将降维扩展到加速度与投影刚体动力学，包括浮动基座和支撑模式；通过循环局部求值、生成式雅可比和依赖感知复用实现闭链输入变化时的局部更新。

Result: 在 Komatsu 挖掘机、Unitree Go2、Franka Panda、Kangaroo 和六-UPS Stewart 平台上评估物理闭环与任务诱导约束；高精度约束动力学和独立 Pinocchio 校验确认机械一致性，MuJoCo 与 Isaac Sim/PhysX 执行展示原生接触下的任务性能与模型复用；对 Kangaroo，编译使残差与雅可比评估时间降低 96.7%，闭环 rollout 墙钟时间降低 66.8%，且动力学与控制保持不变。

Conclusion: RoboCompiler 为闭环、耦合驱动和接触丰富机器人提供了控制与仿真共享的机械接口，在保证闭环与驱动一致性的同时显著提升求值和闭环仿真效率。

Abstract: Robots with kinematic loops, coupled actuators, and changing contacts require consistent models of configuration, motion, force, and dynamics. Yet these interfaces are often reconstructed separately for control and simulation, making closure and actuation consistency difficult to maintain. This paper presents RoboCompiler, a graph-native framework that compiles a canonical mechanism graph into a shared mechanical interface. From bodies, joints, frames, inertias, and actuator ports, it constructs closure paths and analytic residual Jacobians, then assembles feasible configurations through rank-checked continuation and correction. A tangent lift maps independent velocities to full robot and task motion, while paired actuator-port maps preserve virtual work. A constraint-curvature correction extends the reduction to accelerations and projected rigid-body dynamics, including floating-base and support modes. Cycle-local evaluation, generated Jacobians, and dependency-aware reuse enable localized updates when closure inputs change. We evaluate physical loops and task-induced constraints on a Komatsu excavator, Unitree Go2, Franka Panda, Kangaroo, and a six-UPS Stewart platform. High-precision constrained-dynamics and independent Pinocchio checks confirm mechanical consistency; MuJoCo and Isaac Sim/PhysX executions demonstrate task performance and model reuse under native contact. For Kangaroo, compilation reduces residual-and-Jacobian evaluation time by 96.7% and closed-loop rollout wall time by 66.8%, with dynamics and control held fixed.

</details>


### [71] [Humanoid Loco-Manipulation With Discrete VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35709)
*Wenxin Shao,Siqi Chai,Kun Li,Kerou Zhang,Xinzhou Jiang,Wei Xu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Holo-M，首个面向人形机器人全身移动操作（loco-manipulation）的离散VLA模型，通过扩展语言模型词表加入动作token，实现跨本体与多源数据训练，并在SIMPLE基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于离散动作token的VLA模型在机械臂操作上有效，但人形机器人全身动作空间（腿、躯干、臂、手）维度更高且异构，带来tokenization、训练与实时推理挑战，已有VLA方法未能解决。

Method: 设计统一动作tokenizer，将人形动作空间分解为四个身体部位专用tokenizer（末端执行器、身体、手、运动学），并扩展语言模型词表以纳入动作token，从而避免使用独立连续动作专家导致的知识隔离问题；为满足实时控制，采用分组离散扩散解码各身体部位动作token，而非自回归解码。

Result: 在SIMPLE人形移动操作基准上，Holo-M在通用（generalist）与专用（specialist）评估中均取得最高成功率，显著领先第二名；作者将开源全部代码与模型权重。

Conclusion: Holo-M验证了将离散VLA扩展至人形全身移动操作的可行性，通过统一tokenization与分组离散扩散解码兼顾跨本体/多源数据训练与实时控制，推动人形VLA发展。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models using discrete action tokens have proven effective for controling robotic arms on manipulation tasks. For a humanoid, however, the whole-body action space -- legs, torso, arms, and hands -- is far higher-dimensional and heterogeneous, raising tokenization, training, and real-time inference challenges that the previous VLA models do not address. We present Holo-M, to our knowledge the first discrete VLA model for humanoid loco-manipulation that intrinsically exploits the language model by extending its vocabulary with action tokens. In this model, we devise a unified action tokenizer that decomposes the humanoid action space into four body-part-specific tokenizers -- end-effector, body, hand, and kinematics -- enabling training across drastically different embodiments and data sources, including humanoid teleoperation, ego-centric human video, and simulation. By extending the language model's vocabulary with these action tokens, we avoid the knowledge-insulation problem inherent to the models that use separate continuous action experts. To meet real-time control requirements, we decode each body part's action tokens through grouped discrete diffusion decoding, rather than using autoregression on the action tokens. We have conducted extensive experiments on the SIMPLE humanoid loco-manipulation benchmark, in which Holo-M achieves the highest success rates in both the generalist and specialist evaluations, leading the second best by significant margins. We will release all the code and model weights.

</details>


### [72] [LQR-ArUco Fusion: Robust Hierarchical Control for Navigation and Asymmetric Manipulation in Two-Wheeled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35700)
*Anupam Chatterjee,Arpita Kumari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层/双环路控制框架，使两轮倒立摆机器人在非对称抓取与搬运时同时实现可靠导航、抗扰平衡与载荷运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两轮倒立摆平台虽机动性强，但持续平衡调节导致机载里程计不可靠；侧挂机械臂举升载荷会引入无驱动侧倾力矩，且在非平坦地形上问题加剧，造成严重动态失稳与导航漂移。

Method: 将任务解耦为双环路：离板视觉系统通过头顶ArUco标记提供高延迟全局路点导航，绕过里程计漂移；同时，低延迟机载控制环利用惯性测量与编码器数据主动抑制物理扰动。

Result: 物理实验表明，该双环路方法使自制机器人实现精确导航，能抑制减速带瞬态冲击、适应动态跷跷板坡道，并在窄轮距下安全携带载荷而不倾倒。

Conclusion: 分层双环路架构可有效应对TWIP机器人非对称操作中导航与平衡耦合问题，在全局定位与低延迟扰动抑制之间实现分工，从而提升复杂地形下的导航精度、鲁棒性与载荷运输能力。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical control framework to address severe dynamic instabilities and navigational drift that arise when a two-wheeled inverted pendulum (TWIP) robot attempts asymmetric object manipulation. While two-wheeled platforms are highly manoeuvrable, their constant balancing adjustments make onboard odometry highly unreliable for precise navigation. Furthermore, the addition of a side-mounted robotic arm introduces unactuated lateral roll moments when a payload is lifted, a challenge heavily compounded on uneven terrain. To solve these coupled problems, our architecture divides the workload. An offboard vision system tracks overhead ArUco markers to provide high-latency global waypoint navigation, bypassing odometry drift. Simultaneously, a low-latency onboard control loop rejects active physical disturbances using inertial and encoder data. In our physical experiments, this dual-loop approach enabled the custom-built robot to navigate accurately, reject transient impacts from speed bumps, adapt to a dynamic seesaw ramp, and carry a payload securely without falling over its narrow wheelbase.

</details>


### [73] [Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35690)
*Puming Jiang,Tianrun Hu,Haozhe Du,Yibo Li,Zhiwei Xue,Xinhu Li,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 APPL，将策略的训练时结构先验作为技能组合与技能执行之间的接口，以同时提升技能泛化与组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从少量示范学习需要两类泛化：组合泛化，即重组技能解决新任务；技能泛化，即每个技能学到的策略适应新场景。二者相互依赖，但组合层通常只通过名称、指令或符号算子等描述调用技能，丢失了决定技能适用范围的策略结构信息，导致组合与技能之间信息断裂。

Method: 核心思想是把每个策略的结构先验纳入组合与技能之间的接口。结构先验规定行为依赖哪些因素，例如抓取只依赖夹爪相对物体的位姿。训练时注入该先验以塑造策略泛化范围；用语言表述后，可告知组合层该策略的适用条件。APPL 中，构建智能体将完整示范分割为可复用技能，为每个技能提出多个结构先验，并为每个先验训练与验证一个策略；运行时智能体依据接口选择相应的先验特定策略，并将其组合以完成新任务目标。

Result: 在 MetaWorld 和长时序 ManiSkill 任务上，APPL 提升了分布外技能泛化，并实现了此前未见的技能组合；消融接口信息会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，训练时的结构假设可作为技能学习与技能组合之间的桥梁，使组合层感知技能的适用范围，并改善技能泛化与组合泛化。

Abstract: Robots that learn from a few demonstrations often require two forms of generalization. Compositional generalization recombines skills to solve new tasks, and skill generalization lets the learned policy behind each skill work in new situations. The two depend on each other, yet information is lost between composition and the skills it calls. Where a skill works is determined by the structure its policy is trained with, while composition sees the skill only through a separate description, such as a name, an instruction, or a symbolic operator, that omits this structure. Our key idea is to use each policy's structural prior as part of the interface between composition and the skill. A structural prior states what a behavior depends on, for example that a grasp depends only on the gripper's pose relative to the object. Built into training, it shapes where the policy generalizes; stated in language, it tells composition where the policy applies. We instantiate this idea in Agent Priors-guided Policy Learning (APPL). A construction agent segments complete demonstrations into reusable skills, proposes several structural priors for each skill, and trains and verifies one policy per prior. A runtime agent then selects among these prior-specific policies and composes them toward new task goals using their interfaces. Across MetaWorld and long-horizon ManiSkill tasks, APPL improves out-of-distribution skill generalization and enables previously unseen skill compositions; ablating the interface information substantially reduces performance. These results support the use of training-time structural assumptions as a bridge between skill learning and skill composition.

</details>


### [74] [MM-ABC: Towards Generalist Mobile Manipulation via Seeing, Coordinating and Imagining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35652)
*Qiwei Liang,Guangyu Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Zikuan Xiao,Jinxuan Lu,Yifan Xie,Renjing Xu,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出移动操作基础模型 MM-ABC，通过稀疏多级 VLM 特征、仅训练的未来分支监督和 MM-APT 联合注意力机制，统一感知、协调与想象，实现操作与移动动作流的跨流协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需在连续自运动下进行空间感知，并协调机械臂与移动基座两类异构动作；现有方法多依赖显式 3D 表示或预测世界模型，且将移动与操作解耦为独立动作流，缺乏支持高效跨流协作的表示。

Method: MM-ABC 围绕 Seeing、Coordinating、Imagining 构建：使用稀疏多级 VLM 特征进行空间感知；引入仅训练用的未来分支，以世界想象和几何意图作为额外监督；提出 MM-APT，通过掩码联合注意力和 clean-action x-prediction 协调独立的操作与移动流。预训练于 5000+ 小时、400K+ episodes、12 个数据集、17 种形态的异构机器人数据。

Result: 在 EBench 上成功率为 44.71%，RoboCasa365 为 61.2%，LIBERO 为 99.1%，LIBERO-Plus 无扰动训练为 82.8%，真实世界五项任务平均成功率为 83%。消融显示，将 clean-action prediction 替换为 velocity prediction 会使 RoboCasa365 composite-seen 成功率从 32.8% 降至 29.2%；移除未来监督或多级条件导致更大下降。

Conclusion: MM-ABC 表明，移动操作不仅需要解耦动作流，还需支持跨流协作的表示；结合多级感知、未来想象监督与联合注意力协调，可显著提升仿真与真实移动操作性能。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation extends robot interaction beyond a fixed kinematic workspace by making the reachable region itself controllable. This flexibility introduces two central challenges: spatially grounded perception under continuous ego-motion and coordinated control of heterogeneous arm and base actions. Existing approaches strengthen geometry through explicit 3D representations or predictive world models, and often decouple mobility and manipulation into separate action streams. We argue that effective mobile manipulation requires not only decoupling, but also representations that support efficient cross-stream collaboration. We present MM-ABC, a foundation model built around Seeing, Coordinating, and Imagining Arm-Base Collaboration. MM-ABC combines sparse multi-level VLM features for spatial perception; a training-only future branch that uses world imagination and geometric intent as extra supervision, strengthening perception and manipulation-intent prediction and improving the overall learning signal; and MM-APT, which coordinates separate manipulation and mobility streams through masked joint attention and clean-action x-prediction. In controlled ablations, replacing clean-action prediction with velocity prediction lowers success on RoboCasa365 composite-seen tasks from 32.8% to 29.2%, and removing future supervision or multilevel conditioning causes larger drops. We pretrain MM-ABC on 5,000+ hours of heterogeneous robot data spanning 400K+ episodes, 12 datasets, and 17 embodiments. Experiments cover EBench, RoboCasa365, ManiSkill-HAB, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world mobile manipulation. MM-ABC achieves 44.71% success on EBench, 61.2% on RoboCasa365, 99.1% on LIBERO, 82.8% on LIBERO-Plus without perturbation training, and 83% mean success on five real-world tasks.

</details>


### [75] [Denoising Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35651)
*Yuhao Zhang,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于 D4orm 动力学感知扩散去噪框架，提出面向多机器人轨迹规划的可扩展规划器族，并通过仿真与真实机器人实验证明扩散去噪在多机器人协调中兼具高效性、可靠性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人轨迹规划本质上是非凸、多模态且高维的优化问题，计算挑战大；现有数值优化与采样方法在复杂场景下难以同时满足实时性、可扩展性和可靠性，且不同应用需要适配不同计算与通信约束。

Method: 以 D4orm 为基本构建模块：采用基于采样的优化，通过 GPU 等现代并行架构进行大规模并行采样，利用扩散去噪结构迭代优化候选控制轨迹的“形变”，生成满足运动动力学约束且无冲突的轨迹。进一步提出三类规划器：解耦式规划器以提升可扩展性；带反馈控制的在线滚动时域规划器；面向资源受限场景的分布式规划器。

Result: 在 2D 和 3D 环境中的差速驱动与全向机器人上评估，D4orm 类方法相比 MPPI 等采样优化方法以及基于学习扩散模型的方法，能更快、更可靠地找到高质量解。实验还展示了十架真实四旋翼在障碍环境中的零样本部署、100 个仿真机器人的大规模解冲突，以及六台地面机器人的完全机载分布式“终身”运行。

Conclusion: 结果表明，扩散去噪是一种可扩展且可靠的多机器人协调框架，能够支撑从仿真大规模解冲突到真实机器人机载分布式长期运行的多样化规划需求。

Abstract: Multi-robot trajectory planning is a fundamental problem in multi-robot coordination but remains computationally challenging due to its nonconvex, multimodal, and high-dimensional nature. This work builds upon D4orm, a dynamics-aware diffusion-denoising framework, and develops a family of planning architectures for diverse operational requirements. Unlike conventional numerical optimization methods, D4orm employs sampling-based optimization to generate solution trajectories through massively parallel sampling, leveraging modern computing architectures such as GPUs. Its diffusion-denoising structure iteratively optimizes \textit{deformations} to candidate control trajectories, providing an efficient and versatile paradigm for generating kinodynamically feasible and conflict-free trajectories. Using D4orm as the building block for advanced planners, we present a decoupled planner for improved scalability, an online receding-horizon planner with feedback control, and a distributed planner for resource-constrained settings. Evaluations with differential-drive and holonomic robots in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that D4orm-based approaches find high-quality solutions faster and more reliably than other sampling-based optimization methods, such as MPPI, as well as a learned diffusion-model-based method. We further demonstrate zero-shot deployment on ten real quadrotors with obstacles, large-scale deconfliction with 100 simulated robots, and fully onboard distributed `lifelong' operation with six ground robots. Overall, these results establish diffusion denoising as a scalable and reliable framework for multi-robot coordination. Code and video: this https URL

</details>


### [76] [CollisionSplatting: Collision-Aware Motion Planning in 3DGS Scenes with Image-Conditioned Objectives and Adjustable Conservatism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35619)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joaquim Ortiz-Haro,Stephan Weiss,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 CollisionSplatting：一种可直接作用于 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）场景、GPU 加速且受概率启发的距离度量，并与学习到的图像条件奖励函数及 MPPI/RRT 规划器结合，实现兼顾几何碰撞与视觉目标的实时运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将密集视觉信息纳入运动规划仍具挑战：几何规划器依赖抽象场景表示，丢失视觉丰富性；学习式视觉模型又常缺乏几何可解释性与计算效率。

Method: 提出 CollisionSplatting，一种简单、模块化、GPU 加速、概率启发的距离度量，可直接在标准 3DGS 场景上运行，并具有可调保守性。将其与学习到的图像条件奖励函数结合，统一碰撞感知代价与图像空间目标，实现几何与视觉联合规划；并将其集成到 GPU 加速的 Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）与 Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree（RRT）规划器中。

Result: 与代表性基线相比，碰撞分类性能相当或更优，同时碰撞检测吞吐显著更高、VRAM 占用显著更低；在真实世界视觉引导导航与操作任务中验证了该度量的有效性。

Conclusion: 表明 3DGS 可作为丰富感知与实时运动规划之间的实用桥梁。

Abstract: Incorporating dense visual information into motion planning remains challenging, as geometric planners rely on abstracted scene representations that discard visual richness, while learned visual models often lack geometric interpretability and computational efficiency. This paper introduces CollisionSplatting, a simple, modular, GPU-accelerated, probability-inspired distance metric with tunable conservatism that operates directly on standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes. When combined with learned image-conditioned reward functions, this metric enables joint geometric and visual planning by unifying collision-aware costs with image-space objectives. We integrate the metric into GPU-accelerated Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) and Rapidly-Exploring Random Tree (RRT) planners, and show on-par or better collision-classification performance compared to representative baselines while achieving substantially higher collision-checking throughput and significantly lower VRAM usage. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our metric in real-world vision-guided navigation and manipulation tasks, highlighting 3DGS as a practical bridge between rich perception and real-time motion planning.

</details>


### [77] [F4R: Failure-Driven Recognition, Reconstruction, Refinement, and Redeployment for Continual Robot Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35575)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Jiacheng Wang,Tianle Liu,Yihua Ren,Peng Yu,Chen Bai,Ziheng Zhang,Yufei Jia,Jindou Jia,Yuhang Zhang,Xinrui Zhang,Shang Yujing,Yuxiang Chen,Chuhao Zhou,Tiancai Wang,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出失败驱动的实到虚再到实闭环学习框架F4R，将真实世界失败自动转化为针对性策略改进，在四项操作任务上显著提升分布内与分布外成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型的真实表现受限于专家演示覆盖不足及对物理交互理解不够；收集新失败的真实演示成本高、效率低、可能不安全且难以扩展。

Method: F4R先由智能体从rollout中自动识别并诊断失败，将每个失败重建为保留任务相关空间与物理条件的交互式、以物体为中心的桌面环境；随后通过失败条件下的仿真-真实协同训练，并在重建环境中进行针对性强化学习来优化策略；改进策略重新部署后，新失败持续反馈到下一轮重建与学习闭环。

Result: 在四项真实操作任务中，F4R达到93.75%分布内成功率和90.0%分布外成功率；在分布外条件下，无需收集额外真实纠正演示，即比预算匹配的Targeted BC基线高出18.75个百分点。

Conclusion: F4R通过失败驱动的实到虚再到实闭环，将真实失败高效转化为策略提升，避免额外真实纠正演示的采集，并在分布外场景中显著优于基线。

Abstract: The real-world performance of current vision-language-action models is fundamentally constrained by the limited coverage of expert demonstrations and their insufficient understanding of physical interactions. A common remedy is to collect additional real-world demonstrations of newly encountered failures. However, this process is costly, inefficient, potentially unsafe, and difficult to scale. To address this challenge, we propose Failure for Rising (F4R), a failure-driven real-to-sim-to-real closed-loop learning framework that converts real-world failures into targeted policy improvement. F4R first uses an agent to automatically identify and diagnose failures from rollouts. It reconstructs each failure as an interactive, object-centric table-top environment that preserves the task-relevant spatial and physical conditions. The policy is then refined through failure-conditioned sim-real co-training followed by targeted reinforcement learning in the reconstructed environments. The improved policy is subsequently redeployed, while newly observed failures are continuously fed back into the next reconstruction and learning cycle. Real-world evaluations on four manipulation tasks show that F4R achieves 93.75% In-Distribution and 90.0% Out-of-Distribution (OOD) success, outperforming the budget-matched Targeted BC baseline by 18.75 percentage points under OOD conditions without collecting additional real-world corrective demonstrations.

</details>


### [78] [EdgeVLN: Runtime-Aware Deployment Ready Quantized Vision Language Navigation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35570)
*Rithvik Jonna,Man Namgung,Aakash Gurram,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EdgeVLN 是面向边缘部署的运行时感知量化 VLN 模型，结合量化 StreamVLN 与 LATTE 停止头及 VLN 驱动，在 Jetson Orin NX 上实现高成功率、低延迟低能耗导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 模型性能好但依赖高算力平台，难以部署在内存和功耗受限的机器人边缘设备；仅靠压缩无法判断是否满足内存、延迟、能耗预算并保持导航行为。

Method: EdgeVLN 将量化 StreamVLN 与轻量因果 Transformer LATTE 结合，LATTE 预测 Stop Action verifier rank 以改善实时停止；二者通过 VLN 驱动执行，在线重建流式上下文并剪枝记忆 token。作者从 8 到 2 bit 权重量化及多种推理运行时刻画预训练 StreamVLN，LATTE 复用主干隐状态，不需第二视觉编码器或额外主干前向。在 R2R VLN-CE val-unseen 全部 1,839 回合上评估 6 种主干精度和 7 种停止头，并在 NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 16 GB 上测成功率、延迟、能耗和常驻内存。

Result: LATTE 在部署的 4-bit 模型上达到最高 SR 58.02%，超过 BF16 基线且每导航步仅增加 0.013 s 延迟；四种 4-bit 格式 SR 几乎相同，但单步能耗因执行路径相差 36.8 倍；仅 IQ4 NL 配合 VLN 驱动可适配开发板，常驻内存 11.35 GB，比存储流式 BF16 快 20.8 倍、能耗低 13.3 倍；INT2 崩溃。

Conclusion: 运行时选择、记忆 token 剪枝和量化对高效边缘部署至关重要。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) models perform well but target compute-rich platforms, limiting deployment on memory- and power-constrained robotic edge devices. Compression alone does not establish whether a VLN model fits the memory, latency, and energy budgets of an edge platform while preserving navigation behavior. We introduce EdgeVLN, a runtime-aware, deployment-ready quantized VLN model that closes this gap. EdgeVLN combines a quantized StreamVLN model with Latent Trajectory Termination Extractor (LATTE), a lightweight causal transformer that improves real-time stopping by predicting a Stop Action verifier rank. Both execute through our this http URL VLN driver, which reconstructs streaming context and prunes memory tokens on-board. We characterize a pretrained StreamVLN backbone across weight quantization from 8 to 2 bits and multiple inference runtimes to identify a feasible operating point. LATTE reuses backbone hidden states within the budget freed by quantization, requiring neither a second vision encoder nor an additional backbone forward pass. We evaluate six backbone precisions and seven candidate stop heads on BF16 and IQ4 NL across all 1,839 R2R VLN-CE val-unseen episodes. We measure success rate (SR) in simulation and latency, energy, and resident memory on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 16 GB. LATTE achieves our highest SR, 58.02 percent on the deployed 4-bit model, exceeding the BF16 baseline with only 0.013 s additional latency per navigation step. Four-bit formats achieve nearly identical SR, but step energy varies 36.8 times by execution path. Only IQ4 NL under our VLN driver fits the board, using 11.35 GB resident memory while running 20.8 times faster and using 13.3 times less energy than storage-streamed BF16. INT2 collapses. Runtime selection, memory-token pruning, and quantization are essential for efficient edge deployment.

</details>


### [79] [Inspection-SPARS: Task-Oriented Sparse Roadmaps for Inspection Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35516)
*Adir Morgan,Oren Salzman,Kiril Solovey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Inspection-SPARS，一种面向巡检任务的路线图稀疏化方法，在保持 POI 覆盖与路径质量保证的同时，将稠密路线图压缩 4-8 倍，并使图巡检规划求解器得到最多短 25% 的巡检路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样-based 方法将连续巡检规划转化为离散路线图上的图巡检规划问题，但稠密路线图会带来巨大的组合搜索空间，现有图巡检规划求解器难以在实用时间内找到优质解；已有稀疏化方法要么与巡检任务无关，要么只保证 POI 覆盖而不考虑最终巡检路径质量。

Method: 提出 Inspection-SPARS，将任务无关的 SPARS 稀疏化框架推广为任务导向方法，引入巡检感知的顶点准入机制，把 POI 覆盖与连通性、路径质量并列为稀疏化的一等准则，从而在稀疏图上同时提供相对于稠密路线图的 POI 覆盖与路径质量保证。

Result: 在真实感 3D 环境实验中，Inspection-SPARS 将顶点数和边数减少 4-8 倍并保持覆盖，使图巡检规划求解器计算出的巡检路径相比使用稠密路线图或当前最优巡检路线图最多缩短 25%。

Conclusion: Inspection-SPARS 表明稀疏化可以做到任务感知，同时不牺牲解质量保证，为巡检规划中的大规模路线图压缩提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Inspection planning seeks a minimum-length collision-free robot tour that observes a given set of points of interest (POIs). Sampling-based methods reduce this continuous problem to a graph inspection planning (GIP) problem over a discrete roadmap, which is then solved using combinatorial solvers. Dense roadmaps capture diverse inspection viewpoints and motion shortcuts, and thus admit higher-quality solutions, but they induce large combinatorial search spaces on which state-of-the-art GIP solvers struggle to find good solutions within practical time budgets. Roadmap sparsification---restructuring a dense roadmap into a compact representation that preserves connectivity and path lengths---can alleviate this burden. However, existing sparsification approaches are either agnostic to the underlying inspection task, or strive to ensure coverage of the POIs without accounting for the quality of the resulting inspection plan. We present Inspection-SPARS, which is, to our knowledge, the first inspection-roadmap sparsifier with POI coverage and path-quality guarantees relative to the dense roadmap. To this end, we generalize the SPARS framework, a popular task-agnostic sparsifier, from purely geometric criteria to task-oriented ones, introducing an inspection-aware vertex admission mechanism that treats POI coverage as a first-class sparsification criterion alongside connectivity and path quality. Experiments in realistic 3D environments show that Inspection-SPARS reduces vertex and edge counts by 4-8x while preserving coverage, allowing the GIP solver to compute tours up to 25% shorter than with the dense roadmap or state-of-the-art inspection roadmap. More broadly, Inspection-SPARS shows that sparsification can be made task-aware without sacrificing guarantees on solution quality.

</details>


### [80] [Terrain-Aware Autonomous Planetary Exploration for Exteroceptive-Proprioceptive Mapping with Quadruped Scouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35493)
*Alberto Sanchez-Delgado,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向月球样环境的四足机器人地形感知探索框架，融合外部感知与本体感知建图，用于自主探索与低能耗导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主行星探索要求机器人在未知、崎岖地形中导航，并评估风险、可通行性与能量成本；四足机器人适合穿越不规则地表，并能在运动过程中获取移动性相关信息。

Method: 使用机载RGB-D相机构建机器人中心高程图，估计几何可通行性并推导导航代价；并行利用本体感知测量提供交互感知地形线索，以补充几何评估。局部地图增量配准为全局多层表示，探索模块据此在未探索感兴趣区域选择目标；自主导航系统利用地图与代价层引导避障运动。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中的仿真结果表明，可实现自主探索、地图扩展以及地形几何与机器人-地形交互之间的空间关联；利用这些信息进行的后续导航相比初始探索具有更低的平均运输成本（CoT）。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合外部感知与本体感知建图，实现了月球样环境下的地形感知自主探索，并提升了导航能量效率。

Abstract: Autonomous planetary exploration requires robots to navigate unknown, uneven terrain while assessing risk, traversability, and energetic cost. Quadruped scouts are well suited for this task because they can traverse irregular surfaces and gather mobility-relevant information during locomotion. This paper presents a terrain-aware exploration framework that combines exteroceptive and proprioceptive mapping for a quadruped robot in lunar-like environments. An onboard RGB-D camera builds robot-centered elevation maps, estimates geometric traversability, and derives navigation costs for autonomous planning. In parallel, proprioceptive measurements provide interaction-aware terrain cues that complement geometry-based assessment. Local maps are incrementally registered into a global multi-layer representation, which is used by an exploration module to select targets in unexplored regions of interest. The targets are reached by an autonomous navigation system that guides collision-aware motion using the available map and cost layers. Simulation results on NVIDIA Isaac Sim show autonomous exploration, map expansion, and spatial association between terrain geometry and robot-terrain interaction. Subsequent navigation using this information exhibits lower average Cost of Transport (CoT) than initial exploration.

</details>


### [81] [ForVis: An In-Field Dataset and Benchmark for VIO Using Under-Canopy UAV Flights in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35482)
*Arman Kiani,Masoud Ataei,Elvis Gyaase,Jeffrey Eiyike,Aaron Weiskittel,Prabuddha Chakraborty,Vikas Dhiman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForVis，一个面向森林环境中无人机VI-SLAM的在野数据集与基准，包含12次飞行、563.8秒/1096.8米轨迹，并同步采集双相机、惯性与飞控数据，对7种开源VI-SLAM系统进行504次运行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林环境下无人机VI-SLAM因运动、光照变化、重复植被纹理和振动等因素难以在真实场景中可靠评估，缺少标准化在野数据集与基准。

Method: 构建ForVis数据集与基准：在开放草地、冠层上方和冠层下方等环境执行12次飞行，使用Intel RealSense D435i与OAK-D Pro Wide同步采集视觉数据，并记录惯性及飞控数据；选取7个开源VI-SLAM系统，在504次运行中评测速度、精度与鲁棒性。

Result: 传感器选择对轨迹误差的影响大于不同算法之间的差异：7种方法在OAK-D Pro上的中位误差均低于D435i。

Conclusion: ForVis可为挑战性森林飞行中的VI-SLAM速度、精度和鲁棒性评估提供在野数据与基准支持。

Abstract: Visual-inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for UAVs remains difficult to evaluate in real forest environments, where motion, illumination changes, repetitive vegetation, and vibration can all affect estimation. We present ForVis, an in-field dataset and benchmark for evaluating VI-SLAM during UAV flight in forest environments. The dataset contains twelve flights across open meadow, above-canopy, and under-canopy conditions in each environment. In total, it provides 563.8s of flight over 1096.8m of trajectory, recorded simultaneously with an Intel RealSense D435i and an OAK-D Pro Wide together with inertial and flight-controller data. We benchmark seven open-source VI-SLAM systems over 504 runs. The results show that sensor choice has a larger effect on trajectory error than the spread between algorithms: all seven methods achieve lower median error on the OAK-D Pro than on the D435i. ForVis is intended to support evaluation of speed, accuracy and robustness for VI-SLAM in challenging forest flight.

</details>


### [82] [Robot Tool Design from Scratch via Behavior-Aware Hierarchical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35479)
*Yinghan Chen,Xiyao Tian,Yizan Dai,Yuyang Li,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HOT分层优化框架，从任务所需物理效果出发，联合设计工具的结构、形状与动作；在四个工具使用任务中高效搜索离散结构并优化连续几何/动作，最终3D打印并在真实机器人上验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计工具的能力比理解、选择或使用工具更具智能。现有机器人工具设计方法通常只优化预设或预先生成结构内的连续形状与动作，工具结构本身未进入物理优化闭环。本文研究从零开始、由期望物理结果驱动工具结构、形状和动作的任务导向工具设计。

Method: HOT是一种分层优化方法：上层使用BASS在离散工具结构空间中搜索；下层执行物理优化以评估候选结构的任务行为，并将里程碑进展作为行为证据返回给上层搜索；最终联合优化出工具形状与动作。该方法使工具结构、形状和动作均从目标物理结果中推导得到。

Result: 在四个具有不同物理功能的工具使用任务上，HOT仅评估了最多包含5600万个结构的搜索空间中的一小部分，便发现了功能性结构；后续几何细化降低了所有任务的任务损失并保持成功，且变形具有功能可解释性。3D打印后，工具配合仿真中得到的动作在真实机器人上完成了全部任务。

Conclusion: 从所需物理效应而非已知工具目录出发设计工具，是迈向人类和动物所展现的开放式工具制造的重要一步。

Abstract: The ability to design a tool for a task marks a level of intelligence beyond merely understanding, selecting, or using one. Existing methods for robotic tool design typically optimize a tool's continuous shape and action within a structure that is prescribed or generated beforehand, so the structure itself stays outside the physical optimization loop. We study task-driven tool design from scratch, where tool structure, shape, and action are all derived from the desired physical outcome. Here we show that the three elements can be designed jointly by HOT, a hierarchical optimization whose upper level searches over discrete tool structures with BASS, while lower-level physical optimization evaluates their task behavior and returns milestone progress as behavioral evidence for the search, ultimately providing jointly optimized shape and action. On four tool-use tasks with distinct physical functions, HOT discovers functional structures after evaluating only a small fraction of search spaces containing up to 56 million structures, and the subsequent refinement of their geometry lowers the task loss on all tasks while preserving success, through deformations that are functionally interpretable. Once 3D printed, the tools accomplish all tasks on a real robot with the actions found in simulation. Designing tools from required physical effects, rather than a catalog of known tools, is a step toward the open-ended tool making seen in humans and animals.

</details>


### [83] [CoBrush: A Hierarchical Planning Framework for Human-Robot Co-Painting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35476)
*Dantong Qin,Yike Guo,Qinlin Liu,Alessandro Bozzon,Pan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoBrush分层框架，将多轮人机协同绘画分解为语义、空间与执行过程，在真实丙烯画布上实现渐进式复杂场景创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考驱动绘画或反应式助手多面向单次渲染或草图补全，难以在人类意图随交互演化的条件下维持多轮连贯协作，也难以逐步构建内容丰富场景。

Method: CoBrush将多轮协同绘画建模为协调的语义、空间与执行流程，分离高层意图推断、空间落位与笔画级控制，支持在真实丙烯画布上渐进式场景开发。

Result: 通过真实人机绘画实验、压力测试与用户研究评估，相比单轮基线，方法在语义对齐、空间推进稳定性及机器人动作合理性感知上均更优。

Conclusion: 结构化多阶段推理可提升交互绘画的连贯性与鲁棒性，并支持内容丰富的实体艺术作品的渐进式创作。

Abstract: Embodied co-painting requires a robot to repeatedly update a shared physical canvas while human intent evolves over interaction. Existing reference-driven painters or reactive assistants are typically optimized for single-shot rendering or sketch completion, limiting their ability to sustain coherent multi-round collaboration or to construct complex, content-rich scenes over time. We present CoBrush, a hierarchical framework that formulates multi-round co-painting as a coordinated semantic, spatial, and execution process. By separating high-level intent inference from spatial grounding and stroke-level control, the system supports progressive scene development on real acrylic canvases. We evaluate the framework through real human-robot painting sessions, stress tests, and user studies. Compared to single-turn baselines, our approach achieves stronger semantic alignment, more stable spatial progression, and higher perceived plausibility of robot actions. These results demonstrate that structured multi-stage reasoning improves the coherence and robustness of interactive painting and supports the progressive development of content-rich physical artworks.

</details>


### [84] [Rethinking Causal Action Tokenization with Conditional Annealing in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35469)
*Chenyu Zhang,Yuhang Cao,Daru Du,Yingxi Lu,Jing Shao,Ruoqu Chen,Jiajun Liu,Liu Cao,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CATok，一种面向自回归VLA的因果动作分词器，将动作分词重构为因果结构化生成过程，以提升重建-压缩权衡、推理效率及VLA任务成功率与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作分词器多将分词视为压缩问题，产生的动作表示与自回归骨干网络在语义上不对齐，限制了VLA模型的可扩展性与性能。

Method: CATok将分词视为因果结构化生成过程，引入条件退火机制：通过逐步退火流匹配过程提取动作token，每个token以前序token为条件并编码特定噪声水平下的残差重建信号，形成由粗到细且与自回归建模结构对齐的因果token空间；同时使用基于MMDiT的token条件流匹配解码器，从离散token重建连续动作块，达到混合扩散头架构的精度。离散瓶颈在设计中实现知识隔离，无需显式注意力掩码即可分离高层语义推理与低层运动执行。

Result: 在三个仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务上的广泛评估表明，CATok在重建保真度-压缩权衡和推理效率上持续优于现有分词方法，同时提升VLA任务成功率和训练效率。

Conclusion: CATok为纯自回归VLA系统建立了高性能、可扩展的基础，表明因果结构化动作分词是连接离散自回归建模与连续机器人动作的有效方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a scalable path to robot learning, yet existing action tokenizers treat tokenization as a compression problem, producing representations that are semantically misaligned with the autoregressive backbone. We propose CATok, a causal action tokenizer that reframes tokenization as a causally structured generative process. CATok introduces a conditional annealing mechanism that extracts action tokens by progressively annealing a flow-matching process: each token is conditioned on all preceding tokens and encodes the residual reconstruction signal at a specific noise level, establishing a coarse-to-fine causal token space whose generative semantics are structurally aligned with autoregressive modeling. A token-conditioned flow-matching decoder built on Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (MMDiT) reconstructs continuous action chunks from these discrete tokens with the precision of hybrid diffusion-head architectures. This discrete bottleneck enforces knowledge insulation by design, cleanly separating high-level semantic reasoning from low-level motor execution without requiring explicit attention masking. Extensive evaluations across three simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that CATok consistently surpasses existing tokenization methods in both reconstruction fidelity-compression tradeoff and inference efficiency, while improving VLA task success rate and training efficiency, establishing a high-performance, scalable foundation for purely autoregressive VLA systems.

</details>


### [85] [Uni-VLaT: Whole-Body Tactile Adaptation of VLA Policies for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35450)
*Zihao Wang,Shutong Liu,Siqi Zheng,Liu Cao,Ruoqi Chen,Rundong Liu,Yanchao Yang,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Uni-VLaT，将全身分布式触觉引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，并通过预测未来触觉、本体感觉和视觉表征来学习触觉锚定的多模态上下文，从而提升接触丰富的 loco-manipulation 控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理接触往往决定人形机器人应如何响应，但仅靠视觉和本体感觉难以表征物理交互，尤其在接触区域被遮挡时；相比预定义区域的稀疏力/力矩测量，分布式触觉能保留机器人全身空间分辨的接触模式，因此需要研究如何将全身触觉信息整合进 VLA 策略。

Method: Uni-VLaT 为 VLA 策略引入一条触觉通路，其潜在状态不仅用于动作生成，还被训练以预测未来的触觉、本体感觉和视觉表征。该预测目标构建了触觉锚定的多模态上下文，促使模型更结构化地理解物理世界。

Result: 在五个真机任务上评估，涵盖触觉触发运动、持续物理交互、人-机器人接触和 loco-manipulation。Uni-VLaT 平均成功率为 75%，比无触觉输入基线高 43 个百分点，比有触觉输入但无预测监督的基线高 7 个百分点。在两个预训练 VLA 骨干上，Table Sweeping 均提升 30 个百分点，Back-Tap Walking 提升 85-90 个百分点。消融实验表明，上下文化触觉预测和绝对未来目标对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 预测性触觉学习为将预训练 VLA 策略扩展到全身物理交互提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Physical contact often determines how a humanoid should respond during loco-manipulation, yet vision and proprioception alone are often insufficient to characterize physical interaction, especially when the contact region is occluded. Unlike sparse force or torque measurements at predefined regions, distributed tactile sensing preserves spatially resolved contact patterns across the robot body. We therefore study how to integrate such whole-body tactile information into vision-language-action (VLA) policies for contact-rich control. Our approach, Uni-VLaT, introduces a tactile pathway whose latent state is trained not only for action generation, but also to predict future tactile, proprioceptive, and visual representations. This predictive objective builds a tactile-anchored multimodal context, encouraging a more structured understanding of the physical world. We evaluate Uni-VLaT on five real-robot tasks covering tactile-triggered locomotion, sustained physical interaction, human-robot contact, and loco-manipulation. Uni-VLaT achieves a 75% average success rate, outperforming a baseline without tactile input by 43 points and a tactile-input baseline without predictive supervision by 7 points. Across two pretrained VLA backbones, our method improves Table Sweeping by 30 points on both backbones and Back-Tap Walking by 85-90 points. Ablations further show that contextualized tactile prediction and absolute future targets are critical to performance. These results indicate that predictive tactile learning provides an effective route for extending pretrained VLA policies to whole-body physical interaction.

</details>


### [86] [Revision, Not Restart: Revisable Visual Plans for Closed-Loop World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35439)
*Pengyiang Liu,Junbo Niu,Wenhao Zheng,Xinchen Chen,Canyu Li,Zhongyue Shi,Jiahao Xie,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可修正时序规划（RTP），将预测的视觉未来作为持续的动作条件，并在执行反馈后通过学习的修正桥接对其局部修正，从而提升闭环机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world-action模型用预测视觉未来条件化机器人动作，但执行反馈可能使预测的部分内容失效；然而其中的任务结构仍可能有用，因此不宜完全丢弃或重新规划。

Method: RTP将视觉未来维护为持续的动作条件，并在反馈后进行修正。核心是学习的revision bridge：恢复视觉生成过程中保存的中间状态，并使其后续生成适应当前观测；视觉与动作监督将该修正连接到后续控制。时间感知历史提供观测证据，自适应策略在新噪声基础上选择保留、桥接修正或全新重规划，再解码下一动作。

Result: 在RoboMME和RMBench上，RTP分别取得48.6%和84.8%的任务平均成功率。匹配比较支持学习到的续接有效；估计的checkpoint来源与动作前缀效应为正，但精度较低。

Conclusion: 结果表明，反馈驱动的视觉计划修正与闭环任务性能之间存在联系，RTP为world-action模型中的可修正时序规划提供了有效路径。

Abstract: World-action models use predicted visual futures to condition robot actions, yet execution feedback can invalidate parts of a prediction while leaving its task structure useful. We propose Revisable Temporal Planning (RTP), which maintains the visual future as a persistent action condition and revises it after feedback. Its central mechanism is a learned revision bridge: it resumes an intermediate state saved during visual generation and adapts its continuation to current observations. Visual and action supervision connect this revision to subsequent control. Time-aware history supplies observed evidence, and an adaptive policy selects retention, bridge revision, or fresh replanning from new noise before decoding the next action. On RoboMME and RMBench, RTP achieves task-averaged success rates of 48.6% and 84.8%, respectively. Matched comparisons support learned continuation; estimated checkpoint-source and action-prefix effects are positive but less precisely resolved. These results connect feedback-driven visual-plan revision to closed-loop task performance. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [87] [Self-Evolving Coding Agents: From Digital Programs to Physical-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35432)
*Hongcheng Gao,Jingjing Zhou,Zelin Zheng,Shijia Ge,Jay Zhu,Yazhe Wang,Jianshu Zeng,Xuan Shangguan,Di Wu,Lingyu He,Zhiqi Jia,Sihang Wu,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Physical Coding，将物理任务的状态与执行表示为可检查、可修订的代码，并构建HexaAnything；在机器人基准和真实平台上超越VLA基线，并展现数据、模型与工具的自我演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA/WAM将观测和指令直接映射为动作，导致策略与训练强绑定：布局或视角微小变化即失败，指令泛化差。根因在于表示：任务要求、条件、进度和失败恢复被隐式编码在动作序列中，难以检查或修订。数字编码代理以可执行代码调用工具、验证结果、根据反馈修正，启发物理世界也需显式状态、可管理执行和可修订流程。

Method: 提出Physical Coding：Code as World记录对象、关系、约束与进度；Code as Policy组织规划、验证、恢复与执行。实现HexaAnything，调用感知、规划、控制工具（含VLA/WAM策略），基于外部反馈在环决策。验证过的轨迹转化为数据和记忆，支持从工具与Harness到模型权重、架构乃至硬件和任务设计的演化。

Result: 在RoboCasa365上，HexaAnything在Composite-Unseen和总体成功率上优于XR-1 VLA；Harness训练的HexaModel在每个split上均超过base，表明代码轨迹可内化物理执行。在PhyBench和双臂AgileX机器人上，智能体自主完成物理实验和多数桌面任务，常快于已发表结果。观察到数据、模型和工具的自我演化。

Conclusion: 代码轨迹能内化为模型能力；未来工作面向权重内化、自主重设计架构/语言/表示/任务，以及制造与科学场景部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action (WAM) models map observations and instructions directly to robot actions. This directness ties a policy to training: minor layout or viewpoint changes cause failure, and instructions generalize poorly. The root cause lies in representation: task requirements, conditions, progress, and failure recovery are implicitly encoded in action sequences, making them difficult to inspect or revise. Digital coding agents offer a precedent: LLMs call tools, verify results, and revise from feedback as executable code. The same working pattern of explicit state, manageable execution, and revisable procedures underlies generalization and long-horizon execution in the physical world, letting physical experience return as reusable programs, memory, or evidence. We propose Physical Coding, representing task state and execution as code. Code as World records objects, relations, constraints, and progress; Code as Policy organizes planning, verification, recovery, and execution. We build HexaAnything, which calls perception, planning, and control tools, including VLA/WAM policies, and makes in-the-loop decisions from external feedback. Verified traces become data and memory, enabling evolution from tools and Harness to model weights, architectures, and ultimately hardware and task design. On RoboCasa365, HexaAnything improves Composite-Unseen and overall success over XR-1 VLA, and its Harness-trained HexaModel beats the base on every split, indicating code traces internalize physical execution. On PhyBench and a dual-arm AgileX robot, the agent autonomously completes physics experiments and most tabletop tasks, often faster than published results. We observe data, model, and tool self-evolution; future work targets weight internalization, autonomous redesign of architectures, languages, representations, and tasks, and deployment in manufacturing and science.

</details>


### [88] [Memory in the Sky: Low-Altitude Question Answering with Multi-Agent Memory Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35431)
*Chengyang Li,Yujie Wan,Shuai Wang,Kejiang Ye,Weijie Yuan,Boyu Zhou,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向低空问答（LAQA）的以记忆为中心框架MemCen，用生成对抗考试量化记忆质量，并联合优化UAV选择与发射功率以最大化下游QA价值，实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资源分配基于感知、通信、控制或计算指标，而LAQA需要显式度量记忆价值；同时底层caption、检索、推理为黑盒，需仅通过下游QA表现评估候选记忆质量。

Method: 提出生成对抗考试（GAE），通过前向模拟评估记忆检索，并用考试分数量化记忆质量；基于该指标构建MemCen，在通信约束下联合选择UAV并分配发射功率以最大化记忆质量；在噪声受限区推导QoM感知的capped water-filling功率分配律；进一步提出罚函数逐次优化（PSO）与learning to memorize（L2M）求解器。

Result: MemCen在CARLA Town04和Town05静态/动态通信条件下分别达到92.4%和84.0%的QA准确率；在真实全景多智能体系统（PMAS）基准上达到88.5%的QA准确率；UAV到机器狗演示验证了所获取记忆在环境理解与导航中的实用性。

Conclusion: 记忆价值可被显式量化并用于跨层资源优化，MemCen能提升低空问答性能，且在真实场景中具备环境理解与导航的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies low-altitude question answering (LAQA), in which distributed unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) memories are aggregated at a ground server to answer questions about observations over a long horizon. Unlike conventional resource allocation based on sensing, communication, control, or computation metrics, LAQA requires an explicit measure of memory value. We propose a generative adversarial exam (GAE) that uses forward simulation to evaluate memory retrieval and exam scores to quantify memory quality. This enables the downstream QA value of candidate memories to be measured and optimized without accessing the internal mechanisms of the black-box captioning, retrieval, and reasoning pipeline. Building on this metric, we develop a memory-centric (MemCen) framework that jointly selects UAVs and allocates transmit power to maximize memory quality under communication constraints. In the noise-limited regime, we derive a QoM-aware capped water-filling law that explicitly connects task utility with physical-layer power allocation. We further develop penalty successive optimization (PSO) and learning to memorize (L2M) solvers. MemCen achieves QA accuracies of 92.4% and 84.0% in CARLA Town04 and Town05 under static and dynamic communication conditions, respectively. In real-world experiments, MemCen achieves 88.5% QA accuracy on the panoramic multi-agent system (PMAS) benchmark. Finally, UAV-to-robot-dog demonstrations further validate the practical utility of the acquired memories for environmental understanding and navigation.

</details>


### [89] [Adaptive Safety Filtering for Frozen ACC Policies via Conformal Residual Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35415)
*Zhiruo Zhou,Rigaudiere Z. Li,Chen Xiwen,Yucheng Chen,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出残差感知共形动作过滤（RACF），在部署动态与训练条件不一致时，为冻结的ACC策略校准残差分位数并生成操作裕度，实现无需重训练的动作投影与候选选择，从而在安全性与计算效率间取得更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的自适应巡航控制（ACC）策略在部署动态偏离训练条件时容易违反约束，现有基线（如CBF-QP）可能安全性不足或投影频率过高。

Method: RACF校准固定标称预测器的残差，将其分位数转化为有限模型动作投影的操作裕度；已完成的转移用于更新裕度和候选选择，而无需重新训练策略。方法包含Adaptive与Robust变体，并进行注册对比、消融研究与匹配硬件评估。

Result: 在2,400个控制器-试验单元的注册对比中，Adaptive RACF达到94.3%的回合安全性，较所评估的标称CBF-QP基线提升19.9个百分点，并将投影频率从8.11%降至6.63%。控制实验将4.54个百分点的提升归因于残差-裕度注入。在匹配硬件评估中，Adaptive相较Robust CBF-QP将平均摊销展开时间减少21.2%，安全回合为161/180对170/180。研究还刻画了一步残差覆盖与约束满足之间的联系，并量化了安全性-计算权衡。

Conclusion: RACF通过共形残差校准与动作过滤，在无需重训练策略的情况下提升了ACC部署安全性和计算效率，但安全性与计算开销之间存在可量化的权衡，且硬件评估中安全回合数略低于Robust CBF-QP。

Abstract: Frozen adaptive cruise control (ACC) policies can violate constraints when deployment dynamics differ from their training conditions. We propose residual-aware conformal action filtering (RACF), which calibrates residuals of a fixed nominal predictor and converts their quantile into an operating margin for finite-model action projection. Completed transitions update margins and candidate selection without retraining the policy. In a registered comparison over 2,400 controller-trial units, Adaptive RACF achieves 94.3% episode safety, improving by 19.9 percentage points over the evaluated nominal CBF-QP baseline while reducing projection frequency from 8.11% to 6.63%. A controlled study isolates a 4.54-point improvement from residual-margin injection. In a separate matched-hardware evaluation, Adaptive reduces mean amortized rollout time by 21.2% relative to Robust CBF-QP, with 161/180 versus 170/180 safe episodes. We characterize conditions linking one-step residual coverage to constraint satisfaction and quantify the observed safety-computation trade-offs.

</details>


### [90] [From Pixel to Poses: Object-centric Tool Manipulation Learning from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35375)
*Bangjun Wang,Longyan Wu,Yukun Wei,Shenghe Shao,Chaoyi Huang,Wenze Cui,Zetong Xu,Hanlin Wu,Long Chen,Yi Ma,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P2P-T 是一个从像素到位姿的数据高效、以物体为中心的框架，直接从人类演示中学习工具操作，通过两阶段方法实现复杂工具操作的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作扩展受限于真实机器人数据稀缺；现有利用人类视频演示的方法计算昂贵且依赖配对的人-机器人数据；当前最优方法擅长长时程任务，但在复杂工具操作所需的精细精确控制上表现不佳。

Method: P2P-T 采用两阶段方法：首先预训练以物体为中心的世界模型以提取稳定的位姿先验，然后将这些先验集成到高效、位姿感知的低层策略中；借助现代基础模型驱动的自动化数据处理流程，完全绕过人-机器人对齐数据的需求，大幅降低训练开销，仅需少量逐任务微调。

Result: 在复杂真实世界工具操作任务上，P2P-T 相比此前最优方法执行性能提升 73%，这些任务对标准大规模预训练模型仍难以完成。

Conclusion: P2P-T 证明了无需人-机器人对齐数据即可从人类演示中高效学习工具操作，弥合认知与物理执行差距，为数据高效的机器人工具操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scaling up robotic manipulation is primarily bottlenecked by the scarcity of real-world robot data. While recent approaches leverage human video demonstrations to mitigate this shortage, they remain computationally expensive and still rely on paired human-robot data for domain alignment. Although current state-of-the-arts excel at long-horizon tasks, they struggle with the delicate and precise control required for complex tool manipulation. To overcome these limitations, we introduce P2P-T, from Pixel to Poses for Tool Manipulation, a data-efficient, object-centric framework that learns tool use directly from human demonstrations. P2P-T bridges the cognitive and physical execution gap through a two-stage approach. First, pretraining an object-centric world model to extract stable pose priors; second, integrating these priors into an efficient, pose-aware low-level policy. By utilizing a robust automated data processing pipeline powered by modern foundation models, P2P-T completely bypasses the need for human-robot aligned data. This reduces overall training overhead drastically. With minimal per-task fine-tuning, our framework achieves a 73% improvement over the previous state of the art in execution performance on complex, real-world tool manipulation tasks that currently remain out of reach for standard large-scale pretrained models.

</details>


### [91] [DexAgent: An Agentic Human2Sim2Robot Framework for Dexterous Manipulation with Self-Evolving Tool Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35318)
*Youhui Wang,Yunzhu Li,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexAgent 是一个自进化的智能体式 Human2Sim2Robot 框架，仅凭单段第一人称人类视频与任务提示，即可生成物理合理的机器人轨迹用于策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Human2Sim2Robot 流程依赖预定义步骤，难以适应多样化的物体属性与交互，尤其是铰接体和可变形物体；人类视频虽是可扩展的演示来源，但缺乏能处理长时程、多物体任务的通用转换方法。

Method: DexAgent 分四阶段操作：人类视频语义理解、基于属性的仿真重建、机器人轨迹优化、机器人数据生成。每阶段智能体根据任务与物体属性，从工具库中选择合适技能或按需开发新技能；属性特定的验证器评估阶段结果的物理有效性与任务要求并反馈精炼，防止错误在工作流中传播。最终阶段通过变化仿真中的物体与机器人状态，从单段视频生成多样轨迹，并对渲染观测重纹理化以促进 sim-to-real 迁移。新技能与验证器保留在工具库中，使框架自进化、积累可复用能力，从而随着处理更多人类视频而缩短处理时间。

Result: 在十一个真实世界任务中，使用 DexAgent 生成数据训练的策略成功率比竞争基线高 3.5 倍。

Conclusion: DexAgent 通过自适应、验证引导的智能体流程与自进化工具库，有效处理多样物体与长时程任务，显著提升真实机器人策略训练的数据效率与成功率。

Abstract: Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation. However, existing human-to-simulation-to-robot (Human2Sim2Robot) pipelines rely on predefined procedures that struggle to accommodate diverse object properties and interactions, particularly those involving articulated and deformable objects. We introduce DexAgent, an agentic Human2Sim2Robot framework that converts a single egocentric human video and a task prompt into physically grounded robot trajectories for policy training. It operates through four stages: semantic understanding of human videos, property-based simulation reconstruction, robot trajectory optimization, and robot data generation. At each stage, DexAgent adapts its approach to the task and object properties by selecting suitable skills from its tool library or developing new ones when needed. Property-specific verifiers assess stage outcomes for physical validity and task-specific requirements and provide feedback for refinement, preventing error propagation through the workflow. This adaptive, verification-guided process allows DexAgent to process diverse objects and long-horizon tasks. In the final stage, DexAgent varies object and robot states in simulation to generate diverse robot trajectories from a single human video, then retextures the rendered observations to facilitate sim-to-real transfer. Newly developed skills and verifiers are retained in its tool library, making it self-evolving to accumulate reusable capabilities. This reduces processing time as DexAgent encounters more human videos. Across eleven real-world tasks, policies trained with DexAgent-generated data achieve a 3.5x higher success rate than competing baselines. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [92] [GuardPIBT: Counterfactually Gated Neural Guidance for Ultra-Large-Scale 3D Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35267)
*Yuan Zhou,Zhenyu Hou,Guangtong Xu,Xiaoqiang Ji,Yuqing Tang,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GuardPIBT 在 PIBT 执行器上引入神经残差重排序，以提升大规模密集 3D 多智能体路径规划的扩展性与完成可靠性，最高在 10 万智能体场景中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 3D 多智能体路径规划在密集交通下难度显著增加。PIBT 虽具良好扩展性，但其基于一步目标导向的排序在密集交互与大规模拥塞下可能不足，因此需要在不破坏 PIBT 安全机制的前提下改进候选动作排序。

Method: GuardPIBT 不替换 PIBT 执行器，而是对其进行增强：神经网络仅对 PIBT 原生候选提出残差重排序，最终动作仍由 PIBT 决定。方法包含局部图注意力建模邻近交互，以及全局源–目标运输特征提供群体级协调上下文；使用反事实分组门过滤可能恶化闭环协调的重排序。针对超大规模群体，采用种群自适应分组保持决策粒度、异步缓存推理摊销神经计算，并用选择性修复处理长尾智能体。PIBT 全程保留有效性检查、优先级继承与回溯。

Result: 在多达 100,000 个智能体的实验中，方法在 2D 与 3D 环境中均实现可靠完成；其中三个 100,000 智能体仓库运行全部完成，且审计图违规为零。

Conclusion: GuardPIBT 通过神经引导的安全残差重排序，在保留 PIBT 有效性检查、优先级继承和回溯保证的同时，有效改善密集大规模 3D 多智能体路径规划的协调与扩展性。项目网站已提供。

Abstract: Large-scale 3D multi-agent path finding becomes increasingly difficult under dense traffic. Priority Inheritance with Backtracking (PIBT) scales well, but its one-step goal-directed ordering may become insufficient under dense interactions and large-scale congestion. We present GuardPIBT, which augments rather than replaces the PIBT executor: neural predictions only propose residual reorderings of PIBT's native candidates, while final actions remain determined by PIBT. First, local graph attention models nearby interactions, while global source--goal transport features provide population-level coordination context for candidate reordering. Second, a counterfactual group gate filters reorderings whose closed-loop effects may degrade coordination. Third, for ultra-large populations, population-adaptive grouping preserves decision granularity, asynchronous cached inference amortizes neural computation, and selective repair resolves long-tail agents. PIBT retains validity checking, priority inheritance, and backtracking throughout. Experiments with up to 100,000 agents demonstrate reliable completion across 2D and 3D environments, including all three 100,000-agent warehouse runs with zero audited graph violations. The project website is available at {\color{magenta}\texttt{ this https URL }}.

</details>


### [93] [Repair Before You Fuse: Frozen-Host Adaptation for Corrupted-but-Present Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35264)
*Gia-Huy Thai,Quang-Thinh Ly,Anh-Minh Phan,Tuan Dang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出边界特征修复 BFR：一种冻结宿主适配框架，在 Camera-LiDAR 检测器已消费的模态接口学习任务监督残差修正，无需重训练检测器或推理时额外信息，在 KITTI-C 与 nuScenes-R 上提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使两个传感器均存在、同步且标定，Camera-LiDAR 检测器仍可能继续使用不可靠特征。需要一种面向“损坏但存在”传感的定向改造方法，避免重新训练已部署检测器。

Method: BFR 在检测器已消费的模态接口学习残差修正。BFR-C 修复融合读取的每级相机特征；BFR-L 将宿主条件 LiDAR 候选对齐到选定边界，并相对冻结锚点路由逐 site 创新；BFR-CL 为二者联合训练组合。零初始化逐通道缩放使各变体在优化前为精确检测器级恒等。仅训练修复模块，编码器、融合消费者、路由器、检测头与宿主归一化统计保持冻结。推理时无需干净参考、损坏元数据、时序历史或在线更新。

Result: 在 KITTI-C 全部 20 种损坏、5 个严重度网格上，BFR-C 将 MVX-Net 的 RCE 从 14.07 降至 11.92，将 Focals Conv-F 的 RCE 从 14.29 降至 11.00（相对复现冻结基线）。在后者上，BFR-L 将 AP_cor 从 73.65 提升至 74.48；BFR-CL 达到 77.01 AP_cor、10.46 RCE，干净 AP 为 86.02。在 nuScenes-R 上，BFR-CL 将复现 MoME 基线的 mAP 鲁棒性比从 80.1 提升至 81.4。

Conclusion: 结果表明，边界修复是一种针对损坏但存在传感的定向改造方法，可在不重新训练已部署检测器的情况下提升 Camera-LiDAR 检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR detectors can continue to consume unreliable features even when both sensors remain present, synchronized, and calibrated. We introduce \emph{Boundary Feature Repair} (BFR), a frozen-host adaptation framework that learns task-supervised residual corrections at modality interfaces the detector already consumes. BFR-C repairs each camera feature level read by fusion, whereas BFR-L aligns host-conditioned LiDAR candidates to a selected boundary and routes site-wise innovations relative to the frozen anchor. Their jointly trained composition is BFR-CL. Zero-initialized per-channel scales make every variant an exact detector-level identity before optimization; only the repair modules train, while the encoders, fusion consumer, router, detection head, and host normalization statistics remain fixed. At inference, BFR requires neither clean references, corruption metadata, temporal history, nor online updates. Across the complete 20-corruption, five-severity KITTI-C grid, BFR-C reduces RCE from $14.07$ to $11.92$ on MVX-Net and from $14.29$ to $11.00$ on Focals Conv-F relative to their reproduced frozen baselines. On the latter host, BFR-L raises AP$_{\mathrm{cor}}$ from $73.65$ to $74.48$, while BFR-CL reaches $77.01$ AP$_{\mathrm{cor}}$ and $10.46$ RCE with $86.02$ clean AP. On nuScenes-R, BFR-CL raises the reproduced MoME baseline's mAP robustness ratio from $80.1$ to $81.4$. These results establish boundary repair as a targeted retrofit for corrupted-but-present sensing without retraining the deployed detector.

</details>


### [94] [Spatial Grafting: Grounding 3D Features for Flow-Matching Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35249)
*Dingsheng Liu,Yangzheng Wu,Mahboubeh Asadi,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Yixin Xiao,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Grafting，将冻结空间重建特征绑定为机器人相对度量几何token，并通过交叉注意力注入预训练VLA/WAM的flow-matching动作专家，在多个仿真与真实平台提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人操作策略（VLA或WAM）将交互相关度量几何隐式化；空间重建可提供可靠几何，但其特征只描述局部形状，未说明相对机器人的位置，且如何将此类特征有效注入预训练策略仍未解决。

Method: Spatial Grafting是一个通用、轻量空间模块，不修改宿主感知通路；它利用冻结的重建特征构造度量基础、机器人相对的空间token，并通过交叉注意力注入flow-matching动作专家。同一嫁接架构无需按宿主重新设计，适配两个VLA和两个WAM。

Result: 在四个仿真基准和三个真实机器人平台（单臂与双臂配置）上评估。RoboTwin 2.0双臂操作基准中，嫁接提升所有VLA和WAM宿主；Grafted π0.5在干净与随机化场景分别提升11.3%和15.6%，达到94.0%和92.4%，超过最强已发表3D条件策略WAM4D的93.8%和89.9%。BEHAVIOR-1K双臂移动操作挑战中，在六个任务中的五个超过2025挑战赛冠军，最高提升0.47 Q-score，并在双方均报告的三项任务平均值上超过地图条件空间策略。

Conclusion: Spatial Grafting能以通用、轻量方式为预训练操作策略补足机器人相对度量几何，跨VLA/WAM与真实平台有效，且随任务时程延长收益更明显，无需改动宿主感知主干即可保留预训练优势。

Abstract: Pretrained robot manipulation policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) or world-action models (WAMs) leave interaction-relevant metric geometry implicit. Recent breakthroughs in spatial reconstruction can supply the necessary geometry reliably, but their features describe local shape without stating where it lies with respect to the robot. How best to deliver these features to a pretrained policy remains unresolved. We propose Spatial Grafting, a versatile, lightweight spatial module that binds frozen reconstruction features to metric, robot-relative geometry. Spatial Grafting constructs metric-grounded spatial tokens and injects them into the flow-matching action expert through cross-attention, without modifying the host's perceptual pathway, so the host retains the full benefit of its pretraining.
