We evaluate it more broadly than any geometry-aware policy we compare against: one graft architecture, with no per-host redesign, on two VLAs and two WAMs, across four simulation benchmarks that span short-horizon manipulation, visual robustness, clutter and long-horizon mobile manipulation, and on three real-robot platforms with single- and dual-arm configurations. On RoboTwin 2.0, a dual-arm manipulation benchmark, the graft improves every host across VLAs and WAMs. Grafted $\pi_{0.5}$ gains 11.3% and 15.6% on clean and randomized scenes, reaching 94.0% and 92.4%, above the strongest published 3D-conditioned policy, WAM4D (93.8% and 89.9%). The margin widens as the horizon lengthens: on tasks from BEHAVIOR-1K, a dual-arm mobile manipulation challenge scored by average task progress, it surpasses the 2025 challenge winner on five of six tasks,by up to 0.47 Q-score, and exceeds a map-conditioned spatial policy on average across the three tasks both report.

</details>


### [95] [Zero-Shot Reactive Obstacle Avoidance for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35231)
*Weihang Guo,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NUDGE：一种无需训练的障碍物规避方法，可将有符号距离场的梯度注入基于扩散或流匹配的机器人策略中，在推理时实时引导动作远离障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略（如扩散策略和视觉-语言-动作模型）通常缺乏通用且无需重新训练的障碍物规避能力，因此需要一种可即插即用、适用于多种动作参数化的实时避障方法。

Method: NUDGE 在推理阶段将障碍物有符号距离场（SDF）的梯度注入策略，通过可微关节轨迹解码器支持绝对/相对关节位姿、末端执行器位姿等常见动作参数化，从而在不训练的情况下修正动作。

Result: 实验表明，NUDGE 能保持原策略的任务分布，并能够以反应式方式实时运行。

Conclusion: NUDGE 是一种通用、免训练、可实时运行的障碍物规避程序，可增强扩散/流匹配策略及 VLA 模型的安全性和实用性。

Abstract: We propose NUDGE (Nudge Update via Differentiable GEometry), a training-free obstacle-avoidance procedure that can be incorporated in any robot policy based on diffusion or flow matching, including diffusion policies and vision-language-action models. Our work injects gradients from a signed distance field, a function returning each point's distance to the nearest obstacle, into the policy at inference time to steer it away from obstacles. It supports any common action parameterization, from absolute or relative joint poses to end-effector poses, through a differentiable joint-trajectory decoder. Experiments show that NUDGE preserves the policy's task distribution and runs reactively in real time.

</details>


### [96] [ReCAT: Remember, Count, and Time: Structured Recurrent Memory for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35200)
*Pankhuri Vanjani,Mostafa Hatab,Can Mizrakli,Vaisakh Shaj,Zhuoyue Li,Moritz Reuss,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCAT 是一种带结构化循环记忆的语言条件机器人策略，通过 Mamba-2 与因果注意力记忆观察流，并用流匹配 Transformer 解码器分别交叉关注当前与历史表征，在 LIBERO、RMBench 和真实机器人记忆依赖任务上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖型操作要求机器人利用当前传感器已无法获取的信息做决策，例如回忆先前视觉线索、跟踪任务进度、计数重复事件或估计经过时间，因此需要具备结构化记忆能力的策略。

Method: ReCAT 由指令条件编码器从当前观测提取特征；循环记忆通过 Mamba-2 层和一个因果注意力层整合观测流；流匹配 Transformer 解码器在每个模块中通过独立交叉注意力分别读取当前表征与历史表征。论文还进行受控比较，分析观测编码器、每模块记忆条件化以及不同记忆更新规则的影响。

Result: ReCAT 在 LIBERO 上达到 95.3% 平均成功率，在 RMBench 上达到 62.4%，在九项任务中的六项取得最佳或并列最佳。在空间回忆、事件计数和时间间隔三项真实机器人任务中，最佳 ReCAT 变体达到 66.7% 平均成功率，而最强短历史基线仅为 8.3%。受控比较表明观测编码器和每模块记忆条件化对该性能必要；作为机器人记忆时，面向高效序列建模的更新规则表现不同：加性更新在计数与计时任务上观测成功率最高，delta-rule 更新在空间回忆任务上最佳。

Conclusion: ReCAT 能有效支持记忆依赖型机器人操作，结构化循环记忆、每模块记忆条件化以及任务相关的更新规则选择均对性能至关重要；加性更新与 delta-rule 更新分别在不同记忆任务中占优。

Abstract: Memory-dependent manipulation requires robots to make decisions using information that is no longer available to their current sensors, such as recalling an earlier visual cue, tracking task progress, counting repeated events, or estimating elapsed time. We present ReCAT, a language-conditioned policy with structured recurrent memory. An instruction-conditioned encoder forms features from the current observation. A recurrent memory integrates the observation stream through Mamba-2 layers and one causal attention layer. A flow-matching Transformer decoder reads the current and the historical representation through separate cross-attention in every block. ReCAT reaches 95.3\% average success on LIBERO and 62.4\% on RMBench, with the best or tied-best result on six of nine tasks. On three real-robot tasks probing spatial recall, event counting, and interval timing, the best ReCAT variant reaches 66.7\% average success, against 8.3\% for the strongest short-history baseline. Controlled comparisons within ReCAT show that the observation encoder and every-block memory conditioning are needed for this performance. They also show that update rules developed for efficient sequence modeling behave differently as robot memory: additive updates have the highest observed success on counting and timing, and delta-rule updates on spatial recall. Project website is at this https URL

</details>


### [97] [QuadHand: A Compact Quadrotor Aerial Manipulator with MRC-SDF-Based Whole-Body Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35094)
*Rui Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Yi Wang,Yizhuo Yang,Lihua Xi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QuadHand四旋翼空中操作平台，配备3自由度机械臂、夹爪及电池辅助被动重心补偿模块，并引入MRC-SDF表示与时空全身轨迹优化框架，实现复杂环境下的安全可执行空中操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人空中操作器（UAM）将机械臂与空中平台结合进行三维物理交互，但扩大工作空间会加剧机械臂引起的扰动；现有几何表示在近距离交互中难以兼顾几何保真度与计算效率。

Method: 提出QuadHand紧凑型四旋翼空中操作器，含3自由度机械臂、夹爪与电池辅助被动重心补偿模块以抑制主要臂致扰动；提出MRC-SDF（多关节机器人中心符号距离场），在可计算性下保留精细几何细节；并提出时空全身轨迹优化框架，联合优化四旋翼与机械臂以生成安全可执行轨迹。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，该方法能在复杂环境中实现安全且可执行的空中操作。

Conclusion: QuadHand结合MRC-SDF与全身轨迹优化，有效降低了臂致扰动并实现了复杂环境下的安全可执行空中操作。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs) integrate robotic arms with aerial platforms for three-dimensional physical interaction. However, enlarging the workspace increases arm-induced disturbances, while existing geometric representations face a trade-off between geometric fidelity and computational efficiency in close-proximity interaction. This paper presents QuadHand, a compact quadrotor aerial manipulator with a 3-DoF arm, gripper, and battery-assisted passive CoG compensation module to reduce dominant arm-induced disturbances. We further propose MRC-SDF, a Multi-articulated Robot-Centric Signed Distance Field that preserves fine geometric detail with tractable computation, and a spatiotemporal whole-body trajectory optimization framework that jointly optimizes the quadrotor and manipulator for safe and executable trajectory generation. Simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate safe and executable aerial manipulation in complex environments.

</details>


### [98] [EMPIRIC: Experiment-Driven Learning of Residual World Models for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35047)
*Yichao Liang,Amber Li,Dat Nguyen,Emily Bunnapradist,Michelangelo Naim,Sreela Kodali,Matteo Merler,Bowen Li,Kiran Gopinathan,Yiyun Liu,Nikhil Pimpalkhare,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Adrian Weller,Zenna Tavares,Tom Silver,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EMPIRIC 让机器人以物理引擎为基础学习残差世界模型：用可解释程序补充缺失机制，通过贝叶斯推断估计参数与隐藏状态，从而预测动作结果、主动设计实验并在预测失败时修正模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理引擎能提供运动与接触的先验知识，但常遗漏胶水固化、水加热、风等完整机制；机器人需要从实验数据中学习这些未知机制，才能对陌生物体进行有效规划。

Method: 将物理引擎扩展为残差世界模型，学习缺失机制的代码，使程序可引入新力、约束和隐藏状态；利用贝叶斯推断从含噪观测中估计参数与状态；基于模型预测动作结果、选择信息量大的实验，并在预测失败时修订假设。

Result: 在五个仿真域中，EMPIRIC 学到可解释、可复用的模型，以更少环境交互解决更多任务，优于三个基线；在真实机器人上，它学会了风力与多米诺骨牌质量，并完成操作任务。

Conclusion: 将物理引擎先验与程序化残差学习、贝叶斯推断结合，可高效获得可解释且可迁移的物理机制知识，支持机器人主动实验、规划及真实世界操作。

Abstract: A robot should be able to learn through experiments how unfamiliar objects behave and interact, then plan with that knowledge. It need not start from scratch: physics engines supply knowledge of motion and contact, but can omit entire mechanisms, such as glue curing, water heating, or wind. We present EMPIRIC, an agent that learns a residual world model: a physics engine extended with code for the missing mechanisms. The learned programs can introduce new forces, constraints, and hidden state, and Bayesian inference estimates their parameters and states from noisy observations. The resulting model lets the agent predict the outcomes of actions, choose informative experiments, and revise its hypotheses when predictions fail. Across five simulated domains, EMPIRIC learns interpretable, reusable models, and solves more tasks with fewer environment interactions than all three baselines. On a physical robot, it learns wind forces and domino masses to solve a manipulation task. Website and code: this https URL

</details>


### [99] [Do Not Cut When Uncertain: Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads for VLA Policies in Robotic Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35039)
*Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RCDH，将冻结VLA输出头替换为带显式拒绝和有序条件分解的可校准决策接口，在机器人采摘遮挡场景中通过风险感知弃权恢复OOD可用性并保持ID性能，表明拒绝能力比扩大模型更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略仅输出动作轨迹，无法表达“不知道”或“不应行动”；采摘中的遮挡使单帧决策本质歧义，相同像素可能对应可切割茎或无茎，强制决策会导致高置信、不可逆错误。作者认为VLA的失败模式取决于输出接口而非主干规模。

Method: 提出可拒绝与校准决策头RCDH，可附加到冻结VLA主干，无需重训练或新特征。其包含：(i) 带显式拒绝与有序条件分解的决策模式；(ii) 面向风险感知弃权的校准流程。在具可控叶片遮挡的机器人采摘平台评估，比较生成式、枚举式、校准式与可拒绝式接口，并检验拒绝空间排序是否关键。

Result: 仅替换输出头即可在遮挡下恢复分布外可用性，同时保持分布内性能；进一步测试了拒绝空间排序的重要性。结果表明，拒绝权而非更大模型，是可靠不确定操作中缺失的接口。

Conclusion: VLA在不确定操作中的可靠性瓶颈在于输出接口是否支持拒绝与校准；RCDH提供无需重训练即可实现风险感知弃权的方案，强调拒绝权是可靠操作的关键接口。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained with behavior cloning or flow matching are optimized to output an action trajectory, but they cannot express "I don't know" or "I should not act." In robotic harvesting, occlusion makes single-frame decisions fundamentally ambiguous: identical pixels can correspond either to a cuttable stem or to no stem at all. Existing VLAs are forced to commit, leading to high-confidence errors with irreversible consequences. We argue that the failure mode of a VLA is determined not by backbone scale but by its output interface. We propose Rejectable and Calibrated Decision Heads (RCDH), a typed, rejectable, and calibrated output interface that can be attached to a frozen VLA backbone without retraining or new features. RCDH introduces (i) a decision schema with explicit rejection and ordered, conditional decomposition, and (ii) a calibration procedure for risk-aware abstention. We evaluate RCDH on a robotic harvesting platform with controllable leaf occlusion, comparing generative, enumerated, calibrated, and rejectable interfaces. We show that replacing only the output head restores out-of-distribution usability under occlusion while preserving in-distribution performance. We further test whether the ordering of the rejection space is critical. Our results suggest that the right to refuse, rather than a larger model, is the missing interface for reliable manipulation under uncertainty.

</details>


### [100] [Learning to Act under Visual Interruptions with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35003)
*Mingle Jiang,Rui Xu,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究VLA模型在任务执行中相机中断/丢失时的鲁棒操作问题，提出评估基准MAIL-Bench与缓解方法MINT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常在训练和评估时假设所有相机流全程可用，但当相机在任务执行中停止提供帧时，策略必须仅依赖剩余观测继续闭环操作。该问题具有实际重要性，但其对操作性能的影响尚缺乏系统理解与评估。

Method: 作者提出MAIL-Bench基准，通过在每条成功参考轨迹的多个阶段中断不同相机，评估VLA策略在视觉输入缺失时保持任务能力的情况。进一步提出MINT：先训练VLA策略在缺失视觉输入下仍能工作；推理时使用光流外推或动作条件世界模型选择性补充缺失观测，并在预测不可靠时撤回预测视图。

Result: 在π0.5和GR00T N1.5上的实验表明，MINT在相机丢失情况下显著提升任务成功率，优于原始模型；在AgiBot G2上的实验进一步验证了相机丢失条件下的真实机器人部署。

Conclusion: 视觉中断会显著影响VLA闭环操作，MAIL-Bench提供了系统评估手段，而MINT能有效提升模型在相机缺失下的鲁棒性并支持真机部署。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but they are typically developed and evaluated with all camera streams available throughout task execution. When a camera stops delivering frames during task execution, the policy must continue acting without access to subsequent observations from the missing view. Despite its practical importance, how such interruptions affect closed-loop manipulation remains insufficiently understood. To investigate this problem, we introduce MAIL-Bench, a benchmark that evaluates visual interruptions with VLA models. By interrupting different cameras at multiple stages of each policy's successful reference trajectory, MAIL-Bench measures how well policies retain their capabilities when visual inputs become unavailable. Building on this benchmark, we propose MINT, which first trains VLA policies to remain functional under missing visual inputs. At inference time, MINT selectively supplements missing observations using optical-flow extrapolation or an action-conditioned world model, and withdraws predicted views when they become unreliable. Experiments on $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 show that MINT significantly improves task success under camera loss over the original models. Experiments on AgiBot G2 further demonstrate the real-robot deployment under camera loss. The benchmark is available at this https URL

</details>


### [101] [Graph-Based Simultaneous Path and Foothold Planning for Multi-Limbed Intra-Vehicular Robots in Space Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35000)
*Masazumi Imai,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图论的多肢体内舱机器人路径与落脚点同步规划框架，并在国际空间站舱内3D仿真中验证其可行性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间站机器人辅助操作可减轻宇航员负担并提升在轨活动效率；但多肢体内舱机器人依赖扶手、座椅轨道等离散且有限的抓握点，因此运动规划必须与落脚点规划联合考虑。

Method: 基于图论构建路径与落脚点同步规划框架，在满足可操作度约束下高效搜索多肢机器人的可行支撑序列。

Result: 在国际空间站舱内3D模型仿真中验证，能够生成现实舱内环境下可行且高效的移动规划。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效解决离散抓握点约束下的多肢体内舱机器人运动与落脚点联合规划问题，支持空间站机器人辅助操作。

Abstract: Robot-aided operations in space stations are essential for reducing the workload of astronauts and improving the efficiency of on-orbit activities. Multi-limbed intra-vehicular robots (MLIVRs) equipped with grappling end-effectors have emerged as a promising solution, as they can securely grasp pre-existing interfaces, such as handrails and seat tracks, thereby enabling stable locomotion and forceful manipulation in microgravity environments. Since graspable locations on these interfaces are spatially limited and discretely distributed, motion planning for MLIVRs must be addressed jointly with foothold planning. This paper presents a simultaneous path and foothold planning framework based on graph theory for MLIVRs. The proposed method efficiently searches for feasible stance sequences for a multi-limbed robot while satisfying manipulability constraints. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated through simulations in a 3D model of the International Space Station (ISS) cabin, demonstrating its capability to generate feasible and efficient locomotion plans in realistic intra-vehicular environments.

</details>


### [102] [NavJev: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation via Action-Centric Visual Compression and Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34969)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Jinlong Li,Haojie Dai,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavJev 将零样本 VLN 中逐步自回归多模态推理改为紧凑视觉压缩与轻量类型化动作选择，在 R2R-CE 上以 0.65 秒/步实现 27.0% SR 和 22.4% SPL，显著降低推理延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本视觉语言导航方法依赖多模态大模型，在每一步导航中反复进行自回归多模态推理，导致推理延迟高，限制具身智能体的响应速度。

Method: 提出 NavJev 框架：动作中心视觉压缩（ACVC）融合路点几何、BLIP 描述和 RAM 语义标签，生成候选动作的紧凑表示；判别式动作语义记忆（DASM）过滤共享语义并跨导航步维护动作特定判别证据；Jev 基于这些表示直接对可用动作集进行结构化概率决策。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 上达到 27.0% 成功率（SR）和 22.4% 路径加权成功率（SPL），每步仅 0.65 秒，相比基于 MLLM 的 VLN 方法大幅降低推理延迟与成本。

Conclusion: NavJev 通过紧凑视觉压缩和轻量类型化动作选择，在保持导航性能的同时显著提升零样本 VLN 的推理效率与响应能力。

Abstract: Recent zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) methods increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to reason over visual observations, navigation instructions, and candidate actions. Although effective, repeatedly invoking autoregressive multimodal reasoning at every navigation step introduces substantial inference latency, limiting the responsiveness of embodied agents. We propose NavJev, an efficient VLN framework that reformulates online navigation from repeated multimodal generation into compact visual compression followed by lightweight typed action selection. Specifically, Action-Centric Visual Compression (ACVC) integrates waypoint geometry, BLIP captions, and RAM semantic tags into compact representations of candidate actions, while Discriminative Action-Semantic Memory (DASM) filters shared semantics and maintains discriminative action-specific evidence across navigation steps. Based on these representations, Jev directly performs structured probabilistic decisions over the available action set. Experiments on R2R-CE show that NavJev achieves 27.0% SR and 22.4% SPL with only 0.65 s per navigation step, while substantially reducing inference latency and cost compared with MLLM-based VLN methods. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [103] [RoboFL: Federated Expert Assembly for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34968)
*Rongyu Zhang,Ruizhi Fan,Yunfan Lou,Hengyu Fang,Shenli Zheng,Chenrui Wu,Yili Jin,Li Du,Dan Wang,Yuan Du,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFL，将 MoSAIC（槽位适配器混合）用于联邦世界-动作学习；通过将本地 LoRA 适配器装配为服务器 MoE 专家，并结合 FARD 与 PCEA，实现高效联邦微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 与世界-动作模型受限于物理交互数据稀缺、机构孤岛和任务异构。联邦学习结合参数高效微调可避免交换全模型更新，但直接聚合适配器会纠缠不兼容更新，而引入 MoE 路由又可能稀释专家特化并破坏路由稳定性。

Method: MoSAIC 将各客户端本地训练的 LoRA 适配器直接安装为服务器 MoE 的专家分支；服务器路由器在这些先验分支上学习 token 分配，并联合优化路由与专家参数。FARD 对齐模型三条路径的路由，PCEA 将完整专家更新压缩为紧凑全局适配器，用于个性化再分发。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RLBench 和真实 Franka 机械臂上，RoboFL 的结构化专家装配优于集中式 PEFT InternVLA-A1，在 Franka 上提升 12.23%；相比基于 MoE 的联邦 VLA 基线，每轮客户端通信最多降低 86.81%。

Conclusion: RoboFL/MoSAIC 能有效支持联邦世界-动作学习，在保持专家特化的同时提升性能并显著降低通信开销。

Abstract: Vision-language-action and world-action models are increasingly popular, yet remain bottlenecked by physical interaction data that is scarce, institutionally siloed, and task-heterogeneous. A natural federated solution is to let each client adapt a shared foundation model through parameter-efficient fine-tuning, avoiding the exchange of full-model updates. However, federating these adapters is nontrivial, as naive aggregation can entangle incompatible updates, while incorporating MoE-style routing into federated aggregation may dilute specialization and destabilize expert selection. We present RoboFL, which instantiates MoSAIC (Mixture of Slotted Adapters) for federated world-action learning. MoSAIC directly installs locally trained LoRA adapters as the expert branches of a server MoE. Server-side routers learn token assignments over these prior-informed branches while jointly refining routing and expert parameters. Foresight-to-Action Routing Distillation (FARD) aligns routing across the model's three paths, while Path-Consensus Expert Aggregation (PCEA) converts complete expert updates into a compact global adapter for personalized redistribution. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0, RLBench, and a real-world Franka robot arm show the superiority of RoboFL with structured expert assembly, as it outperforms centralized PEFT InternVLA-A1 by 12.23% on the Franka arm, while reducing per-round client communication by up to 86.81% relative to MoE-based federated VLA baselines.

</details>


### [104] [Don't Throw Away the Tail: Action Upcycling for Policy Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34911)
*Taesung Kwon,Jangho Park,Sunwoo Park,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Action Upcycling：复用动作分块中本将被丢弃的动作，在动作速度平滑时延长执行时域，从而减少策略调用且不损失成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人策略从单次观测预测未来动作块，仅执行前缀后重规划；执行时域长度存在反应性与效率的权衡。短时域反应快但策略调用频繁，长时域高效但反应差。现有测试时自适应方法要么依赖模型内部信号且需针对架构选择，要么额外采样，增加成本。

Method: Action Upcycling 是一种免训练算法，不访问模型内部、不额外采样，而是复用策略本会丢弃的动作。核心发现：只要动作速度保持平滑，被丢弃动作与重规划后的动作版本足够接近；因此将执行时域延长至动作速度开始波动为止。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人操作任务、多种 Vision-Language-Action 模型及一个 World Action Model 上，Action Upcycling 将策略调用减少 1.2–1.7 倍，成功率无损失。该方法成本可忽略，且与少步采样、流式动作解码等其他策略加速方法正交。

Conclusion: Action Upcycling 为任意分块策略提供了一种通用、低成本的新加速轴，可与其他策略加速技术结合使用。

Abstract: Modern robot policies predict a chunk of future actions from a single observation, execute only a prefix, and discard the rest before replanning. Choosing the length of this prefix, the execution horizon, poses a trade-off between reactivity and efficiency. A short horizon keeps the policy reactive to the environment, but requires frequent policy calls. Recent test-time methods adaptively select the horizon for each chunk, but they either read model internals, where the signal must be chosen for each architecture, or draw extra samples, which adds cost. We propose *Action Upcycling*, a training-free algorithm that reuses actions the policy would otherwise discard, without accessing model internals or drawing extra samples. We find that discarded actions stay close to their replanned versions as long as the action velocity remains smooth. Action Upcycling therefore extends the execution horizon up to the point where the velocity begins to fluctuate. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that Action Upcycling reduces policy calls by 1.2--1.7$\times$ with no loss in success rate, across multiple Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) and even a World Action Model (WAM). It applies to any chunked policy at negligible cost and is orthogonal to other policy acceleration methods such as few-step sampling and streaming action decoding, opening a new axis for policy acceleration.

</details>


### [105] [AGRO-SUVIDE: Agentic Robotics for Surgical Viscoelastic Debridement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34823)
*Shutong Jin,Ziyang Chen,Preethi Satish,Medow Shen,Gary Guthart,Florian T. Pokorny,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向外科粘弹性清创的智能体机器人框架 AGRO-SUVIDE，用于反复清除粘弹性基底上的小碎片，并在 dVRK 上通过 340 次物理试验验证其单碎片与多碎片清除及泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强灵活性有望减轻外科医生在重复性手术任务中的疲劳；同时，粘弹性基底上小碎片的反复清除具有挑战性，需要自动化与自改进能力。

Method: 采用模块化 agentic robotics 框架：演示分析模块基于视觉与运动学信息，从单次专家演示中自动识别重复技能；构建模块将每项技能构建为基于程序模型的技能（智能体针对脚手架库编码）或无模型策略技能；运行时监控模块根据观察到的碎片数量将技能组合为循环式图，并验证每项技能的前置与后置条件以决定推进或重试。

Result: 在 da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) 上进行 340 次物理试验。AGRO-SUVIDE 单碎片清除平均成功率为 85%；连续三碎片清除成功率为 60%，加入一次人工干预后达到 95%；并可泛化到未见过的五碎片场景，单碎片清除平均成功率为 80%。

Conclusion: AGRO-SUVIDE 表明智能体机器人框架可有效实现外科粘弹性清创中的重复碎片清除，具备较高成功率、多碎片连续操作能力与泛化性，有潜力减轻术者重复性任务疲劳。

Abstract: Augmented dexterity has the potential to reduce the fatigue experienced by surgeons during repetitive surgical tasks. In this paper, we propose the first AGentic RObotics framework for SUrgical VIscoelastic DEbridement (AGRO-SUVIDE), the repeated removal of small fragments attached to a viscoelastic substrate. Leveraging the self-improving and coding capability of agents, AGRO-SUVIDE adopts a modular framework. Specifically, the demonstration analysis module automatically identifies recurring skills from a single expert demonstration, using both visual and kinematic information. The construction module then builds each skill, either as a procedural model-based skill the agent codes against a scaffolded library or as a model-free policy-based skill. At runtime, the monitoring module composes the skills into a loop-style graph sized to the number of fragments it observes, then verifies pre- and post-conditions of each skill to decide whether to advance or retry. We evaluate AGRO-SUVIDE through 340 physical trials on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK). AGRO-SUVIDE achieves an average single-fragment removal success rate of 85%, completing consecutive three-fragment removal at 60% and at 95% with one human intervention. It further generalizes to unseen five-fragment scenarios with an average success rate of 80% for single-fragment removal. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [106] [CoHuB: A Simulation Benchmark for Multi-Humanoid Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34782)
*Hyunjin Park,Jebeom Chae,Minwoo Park,Sunghyun Park,Hanjun Yoo,Seoyeon Choi,Soochul Yoo,Joohwan Seo,Sarmad Idrees,Jae-Sang Hyun,Jongmin Lee,Roberto Horowitz,Youngwoon Lee,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoHuB——首个在自我中心视觉观测下的多类人机器人协作仿真基准，包含10项任务与多操作员VR遥操作同步演示，实验表明现有视觉运动策略在协作感知与控制上面临显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类环境中的许多物理任务依赖协作，但现有人形机器人基准主要关注单机器人技能，缺乏在自我中心视觉观测下对多机器人协作的评估。

Method: 构建CoHuB仿真基准，提供10项协作任务（8项双人形机器人、2项三人形机器人），覆盖多种协作模式；并通过多操作员VR遥操作流水线，让每位操作员从各自机器人的自我中心视角控制一个机器人，采集同步演示数据。

Result: 使用代表性视觉运动策略进行实验，结果显示在不同形式的协调感知与控制上均存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: CoHuB为开发和评估多类人机器人协作策略提供了基础平台。

Abstract: Many physical tasks in human environments require collaboration, from assisting a partner to jointly manipulating an object. Yet, existing humanoid benchmarks largely focus on single-humanoid skills and lack evaluation of multi-humanoid collaboration under egocentric visual observations. We introduce CoHuB (Collaborative Multi-Humanoid Benchmark), a simulation benchmark for multi-humanoid collaboration under egocentric visual observations. CoHuB provides 10 tasks, eight with two humanoids and two with three humanoids, spanning diverse collaboration patterns. We also provide synchronized demonstrations collected through a multi-operator VR teleoperation pipeline, in which each operator controls one humanoid from its egocentric view. Experiments with representative visuomotor policies reveal substantial challenges across different forms of coordinated perception and control. CoHuB provides a foundation for developing and evaluating multi-humanoid collaboration policies.

</details>


### [107] [Simulation for Planetary Robotic Perception and Autonomy: A Concise Survey of Recent Capabilities and Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34743)
*Hoyun Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文综述行星机器人感知与自主性的仿真工作，按四个维度梳理现状，指出仿真虽关键但研究分散，且在公开性、平台覆盖、传感模态和运行约束报告方面存在不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星机器人对科学探索至关重要，但真实环境中的现场测试成本高、受限且常不可重复，因此仿真成为感知与自主性研究、合成数据生成、系统集成和部署前评估的核心工具；然而相关文献分散于不同仿真引擎、实现和应用场景中。

Method: 围绕四个实践维度综述行星机器人感知与自主性仿真工作：开放性与可用性、场景与平台覆盖、传感器与感知支持、环境与运行真实感。

Result: 所综述工作多报告视觉或物理保真度并支持面向感知的工作流；同时显示公开可用性不均衡、以漫游车为中心的覆盖、对专用传感模态支持不完整，以及对星上计算、能量和通信限制等运行约束的报告不均衡。

Conclusion: 行星机器人仿真研究仍存在开放共享、平台多样性、专用传感支持和运行约束建模与报告方面的差距，需要更系统、开放且贴近任务约束的仿真发展。

Abstract: Planetary robotics is an important enabler of scientific exploration in environments where direct human-in-the-loop operation is costly, hazardous, or infeasible. However, developing and validating planetary robotic systems remains difficult because representative field testing is expensive, limited, and often unrepeatable under mission-relevant conditions. In this setting, simulation serves as a central tool for perception and autonomy research, synthetic data generation, system integration, and pre-deployment evaluation. Despite its importance, the literature on planetary robotics simulation remains dispersed across different simulation engines, implementations, and application settings. This paper surveys simulation works for planetary robotic perception and autonomy across four practical axes: Openness and Availability, Scenario and Platform Coverage, Sensor and Perception Support, and Environmental and Operational Realism. The surveyed simulation works report visual or physical fidelity and support perception-oriented workflows. They also indicate uneven public availability, rover-centered coverage, partial support for specialized sensing modalities, and uneven reporting of operational constraints such as onboard computation, energy, and communication restrictions.

</details>


### [108] [Action Sequence Transfer via LLMs for Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34730)
*Choongho Chung,DongHwan Shin,Sung-Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种动作序列迁移系统，可依据源动作序列与源/目标场景图，自适应生成目标空间中的对应动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户动作序列往往依赖特定空间与物体配置，直接迁移到新环境时需适应新的空间和物体约束。

Method: 利用源动作序列以及源、目标环境的场景图表示，并结合多层级用户活动表示，在不同抽象层次上泛化动作，从而预测目标空间的动作序列；同时基于Ego4D GoalStep构建场景图数据集进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，即使源空间与目标空间的物体配置差异巨大，系统仍能生成有效的动作序列。

Conclusion: 场景图表示与多层级活动抽象有助于实现跨空间的动作序列迁移，并支持物体配置显著不同的环境。

Abstract: We present an action sequence transfer system that adaptively transfers user action sequences across different target spaces. Given an input action sequence from a source space and scene graph representations of both the source and target environments, our system predicts a corresponding action sequence in the target space by adapting to the spatial and object constraints of the new environment. To achieve this, we leverage multi-level representations of user activity to generalize actions at varying levels of abstraction. To demonstrate our system, we collect a new scene graph-based dataset derived from the Ego4D GoalStep dataset for evaluation. Results indicate that our system can generate valid action sequences even between spaces with drastically different object configurations.

</details>


### [109] [DexWeave: Learning Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34724)
*Naichuan Sun,Haotian Shen,Yizhang Zhang,Luying Feng,Haoze Wang,Yuanbo Xiangli,Yaochu Jin,Peidong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexWeave 提出统一框架，将交互一致的运动重定向与解剖感知的全身策略学习结合，实现从人类演示到人形机器人灵巧全身移动操作，并在 Unitree G1 上完成真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示学习人形机器人灵巧移动操作时，不仅需迁移人体运动，还需迁移协调的交互结构；但具身差异会扭曲身体运动、手腕位置、手指关节与物体交互之间的耦合，且运动学准确的参考在机器人动力学下未必可执行。

Method: DexWeave 先以两阶段重定向：用专用求解器初始化身体与手部运动，再在上肢交互链上进行耦合细化，同时保持下肢支撑；随后用解剖感知 Transformer 策略，将解剖区域建模为结构化 token，通过有向掩码注意力建模依赖，并让物体信息选择性调节上肢通路；策略联合输出身体与灵巧手动作，直接用强化学习训练，无需预训练跟踪策略、教师-学生蒸馏或后续残差精修。

Result: 相比 MLP 基线，DexWeave 提高重定向保真度与交互一致性，获得更高操作性能并加快策略收敛；在配备 Inspire 灵巧手的 Unitree G1 实体人形机器人上部署，实现了真实世界的灵巧全身移动操作。

Conclusion: DexWeave 可作为连接交互一致重定向与解剖感知全身策略学习的统一方案，有效支持从人类演示到真实人形机器人灵巧移动操作。

Abstract: Learning dexterous humanoid loco-manipulation from human demonstrations requires transferring not only human motion, but also the coordinated interaction structure underlying the demonstrated behavior. This is challenging because embodiment differences distort the coupling among body motion, wrist placement, finger articulation, and object interaction, while kinematically accurate references may still be difficult to realize under robot dynamics. We present DexWeave, a unified framework that connects interaction-consistent motion retargeting with anatomy-aware whole-body policy learning. DexWeave first employs a two-stage retargeting procedure that initializes body and hand motions with specialized solvers and subsequently performs coupled refinement over the upper-body interaction chain while preserving lower-body support. The resulting references are tracked by an anatomy-aware Transformer policy that represents anatomical regions as structured tokens and uses directed masked attention to model their dependencies, with object information selectively conditioning the upper-body pathway for dexterous interaction. The policy jointly outputs body and dexterous-hand actions and is trained directly with reinforcement learning, without pretrained tracking policies, teacher-student distillation, or subsequent residual refinement. DexWeave improves retargeting fidelity and interaction consistency while achieving higher manipulation performance and faster policy convergence than MLP baselines. We further deploy the learned policies on a physical Unitree G1 humanoid equipped with Inspire dexterous hands, demonstrating dexterous whole-body loco-manipulation in the real world. See our project page ( this https URL ) for videos.

</details>


### [110] [ExcavaTwin: Training-Free Geometry-Guided Semantic Elevation Mapping for Autonomous Excavation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34719)
*Yu Deng,Lingshan Zeng,Tong Hu,Rushi Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ExcavaTwin 是一个纯视觉、几何引导的语义高程建图框架,无需挖掘专用训练,通过冻结视觉模型重建几何与语义观测,经几何约束的多视角语义融合后投影为任务导向语义高程图,在公开数据集与真实挖掘场景中实现了可靠感知(平均更新间隔约1.4 s,动态修改区域平均高程误差12.74 cm)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主挖掘需要一种同时刻画地形几何与任务相关语义的空间表示。现有挖掘建图方法大多以高程为中心、缺乏语义,而通用语义模型在非结构化室外场景中表现不稳定,难以直接满足挖掘任务需求。

Method: 提出 ExcavaTwin:一个纯视觉、几何引导的语义高程建图框架,且无需挖掘专用训练。流程为:1) 利用冻结的视觉模型从多视角 RGB 图像重建场景几何与语义观测;2) 推导地形与非地形的几何支撑;3) 在几何约束下进行多视角语义融合,以抑制不合理预测并补全缺失观测;4) 将融合后的状态投影为面向任务的语义高程地图。

Result: 在公开数据集与真实挖掘场景上的实验表明几何与语义感知可靠:真实挖掘中系统平均更新间隔约为 1.4 s,在动态修改区域的平均高程误差为 12.74 cm。较大误差主要出现在地形快速变化以及机器运动导致的瞬态视觉扰动期间。

Conclusion: ExcavaTwin 以无需挖掘专用训练的纯视觉方式实现了几何与语义联合的语义高程建图,可支撑自主挖掘的可靠感知;其当前主要局限在于快速地形变化与机器运动引起的瞬态视觉干扰会显著增大高程误差,是后续需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous excavation requires a spatial representation that jointly captures terrain geometry and task-relevant semantics. Existing excavation mapping is largely elevation-centric, while generic semantic models remain unstable in unstructured outdoor scenes. We present ExcavaTwin, a pure-vision geometry-guided semantic elevation mapping framework without excavation-specific training. Given multi-view RGB images, the framework: 1) reconstructs scene geometry and semantic observations using frozen vision models; 2) derives terrain and non-terrain geometric support; 3) performs geometry-constrained multi-view semantic fusion to suppress implausible predictions and recover incomplete observations; and 4) projects the fused state into a task-oriented semantic elevation map. Experiments on public datasets and real excavation scenes demonstrate reliable geometric and semantic perception. In real excavation, the system achieved an average update interval of approximately 1.4 s and a mean elevation error of 12.74cm in dynamically modified regions. Larger errors mainly occur during rapid terrain changes and transient visual disturbances caused by machine motion.

</details>


### [111] [SOR-Nav: Search or Relocate? Context-Gated Exploration and Cross-Region Relocation for Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34707)
*Yuan Ji,Zirui Li,Yuxin Cai,Shuge Wu,Boon Siew Han,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOR-Nav 是一种面向 ObjectNav 的分层导航系统，通过显式仲裁“继续探索当前区域”与“转移到更有希望的可达区域”，结合语义探索、聚类决策图与上下文门控 LLM 监督器，在 HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2 和 MP3D 上取得最优 SR/SPL，并完成真实世界连续三目标部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ObjectNav 要求智能体在部分可观测和有限运动预算下，在未知环境中寻找目标物体。现有方法主要通过对候选目的地排序来优化“下一步去哪”，但未显式决定应继续探索当前上下文，还是放弃当前区域并转移到更有希望的可达区域。

Method: 首先构建自主语义探索系统，持续累积持久化 3D 物体聚类，并将可达前沿组织为聚类决策图，以提供高效搜索抽象。随后，SOR-Nav 使用上下文门控的 LLM 驱动物体搜索监督器，评估当前搜索上下文的适合性，并决定继续探索或进行跨区域重定位到另一个可达前沿聚类。

Result: 在 HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2 和 MP3D 的完整未过滤验证集上，SOR-Nav 在三个基准上均取得已报告最高 SR 和 SPL。尤其在 MP3D 上，SPL 从先前最佳的 18.1% 提升至 38.5%，超过两倍，同时 SR 从 50.7% 提升至 61.8%。嵌套 HM3D-v2 消融验证了所提出的决策结构，连续三目标物理部署展示了真实场景中的持续 ObjectNav 运行。

Conclusion: SOR-Nav 通过语义聚类决策图与上下文门控 LLM 监督器，显式仲裁探索与跨区域转移，显著提升 ObjectNav 的成功率与路径效率，并支持真实世界中的持续物体导航。

Abstract: Object navigation requires an embodied agent to find an object in an unseen environment under partial observability and a limited motion budget. Existing methods primarily optimize where the robot should go next by ranking candidate destinations. In contrast to these methods, we present SOR-Nav, a hierarchical navigation system that explicitly arbitrates between continuing to explore the current context and abandoning it for a more promising reachable region. First, an autonomous semantic exploration system is built that accumulates persistent 3D object clusters and organizes reachable frontiers into a cluster decision graph to provide an efficient search abstraction. Then, SOR-Nav uses a context-gated LLM-driven object-search supervisor to evaluate the suitability of the current search context and decide whether to continue exploration or perform cross-region relocation to another reachable frontier cluster. Across the complete, unfiltered validation sets of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and MP3D, SOR-Nav achieves the strongest reported Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL) on all three benchmarks. On MP3D in particular, it more than doubles the previous best SPL from 18.1\% to 38.5\% while increasing SR from 50.7\% to 61.8\%. Nested HM3D-v2 ablations validate the proposed decision structure, while a continuous three-target physical deployment demonstrates persistent ObjectNav operation in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [MarsLab: A Martian Rover Simulator for Planetary Rover Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34702)
*Hoyun Kim,Beomsu Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MarsLab：一个开源、ROS2 原生的火星车仿真器，基于 NVIDIA Isaac Sim，面向自主导航算法开发，支持可定制火星地形、光照与大气尘埃，并提供多模态传感器与真值数据，用于 SLAM 和 VPR 基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来火星任务要求火星车在非结构化地形、变化光照、大气尘埃和有限通信条件下实现自主运行。仿真是在部署前研究这些条件的实用手段，但现有火星相关资源范围各异，包括面向任务的仿真器、固定类比数据集、任务特定环境和开放机器人接口，缺乏统一、可定制的评测平台。

Method: MarsLab 将 HiRISE 衍生地形与程序化地形结合，支持可定制的岩石、陨石坑、太阳光照和大气尘埃设置，并在 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 中运行 Perseverance 级火星车模型。运行时通过标准 ROS2 话题发布 RGB、深度、RGB-D 点云、LiDAR、IMU、轮式里程计和真值位姿数据。

Result: 作者用 MarsLab 开展了跨传感模态、尘埃水平、场景几何和路径长度的 SLAM 基准测试，并在重复火星基地穿越中，针对光照和尘埃变化开展 VPR 基准测试。结果表明，受控场景变化与共享真值轨迹可在同一仿真器内比较轨迹级估计和图像级位置识别。

Conclusion: MarsLab 为火星车自主与导航算法开发提供了开源、ROS2 原生、可配置且带真值的仿真基准平台，有助于在统一条件下评估 SLAM、VPR 等能力。项目页面已提供。

Abstract: Future Mars missions will require rover autonomy that can operate across unstructured terrain, changing illumination, atmospheric dust, and limited communication. Simulation is a practical way to study these conditions before deployment, but existing Mars-relevant resources differ in scope, including mission-oriented simulators, fixed analog datasets, task-specific environments, and open robotics interfaces. In this context, we present MarsLab, an open-source, ROS2-native Mars rover simulator for autonomy and navigation algorithm development. MarsLab combines HiRISE-derived and procedural terrain with customizable rock, crater, solar-illumination, and atmospheric-dust settings, and runs a Perseverance-class rover model in NVIDIA Isaac Sim. The runtime publishes RGB, depth, RGB-D point clouds, LiDAR, IMU, wheel odometry, and Ground Truth (GT) pose data through standard ROS2 topics. We demonstrate MarsLab with Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) benchmarks across sensing modalities, dust levels, scene geometry, and route length, and with Visual Place Recognition (VPR) benchmarks over repeated Mars Base traversals under illumination and dust changes. The results illustrate how controlled scene variation and shared GT trajectories can be used to compare trajectory-level estimation and image-level place recognition within the same simulator. Our Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [Sufficiency of Zeroth-Order Reward Shaping for Policy Gradient in Stabilization Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34695)
*Yisheng Zhang,Tao Wang,Sicun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究机器人强化学习中的奖励塑形，论证稳定控制任务中奖励无需包含一阶（速度）项，但必须对目标相关坐标具备零阶（构型）完备性；一阶状态仍需作为策略观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人深度强化学习严重依赖从最优控制和轨迹优化借鉴的启发式奖励设计；现有方法很少区分控制目标固有的奖励项与数值正则项，导致超参数调优脆弱。

Method: 以稳定控制问题为对象，聚焦零阶（构型）与一阶（速度）信息，从理论和实验两方面分析策略梯度方法在不同奖励组成下的表现。

Result: 理论和实验表明，策略梯度方法无需一阶奖励项即可成功求解稳定任务；加入一阶项会随其尺度增大引入严重敏感性。相反，奖励函数必须在目标相关坐标上满足零阶完备性，而在低耗散假设下一阶状态仍对策略观测必要。

Conclusion: 这些结果为机器人强化学习中的奖励设计提供了可操作且有原则的指导。

Abstract: Reward shaping is fundamental to modern robotic control with deep reinforcement learning (RL), yet practitioners still rely heavily on heuristic principles borrowed from classical optimal control and trajectory optimization. Existing methods rarely distinguish reward terms that are intrinsic to the control objective from numerical regularizers, leading to brittle hyperparameter tuning. To determine which quantities a reward must contain, we study the stabilization control problem with a focus on zeroth-order (configuration) and first-order (velocity) information. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that policy gradient methods can successfully solve stabilization tasks without first-order reward terms, adding such terms can instead introduce severe sensitivity as their scale grows. Conversely, our findings confirm that reward functions must be zeroth-order complete over goal-relevant coordinates, while the first-order state remains necessary in the policy observation under our low-dissipation assumptions. Overall, these results provide actionable and principled guidance for reward design in robotic RL.

</details>


### [114] [Natural State-Prediction Accuracy can Hide Weak Controlled Responsiveness in VLA Readouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34684)
*Hyungjoon Kim,Wonbin Son,Mi Young Lee,Jun Young Lee,Seungmin Rho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出评估框架，将VLA模型内部表示的状态解码预测分解为预测准确率、目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性三个维度；发现高自然轨迹准确率可能伴随弱可控目标状态响应性，且三者提供不同诊断信息，并可用于改进策略失败预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从VLA模型内部表示准确解码物体状态，并不等于预测会忠实响应目标物理状态的变化；自然观测中物体状态、机器人构型、遮挡和任务进度共同变化，使上下文线索可能混入预测。因此需要区分预测准确率、目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性。

Method: 构建基于物理验证观测的评估框架，将目标坐标与机器人上下文交叉，以分离预测准确率、目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性；对表示、读出和训练数据进行比较与干预；并将响应性和上下文敏感性加入基于初始状态误差和物理变量的失败预测器。

Result: 固定表示-读出对中，高自然轨迹准确率可与弱受控目标状态响应性共存；三类属性提供不同诊断信息；加入响应性和上下文敏感性后，在新初始化上的策略失败预测误差较指定基线降低，同时同观测受控MAE也具信息量。

Conclusion: 应同时评估目标状态响应性和上下文稳定性，而非仅看自然预测准确率，并需进一步考察这些属性与实际策略行为和任务结果的关系。

Abstract: Accurately decoding object states from the internal representations of vision-language-action (VLA) models does not establish that the predictions respond faithfully to changes in the target physical state. In natural observations, object state, robot configuration, occlusion, and task progress vary together, allowing contextual cues to contribute to prediction. In this paper, we introduce an evaluation framework that separates prediction accuracy, target-state responsiveness, and context stability using physically validated observations that cross target coordinates with robot contexts. We demonstrate that high natural-trajectory accuracy can coexist with weak controlled target-state responsiveness in fixed representation-readout pairs. Comparisons and interventions involving representations, readouts, and training data show that the three properties provide distinct diagnostic information. Furthermore, adding responsiveness and context sensitivity to a failure predictor based on initial state error and physical variables reduces policy-failure prediction error on new initializations relative to the specified baseline while same-observation controlled MAE is also informative. These findings motivate evaluating target-state responsiveness and context stability alongside natural prediction accuracy, and examining their relationship to actual policy behavior and task outcomes.

</details>


### [115] [HOI-Retarget: Contact-Centric Retargeting for Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34674)
*Jihwan Shin,Adrià López Escoriza,Junzhe He,Matthias Heyrman,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以接触为中心的重定向方法HOI-Retarget，将人-物交互迁移到人形机器人，用于大规模运动数据生成，并公开代码和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习示范（LfD）已使人形机器人获得多样全身技能，但扩展至人-物交互（HOI）受限于缺乏与机器人兼容的交互参考数据。

Method: 采用窗口化轨迹优化，在物体坐标系中将每个标注接触作为目标，在机器人运动学限制下平衡身体跟踪、足部支撑和平滑性；可扩展对象尺寸、吸收单目视频重建接触，并支持多机器人操作同一物体。

Result: 方法能够将单个示范增强到不同物体尺寸，利用单目视频重建的接触，并扩展到多个机器人共同操作一个物体；同时公开代码和重定向运动数据集。

Conclusion: HOI-Retarget为将人-物交互迁移到人形机器人提供了可行方案，有助于大规模生成机器人兼容的交互运动数据。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) has enabled humanoid robots to acquire diverse whole-body skills, but extending this paradigm to human-object interaction (HOI) is limited by the availability of robot-compatible interaction references. We present HOI-Retarget, a contact-centric retargeting method that transfers HOI onto a humanoid robot for large-scale motion-data generation. Its windowed trajectory optimization uses every labeled contact as a target in the object frame, balancing body tracking, foot support and smoothness under the robot's kinematic limits. The method can augment a single demonstration across object sizes, absorb contacts reconstructed from monocular video, and extend to several robots manipulating one object. We publicly release the code and the retargeted motion dataset.

</details>


### [116] [On the Numerical Reliability of Differentiable Physics-Based Optimization for Robotic Material Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34666)
*Xintong Yang,Minglun Wei,Yu-Kun Lai,Ze Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究用于机器人材料操作的可微物理仿真中长时程、接触丰富滚转的梯度数值可靠性，基于弹塑性和颗粒操作的两个MPM系统辨识基准，发现三类影响优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微物理广泛用于系统辨识、轨迹/技能优化、演示生成及机器人/末端执行器设计，这些应用依赖长时程接触丰富仿真的梯度，但其数值可靠性直接影响优化结果，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 采用两个从弹塑性和颗粒操作衍生的MPM系统辨识基准，提供受控案例，考察三种效应：GPU多对一求和顺序随线程调度变化、有限差分检查在长滚转下的可靠性、观测与损失定义对优化行为的影响。

Result: GPU非确定性求和改变长时程梯度并相对于确定性参考反转一个参数梯度符号；有限差分检查随滚转变长而不可靠，因重复运行损失变异增长远快于参数扰动引起的损失变化；观测和损失定义改变优化行为及独立指标偏好的解。

Conclusion: 建议采用可复现累加、将扰动引起的损失变化与重复运行变异进行比较的有限差分验证，并在使用可微仿真进行机器人优化时明确报告目标构造。

Abstract: Differentiable physics is increasingly used in robotic material manipulation for system identification, trajectory or skill optimization, demonstration generation, and robot or end-effector design. These applications depend on gradients propagated through long, contact-rich simulation rollouts. We study the numerical reliability of those gradients using two Material Point Method (MPM) system-identification benchmarks derived from elastoplastic and granular manipulation. The benchmarks provide controlled cases for three effects that also arise in broader differentiable physics-based optimization. GPU many-to-one sums whose order depends on thread scheduling changed long-horizon gradients and reversed the sign of one parameter gradient relative to a deterministic reference. Finite-difference checks became less reliable for longer rollouts because repeated-run loss variation grew much faster than the loss change produced by the tested parameter perturbations. Observation and loss definitions changed optimization behaviour and the solution preferred by an independent metric. These results motivate reproducible accumulation, finite-difference validation that compares perturbation-induced loss changes with repeated-run variation, and explicit reporting of objective construction when differentiable simulation is used for robotic optimization.

</details>


### [117] [Efficient World Action Model Inference with Adaptive Intermediate States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34608)
*Zhinnan Liu,Haozhi Han,Ruge Zhang,Teng Ma,Tao Ma,Zheng Liu,Yifeng Chen,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 WAMACHINE，通过保留并自适应推理状态来加速世界动作模型（WAM）推断，在几乎不损失任务成功率的情况下显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 通过联合建模动作与环境动态实现未来感知控制，但迭代扩散或流推断带来较高的去噪延迟。先前推理状态虽可用于加速，然而规划上下文、观测和中间表示的变化会迅速使保留状态过时，因此需要自适应推理状态，而非直接复用。

Method: WAMACHINE 是免训练框架：跨闭环重规划采用 Trajectory Remapping，将前一重规划状态重映射以初始化下一重规划，减少冗余轨迹生成；跨去噪步采用 Observation Rebinding，在动作执行期间进行预推断，并在一致性检查通过后将保留的去噪状态绑定到真实观测以继续推断，降低控制循环暴露的延迟；跨 Transformer 层采用 Residual Rescaling，选择性重缩放保留的层状态，并在探针检查失败时通过中间层全计算刷新，减少重复 Transformer 计算。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 2.0 上对三种代表性 WAM 架构的评估显示，WAMACHINE 在观测到动作延迟上取得 1.47-3.05 倍加速，在每次重规划的 GPU 推断时间上取得 2.23-3.27 倍加速，同时保留原生 WAM 任务成功率的 96.69-99.54%。

Conclusion: WAMACHINE 能有效加速 WAM 推断并近乎保持原生任务性能，表明保留并自适应推理状态是提升未来感知控制效率的实用方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable future-aware control by jointly modeling actions and environment dynamics. However, iterative diffusion or flow inference incurs substantial denoising latency. Prior inference state offers a natural opportunity for acceleration, yet changing planning contexts, observations, and intermediate representations can quickly render retained state stale. Preserving useful computation therefore requires adapting inference state rather than reusing it as-is. To this end, we present $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$, a training-free framework that accelerates WAM inference by preserving and adapting inference state for efficient and accurate continuation as the control loop evolves. Across closed-loop replans, Trajectory Remapping remaps replan state from the preceding replan to initialize the next replan, reducing redundant trajectory generation. Across denoising steps, Observation Rebinding performs anticipatory inference during action execution and rebinds retained denoising state to the real observation for continuation when consistency checks pass, reducing latency exposed to the control loop. Across Transformer layers, Residual Rescaling selectively rescales retained layer state and refreshes it through full computation of the middle layers when probe checks fail, reducing repeated Transformer computation. Evaluations of three representative WAM architectures on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 show that $\mathrm{WAM}{\scriptstyle\mathrm{ACHINE}}$ achieves 1.47-3.05$\times$ speedups in observation-to-action latency and 2.23-3.27$\times$ speedups in GPU inference time per replan, while preserving 96.69-99.54% of native WAM task success.

</details>


### [118] [Aerial GRIPPER: A Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34594)
*Zeshuai Chen,Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GRIPPER框架，将空中抓取非合作目标建模为不完全信息一般和追逃博弈，通过梯度实时逆博弈预测与规划在线推断目标代价参数并迭代求解开环纳什均衡，结合抗扰控制实现>50 Hz的实时预测、规划与精确抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作目标的精确捕获极具挑战，需在信息不完备、强对抗和实时性约束下预测目标策略并规划己方动作。

Method: 将交互建模为不完全信息一般和追逃博弈；在线推断目标底层代价参数，并在滚动时域循环中迭代精炼开环纳什均衡（OLNE）策略；开发计算友好的梯度逆博弈求解器，利用隐式微分和快速Hessian-向量积避免显式Hessian逆，实现>50 Hz优化更新；设计抗扰控制器以克服不确定负载和夹爪驱动扰动，实现规划轨迹精确跟踪与目标准确抓取。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明GRIPPER具有更优的计算效率和任务性能，完成了非合作目标的捕获与递送任务。

Conclusion: 该框架在高度对抗场景中展现出鲁棒性、适应性和实时性能。

Abstract: Accurate capture of non-cooperative targets is critical. In an attempt to tackle this intractable challenge, an aerial gripper system integrated with a Gradient-based Real-time Inverse-game Predictor and PlannER (GRIPPER) framework is proposed. The interaction is formulated as a general-sum pursuit-evasion game under incomplete information. Specifically, underlying cost parameters of the target are inferred online, and the open-loop Nash equilibrium (OLNE) strategy is iteratively refined within a receding-horizon loop. To ensure high-frequency execution, a computationally friendly gradient-based inverse-game solver is developed. Without explicit computation of the Hessian inverse, the optimized solution is updated (> 50 Hz) based on implicit differentiation and fast Hessian-vector products. Meanwhile, an anti-disturbance controller is developed to overcome disturbances of uncertain payload and gripper actuation, enabling precise tracking of the planned trajectory and accurate grasping of the target. Simulations and real-world experiments illustrate the superior computational efficiency and task performance of GRIPPER. The task of capturing and delivering a non-cooperative target is accomplished, highlighting the robustness, adaptability, and real-time performance of the framework in highly adversarial scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [Where Memory Belongs: Ledger, an Object Ledger for Memory-Augmented VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34554)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Jack B. Jedlicki,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Ledger：将短期感知记忆放在策略内，将长期物体记忆外置为可读记录，并在 RoboMME 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、部分可观测的机器人操作需要记忆，但现有 VLA 将记忆直接嵌入策略，单一策略内机制无法覆盖所有时空维度，且显著落后于 oracle 方法。作者认为记忆类型决定记忆应存放的位置：短期感知记忆（重复、计时、回溯）应留在策略内，长期物体记忆（持久空间状态、容器关系、事件历史）应外置为显式可读记录。

Method: Ledger 是一个 harness，基于单个微调的 π_{0.5} 策略，将策略内帧采样记忆与外部时空物体记忆（ledger）配对。ledger 由 SAM3 跟踪器和 VLM 描述器从演示中构建，并由 LLM 规划器在步骤边界读取与决策。系统在运行时根据指令和记录选择记忆来源，从而无需任务级路由器。

Result: 在 RoboMME 上，Ledger 在评估方法中取得最高的四套平均性能 64.3%，优于最强先前方法在相同评估下的 45.9%；物体引用为 60.7% 对 40.3%，物体持久性为 86.7% 对 56.2%，且仅使用一组权重。

Conclusion: 按记忆类型分离内外记忆是有效的：策略内短期感知记忆与显式可读的外置长期物体记忆互补，能够提升长时程部分可观测操作性能，并避免任务级路由。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon, partially observed robotic manipulation: a robot must remember which object was placed in a drawer, whose cup it moved, or how many action cycles have elapsed. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models embed memory directly inside the policy, but benchmarks show no single in-policy mechanism covers all spatio-temporal dimensions, trailing oracle methods by a wide margin. We argue that memory type dictates where memory should reside: short-term perceptual memory (repetition, timing, retracing) belongs inside the policy, while long-term object memory (persistent spatial state, containment, event history) belongs outside as an explicit, readable record. We present Ledger, a harness that realizes this split over a single fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ policy by pairing an in-policy frame-sampling memory with an external spatio-temporal object memory, the ledger, built from a SAM3 tracker and a VLM captioner of the demonstration and read by an LLM planner that decides at step boundaries. On RoboMME, Ledger reaches the highest four-suite average among the evaluated methods, 64.3% (vs. 45.9% for the strongest prior method under identical evaluation), leading object reference (60.7% vs. 40.3%) and object permanence (86.7% vs. 56.2%) using a single set of weights. Choosing the memory source at runtime, from the instruction and the record, removes the need for a task-level router.

</details>


### [120] [Gaze Prompts: Temporally Dense Human Attention for Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34550)
*Yihan Zhou,Rui Yan,Mingcong Li,Zheyuan Huang,Xu Yang,Xueyang Guo,Yilin Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出眼动追踪监督的注视提示（gaze prompting）：利用VR遥操作中记录的注视点，为VLA微调提供帧级视觉引导，显著提升真实双臂操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA微调虽在每步对齐图像与动作，但通常只提供任务级语言指令，导致逐步的视觉相关性隐式、不明确。

Method: 训练时，将记录的注视位置渲染为十字准星叠加在机器人头部相机图像上；部署时，用轻量预测器根据近期图像和指令估计注视位置，提供同类视觉提示，无需眼动仪，也不改变策略架构。基于π0实现。

Result: 在六个真实世界双臂操作任务上，平均成功率从26.3%提升至56.0%；用单一策略训练全部六个任务时也有增益。同时发布GazeMani数据集，包含1,200条带同步注视的遥操作轨迹。

Conclusion: 帧级注视提示可作为有效的视觉监督信号，增强VLA微调性能，并具备无需部署眼动仪的可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) fine-tuning pairs images with actions at every step, yet typically provides only a task-level language instruction, leaving moment-to-moment visual relevance implicit. We introduce \emph{eye-tracker-supervised gaze prompting}, which uses gaze recorded during VR teleoperation to provide frame-level visual guidance for VLA fine-tuning. During training, recorded gaze locations are rendered as crosshairs on the robot's head-camera images. At deployment, a lightweight predictor estimates gaze locations from recent images and the instruction, supplying the same type of visual prompt without an eye tracker or changes to the policy architecture. Instantiated with $\pi_0$, gaze prompting increases mean success from $26.3\%$ to $56.0\%$ across six real-world bimanual manipulation tasks, with gains also observed when a single policy is trained on all six tasks. We release \textsc{GazeMani}, a dataset of $1{,}200$ teleoperated trajectories with synchronized gaze.

</details>


### [121] [MonoEgo: Monocular Metric Egocentric Demonstration Capture with Passive Wrist Constellations and Sparse Workstation Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34512)
*Jie Xu,Kangjin Yu,Ziyi Jin,Beichen Wang,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoEgo 是一种仅用单个 90 FPS 全局快门相机进行离线单目重建的采集系统，通过被动腕部标志星座、稀疏工作站锚点和场景共同成像，替代主动腕部追踪硬件与多设备同步，实现图像对齐的度量演示采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像对齐的度量演示通常依赖专用追踪硬件，并需要跨设备同步，导致采集端部署复杂、成本较高。该工作希望以被动固定装置和离线重建降低采集侧要求。

Method: MonoEgo 用一台 90 FPS 全局快门相机在共享图像时钟下观测标定后的被动腕部星座、稀疏工作站锚点和场景。MonoTag SLAM 结合标记角点与 ORB 几何，并利用视觉证据拒绝模糊的平面标记位姿。其 Metric Atlas 支持区间尺度重锚定、经验证的地图合并，以及在最终地图支持下对早期帧进行回溯定位。相机和腕部星座输出保留有效性与地图来源，不支持的运动被标记为缺失。

Result: 实验表明，系统可在连续锚点可见性之外进行度量追踪，重连受支持的地图组件，并恢复部分缺失相机位姿。与多传感器相机参考及独立静态星座测试的比较刻画了轨迹一致性和精度，同时揭示覆盖不完整和残余几何不确定性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，被动固定装置与离线重建能够减少采集端需求。动态精度、部署效果以及下游策略收益仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Image-aligned metric demonstrations often require dedicated tracking hardware and synchronization across devices. We present MonoEgo, a capture system that replaces active wrist instrumentation with offline monocular reconstruction. One 90-FPS global-shutter camera observes calibrated passive wrist constellations, sparse workstation anchors, and the scene on a shared image clock. MonoTag SLAM combines marker corners with ORB geometry and uses visual evidence to reject ambiguous planar-marker poses. Its metric Atlas supports interval scale re-anchoring, verified map merging, and retrospective localization of earlier frames supported by the final map. Camera and wrist-constellation outputs retain validity and map provenance, and unsupported motion is left missing. Experiments show metric tracking beyond continuous anchor visibility, reconnection of supported map components, and recovery of some missing camera poses. Comparisons against a multisensor camera reference and separate stationary-constellation tests characterize trajectory agreement and precision while revealing incomplete coverage and residual geometric uncertainty. The results indicate that passive fixtures and offline reconstruction can reduce capture-side requirements. Dynamic accuracy, deployment, and downstream policy benefits require further study.

</details>


### [122] [Robot-Assisted Deployment and Maintenance of Inflatable Modules for Lunar Habitation: A Field Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34498)
*Kentaro Uno,Shamistan Karimov,Ashutosh Mishra,Elian Neppel,Hazal Gozbasi,Shreya Santra,Shinichi Kimura,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并演示了由充气模块与模块化可重构机器人MoonBot组成的系统，用于未来月球栖息地的部署与维护任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球长期居住与原位开发需要建设人类前哨，机器人是支撑未来月球栖息地模块部署与维护的关键技术。

Method: 提出充气模块与模块化可重构机器人相结合的系统，使用真实硬件MoonBot和充气模块HIDAS，在实验室或月球模拟试验场执行机器人辅助任务演示。

Result: 演示任务包括：充气过程中的机器人巡检、模块位置对准、最终安全锁定，以及部署后维护所需的三维建图。

Conclusion: 总结的经验教训为机器人应用于未来月球栖息地提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Long-term human habitation and in-situ development on the Moon open a new era of space utilization. In this context, robots are a key technology for facilitating the construction of future human outposts. Toward the deployment and establishment of human habitation modules on the lunar surface, we propose a combined system consisting of inflatable modules and a modular, reconfigurable robotic system. This paper presents a report demonstrating various robot-assisted task executions using real hardware, namely the modular and reconfigurable robot MoonBot and the inflatable module HIDAS, to enhance the reliability of their deployment and maintenance. The demonstrated tasks include robotic inspection during inflation, module position alignment, final safety locking, and three-dimensional mapping for post-deployment maintenance. All demonstrations were conducted either in a laboratory environment or at a lunar analogue test site. Finally, lessons learned are discussed to provide essential insights for this robotic application to future lunar habitation.

</details>


### [123] [Model-Informed Safe Reinforcement Learning for Bipedal Locomotion via Step-to-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34486)
*Victor Paredes,Ayonga Hereid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以ALIP模板为锚的模型信息强化学习框架，用DECBF提供步到步安全证书，并同时作为训练塑形信号与运行时动作滤波器，在Digit人形机器人上减少外部扰动下的安全违规，但存在安全与跟踪的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需在杂乱、以人为中心的环境中实现灵活移动，实际部署要求有原则的安全保障；经典基于模型的步态生成器虽可解释，但鲁棒性和适应性弱于现代强化学习方法。

Method: 提出模型信息强化学习框架，以解析的角动量线性倒立摆（ALIP）模板为锚点；通过离散指数控制屏障函数（DECBF）为ALIP踏步提供步到步安全证书，并将其用作训练时的塑形信号和运行时动作滤波器，以最小程度调整摆动脚落点来满足模板级约束；在MuJoCo中结合全身控制器栈对Digit人形机器人进行全阶安全实证评估。

Result: 与无约束基线相比，该方法在所报告的外部扰动试验中减少了安全违规事件；但在较大的侧向速度瞬态下，表现出安全与跟踪之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 将模型信息与强化学习结合、并以ALIP模板级安全证书约束策略，可提升人形机器人扰动下的安全性，但需进一步平衡安全约束与运动跟踪性能。

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise versatile mobility in cluttered, human-centric environments, but real deployment demands principled safety. Classical model-based gait generators yield interpretable motions but often lack the robustness and adaptability of modern reinforcement learning (RL) based approaches. We propose a model-informed reinforcement learning framework anchored to the analytical Angular Momentum Linear Inverted Pendulum (ALIP) template. We provide a step-to-step safety certificate for ALIP stepping via a discrete exponential control barrier function (DECBF) and use it as (i) a training-time shaping signal and (ii) a runtime action filter that minimally adjusts swing-foot placement to satisfy template-level constraints. Full-order safety is evaluated empirically on the Digit humanoid in MuJoCo with a whole-body controller stack. Compared to an unconstrained baseline, our approach reduces safety-violation events in the reported external-disturbance trial, while larger lateral-velocity transients reveal a safety-tracking tradeoff.

</details>


### [124] [Trajectory-Safe Orienteering for Human-Robot Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34485)
*Songqun Gao,Elena Basei,Marco Roveri,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对共享空间中带时间窗与可变收益的定向问题（OPTWVP），提出两阶段解耦离散-连续优化与服务时间引导轨迹（DeCoST）方法，兼顾无碰撞轨迹与任务执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在共享工作空间中难以同时保证轨迹安全可行并高质量完成任务；因此研究OPTWVP，以支持人机协作等实际应用。

Method: 提出DeCoST：采用两阶段解耦的离散-连续优化，并结合服务时间引导轨迹；同时引入节点的安全感知时间窗与离散化工作空间，确保末端执行器与人类之间无碰撞。

Result: 初步结果验证了DeCoST在生成无碰撞轨迹规划方面的有效性，同时保持定向任务执行质量。

Conclusion: DeCoST可有效求解共享空间中的OPTWVP，在安全性与任务质量之间取得平衡，但当前结果仍为初步验证。

Abstract: Orienteering problem (OP) has wide real-world applications and also great potential in human-robot collaboration. However, existing approaches struggle to simultaneously ensure safe and feasible trajectories while achieving high-quality task execution in shared workspaces. To this end, this work studies the OP with time windows and variable profits (OPTWVP). A two-stage DEcoupled discrete-Continuous Optimization with Service-time-guided Trajectory (DeCoST) approach is proposed to effectively solve OPTWVP in shared spaces. Meanwhile, the safety-aware time windows of nodes and the discretized workspace are introduced to ensure collision-free trajectories between the end effector and the human. Preliminary results validate the effectiveness of DeCoST in generating collision-free trajectory plans while preserving the quality of orienteering tasks.

</details>


### [125] [ARS: Agentic Reward System for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34484)
*Sheng Hu,Weiyi Lu,Lingbing Zeng,Gan Weng,Weiwei Zhang,Kai Xie,Xiaofeng Mou,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic Reward System（ARS）：一种无需额外训练奖励模型、基于通用视觉语言模型（VLM）的进度奖励建模推理框架，通过子智能体提议事件时间线、主智能体验证修正并估计逐帧进度，在语义错配基准、仿真策略学习及真实机器人长时程任务上优于现有奖励基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进度奖励建模旨在估计机器人行为随时间对任务进度的改变，其可靠估计需区分有意义的状态变化与失败尝试、任务无关动作。现有奖励基线在错误物体操作上会产生虚假进度，而直接使用通用 VLM 亦不够可靠。

Method: ARS 以离线轨迹与任务指令为输入，采用自适应视觉检查同时完成事件提议与验证：子智能体提出任务相关事件时间线，主智能体对其进行验证与修订，再估计逐帧进度。框架可选地引入终止结果标签与视觉参考以辅助判断，并可审计外部奖励模型的进度估计。整个流程无需额外训练奖励模型。

Result: 以 27B VLM 在受控语义错配基准上评估，发现多个奖励基线即使在简单 pick-and-place 场景中也会对错误物体操作赋予虚假进度；ARS 更好地抑制此类错误，并在仿真策略学习中超越这些基线。进一步在真实机器人多螺钉紧固任务（工业洗衣机装配线全尺寸实验室复刻）上，展示了从混合质量离线经验中进行长时程策略学习的能力。

Conclusion: 结构化推理与验证可提升通用 VLM 在机器人奖励建模中的实用性。代码已公开。

Abstract: Progress reward modeling is the problem of estimating how a robot's behavior changes task progress over time. Reliable estimation requires distinguishing meaningful state changes from failed attempts and task-irrelevant actions. We introduce the Agentic Reward System (ARS), an inference framework for progress reward modeling with general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs), without additional reward-model training. Given an offline trajectory and a task instruction, ARS uses adaptive visual inspection for both event proposal and verification. A subagent proposes a task-relevant event timeline, which a primary agent verifies and revises before estimating per-frame progress. ARS can incorporate optional terminal outcome labels and visual references to inform its judgments. It can also audit progress estimates from external reward models. We evaluate ARS with a 27B VLM on a controlled semantic-mismatch benchmark and downstream policy learning in simulation and on a real robot. The benchmark reveals that several evaluated reward baselines assign spurious progress to wrong-object manipulation even in simple pick-and-place scenes. ARS better suppresses these errors and outperforms these baselines in simulation policy learning. We further demonstrate that ARS supports long-horizon policy learning from mixed-quality offline experience on real-robot multi-screw fastening in a full-scale laboratory replica of an industrial washing-machine assembly line. These results suggest that structured inference and verification can improve the usefulness of general-purpose VLMs for robot reward modeling. Code is at this https URL

</details>


### [126] [From World Models to World Action Models: Rethinking Next-State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34414)
*Tingyu Yuan,Ziming Ji,Biaoliang Guan,Wen Ye,Wenrui Tian,Zhaopeng Gu,Feihong Zhang,Xu Yang,Yan Huang,Zhaowen Li,Chaoyang Zhao,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CF-WAM 提出动态下一状态预测框架，通过将同一未来状态的视觉、语义、几何和交互投影统一为视频形式进行多投影监督，提升世界动作模型的控制性能与跨具身泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在训练前固定下一状态为RGB、单潜特征或预定义目标的静态组合，使动作学习受限于特定表示的归纳偏置。为突破此限制，需要让下一状态表示动态多样，以暴露更多与动作相关的约束。

Method: CF-WAM 对同一动作条件未来的视觉、语义、几何与交互投影进行采样，并标准化为统一视频形式，用这些投影联合监督统一的WAM。不同投影提供的动作相关约束在训练中累积，促使模型学习支撑同一未来的状态转移结构；该动态机制也为人类与机器人学习提供跨具身动力学参考系，使异构经验绕过外观差异，共同学习共享状态转移。

Result: 实验表明CF-WAM提升训练效率与最终控制性能，并能将人类经验有效转化为策略增益。在RoboCasa-GR1上平均成功率82.50%，LIBERO-Plus上82.65%，真实世界评估最高84.00%。

Conclusion: CF-WAM通过动态多投影下一状态预测缓解固定表示偏置，实现更强的动作学习与跨具身泛化，并在多个基准和真实场景取得SOTA性能。

Abstract: Predicting the next state is a core paradigm of World Models for modeling physical dynamics, emphasizing prediction fidelity. As World Models evolve into World-Action Models (WAMs), existing methods still fix the next state before training as RGB, a single latent feature, or a static combination of predefined targets, thereby constraining action learning to the inductive biases preserved by a particular representation. To address this limitation, we propose CF-WAM, a dynamic next-state prediction framework that samples visual, semantic, geometric, and interaction projections of the same future, standardizes them into a common video form, and supervises a unified WAM across these projections. The action-relevant constraints exposed by these projections accumulate across training steps, forcing WAM to capture the underlying state-transition structure that supports multiple projections of the same action-conditioned future. This dynamic mechanism also provides a natural cross-embodiment dynamics reference frame for Human and Robot learning. By jointly learning across different next-state parameterizations, heterogeneous Human and Robot experience can bypass appearance differences and directly contribute to shared state-transition learning, improving cross-embodiment generalization. Experiments show that CF-WAM improves both training efficiency and final control performance, while translating Human experience effectively into policy gains. CF-WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on RoboCasa-GR1 with an average success rate of 82.50%, while reaching 82.65% on LIBERO-Plus and up to 84.00% in real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [127] [From Language to Task Maps: Compiling Semantic Relations While Preserving Task-Relevant Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34412)
*Jaegyun Park,Jingwang Lee,Jungsoo Lee,Soonwoong Hwang,Wansoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种类型化语义到几何接口，将语言中的实体、关系与阶段编译为保留关系可见几何与自由度的连续控制目标，并用共形几何代数与RMPflow组合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言指令通常只给出定性关系，而连续控制器需要可求值的任务量、微分和完成条件；将定性关系展开为完整位姿会引入未指定的额外约束。因此需要明确语言模型与机器人侧编译器的责任划分，并只约束关系相关维度、保留其余自由度。

Method: 语言层指定实体、关系与阶段，每种关系索引一个注册规范，记录任务相关区分与应保留自由度；机器人侧编译器基于共形几何代数（CGA）对规范进行grounding，构造关系特定任务映射与一致微分，并通过RMPflow组合所得策略。

Result: 在60条指令上比较纯语义拓扑接口与额外要求关系特定几何规范的接口：两者正确共享语义内容均为41/60，但在各自接口要求下的关键错误分别为19/60与58/60。64次grounded评估覆盖8种几何关系形式，任务映射保留注册零方向并响应关系相关扰动；解析方向导数与有限差分一致，Jacobian秩与注册维度匹配。MuJoCo中Franka Emika Panda的三组闭环消融显示，固定关系保留坐标使终端进度误差中位数增加20.24–71.00 mm，而保留关系误差仍在评估界内。

Conclusion: 结果支持将关系可见几何与保留自由度共同编译为可组合的连续目标，从而在自然语言定性关系与连续控制器之间建立更可靠的接口。

Abstract: Natural-language manipulation instructions specify qualitative relations, whereas continuous controllers require state-evaluable task quantities, differentials, and completion conditions. Because a qualitative relation generally leaves part of the relative configuration unspecified, expanding it into a complete pose can introduce unintended constraints. We present a typed semantic-to-geometric interface in which language specifies entities, relations, and phases, while each relation indexes a registered specification of its task-relevant distinctions and preserved freedoms. A robot-side compiler grounds these specifications, constructs relation-specific task maps and consistent differentials using conformal geometric algebra, and composes the resulting policies through RMPflow. To evaluate the division of responsibility between the language model and the compiler, we compared a Semantic Topology interface with one that additionally requires relation-specific geometric specifications over 60 instructions. Both produced correct shared semantic content in 41/60 cases, but critical errors under their respective interface requirements occurred in 19/60 and 58/60 cases. Across 64 grounded evaluations spanning eight geometric relation forms, the task maps preserved registered null directions and responded to relation-relevant perturbations; analytic directional derivatives agreed with finite differences, and Jacobian ranks matched the registered dimensions. In three closed-loop ablations using a simulated Franka Emika Panda in MuJoCo, fixing a relation-preserved coordinate increased median terminal progress error by 20.24--71.00~mm while the retained relation errors remained within their evaluation bounds. These results support compiling relation-visible geometry and preserved freedom together into composable continuous objectives.

</details>


### [128] [Contact-Aware Impedance Controller for Robot-Assisted Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34400)
*MD Miraj Arefin,M Efe Tiryaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于B型超声图像的接触感知方法与接触感知阻抗控制器，用于机器人辅助超声成像；无需力测量即可检测声学接触，并在成像过程中估计探针-组织有效接触点以更新控制器交互坐标系，实现接触点视觉伺服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助超声成像需要可靠地检测并定位探针与组织的交互，以保证安全；传统方法依赖力测量，可能存在残余力/力矩偏置问题，因此需要独立于力测量的接触感知手段。

Method: 基于B型超声图像检测声学接触，不依赖力测量；利用接触状态进行力/力矩条件式归零以减少残余力旋量偏置。接触过程中，沿曲面探针表面连续估计有效接触位置，并用其更新阻抗控制器的交互坐标系，从而对物理探针-组织接触点进行视觉伺服。

Result: 在琼脂仿体实验中，探针滚转角为-15°至15°时，接触定位RMSE为1.46±0.14 mm。静态滚动过程中，所提控制器保持与常规固定坐标系阻抗控制器相当的任务空间跟踪精度，同时将最大压缩交互力从31.56 N降至20.09 N，降低36.3%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，超声图像可作为直接接触反馈，用于安全且准确的机器人辅助超声成像，具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Safe robot-assisted ultrasound imaging requires a reliable controller able to detect and localize probe--tissue interaction. In this paper, we present a B-mode ultrasound image-based contact perception method and a contact-aware impedance controller for robotic ultrasound imaging. The proposed method detects acoustic contact independently of force measurements, enabling contact-conditioned force/torque taring to reduce residual wrench bias. During contact, the method continuously estimates the effective contact location along the curved probe surface and uses it to update the controller interaction frame, enabling visual servoing of the physical probe--tissue contact point during imaging. Experiments on an agar phantom demonstrated a contact-localization RMSE of $\mathbf{1.46 \pm 0.14}$~mm over probe roll angles from $\mathbf{-15^\circ}$ to $\mathbf{15^\circ}$. During static rolling, the proposed controller maintained task-space tracking accuracy comparable to a conventional fixed-frame impedance controller while reducing the maximum compressive interaction force from $\mathbf{31.56}$~N to $\mathbf{20.09}$~N, corresponding to a $\mathbf{36.3\%}$ reduction. These results demonstrate the potential of ultrasound images as direct contact feedback for safe and accurate robot-assisted ultrasound imaging.

</details>


### [129] [RoboIRGBench: Benchmarking Implicit Referential Grounding in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34384)
*Aernaer Akelijiang,Jiannan Li,Zhineng Chen,Jingjing Chen,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究机器人操作中指令信息隐式指代时的指代落地能力，提出RoboIRG-Bench基准，系统评估代表性VLA模型，发现存在明显的指代鲁棒性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基准通常假设任务相关信息在指令中被显式给出，但真实场景中人类常隐式指代物体、数量和关系，机器人需从语言与感知上下文中恢复目标，因此有必要研究隐式指代落地（IRG）这一能力。

Method: 基于RoboMME构建RoboIRG-Bench，包含来自11个任务的40个变体，覆盖直接、推理中介、空间和上下文四类指代落地挑战；评估具有不同记忆机制的代表性VLA模型，并在Franka Research 3机械臂上进行真实世界验证。

Result: 实验揭示明显的指代鲁棒性差距：显式指令下表现良好的模型在需从上下文恢复任务相关信息时性能急剧下降；推理中介和空间指代尤其困难；使用外部VLM的模型鲁棒性更强但仍存在显著失败，且替换为更强VLM并未消除差距；真实机器人上差距持续存在，表现为错误指代落地和下游执行失败。

Conclusion: IRG是可靠机器人指令跟随中一个独特且尚未充分探索的能力；研究结果强调需要能够稳健整合语言、感知、推理与动作的VLA模型。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet existing benchmarks typically assume that task-relevant information is explicitly specified in the instruction. In practice, however, humans frequently refer to objects, quantities, and relations implicitly, requiring robots to recover the intended target from linguistic and perceptual context. We study this capability as Implicit Referential Grounding (IRG) and introduce RoboIRG-Bench, a manipulation benchmark designed to systematically evaluate it. Built upon RoboMME, RoboIRG-Bench contains 40 variants derived from 11 tasks and covers four challenges, including direct, reasoning-mediated, spatial, and contextual referential grounding. As IRG often requires retaining and retrieving previously established context, we evaluate representative VLAs spanning different memory mechanisms. Our evaluation reveals a noticeable referential robustness gap. Models that perform well under explicit instructions can degrade sharply when the same task-relevant information must be recovered from context. Reasoning-mediated and spatial references are particularly challenging, while models using external VLMs show greater robustness but still exhibit significant failures. Moreover, replacing the external VLM with a stronger model does not eliminate these gaps. We further validate these findings on a Franka Research 3 robot arm, where the gap persists under real-world manipulation and manifests as both incorrect referent grounding and downstream execution failures. These results establish IRG as a distinct and underexplored capability for reliable robotic instruction following and highlight the need for VLAs that can robustly integrate language, perception, reasoning, and action.

</details>


### [130] [FutureDuet: Decoupling Observation Access from Future Supervision in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34362)
*Jie Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Junqi Liu,Weichen Zhang,Haibin Huang,Yin Chen,Jingyan Jiang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureDuet 是一种世界动作模型：保留主视角与腕部视角用于控制，但为二者设计不同的未来预测目标，并用 ActionDiT 融合任务状态与交互状态，在 RoboTwin50 和 LIBERO 上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 通常将主视角和腕部视角融合为单一视觉流，并用相同的未来视频目标训练；但两者视觉动态不同：主视角稳定，反映场景级任务演化；腕部视角随末端执行器运动，混杂局部交互变化、视角偏移与自遮挡。因此，两个视角可能更适合不同的未来监督目标。

Method: FutureDuet 分别监督两个视觉流。主视角用未来 RGB 建模任务演化，并用交互掩码和机器人骨架将监督聚焦于任务物体与机器人运动；腕部流用未来潜在表示预测短时交互变化，无需像素级重建。ActionDiT 联合读取由此得到的 Task State 和 Interaction State，结合场景级进展与近距离交互证据。所有辅助预测模块仅用于训练，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在 RoboTwin50 上达到 94.2% clean 和 94.1% randomized 成功率，在 LIBERO 上平均成功率为 99.2%。在六个需要精确交互的 RoboTwin50 任务上提升最明显，相比 Fast-WAM 在 clean 和 randomized 设置下平均增益分别为 9.2% 和 12.8%。控制实验进一步表明，分离腕部通路以及为两个视角分别设计未来监督均带来互补收益。

Conclusion: 针对主视角与腕部视角的不同视觉动态采用差异化未来监督，并融合场景级任务状态与近距离交互状态，可有效提升机器人操作策略的精度与泛化能力，同时不引入额外推理成本。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot action generation with future visual supervision. Existing WAMs commonly fuse main and wrist observations into one visual stream and train both with the same future-video objective, despite their different visual dynamics. A stable main camera reveals scene-level task evolution, whereas wrist cameras move with the end effector, mixing local interaction changes with viewpoint shifts and self-occlusion. These contrasting predictive demands suggest that the two views may benefit from different future objectives. We introduce FutureDuet, which retains both views for control, while allowing each visual stream to receive a different future objective. For the main view, future RGB models task evolution, while interaction masks and robot skeletons focus supervision on task objects and robot motion. For the wrist stream, future latent prediction models short-horizon interaction changes without requiring pixel-level reconstruction. ActionDiT jointly reads the resulting Task State and Interaction State, combining scene-level progress with close-range interaction evidence. All auxiliary prediction modules are training-only, adding no inference overhead. FutureDuet achieves 94.2% clean and 94.1% randomized success on RoboTwin50 and 99.2% average success on LIBERO. The improvements are most pronounced on six RoboTwin50 tasks that require precise interaction, averaging gains of 9.2% and 12.8% over Fast-WAM in clean and randomized settings. Controlled studies further show complementary gains from separating the wrist pathway and designing future supervision separately for the two views.

</details>


### [131] [Predictive Semantic Safety: From Visual Physical Reasoning to Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34356)
*Taekyung Kim,Salem Fradi,Yanning Dai,Mateusz Ostaszewski,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测性语义安全（PSS）框架，将视觉语言模型的物理推理与基于后备控制的安全性滤波结合，以预测未来物体占据并生成最小修正约束；在MuJoCo/Unitree Go1实验中安全回合率达99.3%，显著优于仅用当前几何的Backup CBF基线（43.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人当前几何环境无法反映物理交互可能引发的未来危险（如物体坠落、支撑丧失、接触传播），因此需要预测语义层面的未来事件并将其纳入安全约束。

Method: PSS用视觉语言模型预测物理事件及其发生时间，或直接预测物体位移；用显式运动模型将事件假设转为物体轨迹；通过分裂共形预测跨指定物体、观测时间与未来时间联合校准位置误差，并以几何形状边界将位置区域转为预测占据；随后针对给定后备机动评估该占据，推导输入仿射约束，在机器人动力学和输入限制下以最小修正保持后备可行性。

Result: 在MuJoCo中使用Unitree Go1，针对坠落装置、冲击导致支撑丧失和接触传播等场景，PSS取得99.3%的安全回合率；仅使用当前障碍几何的Backup Control Barrier Function基线为43.3%。

Conclusion: 将视觉物理推理、共形预测和后备安全滤波结合，可有效预测并规避由物理交互引发的未来危险，显著提升动态不确定场景下的机器人安全性。

Abstract: Physical interactions can create future hazards that are not apparent from the robot's current geometric surroundings. We present a framework termed Predictive Semantic Safety (PSS), which connects visual physical reasoning to backup-based safety filtering. A vision-language model (VLM) predicts physical events and their timing or directly predicts object displacements. An explicit motion model converts event hypotheses into object trajectories. Split conformal prediction calibrates position errors jointly across specified objects, observation times, and future times; geometric shape bounds convert the resulting position regions into predicted object occupancy. PSS evaluates a prescribed backup maneuver against this occupancy and derives input-affine constraints for minimally modifying the nominal input while preserving backup feasibility under the robot dynamics and input limits. MuJoCo experiments with a Unitree Go1 consider falling fixtures, impact-driven support loss, and contact propagation. PSS achieves a safe episode rate of 99.3%, compared with 43.3% for a Backup Control Barrier Function baseline that only uses current obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [132] [When World Models Lie: Adaptive Safety Analysis Under Wrong Imaginations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34300)
*John Cao,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应潜空间安全滤波器，利用观测到的世界模型误差和自适应共形推断校准安全推理，在模型准确时保持低保守性、失配时提高谨慎性，并在仿真与硬件实验中减少失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型虽可用于高维机器人安全推理，但其预测可能有偏、校准差或自信错误；基于世界模型动力学学习的Hamilton-Jacobi安全值函数会继承误差，导致潜空间安全滤波器产生过度自信的安全估计。现有依赖集成分歧或值目标一致性残差的自适应信号，即使模型偏离观测也可能很小。

Method: 使用自适应共形推断，根据预测潜在状态与由观测推断的潜在状态之间的差异，在线构造不确定性集；然后在这些集合上最小化已学习的安全值函数，以悲观方式评估安全性，从而动态调节保守程度；并给出自适应不确定性半径的有限时间覆盖保证。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，与最先进的潜空间安全滤波器相比，该方法显著减少失败，同时保持任务完成。

Conclusion: 直接利用观测到的世界模型误差进行校准，可使潜空间安全滤波器在模型准确时最小保守、在模型失配时更谨慎，并为自适应不确定性提供理论保证，提升高维机器人系统的安全推理可靠性。

Abstract: World models offer a powerful substrate for safety reasoning in high-dimensional robotic systems, but they are also fallible: their predictions can be biased, miscalibrated, or confidently wrong.
