The model can then be updated with additional engineering, manufacturer, or system-specific information before formal multi-objective optimization provides feasibility and Pareto-optimality guarantees with respect to the finalized model and specified design space. We evaluate the framework on quadcopter and line-following robot component-selection problems. Across 30 direct LLM design trials, none could be verified as feasible under the corresponding finalized model. Comparisons with an independently developed expert model and successive stages of model refinement further showed that changes in modeling assumptions substantially altered the predicted feasible and Pareto-optimal design sets. Together, these results show that using LLMs to construct explicit engineering models makes the underlying design choices available for inspection and revision before those assumptions determine the optimized designs. Explicit modeling therefore provides an interface for combining LLM-generated engineering knowledge, system-specific information, and formal design optimization.

</details>


### [167] [Recursive Harness Distillation across Agents for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33378)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出递归工具带蒸馏（Recursive Harness Distillation），将强智能体的干预经验蒸馏为可复用操作手册，并通过轻智能体执行反馈递归优化，从而在不更新模型参数的情况下提升机器人操作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要跨任务、跨环境完成操作。VLA 模型虽具备广泛操作能力，但在需要诊断失败并调整行为时表现受限，而强智能体可通过与策略交互发现有效干预。

Method: 强智能体将自身经验蒸馏为给轻智能体的 playbook，随后根据轻智能体的执行反馈递归精炼该 playbook，使智能体能够在新任务实例中复用积累的干预知识，且无需更新模型参数。

Result: 真实世界操作成功率从37.3%提升至64.0%；在SimplerEnv Bridge上，轻智能体使用playbook达到66.7%，高于仅用GR00T的41.7%基线，并超过无playbook的强智能体；同一playbook也使强智能体达到79.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，harness distillation在机器人领域可行：干预经验可以被积累、通过执行反馈精炼，并跨智能体复用，从而提升操作性能。

Abstract: A central goal in robotics is to enable manipulation across changing tasks and environments. Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad manipulation capabilities but can struggle when execution requires diagnosing failures and adapting behavior. Strong agents can discover effective interventions through interaction with these policies. We propose Recursive Harness Distillation to accumulate this experience as reusable guidance across agents. A strong agent distills its experience into a playbook for a light agent, then recursively refines the playbook using the light agent's execution feedback. The resulting playbook enables agents to reuse accumulated intervention knowledge in new task instances without updating model parameters. In real-world manipulation, the harness improves success from 37.3% to 64.0%. On SimplerEnv Bridge, the light agent with the playbook achieves 66.7% success, compared with 41.7% for the GR00T-only baseline, and outperforms the strong agent without a playbook. The same playbook also benefits the strong agent, which reaches 79.2% success. These results demonstrate the feasibility of harness distillation for robotics: intervention experience can be accumulated, refined through execution, and reused across agents to improve manipulation.

</details>


### [168] [Traceable Human-to-Humanoid Sign Language Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33354)
*Ao Liu,Shengeng Tang,Lechao Cheng,Yanbin Hao,Bingkun Bao,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HumanoidCSL-20K 中文手语数据集与基准，通过多版本对齐与可溯源修复，将视频人体动作转化为可执行的人形机器人手语轨迹并进行分阶段评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语数据采集成本高、遥操作难以扩展，因此需要复用大规模视频语料；但视频到人形机器人轨迹中的拟合、人体动作修复、重定向、机器人几何修复和控制误差难以从最终轨迹单独区分。

Method: 构建 20,648 条句级中文手语序列，每条包含源数据、修复后人体动作、直接机器人参考和几何修复后机器人参考四个对齐版本；采用观测支持的局部人体动作修复、全机器人几何修复和跨表示溯源；使用配对评估衡量人体运动连续性、内容保持、机器人参考可行性和物理执行，并提出手语特定运动学参考协议，评分手型、位置、掌向和双手关系。

Result: 全语料结果显示，修复后异常手臂/手部步态更少，双手之间及手与身体之间的穿透减少；控制实验区分了参考可学习性与课程效应，组件分数揭示了剩余执行误差。

Conclusion: 该数据集与基准使从视频到人形机器人手语生成的各处理阶段可追溯、可评估，为手语机器人学习与执行提供诊断工具，并促进大规模视频语料的复用。

Abstract: Sign data collection is costly, and teleoperation scales poorly, motivating reuse of large video corpora. Humanoid signing requires converting video-derived human motion into robot trajectories while preserving linguistic motion cues. Errors from fitting, human-motion repair, retargeting, robot geometry repair, and control are hard to separate from the final trajectory alone. We introduce HumanoidCSL-20K, a dataset and benchmark of 20,648 sentence-level Chinese Sign Language sequences, each with four aligned versions: the source, the repaired human motion, the direct robot reference, and the geometry-repaired robot reference. Observation-supported local human-motion repair, full-robot geometry repair, and cross-representation provenance make each transformation traceable. Paired evaluations measure human-motion continuity and content preservation, robot-reference feasibility, and physical execution. A sign-specific kinematic-reference protocol scores handshape, location, palm orientation, and inter-hand relation over the full planned motion. Full-corpus results show fewer abnormal arm / hand steps and less inter-hand and hand-body penetration after repair. Control experiments separate reference learnability from curriculum effects, while component scores expose remaining execution errors.

</details>


### [169] [SocialHumanoid: Towards Expressive Humanoid Behavior via One-Step Co-Speech Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33311)
*Chengqun Yang,Tengjie Zhu,Liang Xu,Fulong Liu,Guanzhu Ren,Yitong Xing,Xuefeng Lu,Fei Shi,Siyuan Fan,Weijie Dong,Yao Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SocialHumanoid：面向人形机器人的一步式共语动作生成系统，可依据语音与情感条件实时生成全身动作并驱动机器人执行；同时发布 AffectMoCap 情感动作捕捉数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人作为具身社交智能体需要与语音同步、富有情感且可实时执行的身体行为，但现有共语动作生成方法主要面向数字人，缺乏对情感控制与低延迟连续生成的联合支持，难以直接部署到物理实体。

Method: 给定响应语音与指定情感条件，SocialHumanoid 通过单次前向传播生成每个全身动作窗口，并利用运动历史条件连接相邻窗口；生成的人体动作在线转换为与机器人本体兼容的参考，由全身控制器跟踪执行。为提供情感身体表达的显式监督，构建 AffectMoCap 数据集，约4小时，由两名专业演员采集，包含同步语音、身体动作、精细手部动作与情感标注。

Result: 在 BEAT2 上，SocialHumanoid 在所比较的生成方法中取得最优 FGD，语音-动作同步性具竞争力；相同协议下推理速度约为 GestureLSM 的6倍。感知评估表明，使用 AffectMoCap 训练可提升从生成身体动作中进行情感识别的效果；真机实验验证了连续的情感条件行为与稳定的长时程执行。

Conclusion: SocialHumanoid 弥合了数字人与物理人形机器人之间在共语、情感可控、低延迟动作生成上的差距，实现了可在真实人形机器人上连续运行并保持长时稳定的情感化身体行为。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are increasingly expected to serve as embodied social agents that communicate naturally with humans through face-to-face interaction. During such communication, humanoid robots require body behaviors that are synchronized with speech, affectively expressive, and suitable for real-time execution. However, existing co-speech methods are primarily developed for digital humans and lack joint support for affective control and low-latency continuous generation on physical embodiments. To bridge this gap, we present SocialHumanoid, a system for expressive humanoid behavior via one-step co-speech motion generation. Given response speech and a specified affective condition, SocialHumanoid generates each full-body motion window in a single forward pass and connects successive windows through motion-history conditioning. The generated human motion is further converted online into embodiment-compatible robot references and tracked by a whole-body controller for physical execution. To provide explicit supervision for affective body expression, we further introduce AffectMoCap, a 4-hour dataset captured from two professional actors, containing synchronized speech, body motion, fine-grained hand motion, and emotion annotations. On BEAT2, SocialHumanoid achieves the best FGD among the compared generation methods, competitive speech-motion synchrony, and approximately $6\times$ faster inference than GestureLSM under the same protocol. Perceptual evaluations further show that training with AffectMoCap improves affect recognition from generated body motion, while real-robot experiments demonstrate continuous affect-conditioned behavior and stable long-horizon execution. Our project page is this https URL .

</details>


### [170] [CompliantWBC: Whole-Body Compliance for Heavy Humanoids via Force Latent Estimation and Residual Impedance Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33310)
*Tan-Dzung Do,Cuc T.Trinh,Tuan Dat Phuong,Chien Le,Thanh Ly,Vien Anh Ngo,An Thai Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CompliantWBC 是一个面向重型人形机器人的全身柔顺控制框架，可在任意部位外力扰动下实现含下肢参与的柔顺响应，并通过仿真与真实机器人验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型人形机器人在人类中心环境中高负载作业时，全身柔顺控制至关重要。现有基于力感知的学习型方案多局限于末端执行器阻力、上肢逐连杆弹簧或末端刚度调制，未充分解决重型平台任意位置扰动及下肢参与柔顺的问题。

Method: 提出 CompliantWBC，包含三部分：(1) 用强化学习训练 base policy，以多部位全身阻抗参考控制器引导，最大化柔顺保真奖励，并将经典笛卡尔阻抗扩展到任意受控连杆；(2) 在有界残差策略中，于冻结 base 上编辑逐连杆阻抗平衡，校正力编码器提供的粗但结构化 wrench 估计，该编码器在梯度屏障后协同训练；(3) 采用 Phong 加权力原点采样器与轴解耦骨盆锚点，通过两个可解释参数实现含下肢的柔顺课程训练。

Result: 仿真中与柔顺和刚性基线对比，最佳柔顺保真度为偏离解析解 2.58cm；在真实重型人形上完成静态/动态力反应、擦板、负重深蹲和协同搬运任务。

Conclusion: CompliantWBC 实现了任意部位全身柔顺控制，弥补了重型平台下肢参与柔顺的空白，并展现出真实重型人形机器人的应用能力。

Abstract: Whole-body compliant control is essential for deploying heavy humanoids under high payload in human-centric environments. Most prior force-aware learning-based pipelines focus on end-effector resistance, per-link upper-body springs, or end-effector stiffness modulation, leaving arbitrary-site perturbations on heavy platforms with lower-body engagement largely unaddressed. We close this gap with CompliantWBC comprising: (1) A base policy trained with RL to maximize compliance-fidelity reward, guided by a multi-site whole-body impedance reference controller, extending classical Cartesian impedance to any controlled link; (2) A bounded residual policy that edits the per-link impedance equilibrium over a frozen base, correcting the coarse but structured wrench estimate supplied by a force encoder co-trained behind a gradient barrier; (3) A Phong-weighted force-origin sampler with an axis-decoupled pelvis anchor induces lower-body-inclusive compliance curriculum training via two interpretable parameters. We evaluate CompliantWBC in simulation against both compliant and stiff baselines, achieving best compliant fidelity of 2.58cm deviation from analytical solutions, and demonstrate it on a real heavy humanoid across static/dynamic force reaction, board wiping, squat under payload, and cooperative payload transport. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [171] [AquaWAM: A Dynamics-aware World Action Model for Underwater Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33299)
*Cunhao Zhu,Yifeng Wang,Dongliang Xu,Yunzhong Hou,Yue Yao,Chi Harold Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AquaWAM，首个面向水下具身智能体的世界动作模型，通过建模动作条件与被动物理动力学，而非预测未来图像，实现高效、鲁棒的水下动作决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人受惯性、浮力、水阻和持续漂移等被动动力学影响，动作结束后运动仍会持续；现有世界动作模型主要预测动作条件下的视觉观测，未显式建模此类被动运动，因此不适合水下场景。

Method: AquaWAM 同时建模动作条件动力学和被动物理动力学，包括推进器死区、超出指令持续时间的惯性滑行以及环境水流。感知上使用 DVL、IMU、压力传感器和关节编码器，相机仅提供目标与位姿理解所需的语义信息。模型基于紧凑导航状态而非高维视觉观测，显著降低模型规模与计算成本。

Result: 在 USIM 基准的 20 个水下任务上达到 72.6% 任务成功率，优于现有方法；在 NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin 上动作决策速度比 U0 快 2.7 倍。在部分传感器缺失时仍有效：无 DVL 速度测量时成功率为 61.6%，而 U0 为 39.4%。

Conclusion: AquaWAM 通过显式建模水下被动动力学并采用紧凑状态表示，为水下具身智能体提供了更高效、更鲁棒的世界动作建模与决策方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are becoming increasingly important and useful for embodied intelligence, as they enable robots to anticipate the consequences of candidate actions before interacting with the physical environment. However, underwater robots are usually subject to passive dynamics, such as inertia, buoyancy, hydrodynamic drag, and persistent drift, which can continue to affect the vehicle even after an action is completed. Existing WAMs, which primarily predict action-conditioned visual observations, are not explicitly designed to capture such passive motion dynamics. In this paper, we present AquaWAM, the first World Action Model designed for underwater embodied agents. Instead of predicting future images, AquaWAM models both action-conditioned and passive physical dynamics, including the thruster dead band, the inertial glide that outlasts each command, and ambient currents. Specifically, it senses through the DVL, IMU, pressure sensor and joint encoders, while cameras supply only semantics for understanding goals and target pose. By modeling compact navigation states rather than high-dimensional visual observations, AquaWAM substantially reduces the model size and computational cost compared with conventional WAMs. Experimentally, AquaWAM achieves a 72.6% task success rate across 20 underwater tasks on the USIM benchmark, outperforming existing methods while making action decisions 2.7x faster than U0 on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Our model also remains effective when some onboard sensor measurements are unavailable. For example, without DVL velocity measurements, our method still achieves a 61.6% success rate, compared with 39.4% for U0.

</details>


### [172] [Q-WAM: 4-Bit Quantization of World Action Models with Action-Subspace Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33269)
*Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Jingwu Luo,Yuhao Lei,Yi Gao,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Zhenman Fang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q-WAM 是一种面向世界动作模型（WAM）的 4-bit 权重-激活后训练量化方法，通过保护对动作最敏感的子空间与专家，在显著降低内存的同时保持动作生成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 通过迭代扩散联合生成视频与机器人动作，在操作任务中表现强，但计算与显存开销过大，部署困难；现有 PTQ 方法如平滑与旋转不足以维持动作生成精度。

Method: 提出 Q-WAM。定义 Action Observability Gramian（AOG），度量各层输入通道加权组合的舍入误差经全部去噪步对最终动作的影响；提出 Action-Subspace Protection（ASP），将少量最动作敏感的通道组合保留在 16-bit 低秩分支中，其余权重和激活量化为 4-bit，均以 GPU 高效稠密矩阵乘法执行；并通过聚合各专家层 AOG 导出的动作质量，仅对关键专家应用 ASP，以最小开销保持动作质量。

Result: 在三个 WAM 上于仿真和真实部署中评估。RoboTwin 2.0 上平均成功率达 89.6–93.0%，距 16-bit 模型 1.1 个百分点以内，量化块内存降低 3.1–3.4 倍；比最强基线 SVDQuant 高 2.5–8.7 个百分点。在 Unitree G1 人形与双臂 UR3 机器人上，成功率比 SVDQuant 高 12.8–17.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: Q-WAM 能有效实现 WAM 的 4-bit 权重-激活量化，在显著降低内存与计算成本的同时保持动作生成质量，并优于现有 PTQ 基线，适用于真实机器人部署。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate video and robot actions through iterative diffusion and perform strongly in robotic manipulation. However, their prohibitive compute and memory costs pose substantial deployment challenges. Post-training quantization (PTQ) can reduce these costs, but existing PTQ methods such as smoothing and rotation are insufficient to maintain the precision of action generation. To overcome this limitation, we propose Q-WAM, a new 4-bit weight-activation quantization for WAMs that preserves the actions the model generates. Specifically, we introduce the \textit{Action Observability Gramian (AOG)}, which measures how much rounding errors in each weighted combination of a layer's input channels change the final action through all denoising steps. We also develop Action-Subspace Protection (ASP), which keeps the few most action-sensitive channel combinations in a tiny 16-bit low-rank branch and quantizes the complementary weights and activations to 4 bits, both as dense matrix multiplications that run efficiently on GPUs. Finally, to preserve action quality with minimal overhead, we identify the experts that matter most for the generated action by aggregating the AOG-derived action mass across the layers of each expert and apply ASP only to those experts. We evaluate Q-WAM on three WAMs, both in simulation and in real-world deployment. On the RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, it reaches 89.6--93.0\% average success rate, within 1.1 percentage points of the 16-bit models, while reducing the memory of the quantized blocks by 3.1--3.4$\times$. Our method outperforms the strongest baseline, SVDQuant, by 2.5--8.7 percentage points. On a Unitree G1 humanoid and a bimanual UR3 robot, it improves success over SVDQuant by 12.8-17.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [173] [PORTER: Edge-Cloud Residency for Persistent 3D Scene Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33258)
*Yue Chang,Yifan Tian,Jiajing Peng,Dazhi Huang,Rufeng Chen,Zhaofan Zhang,Li Chen,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PORTER：将3D场景图的持久化与驻留解耦，轻量锚点常驻边缘，重型对象负载在边缘与云间迁移；通过不可替代支持擦除（ISE）和预算感知卸载，在JITOMA-Bench上实现91%负载字节卸载时相对mR@3仍为100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务驱动与即时（just-in-time）3D场景图方法仅构建或激活任务相关信息，虽降低单任务表示，却无法限制机器人生命周期内的边缘内存增长。任务切换后，旧任务负载可能当前无关但未来可复用：全部保留会导致本地内存膨胀，直接丢弃则未来需重复构建相同负载。此外，仅以相关性决定驻留不够，因为多个相关负载可能提供冗余信息。

Method: PORTER解耦持久化与驻留：轻量锚点留在边缘机器人有限内存中，重型对象负载在边缘与云端之间迁移。将每个任务分解为功能需求，并定义不可替代支持擦除（ISE），度量卸载造成的需求覆盖损失；ISE对可替代支持进行折扣，并在某需求剩余覆盖较弱时更强惩罚损失。PORTER按最小边际ISE每字节反复卸载负载，构建预算感知的本地工作集。

Result: 在JITOMA-Bench上跨四种3DSG构建器评估PORTER。在渐进压缩下，负载字节卸载率达到91%时，汇总相对mR@3仍保持100%。

Conclusion: PORTER通过基于需求覆盖与不可替代性的驻留决策，可在显著降低边缘内存占用和卸载重型负载的同时维持任务性能，支持长期任务切换与扩展环境下的3DSG边缘部署。

Abstract: Recent task-driven and just-in-time 3D Scene Graph (3DSG) methods reduce per-task representations by constructing or activating only task-relevant information. Yet sparse per-task working sets do not bound onboard memory usage over a robot's lifetime: as tasks change, payloads accumulated for earlier tasks may become irrelevant to the current task but can be useful again in future tasks. Over repeated task switches and expanding environments, retaining such reusable payloads causes local memory to grow, whereas discarding them entirely can lead to costly repeated construction of the same payloads later. We introduce PORTER, which decouples persistence from residency: lightweight anchors remain in the limited memory of the edge robot while heavy object payloads migrate between the edge and the cloud. Relevance alone is insufficient for deciding residency because multiple relevant payloads may provide redundant information. We therefore decompose each task into functional requirements and introduce Irreplaceable Support Erasure (ISE), which measures the loss in requirement coverage caused by offloading. ISE discounts replaceable support and penalizes losses more strongly when the remaining coverage of a requirement is weak. PORTER constructs a budget-aware local working set by repeatedly offloading the payload with the smallest marginal ISE per byte. Experiments on JITOMA-Bench evaluate PORTER across four 3DSG builders. Under progressive compression, pooled relative mR@3 remains at 100% through 91% payload-byte offloading.

</details>


### [174] [ActionGround: Training-Free Runtime Refinement of Frozen VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33256)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Shriram Damodaran,Addison Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActionGround 是一种无需训练、包装冻结 VLA 策略的神经符号运行时层，通过符号化阶段状态机与惯性加权欧拉-拉格朗日动力学项，在极低开销下提升机器人操作的成功率、稳定性与轨迹效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vision-Language-Action 模型直接将视觉观测和语言指令映射为机器人动作，但未显式建模操作任务的阶段结构，也未利用执行过程中的刚体动力学，因此可能在抓取、搬运、放置等阶段缺乏结构化校正与物理一致性。

Method: ActionGround 作为训练无关的运行时层包裹冻结 VLA 策略，无需重训练、微调或访问权重，每控制步开销小于 1 ms。其符号化阶段感知有限状态机识别 approach、grasp、transport、place 等操作阶段，并施加阶段特定规则校正；同时始终启用的惯性加权 Euler-Lagrange 项将机器人运动方程融入控制步，动力学残差仅作为一致性诊断记录而非门控信号。

Result: 在 7-DoF Franka Panda 上，针对 OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、Force-VLA 和 Generalist-VLA，在十个 LIBERO-Spatial 拾放任务中使用固定参数评估：成功率最高提升 6 个百分点，稳定性最高提升 19.3 个百分点，轨迹效率最高提升 15%。Robosuite 噪声扫描中，注入动作噪声下轨迹抖动鲁棒性约提升 10 倍；与真实 Agilex Piper 试验匹配的 Robosuite 仿真中，基线成功率从 35% 提升至 95%。

Conclusion: ActionGround 在刚性物体拾放操作中有效增强冻结 VLA 策略，但评估范围限于刚性物体拾放；真实硬件实验仅作为定性部署演示，真实机械臂的逐次试验定量成功率评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but they do not explicitly represent the phase structure of manipulation tasks or the rigid-body dynamics governing execution. We present ActionGround, a neuro-symbolic, training-free runtime layer that wraps a frozen VLA policy without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, adding less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. A symbolic phase-aware finite-state machine identifies the manipulation phase (approach, grasp, transport, or place) and applies a phase-specific rule-based correction. In parallel, an always-on, inertia-weighted Euler-Lagrange term incorporates the robot's equations of motion into each control step, while its dynamics residual is logged as a consistency diagnostic rather than used as a gate. We evaluate ActionGround across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on ten LIBERO-Spatial pick-and-place tasks using a 7-DoF Franka Panda. With fixed parameters across tasks and backbones, ActionGround improves success rate by up to 6 percentage points and stability by up to 19.3 percentage points, while improving trajectory efficiency by up to 15%. In a separate Robosuite noise sweep, ActionGround provides approximately a 10x improvement in trajectory-jerk robustness under injected action noise. In a matched-seed Robosuite simulation companion to a real Agilex Piper trial, simulated baseline success increases from 35% to 95%. The physical-hardware experiment is presented as a qualitative deployment demonstration; quantitative per-trial success on the real arm is left for future work. Our evaluation is limited to rigid-object pick-and-place manipulation.

</details>


### [175] [SurgFlow: 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow for Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33237)
*Changwei Chen,Xiao Liang,Yinuo Yang,Nicole Shen,Peihan Zhang,Sara Wickenhiser,Zekai Liang,Soofiyan Atar,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgFlow 从立体手术视频中无动作标签地学习 3D 物体中心接触流，预测未来轨迹与接触分数，用于触发抓取/释放并优化末端执行器运动，在 dVRK 多任务上成功率高，并可零样本迁移至人形腹腔镜机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对视频-动作演示数据稀缺：机器人仅完成约 1% 手术，而纯视频数据丰富。3D 物体流虽与本体无关，但只指定物体如何运动，不指定工具应在何处、何时接触，这一区分在手术中至关重要。

Method: 提出 SurgFlow 框架，从立体手术视频学习 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow，无需动作标签。对每个物体点预测未来 3D 轨迹和接触分数；通过 3D 跟踪与工具-物体邻近度提取目标；训练流匹配生成器进行预测；利用预测接触触发抓取和释放，并基于流优化末端执行器运动。

Result: 在 da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) 上，SurgFlow 在组织牵拉、双臂显露、拾针和交接任务中，39 次阶段评估成功 37 次，优于使用同等数据且带或不带动作标签的基线。零样本迁移至人形腹腔镜机器人，在相似和新颖相机视角下平均成功率分别为 85% 和 70%。

Conclusion: SurgFlow 表明可利用丰富的纯视频数据学习面向手术的接触流表示，实现无需动作标签的自主手术行为，并具备跨平台、跨视角的有效迁移能力。

Abstract: Paired video-action demonstrations enable autonomous surgical behavior, but such data is scarce: robots perform roughly 1% of surgeries, while video-only data is abundant. Learning 3D object flow offers an embodiment-agnostic way to utilize video data, but flow alone specifies how an object should move, not where and when the tool should engage it, a distinction that is critical in surgery. We introduce SurgFlow, a framework that learns 3D Object-Centric Contact Flow from stereo surgical video without action labels. For each object point, it predicts a future 3D trajectory and contact scores. We extract targets via 3D tracking and tool-object proximity, train a flow matching generator to predict them, and use predicted contact to trigger grasp and release while optimizing end effector motion from flow. On the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK), SurgFlow succeeds in 37 of 39 stage evaluations across tissue retraction, bimanual reveal, needle pickup, and handover, outperforming baselines trained on equal data with or without action labels. Zero-shot transfer to a humanoid-based laparoscopic robot achieves 85% and 70% average success under similar and novel camera viewpoints, respectively.

</details>


### [176] [Learning with Object-centric Representations of Tactile Interactive Perception for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33235)
*Xinyi Yang,Zilin Si,Zhuowei Xu,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以物体为中心、情境感知的机器人操作框架，通过触觉探索学习任务无关的物体表示，并将其作为语义上下文提升视觉歧义物体上的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料、容器内容物、软硬度等隐式物体属性难以仅从视觉推断，但可由触觉感知与探索性交互揭示；然而触觉信号瞬态且稀疏，如何提取有效触觉事件并融入机器人操作仍是挑战。

Method: 框架通过长时程触觉探索学习任务无关物体表示：token learner自主选择代表性触觉片段，并与描述性文本嵌入进行对比对齐，形成能捕获多种物理属性的潜在空间；随后将这些表示作为语义上下文，引导以物体为中心的操作策略，并依据物体属性自适应调整操作策略。

Result: 学习到的表示在已见和未见物体上的属性估计准确率分别达93%和84%；在多物体重排、倒液和开箱三个涉及视觉歧义物体的任务中，相比无物体上下文条件的基线策略，已见物体任务成功率从41%提升至92%，未见物体从19%提升至67%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，触觉探索学习到的物体表示可有效作为上下文，改善目标选择与依赖物体属性的操作适应，并显著提升视觉歧义场景下的机器人操作成功率。

Abstract: Implicit object properties that are difficult to directly infer from vision, such as material, container contents, or softness, can be revealed through tactile sensing and exploratory interactions. However, because tactile signals are transient and sparse, extracting informative tactile events and effectively incorporating them into robotic manipulation remains a challenge. In this work, we present an object-centric context-aware manipulation framework that learns task-agnostic object representations through tactile exploration. A token learner autonomously selects representative tactile segments from long-horizon exploration, while contrastive alignment with descriptive text embeddings enables a latent space that captures multiple physical object properties. These learned representations are then used as semantic context to guide object-centric manipulation policies and adapt strategies based on object properties. Experiments show that the learned representations achieve 93% and 84% property estimation accuracy on seen and unseen objects. Evaluated on three tasks involving visually ambiguous objects, i.e. multi-object rearrangement, pouring, and box opening, the proposed framework improves both target selection and property-dependent manipulation adaptation, raising task success, aggregated over all evaluation trials, from 41% to 92% on seen objects and from 19% to 67% on unseen objects over baseline policies without object-context conditioning. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [177] [TAO-DA: Towards Autonomous Operation--A Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Coordinated Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33197)
*Yongsheng Zhao,Han Gao,Baoping Cheng,Jingyao Tang,Dian Zhou,Deng Liang,Ji Ge,Xuanzhang Wen,Lei Zhao,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对称双臂专家（DAE）架构，通过共享VLM主干与解耦的臂特定专家塔及两阶段意图路由，解决VLA模型中的跨臂干扰问题，并引入任务进度预测模块支持双臂协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏显式机制解耦双臂状态与意图，导致非预期的跨臂干扰，降低任务执行成功率。

Method: 在共享VLM主干上构建对称双臂专家（DAE）架构，各臂使用解耦的专家塔；采用两阶段双臂意图路由：第一阶段基于显式语言指令选择专家，第二阶段基于隐式视觉语义路由。另引入轻量任务进度预测模块，利用预动作块时序特征与本体/视觉观测语义表示的交叉注意力，估计逐帧任务完成进度，实现任务进度同步以支持多机器人协作调度。

Result: 实验表明，该模型在双臂意图路由和跨臂干扰解耦方面有效，并初步验证了单臂与双臂任务间的涌现技能泛化（双向）以及跨臂运动域技能迁移。

Conclusion: DAE能有效缓解跨臂干扰，提升双臂VLA任务执行与协调能力，并展现出技能泛化与迁移潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework for grounding high-level semantic information into low-level robot actions, enabling scalable robotic manipulation across diverse tasks. However, existing VLA models lack explicit mechanisms to disentangle the states and intents of the two arms, leading to unintended cross-arm interference that degrades task execution success. To address this issue, we propose a symmetric Dual-Arm Expert (DAE) architecture built upon a shared Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone with decoupled, arm-specific expert towers. Expert selection is carried out through a two-stage dual-arm intent routing scheme, in which experts are routed either by explicit language instructions in the first stage or by implicit visual semantics in the second stage. Moreover, we introduce a lightweight task progress prediction module that leverages cross-attention between the pre-chunk temporal features and semantic representations of proprioceptive and visual observations to accurately estimate frame-wise task completion progress. This module facilitates task progress synchronization to support coordinated scheduling for collaborative multi-robot tasks. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our model in dual-arm intent routing and the disentanglement of cross-arm interference, and further provide preliminary evidence of emergent skill generalization from single- to dual-arm tasks (as well as the reverse), together with cross-arm motion-domain skill transfer.

</details>


### [178] [DeltaWAM: Change-Centric Visual Foresight via Delta Tokens for an Efficient World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33177)
*Tianyun Jiang,Wenrui Bao,Bingxin Xu,Yu Tian,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeltaWAM提出以变化为中心的WAM，用紧凑delta token预测未来帧间变化，在LIBERO上达92.8%成功率，推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）在像素空间重建完整未来场景，计算成本高且存在时空冗余；而连续帧大部分视觉上下文相同，动作策略真正需要预测的是帧间变化。

Method: DeltaWAM以单个向量（delta token）编码连续DINO特征图之间的变化作为预测单元，基于大规模视频预训练的潜在世界模型DeltaWorld，自回归预测每帧一个delta token；流匹配动作专家以预测的过渡和当前DINO特征为空间锚点生成动作块。

Result: 模型0.725B参数，在2张H100上训练256 GPU小时，LIBERO平均成功率92.8%，在LIBERO-Pro程序化扰动下泛化稳健；推理每动作块142.1 ms，峰值显存3.86 GB。

Conclusion: DeltaWAM通过预测帧间变化而非重建完整场景，实现了高效、高成功率且泛化良好的机器人操作世界-动作模型。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) offer visual foresight for robotic manipulation, but pixel-space models repeatedly reconstruct entire future scenes, incurring high computational cost and spatio-temporal redundancy. In physical manipulation, consecutive frames often share most of their visual context; the changes between them are what an action policy needs to anticipate. We introduce DeltaWAM, a change-centric WAM that makes a compact delta token the unit of future prediction. Each token is a single vector encoding changes between consecutive dense DINO feature maps. DeltaWAM builds on DeltaWorld, a latent world model pretrained on large-scale videos, to autoregressively predict one delta token per future frame. A flow-matching action expert then conditions on the predicted transitions and current DINO features, which serve as spatial anchors, to generate action chunks. Trained for 256 GPU hours on two H100 GPUs, DeltaWAM has 0.725B parameters and achieves 92.8% average success on LIBERO. It also shows robust generalization under procedural perturbations on LIBERO-Pro. Inference takes 142.1 ms per action chunk with 3.86 GB peak memory.

</details>


### [179] [Multi-Terrain Mastery: A Comprehensive Controller for Bipedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33174)
*Oluwami Dosunmu-Ogunbi,Aayushi Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于双足机器人的多地形控制器，通过引入新的冲击映射，在20自由度Cassie机器人上实现对斜坡、沙石、楼梯等多种地形的稳健行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动控制器仅在特定表面表现良好，难以适应真实世界多变的非结构化地形；单一通用控制器可避免为每种地形设计专用控制器，从而提升实用性与鲁棒性。

Method: 在前期利用支撑踝电机提升斜面与粗糙地形稳定性的工作基础上，将能力扩展至陡峭湿滑不平草地、沙地、砾石、岩石等柔顺地形以及楼梯等受限地形；提出新的冲击映射以应对未知地形带来的挑战；并详细讨论用于机器人实时部署的控制结构。

Result: 在20自由度Cassie双足机器人上验证了该控制器，表明其在多种地形下能够保持性能与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该多地形控制器提升了双足运动的通用性和鲁棒性，展示了单一控制器应对多样真实地形的潜力。

Abstract: Advancing bipedal robots to navigate diverse terrains remains a significant challenge in robotics. Traditional locomotion controllers excel on specific surfaces but struggle across varied environments, limiting their practical applications. Given the unpredictable nature of real-world environments, a single controller capable of handling multiple terrains is ideal, eliminating the need for multiple specialized controllers. We propose a multi-terrain controller to enhance the versatility and robustness of bipedal locomotion. Building on previous work with a stance ankle motor for stability on inclined and rough surfaces, this paper extends capabilities to steep wet uneven grassy slopes, and compliant terrains such as sand, gravel, rocks, and constrained terrains like staircases. To address the unique demands of these terrains, we introduce a new impact map that is essential for maintaining performance and robustness against unseen terrains. We also discuss in detail the control structure for real-time deployment on the robot. We validate our controller on the 20 degree-of-freedom Cassie bipedal robot.

</details>


### [180] [Dynamic Manipulation with World-Action Models via Counterfactual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33172)
*Sunwoo Park,Wonbin Lee,Seonghyun Jin,Youngmin Kim,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出动态预测规划（DPP）框架，将动态操作建模为反事实规划，通过解耦“计划生成所用上下文”与“执行所用物理状态”，使基于静态演示训练的世界-动作模型（WAM）无需额外动态数据训练即可实时操作移动目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于静态演示训练的WAM即便具备所需操作技能，也往往无法操作移动目标。作者将失败归因于“目标响应坍缩”（target-response collapse）：随着执行推进，策略越来越偏向延续已学习到的既定行为，对目标位置变化的响应性下降。核心动机是弥合模型已学能力与当前上下文可生成行为之间的差距。

Method: DPP将动态操作形式化为反事实规划。首先利用WAM的预测性rollout估计交互预计发生的时机，并结合观测到的目标运动预测目标未来的交互位置；随后构造一个反事实观测，将该预测目标位置置于熟悉的机器人上下文中，使模型能够调用已有操作技能，而非在不熟悉的机器人-目标构型下生成恢复行为；最后在执行阶段将所得计划与机器人真实状态连接。整个过程无需在动态数据上额外训练。

Result: DPP可在单张消费级GPU上实现实时动态操作。仿真与真机实验表明，面对多种目标运动均取得一致提升；在仿真中性能超越所有评估基线，包括额外使用动态数据训练的方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦用于计划生成的上下文与用于执行的物理状态，并以反事实观测复用既有技能，DPP有效缓解了目标响应坍缩，实现了无需动态数据微调的实时动态操作。

Abstract: World-Action models (WAMs) trained on static demonstrations often fail to manipulate moving targets even when they possess the required manipulation skills. We attribute this failure to target-response collapse: as execution advances, the policy becomes increasingly biased toward the learned continuation of its ongoing behavior and less responsive to target relocation. To bridge the gap between what the model has learned and what it can generate from the current context, we formulate dynamic manipulation as counterfactual planning by decoupling the context used for plan generation from the physical state used for execution. Our framework, Dynamic Predictive Planning (DPP), first uses the WAM's predictive rollout to estimate when an interaction is expected to occur, and combines this timing estimate with observed target motion to predict the target's future interaction position. DPP then constructs a counterfactual observation that places this predicted target position in a familiar robot context, allowing the model to invoke an existing manipulation skill rather than generate a recovery behavior from an unfamiliar robot-target configuration. The resulting plan is connected to the robot's actual state during execution. DPP enables real-time dynamic manipulation on a single consumer GPU without additional training on dynamic data. Experiments in simulation and on a real robot demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse target motions, with simulation performance surpassing all evaluated baselines, including methods additionally trained on dynamic data. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Tasks: A Vision for Reproducing an Animal-like Behavioral Substrate Using Modern Robot Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33165)
*Samiyuru Menik,Hemadri Jayalath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出以“动物行为学行为基底”为概念视角，研究具身智能体在物理、环境和社会需求持续变化下保持情境适应、响应性与行为连贯性的能力，并建议以机器人动物伴侣作为长期交互与适应的研究场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习已产生能力日益增强的具身智能体，但较少关注动物持续展现的一种更基础能力：在动态变化中保持行为连贯、响应和适应。现有研究多将动物行为视为孤立技能，而忽视竞争需求下的持续协调。

Method: 这是一篇观点/展望性论文，提出“行为学行为基底”概念框架，主张将竞争需求下的持续行为协调视为机器人学习的重要且尚待探索的目标，并提出机器人动物伴侣作为研究设置，以考察社会行为、记忆和持续学习在长期交互中的发展。

Result: 无实验或定量结果；论文论证该方向尚未被充分探索，并指出机器人动物伴侣可为研究长期人机交互、适应与持续学习提供机会。

Conclusion: 持续的行为胜任力可补充具身智能体的高层能力，因此值得进一步研究其与高级能力的结合。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have produced increasingly capable embodied agents. Yet comparatively less attention has been given to a more basic form of competence that animals exhibit continuously: the ability to remain situated, responsive, and behaviorally coherent as physical, environmental, and social demands change over time. We propose the ethological behavioral substrate as a conceptual lens for studying this form of competence in artificial agents. Rather than treating these behaviors that animals exhibit as a set of isolated skills, we argue that their continual coordination under competing demands constitutes an important and underexplored target for modern robot learning. We further propose robotic animal companions as a useful research setting for studying sustained interaction and adaptation in human-centered environments. Such systems provide an opportunity to investigate how social behavior, memory, and continual learning develop over long periods of interaction. This perspective motivates further investigation of how such persistent behavioral competence may complement higher-level capabilities in embodied agents.

</details>


### [182] [TimelyDAgger: Timing-Aware Expert Querying for VLA Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33157)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Peiyan Li,Enhao Zhang,Yueran Tao,Hao Wang,Chenghao Yue,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TimelyDAgger，通过Bridge-PCA监控VLA内部特征并基于专家反馈自适应调整阈值，优化机器人门控DAgger的接管时机，并引入TASR评估监督质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人门控DAgger主要关注是否需要协助，但专家接管时机同样影响示范内容及其对策略学习的价值。

Method: TimelyDAgger结合Bridge-PCA对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型内部特征进行监控，并利用基于专家行为的反馈引导阈值自适应，以改进接管时机；同时提出连接故障检测、接管时机与策略改进的评估框架，包括无需重训练即可评估监督质量的目标对齐监督比（TASR）。

Result: 实验表明接管时机影响策略学习；在匹配专家动作预算下，TimelyDAgger在大多数评估设置中实现有竞争力的故障检测，并获得更高的训练后成功率。

Conclusion: 优化接管时机对机器人门控DAgger至关重要，TimelyDAgger能提升监督质量与策略性能。

Abstract: DAgger improves robot policies by aggregating expert supervision from states visited during policy execution. Robot-gated DAgger automates expert queries, allowing the robot to decide when to request expert takeover. While existing gates emphasize detecting the need for assistance, takeover timing also shapes the content of these demonstrations and their value for policy learning. We propose TimelyDAgger, combining Bridge-PCA monitoring of internal vision-language-action (VLA) features with Feedback-guided Threshold Adaptation based on expert behavior to improve takeover timing. We introduce an evaluation framework linking failure detection, takeover timing, and policy improvement, including Target-Aligned Supervision Ratio (TASR) for assessing supervision quality without retraining. Experiments show that takeover timing affects policy learning, with TimelyDAgger achieving competitive failure detection and higher post-training success in most evaluated settings under matched expert-action budgets.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond State-as-Action: Exploiting Command-State Discrepancy for Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33145)
*Peiyan Li,Yueran Tao,Enhao Zhang,Zhixuan Zhao,Chenghao Yue,Hao Wang,Lei Lv,Wentao Zhao,Jiahao Chen,Xin Liu,Kangyao Huang,Yu Luo,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究交互约束下命令-状态差异对模仿学习的价值，提出CSDW加权命令监督，在受限任务上优于均匀监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习常从实测机器人运动构造动作目标，但在交互约束下，命令与状态差异可能反映运动本身未捕捉的控制需求；需探究该信息何时重要以及如何利用。

Method: 在三个真实机器人任务上进行任务与阶段分析，发现受限交互下监督缺口更大，选择性保留命令具有局部价值；据此提出CSDW，考虑机器人响应时间，将后续进展、持续未满足需求和需求变化组合为连续权重，用于命令监督，且无需任务阶段标注或改动策略架构与推理。

Result: CSDW在受限任务上优于均匀命令监督，而在约束较少的任务上与基线方法表现相近。

Conclusion: 命令-状态差异信息在交互约束下可有效利用，CSDW提供无需额外标注和架构修改的加权监督方案，提升受限任务中的模仿学习性能。

Abstract: Constructing action targets from measured robot motion is an established approach in imitation learning. Under interaction constraints, however, command-state discrepancy may reflect control demands that motion alone does not capture. We investigate when this information matters and how to exploit it. Across three real-robot tasks, task and phase analyses reveal larger supervision gaps under constrained interaction, while selective command retention provides evidence of locally useful command information. Building on these findings, we propose Command-State Discrepancy Weighting (CSDW), which accounts for robot response times and combines subsequent progress, persistent unmet demand, and demand changes into continuous weights for command supervision. The method requires no task-phase annotations or changes to policy architecture or inference. CSDW improves over uniform command supervision on constrained tasks, while methods perform similarly in the less constrained task.

</details>


### [184] [Multi-Modal Non-Prehensile Estimation of Physical Parameters via Press-and-Pull Tipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33138)
*Steven M. Hyland,Jing Xiao,Cagdas D. Onal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态非抓取交互估计框架，结合滑动与按压-拉动倾倒，无先验几何模型地估计物体质量、质心高度和表面摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取交互中单一操作原语无法揭示全部物理参数：平面推动使质量与摩擦耦合，传统倾倒无法恢复摩擦，且对低摩擦或曲面底座物体可能因滑动/旋转而失效。

Method: 设计结合滑动交互与按压-拉动倾倒原语的多模态框架；按压-拉动提高物体-桌面滑动阈值并稳定支点，实现可控倾倒；融合腕部力/力矩传感、RGB-D感知和机器人本体感知，从两种互补交互模式中估计质量、质心高度和表面摩擦。

Result: 在ABB IRB120上对四个几何、质量、质心和摩擦各异的物体进行实验，取得较低相对误差，并成功处理传统前向倾倒会失败的曲面底座物体。

Conclusion: 互补的非抓取交互无需抓取、先验物体模型或学习交互动力学，即可恢复一组紧凑的物理参数。

Abstract: Recovering physical properties of unknown objects through non-prehensile interaction is challenging because no single manipulation primitive reveals all relevant parameters. Planar pushing couples mass and friction, while conventional tipping cannot recover friction and may fail entirely when low-friction or curved-base objects slide or rotate instead of tipping. We introduce a multi-modal estimation framework that combines a sliding interaction with a press-and-pull tipping primitive to recover object mass, center of mass height, and surface friction. The press-and-pull interaction increases the object-table sliding threshold and stabilizes the pivot, enabling controlled tipping without any prior geometric object model. Wrist force/torque sensing, RGB-D perception, and robot proprioception are fused to estimate the physical parameters from the two complementary interaction modes. Experiments on an ABB IRB120 across four objects with varied geometry, mass, center of mass, and friction achieve low relative error, while successfully operating on curved-base objects that fail under conventional forward tipping. The results demonstrate that complementary non-prehensile interactions can recover a compact set of physical parameters without grasping, a prior object model, or learned interaction dynamics.

</details>


### [185] [VPTwin: Real-Sim-Real Video Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33104)
*Zhenghao Xiao,Minting Pan,Nantian He,Dongzhan Zhou,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VPTwin，一种 Real-Sim-Real 视频预测框架，利用与真实世界同步的仿真孪生作为上下文参考，提升动作条件视频预测的物理合理性与长时程机器人操作规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的动作条件视频预测在长时程展开中易出现复合误差和物理上不合理的幻想，严重损害下游动作规划；因此需要借助仿真先验增强真实未来预测的物理一致性。

Method: 针对目标操作任务，用 VLM 从真实演示片段重建可执行数字孪生；在 Isaac Sim 中针对候选动作轨迹，在随机物理配置下进行多次前向动力学展开；将这些展开作为上下文参考，融合仿真动力学以约束物理合理性，同时从真实视频中捕捉未建模接触交互；并构建基于 VPTwin 的预测规划闭环，对 VLM 提议动作进行视觉验证并指导真实执行。

Result: 评估显示，视频预测中的物理幻想显著减少，操作规划性能明显提升。

Conclusion: VPTwin 通过 Real-Sim-Real 框架将仿真动力学与真实视频信息结合，为机器人操作提供更物理可信的视频预测和更可靠的规划闭环。

Abstract: While action-conditioned video prediction provides an intuitive world model for robotics, purely data-driven predictors often suffer from compounding errors and physically implausible hallucinations in long-horizon rollouts, severely undermining downstream action planning. We propose VPTwin, a Real-Sim-Real video prediction framework that anchors real-world future prediction using real-synchronized simulation twins. For a target manipulation task, a VLM reconstructs an executable digital twin from a real demonstration episode. To accommodate the ill-posed estimation of unobserved physical properties, Isaac Sim simulates multiple forward dynamic rollouts across randomized physical configurations under candidate action trajectories. Using these rollouts as in-context references, VPTwin harmonizes both domains, using simulation dynamics to enforce physical plausibility while capturing unmodeled contact interactions from real video. Furthermore, we establish a predictive planning loop using VPTwin to visually verify VLM-proposed actions and guide reliable real-world execution. Evaluations show substantial reductions in physical hallucinations during video prediction and marked improvements in manipulation planning performance.

</details>


### [186] [Evolving Dexterous Robots from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33101)
*Zihan Guo,Shuzhe Zhang,Muhan Li,Peiyang Li,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过进化算法自动设计自由形态机器人，使其能抓取、保持、旋转和使用多种物体，并将优胜设计制造并在真实世界中零样本测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计灵巧操作智能体非常困难；动物操作物体的结构和行为难以被仿生复制，且未必适合人工机器。因此作者希望不预设身体部件、关节或几何形状，直接进化出自由形态的操作器。

Method: 使用对比学习构建可高效搜索的设计空间遗传嵌入，用自回归发育模型解码设计，用进化策略寻找优秀设计，并用强化学习训练每个进化出的设计。获胜设计自动转换为可制造蓝图，打印、组装后零样本在真实世界测试。

Result: 某些条件下可自发出现尾巴、喙、爪等熟悉抓取形式；从头设计也能发现被忽视或未知的新结构。结果在性能、多样性和复杂性上达到进化机器人领域的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 不依赖仿生预设的从头进化设计，可发现更适合任务且可制造、可现实部署的灵巧操作器，为进化机器人设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Little is known about how to manually design agents capable of dexterous manipulation. Some design principles have been inferred from close examination of how animals manipulate objects, but these structures and behaviors have so far resisted biomimicry and may not be optimal for artificial machines. Here we evolve freeform robots to pick up, hold, rotate, and use diverse objects. Unlike other approaches to optimizing robot hands, we do not presuppose the presence, articulation, or geometry of any part of the body. Although familiar prehensile forms such as tails, beaks, paws and claws may emerge spontaneously under certain conditions--and while such conditions could be of interest to evolutionary biologists--de novo manipulator design can also reveal whole new solutions, overlooked or unknown structures which may be better suited for the task at hand. We use contrastive learning to create a highly searchable genetic embedding of design space, an autoregressive developmental model to decode designs, evolutionary strategies to find good designs, and reinforcement learning to train each evolved design. Winning designs were automatically converted into a manufacturable blueprint, printed, assembled and tested in the real world in a zero-shot manner. The results represent the state-of-the-art in evolutionary robotics in terms of performance, diversity and complexity.

</details>


### [187] [Humanoids for Robot-Assisted Surgery: Bimanual Base Placement and Tool-Mount Optimization via Capability Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33096)
*Peihan Zhang,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Nikita Thareja,Ryan Broderick,Shanglei Liu,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过基于能力地图的机器人配置优化框架，定量评估人形机器人用于机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的工作空间可达性，发现其在部分术式中接近90%，但复杂术式覆盖仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在医疗与临床任务中的应用受到关注，但尚不清楚现有人形机器人能否满足机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的运动学需求，尤其是最优机器人定位与配置问题。

Method: 提出基于能力地图的机器人配置框架，联合优化人形机器人基座位置与器械安装方向，以最大化双臂可达性，并考虑器械末端运动学与远程运动中心（RCM）约束；在三种不同体型与运动冗余度的人形平台上，针对胆囊切除术、腹股沟疝修补术和袖状胃切除术评估工作空间可达性。

Result: 基座位置与器械安装的联合优化持续优于仅优化基座和启发式基线。对于工作空间较小且重叠较少的胆囊切除术和腹股沟疝修补术，人形机器人可达性接近90%；对于工作空间更大且多孔臂重叠的袖状胃切除术，覆盖率显著降低。

Conclusion: 结果表明人形机器人在部分腹腔镜手术中具有近期应用潜力，同时明确了更广泛部署前需解决的关键运动学与工作空间限制。

Abstract: Rapid advances in humanoid robotics have motivated growing interest in the application of humanoids for healthcare and clinical tasks. However, it remains unclear how close contemporary humanoids are to meeting the kinematic demands of robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery. In this work, we address the question of optimal robot positioning through a quantitative analysis of workspace and robot setup configurations. We present a capability-map-based robot setup framework that optimizes humanoid base placement and tool mounting orientation to maximize bimanual humanoid reachability while accounting for tool-tip kinematics and remote-center-of-motion (RCM) constraints. We evaluate three humanoid platforms spanning different body dimensions and kinematic redundancy on workspace reachability for three representative general surgery procedures: cholecystectomy, inguinal hernia repair, and sleeve gastrectomy. The proposed joint optimization of base placement and tool mounting consistently outperforms base-only optimization and heuristic baselines. For cholecystectomy and inguinal hernia repair, which are characterized by relatively small and minimally overlapping workspaces, humanoid reachability approached 90%. For the larger, overlapping multi-port arm workspace of sleeve gastrectomy, humanoids yield substantially lower coverage. These results quantify the near-term promise of humanoids for selected laparoscopic procedures and clarify key limitations that must be addressed for broader deployment.

</details>


### [188] [REALM: A Coarse-to-Fine Generative Framework for Embodied Reactive Listening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33095)
*Peizhen Li,Longbing Cao,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 REALM 粗到细音频驱动反应式聆听面部动作生成框架，结合历史感知时序对齐与随机表情细化，在 ViCo/L2L 上提升，并部署到 Ameca 人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成听者面部反应动作需应对两个核心建模挑战：一是考虑说话者线索时序，同时保持与听者已有动作的连续性；二是同时捕捉整体运动轨迹与局部可变的面部事件。听者反应可能相对先前线索存在时间滞后，短暂表情和眨眼也难以确定性预测。因此需要结合历史感知时序对齐与随机表情细化。

Method: 提出 REALM（Reactive Embodied Audio-driven Listening Model），一种音频驱动反应式聆听的粗到细框架。Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion 模块通过延迟中心注意力先验和自适应门控，融合听者动作历史与说话者音频。粗解码器预测基础运动轨迹，随后在表情子空间中加入音频条件随机残差进行增强，同时保留粗姿态参数。

Result: 在 ViCo 和 L2L 数据集上，多项运动质量指标优于所评估基线。额外分析考察了延迟敏感性、门控行为和眨眼动态。最后，在 Ameca 人形机器人上部署，并通过感知用户研究证明生成行为可应用于物理实体。

Conclusion: REALM 能生成兼具历史感知时序对齐和局部随机表情变化的反应式聆听面部动作，并具有面向实体机器人交互的适用性。

Abstract: Generating responsive listener facial motion is an important task for embodied conversational AI. Two modeling challenges are central: accounting for the timing of speaker cues while maintaining continuity with the listener's ongoing motion, and capturing locally variable facial events alongside the overall motion trajectory. Listener responses may follow preceding cues with a temporal lag, while brief expressions and blinks introduce variation that is difficult to predict deterministically. These challenges motivate a framework that combines history-aware temporal alignment with stochastic expression refinement. We propose REALM (Reactive Embodied Audio-driven Listening Model), a coarse-to-fine framework for audio-driven reactive listening. A Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion module combines listener motion history with speaker audio through a delay-centered attention prior and adaptive gating. A coarse decoder predicts a base motion trajectory, which is augmented by audio-conditioned stochastic residuals in the expression subspace while retaining the coarse pose parameters. Evaluations on ViCo and L2L show improvements over the evaluated baselines across multiple motion-quality metrics. Additional analyses examine delay sensitivity, gate behavior, and blink dynamics. Finally, deployment on an Ameca humanoid robot and a perceptual user study demonstrate the applicability of the generated behavior to physical embodiment. Code: this https URL Demo: this https URL

</details>


### [189] [SwingRL: Adaptive Observation Reinforcement Learning with World-Model Prediction for Cable-Suspended Hoisting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33053)
*Guangming Wang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Yucheng Xin,Wanli Ma,Jiucai Liu,Yunxiang Ma,Joe Ingham,Haibing Wu,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SwingRL，一种用于缆索悬吊机器人精准插入任务的残差强化学习框架，融合时效感知世界模型、经典抗摆先验与循环残差策略，以应对感知延迟/丢失和动力学不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索悬吊广泛用于起重机辅助施工等重载/大件搬运，但柔性缆索使载荷运动欠驱动，外部扰动多变，且视觉观测延迟或丢失会导致控制执行时感知状态过期；在将悬吊载荷插座精准套入钢筋销等常见施工任务中，这些问题尤为关键。

Method: SwingRL包含三部分：年龄感知世界模型利用已执行控制命令，将最新接收的载荷观测传播到当前控制步，提供延迟和丢包感知下的时间对齐状态估计；经典抗摆先验提供名义跟踪与摆动阻尼；循环残差策略仅学习对先验的有界修正，而非完整控制律，以补偿系统参数变化、外部扰动和残余状态估计误差。评估覆盖系统参数变化、风扰、感知退化和强阵风的递进难度阶梯，并与经典及学习基线对比，同时进行世界模型消融和物理平台验证。

Result: 在最困难设置下，SwingRL取得69.5%严格成功率和77.3%宽松成功率，分别至少超过所有基线60个百分点；世界模型消融支持时间对齐状态估计在观测丢失增加时维持插入成功率的作用；无需真机微调，SwingRL在物理平台上达到90%成功率。

Conclusion: SwingRL表明，将时间对齐状态估计、经典抗摆先验与残差强化学习相结合，可有效应对延迟/有损感知和不确定动力学，在仿真困难条件及真实缆索悬吊插入任务中实现鲁棒高成功率。

Abstract: Cable-suspended hoisting is widely used to move heavy or bulky payloads that cannot be handled conveniently by rigid pick-and-place systems, for example in crane-assisted construction. Robotic hoisting using flexible cables is challenging because payload motion is underactuated, external disturbances vary, and delayed or lost visual observations can make the perceived payload state stale at control execution. These effects are particularly critical during precise insertion of a suspended payload's sockets onto rebar pins, which is a very common task in construction environments. We present SwingRL, a residual reinforcement-learning (RL) framework that combines an age-aware world model, a classical anti-swing prior, and a recurrent residual policy to address two coupled problems: stale feedback and uncertain dynamics. The world model propagates the newest received payload observation to the current control step using the executed commands, providing a time-aligned state estimate under delayed and lossy sensing. The prior supplies nominal tracking and swing damping. The residual policy learns bounded corrections to the prior rather than the complete control law, compensating for system-parameter variation, external disturbances, and remaining state-estimation errors. We evaluate SwingRL against classical and learning-based baselines across a cumulative difficulty ladder covering system-parameter variation, wind disturbance, degraded sensing, and strong gusts. Under the most difficult setting, SwingRL achieves 69.5% strict and 77.3% broad success, exceeding all baselines by at least 60 percentage points, respectively. World-model ablations support the role of time-aligned state estimation in maintaining insertion success as observation loss increases. Finally, without real-robot fine-tuning, SwingRL achieves 90% success on the physical rig.

</details>


### [190] [Is Online Interaction Necessary for Recovery? A Minimalist Approach to Robust Planning via Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33049)
*Bumgeun Park,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PARS，一种仅用现有演示的BC训练方法，通过扰动本体状态并仅锚定轨迹后段，提升动作序列策略对协变量偏移的恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在闭环执行中易受协变量偏移影响，微小预测或执行误差会使机器人进入演示数据覆盖不足的状态；现有纠正方法通常需额外收集演示、环境交互或专家/参考策略。

Method: PARS在训练时扰动机器人本体感知状态，并只将预测动作序列的后期部分锚定到原始演示，让早期部分自由生成纠正动作，从而在规划时域内恢复至演示行为；与保留全时域监督目标的输入噪声增强不同，PARS提供轨迹级恢复监督，且可仅小幅修改流、Transformer、扩散等动作序列策略的BC目标。

Result: 在51个RLBench操作任务上，基于流、Transformer和扩散的策略实验表明，PARS提升了闭环执行中对协变量偏移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PARS是一种简单、通用且无需额外环境交互或专家查询的恢复监督方法，可有效增强动作序列BC策略在闭环执行中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) is vulnerable to covariate shift during closed-loop execution, where small prediction or execution errors can drive the robot toward states poorly covered by the demonstration data. We focus on action-sequence planning, where a policy predicts a finite-horizon sequence of actions as a reference trajectory for robot execution. Existing approaches to covariate shift often rely on collecting additional corrective demonstrations, requiring further environment interaction and access to an expert or reference policy. We propose Perturbation-Augmented Recovery Supervision (PARS), a simple training approach for improving recovery using only existing demonstrations. PARS perturbs the robot's proprioceptive state and anchors only the later portion of the predicted trajectory to the original demonstration, leaving the earlier portion free to generate corrective motion and recover toward the demonstrated behavior within the planning horizon. Unlike conventional input-noise augmentation, which preserves the original supervision target over the entire prediction horizon, PARS explicitly provides trajectory-level supervision for recovery from perturbed states. PARS requires neither additional environment interaction nor expert queries and can be instantiated across different action-sequence policy classes with only minor modifications to their BC objectives. Experiments on 51 RLBench manipulation tasks with flow-based, transformer-based, and diffusion-based policies demonstrate that PARS improves robustness to covariate shift during closed-loop execution.

</details>


### [191] [CAPEX: Efficiently Distilling Foundation Model Behavior into Deployable Robot Policies through Experience-Adaptive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33007)
*Shivam Aarya,Zhang Xi-Jia,Chengyue Huang,Junhyun Kim,Huishu Xue,Hrishit Leen,Roman Yakunin,Animesh Garg,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CAPEX——一种以执行经验为条件的演示收集框架，将通用多模态基础模型作为自主演示者，动态调整其观测与重规划频率，从而低成本地生成可训练机器人策略的演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习长期依赖人类遥操作演示，但人工采集过程不直观、难以规模化且本质异步；同时，能力足够的基础模型虽能零样本生成操作轨迹，却因物理执行中反复调用而缓慢昂贵，难以作为可扩展的数据生成器。

Method: CAPEX 利用先前尝试的执行经验，自适应地决定基础模型何时需要观测、推理与重规划，从而减少调用次数；在 RoboCasa 任务以及实体 Franka 与双臂 YAM 平台上评估任务成功率、模型调用次数、token 用量、收集时间与成本，并用匹配的人类遥操作演示与基础模型生成演示分别训练 Diffusion Policy 与 ACT，以评估自主采集数据的下游学习价值。

Result: CAPEX 将成功演示数量提升 4.3 倍，同时将每个成功演示的成本降低 80%；基于 CAPEX 生成数据训练的策略性能接近匹配人类演示训练的策略，且从零训练时随训练时间延长该差距基本消失。

Conclusion: 基础模型可作为可扩展、可复用的机器人经验来源，为机器人策略学习提供替代人工遥操作的数据收集范式。

Abstract: Robot learning has largely relied on human-teleoperated demonstrations to acquire effective learnable behaviors. However, human-operated data collection processes can be unintuitive, difficult to scale, and inherently asynchronous. We explore an alternative: distilling physical behavior from general-purpose multimodal foundation models into deployable robot policies by using the foundation model itself as an autonomous demonstrator. While sufficiently capable models can generate successful zero-shot manipulation trajectories, repeatedly invoking them during physical execution is slow and expensive, limiting their utility as scalable data generators. As a solution, we introduce CAPEX, an experience-conditioned demonstration collection framework that uses execution experience from previous attempts to adapt how frequently the foundation model must observe, reason, and replan. We evaluate across RoboCasa tasks and on physical Franka and bimanual YAM-arm platforms, measuring task success, model calls, token usage, collection time, and cost. We further train Diffusion Policy and ACT on matched sets of human-teleoperated and foundation-model-generated demonstrations to evaluate the downstream learning value of autonomously collected data. We find that CAPEX increases the number of successful demonstrations by 4.3x while reducing the cost per successful demonstration by 80%. Policies trained on CAPEX-generated data approach the performance of those trained on matched human demonstrations; with longer training, this gap largely closes for policies trained from scratch. These results suggest that foundation models can serve as scalable sources of reusable robot experience. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [192] [TriDrive: Joint Driver, Vehicle, and Road Modeling for Forecasting and Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33000)
*Yuhang Wang,Jingxin Yang,Chuheng Wei,Yuechen Guo,Jinghan Xu,Zhao Han,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriDrive 是首个联合预测驾驶员运动学、车辆动力学与道路需求的统一框架，通过自动化条件转移模型和定向残差连接实现驾驶、车辆、道路的联合建模，并已在公开基准、自然驾驶数据和实车实时系统中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员、车辆与道路场景的交互与演化预测是驾驶员监控的核心，但现有工作通常孤立地建模车内行为或受交通条件影响的驾驶员运动，因此需要联合建模驾驶员-车辆-道路，并进行车载实时评估。

Method: 提出 TriDrive：一个自动化条件转移模型，联合预测驾驶员运动学、车辆动力学和道路需求。使用模态特定编码器：驾驶员锚定运动学表示、因果 CAN 总线动力学、冻结的 V-JEPA 2 道路潜变量及结构化道路边缘；通过有向残差连接让驾驶员与道路上下文精炼车辆预测。为实时部署，蒸馏道路编码器，并在 comma four 搭配外置 8 GB GPU 上运行；轻量当前状态预警探针以 5 Hz 更新，p95 延迟 177 ms，同时联合模型并行预测。

Result: 在公开 AIDE 基准上，其运动学编码方案在全集 All-MPJPE 上取得已发表基线中的新 SOTA（48.05 对 71.47）。在 197.2 小时自然驾驶 BATON 子集上，有向连接与道路边缘将辅助介入场景的 PR-AUC 提升：转向起始 +0.084，碰撞时间下降 +0.286。实时系统中，预警探针在人工标注的手动驾驶预警上 AUROC 为 0.725，优于基于 openpilot 的基线 0.563；在 14 名驾驶员的配对道路研究中，其预警被认为更合适（+1.79）且更及时（+2.67）。

Conclusion: TriDrive 实现了驾驶员-车辆-道路的联合预测，并证明其在车载实时条件下可行，能提升预警性能与驾驶员接受度，优于 openpilot 的驾驶员监控系统。

Abstract: Predicting how drivers, vehicles, and road scenes interact and evolve together is central to driver monitoring. Prior work models in-cabin activity or traffic-conditioned driver motion in isolation, motivating joint driver, vehicle, and road modeling with real-time on-vehicle evaluation. We introduce TriDrive, to our knowledge the first unified framework that jointly forecasts driver kinematics, vehicle dynamics, and road demands through an automation-conditioned transition model. Modality-specific encoders (an anchored kinematic representation of the driver, causal CAN-bus dynamics, and frozen V-JEPA 2 road latents with structured road margins) are connected by directed residual connections through which driver and road context refine vehicle forecasts. We evaluate TriDrive on three downstream tasks. On the public AIDE benchmark, its kinematic encoder recipe sets a new full-set state of the art (SOTA) among published baselines (48.05 versus 71.47 All-MPJPE). On 197.2 hours of naturalistic BATON subset, directed connections and road margins raise assistance-engaged PR-AUC by 0.084 for steering onset and 0.286 for time-to-collision drops. For real-time use, we distill the road encoders and run TriDrive on a comma four with an external 8 GB GPU, where a lightweight current-state warning probe updates at 5 Hz with 177 ms p95 latency while the joint model forecasts concurrently. The probe is above an openpilot-based baseline on human-labeled manual-driving warnings (AUROC 0.725 versus 0.563), and in a paired on-road study 14 drivers rate its warnings as more appropriate (+1.79) and timely (+2.67) than those of openpilot's driver-monitoring system.

</details>


### [193] [Learning Geometry-Aware Virtual Fixtures From Sparse Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32958)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Marcella Piacentino,Raffaella Mancino,Paolino De Risi,Bernhard Weber,Margarida Campos,Thomas Hulin,Sylvain Calinon,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,Julian Klodmann,Fanny Ficuciello,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于运动先验与黎曼流形线性二次跟踪（LQT）的虚拟夹具生成方法，仅需少量示教点即可构建带方向引导、刚度自适应且具有有效区域的轨迹引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率学习示教（LfD）通常需要大量演示，在遥操作中难以收集；因此希望让操作者通过少量via points直观创建虚拟夹具轨迹，并解决方向引导以及任务中可自由离开引导区域的问题。

Method: 使用线性二次跟踪（LQT）从少量via points定义引导轨迹；将经典LQT重新表述到黎曼流形上并引入几何先验以处理姿态引导；对LQT解进行概率解释，得到每个轨迹点的协方差，用于调节虚拟夹具刚度：via points附近强引导、远离时弱引导；同时用协方差定义夹具有效区域，使操作者可离开影响区执行未建模任务。

Result: 在玩具示例中评估了所提出的黎曼LQT公式，并在da Vinci Research Kit（dVRK）上针对微创手术中需高精度的切割任务验证了完整框架。

Conclusion: 该方法能在少量示教下生成直观、方向感知、刚度可变且可退出的虚拟夹具，适用于高精度遥操作手术任务。

Abstract: In many teleoperation applications, collecting a large number of demonstrations as required for traditional probabilistic learning from demonstration (LfD) approaches may not be feasible. To still give operators the ability to intuitively create trajectories as Virtual Fixtures (VFs), we propose to leverage a motion prior in the learning process. Particularly, by using Linear Quadratic Tracking (LQT), users are able to define guiding trajectories from the demonstration of just a few via points. To account for orientation guidance, we further reformulate classical LQT on Riemannian manifolds, introducing an additional geometric prior. Through a probabilistic interpretation of the LQT solution, we derive a covariance estimate at each trajectory point which we use to modulate the stiffness of the resulting fixture, resulting in strong guidance around the via points and softer guidance when far away. The covariance information is also used to define a validity region of the fixture, allowing the operator to leave its influence area and conduct unmodeled tasks. We evaluate the proposed Riemannian LQT formulation in a set of toy examples and the full framework on a cutting task requiring high precision in a minimally invasive surgery setting on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK).

</details>


### [194] [Communication-Aware Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Decentralized Multi-Human Multi-Robot Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32935)
*Ziqin Yuan,Ruiqi Wang,Baijian Yang,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CommHG：一种通信感知的异构图学习框架，用于去中心化多人类-多机器人任务分配，通过联合优化分配与通信提升大规模协作下的任务完成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人类-多机器人团队需协调稀缺且动态可用的人类支持与分布式机器人执行，但有限的机器人-机器人、人-机器人通信会延迟信息交换与监督干预，使任务分配更复杂。

Method: CommHG用局部图建模人-机器人-任务耦合交互，并以信息可用性和信息年龄为条件；学习通信动作，使机器人决定何时向哪个人类操作员查询、何时与同伴共享信息；通过协作式多智能体强化学习联合优化任务分配与通信，在有限通信与监督资源下获取有用信息。同时提出集成异构人类与机器人、动态任务与运行状态、受限机器人-机器人及双向人-机器人链路的基准。

Result: 在最多16个机器人、6个人类和112个任务的异构团队实验中，CommHG随着协调规模增大提升及时加权任务完成率，并在Large场景中优于最强基线。

Conclusion: CommHG表明，将通信决策与任务分配联合优化，可有效应对去中心化多人类-多机器人协作中的通信受限与动态可用性问题，提升大规模团队协调性能。

Abstract: Multi-human multi-robot (MH-MR) teams combine robotic autonomy with human expertise, but effective task allocation requires coordinating scarce, dynamically available human support with distributed robot execution. Limited robot-robot and human-robot communication further complicates this coupling by delaying information exchange and supervisory intervention. We introduce CommHG, a communication-aware heterogeneous graph learning framework for decentralized MH-MR task allocation. CommHG represents coupled human-robot-task interactions through local graphs conditioned on information availability and age. Learned communication actions enable robots to decide when and which operator to query, and when to share information with peers. Allocation and communication are jointly optimized through cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, allowing the team to acquire useful information while managing limited communication and supervisory resources. We also introduce a benchmark integrating heterogeneous humans and robots, dynamic tasks and operational states, and constrained robot-robot and bidirectional human-robot links. Experiments across heterogeneous teams with up to 16 robots, 6 humans, and 112 tasks show that CommHG improves timely weighted mission completion as coordination scale increases, outperforming the strongest baseline in the Large scenario.

</details>


### [195] [RoboFoundry: System-as-Policy Evolution for Self-Learning Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32862)
*Jingsong Liang,Shuhao Liao,Shizhe Zhang,Diyuan Hou,Yuxin Cai,Xinjian Deng,Chengyang He,Wenhui Huang,Runjia Tan,Zhidong Wang,Lan Yu,Xuesong Tian,Guillaume Sartoretti,Jie Luo,Yao Mu,Wenjun Wu,Wanhua Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFoundry，将具身智能体的支撑系统整体建模为可自我演化的策略（Self-Evolving System-as-Policy），通过经验驱动的系统级更新实现跨机器人、跨任务的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型不应作为孤立具身智能体运行，但现有方法多只优化记忆、上下文、技能或动作接口等单个组件，未将支撑系统视为统一策略；且仅靠交互不足以自我改进，必须把执行经验转化为持久、经验证的系统变更。

Method: RoboFoundry 将过程形式化为 Self-Evolving System-as-Policy：诊断决策与记忆管理中的能力缺口，把执行轨迹转化为经验证的任务特定系统更新，并将重复出现的改进提升为通用系统能力。演化作用于两个互补层面：上下文系统（管理活跃内部上下文与持久文件系统记忆）和分层技能系统（组织原子技能、可复用组合与失败条件恢复）。共享语义接口分离具身无关决策与具身特定执行，使演化后的系统能力可迁移至异构机器人。

Result: 在 EmbodiedBench 上达到 SOTA，将 GPT-5.5 提升 27.8%；使 Qwen3.7-Plus 接近 GPT-5.5（70.3% vs. 72.7%）。在长时程记忆 RoboMemArena 上至少超越所有基线 39.0%，即使对手有外部基础模型辅助。在 LIBERO-PRO 上，对所有扰动类型超越 Cap-Agent0 243.8%-679.7%。真实部署中展示跨机器人、跨任务的零样本迁移与在线演化。

Conclusion: RoboFoundry 表明系统级策略演化能为不同基础模型带来一致增益，并具备实现完全自主具身智能体的潜力。

Abstract: A foundation model should not act in isolation as an embodied agent. Yet, existing methods often optimize individual components of the agent stack, such as memory, context, skills, or action interfaces, rather than treating the supporting system itself as a unified policy. Moreover, interaction alone does not yield self-improvement unless execution experience is converted into persistent, validated system changes. We therefore propose RoboFoundry, the first embodied agentic framework that formulates this process as Self-Evolving System-as-Policy. RoboFoundry diagnoses capability gaps in decision-making and memory management, converts execution traces into validated task-specific system updates, and promotes recurring improvements to the general system. Evolution operates over two complementary surfaces: a context system that manages active internal context and persistent file-system memory, and a hierarchical skill system that organizes atomic skills, reusable compositions, and failure-conditioned recovery. A shared semantic interface separates embodiment-invariant decisions from embodiment-specific execution, allowing evolved system capabilities to transfer across heterogeneous robots. On EmbodiedBench, RoboFoundry achieves state-of-the-art performance, notably improving GPT-5.5 by 27.8%. It also brings Qwen3.7-Plus to near parity with GPT-5.5 (70.3% vs. 72.7%), showing consistent gains from system-as-policy evolution across foundation models. For long-horizon memory, RoboFoundry outperforms all baselines on RoboMemArena by at least 39.0%, even against methods assisted by external foundation models. On LIBERO-PRO, it further outperforms Cap-Agent0 by 243.8%-679.7% across all perturbation types. In real-world deployments, RoboFoundry demonstrates zero-shot transfer and online evolution across robots and tasks, highlighting its potential for fully autonomous embodied agents.

</details>


### [196] [FINE: Future-Informed Navigation Encoding for Data-Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32855)
*Khang H. Nguyen,Hoang Pham Quang Nguyen,Ha Phuong Nguyen,Khanh Dinh Binh,Xuan Ha Nguyen,Vien Ngo,Duy Ho Nguyen Minh,Huan Nguyen,An T. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FINE，一种未来信息导航编码框架，从已有演示轨迹的未来观测中提取潜在监督，提升视觉语言导航（VLN）策略，并在演示数据有限时获得更大收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 VLN 策略适配到新环境成本高昂，因为每条额外路线和指令都需要具身演示；而标准 observation-to-action 训练仅利用了轨迹中很小一部分信息，尤其是未来观测中已包含的指令相关地标外观及其 3D 排列信息。

Method: FINE 为 VLN 主干网络引入两类互补辅助表示：一是显式地标 token，按指令指定的有序地标进行训练，在语义 2D patch 特征空间和视角相关的 3D 几何特征空间中预测未来地标区域；二是隐式未来 token，利用视频世界模型生成同场景合理反事实未来，学习区分实际到达的地标状态与反事实未来。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE val-unseen 上，全量训练数据下 FINE 将 InternVLA-N1 的成功率分别提升 2.6 和 4.5 个百分点；在 70% 演示预算下成功率提升 6.8 个百分点，约恢复因减少训练演示而损失性能的三分之一。

Conclusion: 利用未来观测作为潜在监督可有效增强 VLN 策略，且演示数据越有限增益越明显，为降低对具身演示数据的依赖提供了有前景的方向；项目页面已公开。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language navigation (VLN) policies to new environments is expensive because every additional route and instruction requires an embodied demonstration. Yet standard observation-to-action training uses only a small fraction of the information already contained in each trajectory. In particular, future observations reveal the instruction-relevant landmarks that the agent will encounter, including what they look like and how they are arranged in 3D. We introduce FINE, a Future-Informed Navigation Encoding framework that extracts this latent supervision from existing demonstrations. FINE equips a VLN backbone with two complementary auxiliary representations. First, explicit landmark tokens follow the ordered landmarks specified by the instruction and are trained to predict future landmark regions in both semantic 2D patch-feature space and viewpoint-dependent 3D geometric feature space. Second, an implicit future token learns to distinguish the landmark state that is actually reached from plausible same-scene counterfactual futures generated by a video world model. On R2R-CE and RxR-CE val-unseen, FINE improves InternVLA-N1 by 2.6 and 4.5 success-rate points, respectively, at full training data. More importantly, as demonstrations become limited, the benefit grows: at a 70% demonstration budget, FINE improves success rate by 6.8 points, recovering roughly one-third of the performance lost by reducing the training demonstrations. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [197] [Scanning While Imagining: A Scene-Graph World Model for Robotic Ultrasound Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32837)
*Xuesong Li,Shuai Chen,Feng Li,Zhongliang Jiang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SonoGraph-WM：一种动作与目标条件的世界模型，用于超声探头前瞻性导航。该方法以场景图表征解剖结构，用Transformer联合预测未来场景图与探头位姿，并通过滚动时域规划实现目标视图导航；训练数据由CT标签图沿表面约束轨迹生成，减少对标注超声序列的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声图像采集高度依赖操作者解读解剖并预判探头运动带来的视图变化，而现有机器人超声导航方法多直接选择动作，未显式预测解剖结构变化，因此需要可预测解剖变化的世界模型来支持探头规划。

Method: 将解剖表示为场景图，包含可见结构、几何信息与空间关系，不合成超声图像。给定历史场景图和探头位姿，统一Transformer联合预测未来场景图与位姿。滚动时域规划器递归想象候选轨迹，选择到达目标图的最短预测路径，执行短时程后再根据新观测重规划。为降低对追踪且解剖标注超声序列的依赖，从CT标签图沿表面约束探头轨迹生成对齐的场景图-位姿训练数据。

Result: 在四个留出CT病例上，空间关系F1在20个预测步内保持93%以上；基于标注衍生场景图的闭环导航中，胆囊和胰腺成功率分别为77.50%和75.00%。在机器人-体模导航实验中，使用标签图衍生场景图的规划器在73.7%的试验中到达目标视图。

Conclusion: 结果表明，CT监督的解剖世界建模可用于探头规划，并强调频繁观测更新对可靠导航的重要性。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) acquisition depends on the operator's ability to interpret anatomy and anticipate how the view will change with probe motion. Many robotic US navigation methods select actions without explicitly predicting these anatomical changes. We propose SonoGraph-WM, an action- and goal-conditioned world model for anticipatory probe navigation. The model represents anatomy as scene graphs (SGs), capturing visible structures, their geometry, and spatial relationships without synthesizing US images. Given a history of SGs and probe poses, a unified Transformer jointly predicts future SGs and poses. A receding-horizon planner recursively imagines candidate trajectories, selects the shortest predicted path reaching a goal graph, and follows it over a short execution horizon before replanning from new observations. To reduce reliance on tracked and anatomically annotated US sequences, we generate aligned SG--pose training data from computed tomography (CT) label maps along surface-constrained probe trajectories. On four held-out CT cases, spatial relation F1 remains above 93% over 20 prediction steps, and closed-loop navigation achieves 77.50% and 75.00% success for the gallbladder and pancreas, respectively, using annotation-derived SGs. In robot--phantom navigation experiments with label-map-derived SGs, the planner reached the target view in 73.7% of trials. These findings support CT-supervised anatomical world modeling for probe planning and highlight the importance of frequent observation updates for reliable navigation. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [198] [Towards Kinematic Actionable Infeasibility Detection in Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32806)
*Aayush Rath,Lakshya Jindal,Antony Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于几何与构型空间拓扑的不可行性认证框架，利用符号距离场追踪障碍边界诱导的分离流形，并通过GPU并行前沿扩展实现高维单纯复形重建；在4-DOF和5-DOF机器人场景中分别于数秒和四分钟内完成不可行性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划不仅需计算无碰撞路径，还需在无可行路径时给出不可行性证明。完备方法通常仅适用于低维空间，而采样规划器虽可扩展至高维，却无法提供有限时间内的不可行性证书，导致高维空间中的不可行性认证问题长期未解。

Method: 提出几何驱动的不可行性认证框架，对构型空间拓扑进行显式的分辨率相关分析。基于符号距离场表示，在构型空间中直接追踪由障碍边界诱导的分离流形，从而检测不可行性并识别具体几何成因。为应对计算挑战，开发并行前沿扩展算法，利用GPU加速高维空间中的单纯复形重建。

Result: 在4-DOF和5-DOF机器人场景中验证方法：4-DOF案例可在数秒内完成不可行性认证，5-DOF案例可在四分钟内完成。文中进一步讨论了提升更高维空间可扩展性的途径。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维运动规划中的不可行性认证提供了几何驱动且可并行加速的解决思路，能够在中等自由度场景中给出有限时间内的不可行性证书，并为向更高维空间扩展奠定基础。

Abstract: Motion planning in robotics requires not only computing collision-free paths but also certifying infeasibility when no such path exists. Complete methods are limited to low-dimensional spaces, while sampling-based planners scale efficiently but cannot provide finite-time infeasibility certificates, leaving this problem largely unresolved in high-dimensional spaces. In this letter, we present a geometry-driven framework for certifying infeasibility through an explicit resolution-dependent analysis of configuration space topology. Leveraging signed distance field representations, the proposed method traces separating manifolds induced by obstacle boundaries directly in configuration space, enabling both detection of infeasibility and identification of the specific geometric cause. To address computational challenges, we develop a parallel frontier-expansion algorithm that exploits GPU acceleration for efficient simplicial reconstruction in high-dimensional spaces. We validate the approach on 4-DOF and 5-DOF robot scenarios, certifying infeasibility within seconds for 4-DOF cases and under four minutes for 5-DOF cases. We further discuss avenues for improving scalability to higher-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [199] [CollisionGAT: Controller-Agnostic One-Step Collision Screening for Multi-Agent Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32783)
*Alan Debbas,Edwin Meriaux,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CollisionGAT：一种图注意力网络，为多机器人团队中每个移动智能体输出碰撞风险分数，可被控制器用于接受、修复、重规划或推迟提议步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队移动前需检查提议步骤与机器人及障碍物的碰撞；需要一种能快速评估局部碰撞风险并便于集成到不同规划/控制流程的方法。

Method: CollisionGAT读取移动智能体的当前状态、提议状态及局部相关静态障碍，输出每个移动智能体的碰撞风险分数；将其挂载到连续路径跟踪控制器和GATeD（一种对障碍盲的D* Lite规划器，使用类型化否决更新规划图），并由精确几何检查提供训练标签和独立审计每一步。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量实验结果或性能指标，仅报告了网络设计、集成方式以及精确几何检查的训练标签与独立审计作用。

Conclusion: CollisionGAT提供通用的碰撞风险评分，可辅助控制器接受、修复、重规划或推迟提议步骤，从而提升多机器人运动规划与执行的安全性。

Abstract: Before a team of robots moves, each proposed step must be checked for collisions with other robots and with obstacles. We present CollisionGAT, a graph-attention network that reads the current and proposed states of moving agents together with locally relevant stationary obstacles and returns one collision-risk score per moving agent. Any controller can use these scores to accept, repair, replan, or postpone a proposed step. We mount CollisionGAT on a continuous path-following controller and on GATeD, an obstacle-blind D* Lite planner that uses typed vetoes to update its planning graphs. Exact geometric checks supply the training labels and independently audit every executed step.

</details>


### [200] [Copper-Policy: Focus on the Representation for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32779)
*Zexin Feng,Yixu Feng,Lingyu Xiao,Shang Su,Kexin Zheng,Chang Xu,Mengkai Shi,Shuo Feng,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Copper-Policy 通过学习紧凑的世界表征并与策略联合训练，避免像素级未来重建，在 RoboTwin、LIBERO-Plus 和真实机器人任务上取得强控制性能，同时显著提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过建模未来场景演化获取行为先验，但在像素或潜空间预测详细未来代价高昂；且近期证据表明无需测试时生成也能从协同训练中获益，因此核心问题是：WAMs 究竟需要学习什么才能提升控制？

Method: 提出 Copper-Policy，不依赖预定义目标空间，而是与策略联合学习紧凑的世界表征。通过时序联合嵌入预测，在任务意图条件下预测未来观测嵌入，不重建像素；预测与动作解码共同塑造表征，同时策略保留当前帧空间细节用于执行。紧凑预测目标减少每样本训练 token 数。

Result: 表示分析显示，所学特征能更好区分任务驱动变化与扰动，并为控制提供互补信息。2B 参数模型在 8× RTX 5090 上 9.67 小时完成训练，在匹配 A100 上比 Fast-WAM 快 6 倍。Copper-Policy 在 RoboTwin 上优于所有对比方法且无需具身预训练，在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 80.85%，优于多个具身预训练 VLA；在三个挑战性真实机器人任务上表现与 π_{0.5} 相当，平均分更高。

Conclusion: Copper-Policy 表明，学习紧凑且面向控制的联合嵌入世界表征，可以在不进行像素级未来生成的情况下，同时实现强控制性能与高效训练。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) acquire behavioral priors by modeling future scene evolution, but predicting detailed futures in pixel or latent space incurs substantial cost. Recent evidence that co-training gains persist without test-time generation raises a question: what must a WAM learn to improve control? We introduce Copper-Policy, which learns a compact World representation with the policy rather than relying on a predefined target space. Through temporal joint-embedding prediction, it predicts future observation embeddings conditioned on task intention without reconstructing pixels. This prediction and action decoding shape the representation jointly, while the policy retains access to current-frame spatial detail for execution. Representation analyses show that the learned features better separate task-driven change from perturbations and provide complementary information for control. Compact prediction targets reduce training tokens per sample, enabling a 2B-parameter model trained in 9.67 hours on 8$\times$ RTX 5090 GPUs and 6$\times$ faster than Fast-WAM on matched A100 GPUs. Copper-Policy outperforms every compared method without embodied pretraining on RoboTwin and several embodied-pretrained VLAs on LIBERO-Plus (80.85%). On three challenging real-robot tasks, it performs comparably to $\pi_{0.5}$ and attains a higher average score. Together, these results show that Copper-Policy combines strong control performance with efficient training.

</details>


### [201] [CLAP: Closed-Loop Alignment with Pressure for Precise Suction Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32767)
*Yixian Zou,Chongyang Xu,Yuling Xin,Ziliang Feng,Fanman Meng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CLAP：将真空管路压力信号引入 VLA 策略，使吸附/附着状态可观测，并用于与视觉特征融合及中断当前开环执行以重新推理，从而提升真实机器人叠放与码垛的精确放置成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠放和码垛对放置精度要求高：一层误差会被下一层继承，平面吸盘缺乏可纠正错误位姿的特征；自上而下吸盘适合密集排列，但现有 VLA 工作未报告基于实测真空信号的条件策略，也未利用该信号中止已开始的抓取动作。视觉在此关键遮挡时刻无法解决杯子与待放置面相互遮挡的问题。

Method: CLAP 在真空管路上接入压力模块，将解码后的压力读数替换策略本体感知中的吸力命令，并与视觉特征融合；同时用该信号终止开环执行窗口，使策略基于新观测重新推理。数据方面，在真实机器人上记录目标状态拆解，再离线反转关节状态序列，无需仿真回放；并加入定向阶段演示，仅占训练帧的 8.3%，覆盖吸附转换及中断抓取后的构型。

Result: 在真实 Unitree Z1 上，单个多任务检查点在两种颜色设置下达到 96.67% 平均成功率，分别高于最强基线 16.67 和 10.00 个百分点；单色四块任务成功案例平均误差为 15.92 mm。四个消融设置分别低 5.00 至 13.33 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，利用实测真空压力信号实现吸附状态闭环感知，可有效提升吸盘式 VLA 策略在叠放与码垛中的鲁棒性和精度；作者将公开代码与训练权重。
