Abstract: Stacking and palletising demand precise placement: error left in one layer is inherited by the next, and a flat pad offers no feature to funnel a wrong pose into the right one. Top-down suction suits such dense arrangements, and suction has already been brought into vision-language-action (VLA) policies. What that work does not report, however, is a policy conditioned on a measured vacuum signal, or one that uses it to abandon an action already under way. Vision does not settle the question here, because at the moment it matters the cup and the face it holds occlude each other. We present CLAP, which makes the attachment state observable through a pressure module tapped into the vacuum line. The decoded reading replaces the suction command in the policy's proprioception, is fused with the visual features, and terminates the open-loop execution window so that the policy re-infers from a fresh observation. For data, we record goal-state disassembly on the physical robot and reverse the joint-state sequence offline, without a simulation replay. Targeted phase demonstrations, 8.3% of the training frames, cover the suction transitions and the configurations an interrupted grasp leaves behind. On a real Unitree Z1, one multi-task checkpoint reaches 96.67% average success in both colour settings, 16.67 and 10.00 points above the strongest baseline, its monochromatic four-block successes averaging 15.92 mm of error. Four ablation settings fall 5.00 to 13.33 points short. We will release code and trained weights.

</details>


### [202] [An Empirical Study on What Matters for Viewpoint-Generalizable Policies in Visual Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32762)
*Mino Nakura,Sriram Krishna,Yufei Wang,Shubham Tulsiani,Zackory Erickson,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实证研究探究视觉运动策略跨视角泛化的设计选择，发现保留密集视觉 token 并让动作头参与几何推理可显著提升跨视角性能，并实现随机相机配置下的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿学习在机器人操作中潜力巨大，但现有策略对视角扰动脆弱，难以部署到多样环境，因此需要明确哪些设计选择能提升跨视角泛化能力。

Method: 开展受控实证研究，在覆盖广泛相机位姿的仿真任务套件中，比较不同设计选择对视觉运动策略的影响；重点考察密集视觉 token 的保留，以及动作头是否参与几何推理。

Result: 保留密集视觉 token 且动作头参与几何推理时，策略在跨视角任务中保持良好性能；同时，该策略在随机相机配置下可从仿真零样本迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: 密集视觉 token 与动作头的几何推理是提升视觉运动策略视角泛化性的关键设计，并有助于实现随机相机配置下的零样本现实部署。

Abstract: Visual imitation learning is a promising approach to training robot manipulation policies capable of completing a wide variety of tasks. However, policies today remain brittle to viewpoint perturbations, making deployment in diverse environments a challenge. We present a controlled empirical study of which design choices allow visuomotor policies to generalize across viewpoints. We find that viewpoint generalization improves when dense visual tokens are retained and the action head participates in geometric reasoning. On a suite of simulated tasks that span a wide range of camera poses, we show that these design choices yield a policy that remains performant across viewpoints. As a practical consequence, a policy trained with these design choices also transfers zero-shot from simulation to the real world under random camera configurations.

</details>


### [203] [MORPH: Self-Organising Multi-Robot Task Allocation via Neuroplasticity-Inspired Adaptive Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32745)
*Xuezhi Niu,Didem Gürdür Broo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MORPH 是一种无需训练的在线多机器人任务分配（MRTA）框架，通过四种局部可塑性规则在线学习有向成对分配偏好，在动态环境和任务分布偏移下优于依赖先验知识或固定邻近规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的 MRTA 存在根本矛盾：有效协调需要学习到的结构，但条件变化时该结构必须能自适应。现有方法通常假设已知任务知识、效用函数、成本矩阵或已训练策略，因此在缺少这些知识或任务分布变化时表现脆弱。

Method: 提出 MORPH（Multi-agent Online Rewiring through Plasticity-guided Hierarchy），一个 training-free MRTA 框架。全局分配质量由四条局部可塑性规则（突触、稳态、结构和元可塑性）作用于有向成对偏好矩阵而涌现，该矩阵根据运行时共现与任务完成反馈更新。MORPH 不需要任务模型、投标计算或离线训练；响应决策使用学习到的 AGV-to-Picker 偏好，而非固定邻近规则。在 Gerkey-Mataric MRTA 分类中，MORPH 是单任务、单机器人、瞬时分配类别中首个在线学习有向成对分配偏好的方法。

Result: 在 TA-RWARE 仓库基准（8–24 个智能体、4 张地图、每回合 800 步、5 个随机种子）上，MORPH 在 N=24 时达到 all-to-all 吞吐量的 110%，同时仅使用 21% 的可能协调链路，且效率优势随车队规模单调增长。在空间任务分布偏移下，其性能退化比基于邻近的方法少 3 倍，且学习到的偏好与曼哈顿距离不相关。系统性消融证实四条可塑性规则均有可测贡献。无需编程即涌现出两种分配特性：跨类型偏好主导和渐进式偏好稀疏化，类似生物神经回路的发育性精炼。

Conclusion: 学习到的偏好由任务共现历史驱动，而非空间邻近性；结果表明基于在线可塑性、无需离线训练的自适应偏好学习可在动态 MRTA 中实现高效且鲁棒的协调。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocation (MRTA) in dynamic environments faces a fundamental tension: effective coordination requires learned structure, but that structure must adapt when conditions change. Existing methods resolve this by assuming prior task knowledge, a utility function, a cost matrix, or a trained policy making them brittle when deployed without such knowledge or when task distributions shift. We present MORPH(Multi-agent Online Rewiring through Plasticity-guided Hierarchy), a training-free MRTA framework where global allocation quality emerges from 4 local plasticity rules (synaptic, homeostatic, structural, and metaplasticity) applied to a directed pairwise preference matrix updated from runtime co-occurrence and task-completion feedback. MORPH requires no task model, no bid computation, and no offline training; response decisions use learned AGV-to-Picker preferences rather than a fixed proximity rule. Within the Gerkey-Mataric MRTA taxonomy, MORPH is the first method in the single-task, single-robot, instantaneous-assignment class to learn directed pairwise allocation preferences online. Evaluated on the TA-RWARE warehouse benchmark (8-24 agents, 4 maps, 800 steps per episode, 5 seeds), MORPH achieves 110% of all-to-all throughput at N=24 while using only 21% of possible coordination links as an efficiency advantage that grows monotonically with fleet size. Under spatial task distribution shift, MORPH degrades 3x less than proximity-based methods while its learned preferences remain uncorrelated with Manhattan distance. Systematic ablation confirms all four plasticity rules contribute measurably. Two allocation properties emerge without programming: cross-type preference dominance and progressive preference sparsification, mirroring the developmental refinement of biological neural circuits. Learned preferences are driven by task co-occurrence history, not spatial proximity.

</details>


### [204] [SEES: A Self-Evolving Embodied System via Failure-Guided VLA Policy Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32698)
*Ziwen Li,Hanlue Zhang,Zhenyang Ren,Tianyu Huang,Runqi Lin,Haoyu Wang,Zhengqing Gao,Yandong Guo,Fakhri Karray,Tongliang Liu,Chris Russell,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自进化具身系统SEES：无需额外专家演示，通过失败驱动的仿真在线强化学习更新共享VLA适配器，持续提升VLA策略的长程任务性能，并能迁移到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略在短程任务上泛化较好，但在长程任务上仍不可靠，部分原因是训练数据偏向单阶段操作任务；单个弱原子技能即可导致多个多阶段任务失败。现有改进方法通常需要专家定位瓶颈并提供额外演示，成本高且部署后不现实。

Method: SEES将长程任务分解为原子任务并路由至对应family policy；每个family包含相关原子技能并共享一个VLA adapter。执行时自动监控原子任务结果，识别最频繁失败的原子技能作为当前瓶颈。随后在仿真中恢复曾遇到的状态，并利用LLM生成任务特定成功准则以构建定制RL任务；通过在线RL更新共享family adapter，促进相关原子技能间的正迁移和跨进化轮次的累积改进。

Result: 大量实验表明，SEES可与不同VLA骨干集成，逐步提升其长程任务性能；同时在未见任务上也观察到持续改进，表明其迁移能力超出进化设置。

Conclusion: SEES提供了一种无需专家演示、从失败中自进化的VLA改进框架，可有效缓解长程任务中的原子技能瓶颈，并展现出跨任务迁移与持续泛化潜力。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) policies demonstrate promising generalization across diverse short-horizon tasks. However, they remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks, partly because the large-scale training data is biased toward single-stage manipulation tasks that are cheaper to demonstrate. A single weak atomic skill can cause failures across multiple multi-stage tasks. To address such failures, existing methods often require experts to identify the bottleneck and provide additional demonstrations, making the improvement costly and potentially impractical after deployment. To this end, we present a Self-Evolving Embodied System (SEES) that learns from failures and improves the VLA policy without additional expert demonstrations. SEES decomposes long-horizon tasks into atomic tasks and routes them to corresponding family policies. Each family consists of related atomic skills that share one VLA adapter. During execution, the system automatically monitors atomic-task outcomes to identify the most frequently failing atomic skills as the current bottlenecks. To overcome these bottlenecks, SEES constructs tailored RL tasks in simulation by restoring previously encountered states and generating task-specific success criteria with an LLM. Online RL updates the shared family adapters to promote positive transfer among related atomic skills and cumulative improvement across evolution rounds. Extensive experiments show that SEES can be integrated with different VLA backbones to progressively improve their long-horizon performance. We also observe continued improvement on unseen tasks, providing evidence of transfer beyond the evolution settings.

</details>


### [205] [PF-RL: Progress Field Reinforcement Learning via Goal-Conditioned Value Geometry for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32634)
*Yunpeng Qing,Yilun Kong,Sixu Lin,Ming Zhou,Yiming Fei,Shuang Luo,Yixiao Chi,Haoming Gu,Jingyuan Liu,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou,Changqing Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL)，通过在学习到的目标条件进度表示上计算几何距离来获得密集价值与进度优势，从而为 VLA 策略提供细粒度信用分配，并在仿真与真实双臂操作任务上稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（RFT）虽能提升 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）策略，但任务级稀疏结果难以对中间转移进行有效信用分配，尤其在长时程操作中。已有进度感知方法多将任务进度建模为显式标量预测，缺乏对中间观测与任务目标关系的结构化建模，可能限制转移级信用分配。

Method: PF-RL 在预训练 VLA 特征上学习结构化的目标条件进度表示，并转化为策略优化的密集信用。其核心是一个轻量共享 Progress Field 头，将当前表示与目标表示映射到紧凑进度空间；该空间中几何距离诱导目标条件价值，同时用互补的时间目标与目标结构目标塑造学习到的几何。转移级价值变化自然产生密集进度优势，用于离线策略改进与在线强化微调中的细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0 以及真实世界双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，PF-RL 相比强监督微调、强化微调以及进度感知基线均能持续提升策略性能。

Conclusion: PF-RL 通过学习结构化的目标条件进度场并提供密集转移级信用，有效缓解稀疏奖励下的信用分配难题，是一种能同时适用于离线策略改进和在线强化微调的 VLA 策略优化方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning~(RFT) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving Vision-Language-Action~(VLA) policies, yet sparse task-level outcomes provide limited credit for intermediate transitions, especially in long-horizon manipulation. A natural approach is to model intermediate task progress and use it as dense feedback for policy improvement. Despite their architectural differences, existing progress-aware methods commonly formulate task progress as an explicit scalar prediction, providing limited structure for modeling how intermediate observations relate to the task goal, which may hinder effective transition-level credit assignment. We introduce Progress Field Reinforcement Learning (PF-RL), which learns a structured goal-conditioned progress representation over pretrained VLA features and converts it into dense credit for policy optimization. A lightweight shared Progress Field head maps current and goal representations into a compact progress space, where geometric distance induces goal-conditioned value, while complementary temporal and goal-structure objectives shape the learned geometry. Transition-level value changes naturally yield dense progress advantages, enabling fine-grained credit assignment for both offline policy improvement and online reinforcement fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that PF-RL consistently improves policy performance over strong supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement fine-tuning, and progress-aware baselines.

</details>


### [206] [World SLAM Model: Joint World Modeling for SLAM and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32626)
*Minghui Qin,Yijun Yuan,Weicheng Zheng,Kenan Li,Weibang Wang,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Anmin Liu,Yicheng Yao,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出World SLAM Model（WSM），将SLAM范式内嵌到下游导航中，联合维护一致世界状态、预测未来视觉状态并估计相机运动与稠密几何，以端到端导航-SLAM目标训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法把SLAM仅视为上游模块，只提供位姿、地图或token；本文希望让导航直接受益于SLAM的增量状态更新、持久记忆和后端误差精化，从而在交互中维持一致的世界状态。

Method: WSM采用SLAM核心机制：增量状态更新与持久记忆、后端对累积误差的精化。给定当前观测和导航目标，WSM预测未来视觉状态，并联合估计其相机运动与稠密几何，将视觉预测锚定于不断演化的空间世界状态；该状态随新观测持续更新，并作为动作生成和闭环导航的基础。模型以联合导航-SLAM目标进行端到端训练。

Result: 实验表明，WSM在提升导航性能的同时保持了较强的SLAM精度。

Conclusion: 结果表明，SLAM可作为长时程世界建模与具身交互的内在机制，而不仅是上游定位建图模块。

Abstract: We introduce World SLAM Model (WSM), a unified framework that brings the SLAM paradigm directly into downstream navigation. Rather than treating SLAM merely as an upstream module that provides poses, maps or tokens, WSM adopts its core mechanisms, including incremental state updates with persistent memory and backend refinement of accumulated errors, to maintain a consistent world state during interaction. Given the current observation and a navigation goal, WSM predicts future visual states and jointly estimates their camera motion and dense geometry, grounding visual prediction in an evolving spatial world state. This spatial state is continuously updated as new observations arrive and provides the basis for action generation and closed-loop navigation. WSM is trained end-to-end with a joint navigation--SLAM objective, enabling downstream navigation to benefit directly from SLAM-style state maintenance and refinement while preserving accurate geometric estimation. Experiments demonstrate improved navigation performance together with strong SLAM accuracy, highlighting the potential of SLAM as an intrinsic mechanism for long-horizon world modeling and embodied interaction.

</details>


### [207] [RECAST: Recasting Vision-Language Semantics into an Actionable Cost Map for Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32595)
*Incheol Cho,Jintae Park,Jinkyu Kim,Jungbeom Lee,Jaegul Choo,Seokha Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RECAST 将 VLM 的推理与视觉基础模型的空间定位结合，构建可操作代价地图，用于机器人导航，显著提升成功率并降低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型模型在训练分布外易失效，而基于 VLM 的方法虽具备高层理解，却缺乏场景空间定位且未将动作与理解对齐。机器人导航需要复杂场景的高层理解，并转化为稳定运动。

Method: 给定机器人前视图和用户指令，先用 VLM 分解场景，判断可通行表面、风险物体、偏好朝向和可通行间隙；再由视觉基础模型在图像中定位这些表面和物体，并将四项判断空间重铸为一个紧凑代价地图。该地图既作为轨迹解码器的条件，也用于对候选轨迹打分并选择执行。由于 VLM 回答滞后于实时场景，方法同时利用两个时刻的代价地图：VLM 判断的枢纽帧（包含全部四项判断）和当前帧（从当前图像重建地形与碰撞代价）。

Result: 在仿真中成功率比最强先前方法提升 13.3 个百分点，在真实四足机器人上提升 31.4 个百分点；碰撞率分别降低 9.6 和 14.3 个百分点，达到所有方法中最低碰撞率。

Conclusion: RECAST 通过融合 VLM 推理与空间定位，有效提升了多样环境下机器人导航的鲁棒性，并显著优于现有方法。项目页面见论文给出的 URL。

Abstract: Safe and robust robot navigation across diverse environments requires a high-level understanding of complex scenes and the ability to carry it into stable motion. Recent works tackle this with learning-based models trained at scale and with approaches built on vision-language models (VLMs). However, learning-based models break down outside their training distribution, while VLM-based approaches bring that understanding but rarely ground it in the scene or align the action with it. To address these limitations, we present RECAST, a robot navigation framework that combines the reasoning of a VLM with the spatial grounding of vision foundation models to build an Actionable Cost map. Given the robot's front view and the user's instruction, we first decompose the scene with the VLM, judging which surfaces are traversable, which objects pose a risk, which heading to prefer, and which gaps are passable. Vision foundation models then ground these surfaces and objects in the image, and all four judgments are spatially recast into one compact cost map. This map both conditions the trajectory decoders and scores their proposals to select the one to execute. As the VLM's answers trail the live scene, both steps draw on cost maps from two points in time: the pivot frame the VLM judged, which carries all four judgments, and the current frame, whose terrain and collision costs are rebuilt from the current image. RECAST improves success over the strongest prior method by 13.3 points in simulation and 31.4 points on a real quadruped, and reduces the collision rate relative to it by 9.6 and 14.3 points, reaching the lowest collision rate among all methods. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [208] [Think Fast, Plan Selectively: Adaptive Deliberation for Efficient Data-Driven MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32591)
*Yi Xian Goh,Sze Jue Yang,Hao Luan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-TD-MPC 受人类双过程认知理论启发，在快速策略执行与测试时规划之间自适应路由，在103个连续控制任务上保持竞争性性能并实现最高约4倍推理加速，且在外扰下按需回退规划以维持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动MPC结合学习到的世界模型与在线轨迹优化，在连续控制中性能强，但每步需采样和评估数百条候选轨迹，计算成本高，导致控制频率远低于实时机器人需求。受人类认知中快速直觉（System 1）与慢速审慎推理（System 2）的双过程理论启发，作者追问是否每个决策都需要同等程度的计算审慎，从而希望仅在真正需要的状态上付出昂贵规划代价。

Method: 提出Fast-TD-MPC，一个轻量框架，能够在快速策略执行与测试时规划之间进行自适应路由，将昂贵的审慎推理保留给最需要的状态；在遇到外部干扰时，可选择性地回退到规划器，以兼顾效率与鲁棒性。

Result: 在103个连续控制任务上取得具有竞争力的任务性能，同时实现最高约4倍的推理加速；在外部扰动下，Fast-TD-MPC能有选择地回退到规划，并保持与原始规划器相当的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，并非所有决策都需要相同程度的计算审慎；通过自适应路由快速策略与测试时规划，可以在显著降低推理成本的同时保持任务性能与鲁棒性，为实时机器人控制等场景提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) combines learned world models with online trajectory optimization, achieving strong performance in continuous control. However, the per-step cost of sampling and evaluating hundreds of candidate trajectories restricts deployment to control frequencies well below what real-time robotics demands. Motivated by the dual-process theory of human cognition, which distinguishes between fast, intuitive processing (System 1) and slower, deliberative reasoning (System 2), we ask whether every decision requires the same degree of computational deliberation. We propose Fast-TD-MPC, a lightweight framework that adaptively routes between fast policy execution and test-time planning, reserving costly deliberation for states where it is most needed. Fast-TD-MPC delivers competitive task performance across 103 continuous control tasks while achieving up to ~4x faster inference. Under external disturbances, Fast-TD-MPC selectively falls back to planning, maintaining robustness comparable to the original planner.

</details>


### [209] [Assisting for Open-Ended Tasks: Goal-Oriented Shared Autonomy as a Particle Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32576)
*Mengxue Fu,Ethan Xu,Sam Iyer-Singh,Yinlong Dai,Michael Hagenow,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将开放式且动态变化目标下的共享自治建模为粒子滤波，并利用基础模型动态提出语义目标与生成辅助技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享自治方法通常假设候选人类目标集合是静态且预先已知的，这限制了其在非结构化辅助场景中的适用性；因此需要支持开放式、动态变化目标的共享自治。

Method: 作者将目标导向共享自治形式化为粒子滤波，其中粒子表示候选人类目标；与固定目标集方法不同，其转移模型在交互过程中动态提出新候选目标，人类动作实时更新目标信念。该框架结合基础模型（如视觉 grounding 和大语言模型）提出上下文相关的语义目标，基于低级技能原语生成目标条件辅助，并利用人类纠正进行技能精炼。

Result: 通过包含12名参与者的用户研究，在多种桌面操作任务上与最先进共享自治基线进行比较，结果表明该方法减少了用户遥操作时间并提高了用户满意度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，将粒子滤波与基础模型结合，可有效实现面向开放式、动态变化目标的共享自治，在非结构化辅助任务中减少遥操作负担并提升用户体验。

Abstract: A common approach for shared autonomy blends human inputs with autonomous assistance based on the human's likely goal. However, most existing approaches assume that a static set of possible goals is known a priori, which limits the use of such methods in unstructured assistive settings. We instead investigate how to enable shared autonomy with open-ended and dynamically changing goals. We formulate goal-oriented shared autonomy as a particle filter in which particles represent candidate human goals. Unlike conventional approaches with a fixed goal set, our transition model dynamically proposes new candidate goals as the interaction evolves, and human actions update the belief over these goals in real time. We instantiate this framework with foundation models (e.g., vision grounding and large language models) that propose context-relevant semantic goals, generate goal-conditioned assistance from low-level skill primitives, and refine those skills from human corrections. We assess our approach through a user study where 12 participants perform a variety of tabletop manipulation tasks with our method and state-of-the-art shared autonomy baselines. The results show that our particle filter-based approach reduces the amount of time users spend teleoperating the system and improves user satisfaction. User study videos: this https URL

</details>


### [210] [GlowTact: Simple and Compact Vision-Based Tactile Sensing with High Sensitivity and Spatial Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32471)
*Yuxiang Ma,Megha Tippur,Pengfei Ye,Sandra Q. Liu,Haonan Chen,Francis Richard Cottrell,Edward Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GlowTact，一种通过单色非定向照明直接可视化接触压力的视觉触觉传感器，面向紧凑型人形指尖，实现克级检测、法向力估计与精细几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉触觉传感器信息丰富，但设计复杂、难以简化并适配人形指尖的尺寸与约束。

Method: GlowTact利用压力响应机制使原始触觉图像直接表征压力分布，无需显式几何重建；仅需单色非定向照明，结构简单、可定制，结合耐用丁腈膜与简洁光学设计。

Result: 实现克级接触检测、准确的法向力估计，并能重建精细接触几何，包括M1螺纹。

Conclusion: GlowTact为紧凑型人形指尖提供了一种实用的新型触觉传感技术，兼具高灵敏度、丰富空间信息与简单耐用的设计。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors (VBTS) provide rich contact information for robotic manipulation, but existing designs can be hard to simplify and adapt to the size and constraints of humanoid fingertips. We introduce \textbf{GlowTact}, a pressure-responsive vision-based tactile sensing mechanism that directly visualizes contact pressure. \textbf{GlowTact} requires only single-color, non-directional illumination, and the raw tactile image directly represents the pressure distribution without explicit geometry reconstruction. This simple sensing principle enables compact, customizable tactile sensors while preserving high sensitivity and rich spatial detail. We demonstrate gram-scale contact detection, accurate normal-force estimation, and reconstruction of fine contact geometry, including M1 screw threads. These results establish \textbf{GlowTact} as a practical new sensing technology for compact humanoid fingertips, combining a durable nitrile membrane and simple optical design with sensitive and information-rich tactile perception.

</details>


### [211] [DRAM: Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory for Robot Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32453)
*Xinyu Zhao,Yixiang Shan,Tao Yang,Runyu Lei,Yiming Zhao,Jiaxin Fan,Zongbao Feng,Peng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DRAM，一种即插即用的固定大小长期记忆模块，可附着于多种预训练机器人策略，在不修改架构、不重训骨干网络的情况下增强长时程记忆与操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作本质依赖历史信息，但现有预训练策略通常仅基于当前观测或短时间窗口，缺乏长期记忆；已有方案要么输入多帧观测导致推理成本显著增加，要么依赖预定义语义特征，限制任务通用性且可能需要重训骨干网络。

Method: DRAM（Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory）使用门控 delta-rule 线性注意力维护固定大小关联记忆，并修改更新规则以并行纳入每帧内所有 token；通过架构无关的读出机制将历史上下文融入动作预测，可跨不同策略架构使用。训练时冻结预训练骨干，仅对记忆模块和动作专家进行任务特定后训练。

Result: 实验表明，DRAM 在冻结的预训练策略上稳定优于短上下文基线及替代性紧凑记忆设计，验证了其作为固定大小、后置式记忆模块的有效性。

Conclusion: DRAM 能在不修改架构、不重训骨干网络的前提下，为预训练机器人策略提供有效的长期记忆能力，是一种通用且实用的即插即用记忆增强方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is inherently history-dependent, yet most pretrained robotic policies condition on only the current observation or a short temporal window. Equipping such policies with long-term memory remains challenging: existing approaches either feed the backbone multi-frame observation windows, which substantially increase inference cost, or rely on pre-defined semantic features, which limit task generality and may also require the retraining of the backbone to adapt to the memory. We introduce DRAM (Delta-rule Recurrent Associative Memory), a plug-and-play memory module that can be attached to a wide range of pretrained robotic policies, endowing them with long-horizon memory without architectural modification or backbone retraining, requiring only task-specific post-training of the memory module and action expert. DRAM maintains a fixed-size associative memory using gated delta-rule linear attention, with a modified update that incorporates all tokens within each frame in parallel. An architecture-agnostic readout integrates historical context into action prediction across different policy architectures. Experiments show that DRAM consistently improves frozen pretrained policies over short-context baselines and alternative compact memory designs, validating its effectiveness as a fixed-size, post-hoc memory module trained with the backbone frozen.

</details>


### [212] [RE-0: Verified Recursive Improvement of Embodied Code-as-Policy Agents through Local On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32416)
*Jiawei Zhang,Xiangrong Zhang,Rui Song,Huanbin Zhou,Chengye Song,Hongzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RE-0与RE-OPD框架，通过递归验证教师局部纠正，将反事实有效的干预转化为on-policy蒸馏监督，实现Code-as-Policy智能体的持续策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法通常将教师完整行为视为监督目标，在教师失败或不全局可靠的状态上错误分配训练信用，难以利用更强但非全局可靠的教师持续提升学生策略。

Method: RE-0不假设教师全局优于学生，而是在学生自身失败历史上请求教师局部纠正，并在环境中验证该纠正是否真正有益；经验证的纠正带来即时改进。RE-OPD进一步将验证干预转为on-policy蒸馏监督：仅对反事实验证通过的教师干预提供分布级监督，按其局部收益加权，并通过投影将改进回注独立学生，使监督位置与教师信用随学生策略共同演化。理论上证明学生每轮增益下界为验证干预增益减去验证与投影误差。

Result: 多个Code-as-Policy具身任务实验表明，RE-0同时提升教师辅助执行与独立学生性能，并能泛化到新机器人及新场景。

Conclusion: 递归验证与反事实信用分配可有效利用非全局可靠教师进行持续策略改进，为Code-as-Policy智能体提供可验证、可投影的蒸馏与自我提升范式。

Abstract: Code-as-Policy agents accomplish long-horizon embodied tasks by generating and executing code, yet continually improving them with teachers that are stronger but not globally reliable remains a key challenge. Existing distillation methods typically treat the teacher's complete behavior as the supervision target and thus misassign training credit on states where the teacher fails. We propose RE-0, a recursively verified policy improvement framework: rather than assuming that the teacher globally outperforms the student, RE-0 requests local corrections from the teacher on the student's own failure histories and checks in the environment whether each correction is genuinely beneficial; verified corrections yield immediate improvement. Building on this, we propose RE-OPD, which turns verified interventions into supervision for on-policy distillation. Only counterfactually verified teacher interventions provide distribution-level supervision, weighted by their measured local benefit, and the improvement they induce is projected back into the standalone student, so both where supervision is applied and how much credit the teacher receives co-evolve with the student policy. We further prove that the student's per-round gain is lower-bounded by its verified intervention gain up to verification and projection error terms. Experiments across multiple Code-as-Policy embodied tasks show that RE-0 improves both teacher-assisted execution and the standalone student, and generalizes to novel robots and scenes.

</details>


### [213] [Proactive Motion Planning for Human-Robot Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32354)
*Elena Basei,Edoardo Lamon,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将基于图的深度学习人体运动预测融入主动、动态的人类感知运动规划中，并结合静态路网与时变A*算法调整UR5e机械臂轨迹，以实现安全人机协作和主动避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现人类与机器人的安全协作，需要机器人能够预测人类运动，并据此进行主动、动态的运动规划，而非仅被动避障。

Method: 采用深度学习、基于图的人体运动预测模型，将其集成到规划框架中；该框架使用静态roadmap和时变A*算法修改UR5e机械臂的运动轨迹。

Result: 该方法显著改善了人机交互，并通过结合精确运动预测与自适应轨迹规划，实现了主动碰撞避免。

Conclusion: 将精确的人体运动预测与自适应轨迹规划相结合，可有效提升人类感知的动态运动规划能力，促进安全的人机协作。

Abstract: This abstract addresses the incorporation of human motion prediction into proactive and dynamic human-aware motion planning, with the goal of enabling safe collaboration between humans and robots. A deep learning, graph-based model is used to forecast human motion and is integrated into a planning framework. This framework employs a static roadmap along with a time-variant A* algorithm to modify the trajectory of a UR5e manipulator. This method greatly improves human-robot interaction and enables proactive collision avoidance by combining precise motion forecasts with adaptive trajectory planning.

</details>


### [214] [Human Motion Prediction for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32346)
*Placido Falqueto,Elena Basei,Edoardo Lamon,Giovanni Perantoni,Matteo Saveriano,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种人体运动预测方法——基于GMM的聚类、TransFusion与Graph-Mixer，并评估其在人机协作中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动预测可通过预判人的动作来改进机器人运动规划，从而提升动态环境中的安全性与效率，因此需要评估不同方法在人机协作中的适用性。

Method: 对基于GMM的聚类、TransFusion和Graph-Mixer三种人体运动预测方法进行比较与效果评估，场景面向人机协作。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据或定量结果，仅说明对这些方法在人机协作中的有效性进行了评估。

Conclusion: 这些预测方法有望用于改进机器人运动规划，增强人机协作的安全与效率；但摘要未指出哪种方法最优。

Abstract: This paper compares three human motion prediction methods - GMM-based clustering, TransFusion, and Graph-Mixer - evaluating their effectiveness in human-robot collaboration. These predictions are valuable for improving robot motion planning by anticipating human movements and enhancing safety and efficiency in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [215] [WSM-Aware HRI: An IoT-Enhanced Framework for Early Detection and Norm-Guided Repair of Failures with LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32336)
*Hanlin Zhang,Yuquan Wang,Tianwei Zhang,Zhenglong Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WSM-Aware HRI框架，将人机交互失败统一建模为人与机器人之间的世界状态不匹配（WSM），并在意图形成阶段主动检测，而非执行后恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互失败是机器人落地的主要障碍；既有工作多把失败视为孤立技术故障或关注事后恢复，但实际中许多故障源于人和机器人对当前世界状态的假设不一致。

Method: 构建IoT增强的模块化框架，将人类指令隐含假设与机器人基于多模态感知和数字增强建立的世界模型进行对齐；利用LLM显式化隐含假设、映射到少量不匹配类型，并规定需对照机器人世界状态验证的证据；将失败处理从执行时恢复前移到意图形成期的主动不匹配检测，以支持安全、规范合规、多用户协调和透明解释。

Result: 在十个涵盖视觉和潜在状态不匹配的日常案例中评估不匹配识别，系统能准确产生预期输出；消融实验表明，可靠识别依赖合适的 grounding 表示和面向验证的细化。

Conclusion: 将交互故障视为显式的世界状态不匹配，可更早发现潜在失败，并为把人机交互中的外部证据和社会约束整合进来提供原则性机制。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) failures remain a major barrier to deploying robots in real-world environments. Prior work often treats failures as isolated technical faults or focuses on post-hoc recovery behaviors. In practice, many breakdowns arise because humans and robots operate under inconsistent assumptions about the current world state. We propose WSM-Aware HRI, an IoT-enhanced modular framework that unifies diverse HRI breakdowns as World-State Mismatches (WSMs) between a human's instruction-implied assumptions and a robot's grounded world model built from multimodal perception and digital augmentation. A Large Language Model (LLM) is used to make implicit assumptions explicit, map them to a small set of mismatch types, and specify the evidence needed for verification against the robot's world state. WSM-Aware HRI shifts failure handling from execution-time recovery to proactive mismatch detection during intention formation, enabling interventions guided by safety, norm compliance, and multi-user coordination with transparent explanations. We evaluate mismatch identification in ten everyday cases spanning both visual and latent-state mismatches. The system can accurately produce the expected output results, and ablations show that reliable identification depends on appropriate grounding representations and verification-oriented refinement. These results indicate that treating interaction breakdowns as explicit world-state mismatches enables earlier detection of impending failures and offers a principled mechanism for integrating external evidence and social constraints into human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [216] [MemTransfer: Benchmarking Memory Beyond Matched Experience in Embodied Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32313)
*Haiming Tang,Xianjie Dai,Gujie Shao,Zuyi Guo,Jingguang Li,Kailang Ma,Yihong Tang,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MemTransfer 基准，在共享冻结 VLM 策略下比较 6 种记忆表示（1 种工作记忆基线与 5 种过去经验表示），系统评估具身导航智能体在条件变化时能否有效使用记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆能让具身智能体复用过去经验，但仅保留有用信息并不能保证在条件变化时能正确应用，因此需要同时评估存储信息及其在决策时的使用。

Method: MemTransfer 包含模拟仓库中 10 类任务、100 个导航案例，由专家演示提供历史；在共享冻结视觉-语言模型策略下比较 6 种记忆表示。三组比较分别改变起始位姿、路线可用性，以及历史数量与任务相关性。

Result: 每个任务 1 个演示时，Full-context 与 Episodic 在原始演示起点成功率为 95.3% 和 100.0%，但在新测试起点下降 48–49 个百分点。Summary 在两个测试起点间变化较小，但每任务 4 个演示时，路线阻断后仅保留其未阻断路线成功率的 39.3%，低于 Working memory 的 44.8% 和两种轨迹记忆的 56–58%。新测试起点下，相关演示从 1 增至 4 使 Episodic 成功率提升 14.3 个百分点，而其他表示提升不超过 1.3 个百分点；将一半相关历史替换为其他任务经验会降低两种轨迹记忆的成功率。

Conclusion: 对一种失配的鲁棒性并不代表对另一种失配也鲁棒；记忆评估应同时关注所存储信息及其在决策时的使用。

Abstract: Memory lets an embodied agent reuse past experience, yet retaining useful information does not ensure that the agent can apply it when conditions change. We present MemTransfer, a benchmark comparing six memory representations, a working-memory baseline and five representations of past experience, under a shared frozen vision-language-model policy. It comprises 100 navigation cases across ten task types in a simulated warehouse, with expert demonstrations supplying the history. Three comparisons vary the starting pose, route availability, and amount and task relevance of history. With one demonstration per task, Full-context and Episodic memory reach 95.3% and 100.0% success at the original demonstration start, but lose 48-49 percentage points at a new test start. Summary changes little between these two test starts, yet with four demonstrations per task it retains a smaller fraction of its unchanged-route success after blocking (39.3%) than Working memory (44.8%) or the two trajectory memories (56-58%). At the new test start, increasing from one to four relevant demonstrations raises Episodic success by 14.3 percentage points, while the other evaluated representations gain no more than 1.3 percentage points. Replacing half of the relevant histories with other-task experience lowers success for both trajectory memories. These results show that robustness to one kind of mismatch does not imply robustness to another, motivating evaluation of both stored information and its use at decision time.

</details>


### [217] [Affordance-Conditioned Decision Making: Bridging the Semantic-Spatial Gap in Zero-Shot Cross-Floor Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32292)
*Xuekang Yang,Lu Chen,Shuang Luo,Jialing Zhu,Qi Zhang,Yue Gao,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PACE，一种偏好精炼的可通行可供性条件执行模块，用于增强冻结的零样本语义规划器，实现可靠的跨楼层视觉语言导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航依赖通用语义规划器，但在楼梯、门道、狭窄通道等空间受限过渡场景中，正确的高层语义意图难以转化为可靠物理执行；到达过渡区域不等于能通过，智能体需识别可执行的可供性位姿并纠正累积动作误差。

Method: PACE将过渡相关语义落地为长时程、以智能体为中心的可通行可供性位姿，并以此空间目标为条件生成短时程动作，从而对齐语义目标与物理执行；进一步利用 rollout 衍生的偏好对进行失败感知偏好后训练，对比正常/恢复行为与偏离放大行为，提升闭环纠错能力。

Result: 将PACE集成到六个开源零样本VLN导航器中，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE跨楼层子集上取得一致提升：平均成功率分别从16.35%提升至27.65%，从4.76%提升至12.06%；真实世界实验也验证了其在未见环境中的适用性。

Conclusion: 可通行可供性能够桥接语义意图与可靠具身行为，PACE作为监督式局部执行模块可有效增强零样本语义规划器的跨楼层导航性能与闭环鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation increasingly relies on general-purpose semantic planners, yet translating correct high-level intent into reliable physical execution remains difficult in spatially constrained transitions. Reaching a staircase, doorway, or narrow passage does not ensure traversal; the agent must identify an executable affordance pose and recover from accumulated action errors. We propose PACE (Preference-refined Affordance-Conditioned Execution), a supervised local execution module that augments frozen zero-shot semantic planners for reliable cross-floor navigation. PACE grounds transition-related semantics into a long-horizon, agent-centric traversable affordance pose and conditions short-horizon action generation on this spatial target, thereby aligning semantic goals with physical execution. We further post-train PACE through failure-aware preference refinement using rollout-derived pairs that contrast normal or recovery behaviors with deviation-amplifying behaviors, thereby improving closed-loop correction. We integrate PACE into six open-source zero-shot VLN navigators and demonstrate consistent improvements on the cross-floor subsets of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, increasing the average success rate from 16.35% to 27.65% and from 4.76% to 12.06%, respectively. Real-world experiments further demonstrate PACE's applicability in unseen environments, highlighting the potential of traversable affordances to bridge semantic intent and reliable embodied behavior.

</details>


### [218] [DS-VLA: A Dendritic-inspired Vision-Language-Action Model for Robust Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32253)
*Yaxing Lyu,Jingyi Li,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DS-VLA 将树突脉冲动力学引入视觉-语言-动作（VLA）控制，通过多树突分支、异质可学习衰减和神经元级抑制门提升闭环动作扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在名义评估中表现良好，但执行动作短暂损坏时闭环鲁棒性不足；作者希望不依赖单纯扩大视觉-语言骨干或生成式动作解码器，而是从脑启发计算机制中寻找更稳健的架构先验。

Method: DS-VLA 为动作神经元配备多个稀疏连接的树突分支，各分支具有异质、可学习的衰减因子，以实现模块化特征处理与时间信息整合；同时引入神经元级抑制门，在胞体动力学之前自适应调节新多模态证据进入树突状态，抑制不可靠状态更新并保留任务相关历史信息。

Result: 在 LIBERO 四个套件上，DS-VLA 平均名义成功率为 91.6%，统一闭环动作扰动协议下平均扰动成功率为 87.35%，保留 95.4% 的名义性能；相同扰动设置下 OpenVLA-OFT、FAST、π0、GR00T 分别仅为 39.45%、23.90%、28.55%、30.75%。消融实验分离了神经元级共享抑制的贡献，神经动力学与扰动后轨迹分析表明鲁棒性与选择性证据抑制及有效行为恢复相关。

Conclusion: 将脑启发的树突脉冲与抑制门机制整合进 VLA 控制，可作为提升具身智能鲁棒性的有效架构先验，其价值超越单纯扩展视觉-语言骨干或生成式动作解码器。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in language-conditioned manipulation, yet success under nominal evaluation does not necessarily translate into robust closed-loop behavior when executed actions are transiently corrupted. We introduce DS-VLA, a dendritic-inspired action architecture that incorporates dendritic spiking dynamics into VLA control to address this limitation. Specifically, to enable modularized feature processing and temporal information integration, DS-VLA equips action neurons with multiple sparsely connected dendritic branches, each featuring heterogeneous, learned decay factors. Furthermore, to suppress unreliable state updates while preserving task-relevant historical information, we introduce a neuron-wise inhibitory gate that adaptively regulates the admission of new multimodal evidence into dendritic states prior to somatic dynamics. We evaluate DS-VLA on all four LIBERO suites under both nominal rollouts and a unified closed-loop action-perturbation protocol. DS-VLA achieves a 91.6\% average nominal success rate and an 87.35\% average perturbed success rate, retaining 95.4\% of its nominal performance. Under the same reported perturbation setting, OpenVLA-OFT, FAST, $\pi_0$, and GR00T achieve 39.45\%, 23.90\%, 28.55\%, and 30.75\%, respectively. A controlled ablation isolates the contribution of neuron-wise shared inhibition, while analyses of neural dynamics and post-perturbation trajectories associate robust performance with selective evidence suppression and effective behavioral recovery. Together, these results demonstrate that integrating brain-inspired computational mechanisms offers a promising architectural prior for robust embodied intelligence beyond merely scaling vision-language backbones or generative action decoders.

</details>


### [219] [Federated Subspace Guided Vision-Language-Action Policy Distillation for Non-IID Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32239)
*Biprodip Pal,Kaushik Roy,Yanming Zhu,Brendan Tidd,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FedDRMan是一种面向异构机器人操作的联邦子空间引导蒸馏框架，通过教师蒸馏、低秩多模态子空间与动作分布蒸馏，以及基于更新兼容性的聚类聚合和谱重平衡，缓解非IID导致的表示漂移与聚合破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人可在不集中训练演示的情况下通过联邦学习共同改进操作策略，但任务与环境的非IID分布会引起表示漂移和机器人策略更新互不兼容，使朴素参数聚合具有破坏性。

Method: 每轮通信中，服务器模型作为冻结教师用于本地行为克隆；同时进行低秩多模态子空间蒸馏和动作分布蒸馏，以保留全局有用的表示几何与策略行为。针对异构聚合，按更新兼容性对客户端分组，并为每个簇维护持续模型；服务器再对每个兼容聚合进行谱重平衡，以缓解较弱任务相关策略更新方向的衰减。

Result: 在LIBERO上跨多种非IID设置、异构程度和客户端参与变化的实验，以及消融和聚合分析表明，FedDRMan显著提升知识迁移并持续优于强联邦基线，峰值平均成功率达80.7%，比最强评估联邦基线高11.6个百分点。

Conclusion: FedDRMan能有效应对异构机器人操作中的非IID联邦学习挑战，通过子空间引导蒸馏、兼容性聚类和谱重平衡实现更优的知识迁移与策略性能。

Abstract: Federated learning offers a natural way for multiple robots to jointly improve manipulation policies without requiring centralized access to training demonstrations. However, non-IID task and environment distributions can induce representation drift and mutually incompatible robot-policy updates, making naive parameter aggregation destructive. We present FedDRMan, a federated subspace-guided distillation framework for heterogeneous robot manipulation. At each communication round, the server model provides a frozen teacher for local behavior cloning, while low-rank multimodal subspace and action-distribution distillation preserve globally useful representation geometry and policy behavior. To address heterogeneous aggregation, FedDRMan groups clients by update compatibility and maintains a persistent model for each cluster. The server then spectrally rebalances each compatible aggregate to mitigate attenuation of weaker task-relevant robot-policy update directions. Extensive experiments on LIBERO across diverse non-IID settings, heterogeneity levels, client participation variation, together with ablations and aggregation analyses, show that FedDRMan substantially improves knowledge transfer and consistently outperforms strong federated baselines achieving a peak mean success rate of 80.7%, 11.6 percentage points above the strongest evaluated federated baseline.

</details>


### [220] [RoboFFT: Finetuning generative robot policy via online reinforcement learning with forward process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32236)
*Yu Li,Shenghe Hu,Yuhan Wang,Yaoxiang Pu,Haotong Zhang,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFFT，一种基于前向加噪与加权 score/flow matching 损失构建代理策略比率的强化学习微调框架，用于提升扩散/流式生成式机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略能通过模仿学习捕捉多模态动作分布，但常受不完美演示和分布偏移影响，进一步改进通常依赖额外专家数据；强化学习可通过环境交互改进策略，但生成式策略因似然估计难处理而难以有效微调。

Method: 对采样动作施加前向加噪，利用加权 score/flow matching 损失构造代理策略比率，从而支持 PPO 式更新，实现生成式机器人策略的前向过程强化学习微调。

Result: 在长时程规划和稀疏奖励等代表性仿真基准上，使用多种主流生成式机器人策略评估，RoboFFT 一致提升性能，并具有更好的稳定性和训练效率；进一步集成到真实世界 RL 框架，在真实任务中有效。

Conclusion: RoboFFT 为生成式机器人策略提供了一种可行且高效的强化学习微调方案，可缓解模仿学习局限，并适用于仿真与真实世界任务。

Abstract: Generative models, such as diffusion and flow-based models, have shown strong promise for robot policy learning by capturing complex and multimodal action distributions from demonstrations. However, policies trained solely with imitation learning often suffer from imperfect demonstrations and distributional shifts, while further improvement typically requires additional expert data. Reinforcement learning offers a natural solution through environment interaction, but effectively finetuning generative robot policies remains challenging due to the intractability of likelihood estimation. In this work, we propose RoboFFT, a forward-process reinforcement learning framework for finetuning generative robot policies, which applies forward noising to sampled actions and uses the weighted score / flow matching loss to construct a surrogate policy ratio for PPO-style updates. We evaluate RoboFFT with popular generative robot policies on representative simulation benchmarks, including long-horizon planning and sparse reward settings. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate that RoboFFT consistently improves performance while achieving better stability and training efficiency. We further integrate RoboFFT into a real world RL framework and demonstrate its effectiveness in real world tasks. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [221] [FutureRay: Control-Aligned Future Range for Agile Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32158)
*Tianhao Zang,Shanze Wang,Ziqian Wang,Liyou Luo,Zihan Liu,Xingjian Xie,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FutureRay，通过联合预测未来距离范围与遭遇风险，提升四足机器人对移动障碍物的避障导航性能，且无需重新训练运动控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动障碍物可能阻塞原先畅通的路径，而四足机器人执行运动指令时需要预判间隙变化；同时，运动选择应考虑机器人足印以及反应和制动所需时间。

Method: FutureRay 基于深度导出的距离历史和可观测机器人运动，预测不同观察方向与未来时刻的距离范围及遭遇风险；训练强调近期间隙并惩罚高估可用空间的误差。局部规划器对候选朝向查询同一预测，并与当前观测结合，检查机器人足印周围间隙；基于模型的反制-制动限制指导速度选择，输出速度指令给固定的运动策略。

Result: 在60个静态与动态仿真场景的配对评估中，FutureRay 完成率为93.3%，优于当前距离持续法的75.0%和笛卡尔卡尔曼推演的80.0%，且感知、规划与运动控制保持一致；碰撞次数也少于两个基线。物理四足机器人定性试验显示，当障碍物接近路径时即启动避让，随后恢复目标推进。

Conclusion: 联合距离范围与遭遇风险预测可改善避障能力，并无需重新训练运动控制策略。

Abstract: Moving obstacles can block a previously clear route while a quadruped robot executes a motion command. We investigate whether predicting changing clearance improves navigation when motion selection accounts for the robot footprint and the time needed to react and brake. We present FutureRay, which predicts ranges across viewing directions and future times, together with encounter risk, from depth-derived range history and observable robot motion. Training emphasizes near-term clearance and penalizes errors that overstate available space. A local planner queries the same forecast for candidate headings and combines it with current observations to check clearance around the robot footprint. Model-based reaction--braking limits guide speed selection, and the resulting velocity commands are passed to a fixed locomotion policy. In paired evaluations on 60 static and dynamic simulation scenes, FutureRay achieves 93.3% completion, compared with 75.0% for current-range persistence and 80.0% for Cartesian Kalman rollout, with perception, planning, and locomotion held fixed. FutureRay also records fewer collisions than both baselines. Qualitative trials on a physical quadruped show avoidance initiated while an obstacle is approaching the route, followed by renewed goal progress. These results show that joint range and encounter-risk prediction can improve obstacle avoidance without retraining the locomotion policy.

</details>


### [222] [AquaBEV-Nav: Learned BEV Occupancy for Underwater Navigation and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32156)
*Trung Tien Dong,Zhenqi Wu,Sahasra Kondapalli,Jiayi Wu,Yi Sheng,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaBEV-Nav 提出一种不依赖显式单目深度估计的水下探索框架，通过直接预测鸟瞰图占据栅格来提升水下建图与导航可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下安全探索需要机器人理解周围结构位置及可通行区域。现有基于视觉的水下探索系统通常先估计单目深度，再反投影到3D并累积为2D鸟瞰图占据地图；但水下散射和波长相关衰减会破坏视觉线索，使单目深度估计尤其不可靠。

Method: AquaBEV-Nav 基于 CORAL 分层探索框架，用 AquaBEV 替换其基于深度的感知前端。给定单帧 RGB，AquaBEV 将视觉特征映射到学习到的极坐标表示，沿距离维度进行因果推理，并直接重建局部笛卡尔占据，无需中间深度预测。所得占据图累积到 CORAL 的持久空间记忆中，为基于 VLM 的高层规划提供空间上下文，并为动力学感知的局部轨迹生成提供碰撞约束。

Result: 在10个模拟珊瑚礁环境和6种占据主干网络、统一评估协议下，AquaBEV-Nav 达到结构 IoU 37.48、目标 IoU 53.2、闭环覆盖率 88.95%，且零碰撞。

Conclusion: 结果表明，直接进行 BEV 占据预测可规避水下单目深度估计的不可靠性，为安全有效的水下探索与导航提供可行方案。

Abstract: Safe underwater exploration requires a robot to understand where surrounding structures are located and which regions are available for motion. Existing vision-based underwater exploration systems commonly obtain this information indirectly by estimating monocular depth, unprojecting the geometry into 3D space, and accumulating it into a 2D bird's-eye-view occupancy map. This reliance on intermediate depth estimation is particularly problematic underwater, where scattering and wavelength-dependent attenuation degrade visual cues and limit the reliability of monocular depth estimates. We introduce AquaBEV-Nav, an underwater exploration framework that bypasses explicit monocular depth estimation through direct bird's-eye-view occupancy prediction. Built upon the CORAL hierarchical exploration framework, AquaBEV-Nav replaces its depth-based perception front end with AquaBEV. Given a single RGB frame, AquaBEV maps visual features into a learned polar representation, performs causal reasoning along the range dimension, and reconstructs local Cartesian occupancy without relying on intermediate depth prediction. The resulting occupancy map is accumulated into CORAL's persistent spatial memory, providing spatial context for VLM-based high-level planning and collision constraints for dynamics-aware local trajectory generation. Across ten simulated reef environments and six occupancy backbones evaluated under a single protocol, AquaBEV-Nav reaches 37.48 structure IoU and 53.2 target IoU, 88.95% closed-loop coverage with zero collisions.

</details>


### [223] [RecastVLA: From Past Interaction to Future Control with Adaptive Policy States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32155)
*Wenbo Li,Jun Yang,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RecastVLA，在 flow-matching 视觉-语言-动作策略中通过动作侧测试时训练维护持久自适应策略状态，利用共享 fast weights 跨动作生成深度与流评估更新状态，提升多基准和真实机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序操作要求机器人追踪已发生但当前场景可能不再揭示的历史；显式历史表示可为当前决策提供上下文，但如何让动作生成本身形成后续控制所需的持久状态仍待研究。

Method: 基于动作侧 test-time training，在 flow-matching VLA 策略中维护由共享 fast weights 表示的自适应策略状态，并在整个动作生成过程中保持固定；深度特定接口读取同一状态，跨深度与流评估的特征联合定义下一次策略调用的更新。后续动作损失通过反向传播穿过早期状态转移，训练初始化、接口和更新规则；部署时仅使用策略自身动作生成特征更新，无需专家动作标签。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin、RoboDojo 和 12 个真实机器人任务上，RecastVLA 相比未使用测试时训练的匹配策略提升平均成功率，其中 RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean 提升 10.68 个百分点；受控 RoboTwin 比较表明保留状态可提升成功率，共享设计比独立训练的分层局部 TTT 高 2.58 个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作生成过程本身可作为持久策略状态的来源；共享 fast weights 的测试时更新能有效改善顺序操作控制，且部署时无需专家动作标签。

Abstract: Sequential manipulation requires a robot to track what has already happened, even when the current scene no longer reveals it. Policies with explicit history representations make past interactions available as context for current decisions. We ask how action generation itself can form a persistent state for subsequent control. Building on action-side test-time training, RecastVLA maintains an adaptive policy state within a flow-matching vision-language-action policy. The state is represented by shared fast weights and remains fixed throughout action generation. Depth-specific interfaces read the same state, while features across depths and flow evaluations jointly define one update for the next policy call. Subsequent action losses train the initialization, interfaces, and update rule by differentiating through earlier state transitions. At deployment, updates use the policy's own action-generation features without expert action labels. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin, RoboDojo, and twelve real-robot tasks, RecastVLA improves mean success over a matched policy trained without test-time training, including 10.68 percentage points on RoboTwin Clean-to-Clean. In controlled RoboTwin comparisons, retaining state improves success, and the shared design exceeds independently trained layer-local TTT by 2.58 points.

</details>


### [224] [GAUGE: Planner-Conditioned Active Calibration of Opaque Quadruped Velocity Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32154)
*Tianhao Zang,Zihan Liu,Shanze Wang,Liyou Luo,Xingjian Xie,Chengtai Li,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GAUGE 框架，面向不透明四足速度接口，在规划器相关命令分布下进行目标感知、不确定性引导的主动标定；以更少试验达到任务精度与不确定性联合准则，并在 Isaac Sim 导航地图上不劣于密集标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用四足通常只暴露平面速度指令，底层运动控制器不可访问，导致指令与实际运动之间存在系统性偏差。导航规划器通常只使用指令包络的一个结构化子集，因此需要面向任务、规划器条件的高效标定，而非均匀或任务无关的探索。

Method: GAUGE 维护贝叶斯命令-运动模型，依据规划器诱导命令分布下后验认知不确定性的期望减少量来选择授权试验。所得后验支持验证式停止、有界逆补偿，以及在检测到接口漂移后进行任务相关重标定。

Result: 在三种受控响应族中，GAUGE 比被动、D-最优和任务无关替代方案用更少试验达到任务面向精度与不确定性的联合准则。在六个留出的 Isaac Sim 导航地图上，其满足相对于密集标定的预设非劣效性边界。代码已开源。

Conclusion: GAUGE 能对不透明四足速度接口进行高效、任务相关且可验证的主动标定，在减少试验成本的同时保持导航性能，并支持接口变化后的重标定。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a Goal-Aware Uncertainty-Guided Exploration (GAUGE) framework for planner-conditioned active calibration of opaque quadruped velocity interfaces. Commercial quadrupeds commonly expose planar-velocity commands, but the underlying locomotion controller remains inaccessible and can produce systematic discrepancies between commanded and realized motion. A navigation planner typically uses a structured subset of the command envelope. GAUGE maintains a Bayesian command-to-motion model and selects authorized trials according to their expected reduction of posterior epistemic uncertainty under the planner-induced command distribution. The resulting posterior supports validation-based stopping, bounded inverse compensation, and task-relevant recalibration after detected interface shifts. In three controlled response families, GAUGE reaches the joint criterion for task-facing accuracy and uncertainty with fewer trials than passive, D-optimal, and task-agnostic alternatives. Across six held-out Isaac Sim navigation maps, it meets the declared noninferiority margins against dense calibration. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [225] [Residual Denoising Enables Sample-Efficient Multi-Agent Coordination on Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32129)
*Dayi Dong,Maulik Bhatt,Aayushi Shrivastava,Lasse Peters,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ALTER 将预训练单智能体扩散策略以少量协作数据适配为多智能体策略，在需要时协调、单独时保持原有单智能体技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略具备较强操作能力，但通常局限于单智能体场景；本文研究如何用最少协作数据将单智能体扩散策略适配到多智能体，并同时优化协调性能与单智能体技能保留。

Method: 提出 ALTER：训练一个协调头，预测残差去噪器，在需要时将单智能体行为转化为协调的多智能体行为，同时保留单智能体能力；执行去中心化，每个机器人仅依赖自身视觉观测，无需显式通信；训练时用基础策略生成的自蒸馏数据增强少量协作演示。

Result: 在仿真中，ALTER 相比基线取得更高的协调成功率，并显著更好地保留源技能；硬件实验呈现相似趋势，ALTER 在协调成功与单智能体技能保留之间实现了更好的协同优化。

Conclusion: ALTER 表明可用少量协作数据将预训练单智能体扩散策略有效适配为按需协调的多智能体策略，并在去中心化视觉执行下兼顾协调与单智能体能力保留。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies offer strong manipulation skills but are typically limited to single-agent settings, where a robot acts in isolation. In this work, we study how to adapt pretrained single-agent diffusion policies to multi-agent settings using minimal collaborative data, co-optimizing for two key objectives: high coordination performance and single-agent skill retention. To this end, we introduce ALTER, an adaptation method for coordination on demand: the adapted policy coordinates with other robots when deployed in a team while remaining capable of acting independently when operating alone. Execution is decentralized: each robot acts only on its own visual observations, without explicit inter-agent communication. Our method trains a coordination head that predicts a residual denoiser to transform single-agent behavior into coordinated multi-agent behavior when necessary while also preserving single-agent capabilities. To preserve single-agent capabilities, we augment a small number of collaborative demonstrations with self-distilled data generated by the base policy during training of the residual denoiser. In simulation,ALTER achieves higher coordination success over our baselines while retaining much higher source-skill retention. In our hardware experiments, we find similar trends where ALTER better co-optimizes for coordination success and single-agent skill retention than the baselines

</details>


### [226] [Active 3D weaves for load-bearing and damage-resilient locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32099)
*Guowei Wayne Tu,Evgueni T. Filipov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出将活性材料集成到三维编织壳中的通用框架，构建兼具高轴向承载刚度、低弯曲驱动刚度和系统级损伤容限的编织机器人，并实现承载自重70倍的运动与极端压缩后性能可重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编织/纺织类系统柔顺、安全，适合软体机器人、假肢、可穿戴和外骨骼等人机交互场景，但其固有柔性限制了承载能力；因此需要一种能同时实现承载、高效驱动和损伤容忍的机器人结构设计方法。

Method: 将活性材料集成到三维（3D）编织壳中，以“woven corners”作为3D编织结构的基本模块进行模块化组装；通过特征值计算识别3D编织结构的承载与驱动机制，并据此设计五种能够运动的机器人。

Result: 所提出的3D编织机器人可携带相当于自身重量70倍的载荷运动，并在经历极端压缩后仍保持可重复的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来三维编织机器人系统的设计、制造与仿真提供了路径，可同时满足承载、高刚度、主动功能变形、运动以及系统级韧性等需求。

Abstract: The craft of weaving, where different materials are interlaced, has tremendous potential for creating active and functional systems for use in soft robots, prosthetics, wearables, exoskeletons, and more. These textile-like systems are flexible and safe for human-machine interaction; however, this inherent flexibility limits their ability to carry loads, which is essential for many robotic functions. In this work, we introduce a general framework for integrating active materials into three-dimensional (3D) woven shells to create robotic structures that combine high axial stiffness for load bearing, low bending stiffness for efficient actuation, and system-level resilience for damage tolerance. These woven robots can be modularly assembled from 'woven corners', a fundamental unit of 3D woven structures. We use eigenvalue calculations to identify load bearing and actuation mechanisms of the 3D woven structures, and use that information to make five different robots capable of locomotion. We demonstrate that these 3D woven robots can locomote carrying loads 70 times their self-weight, and can maintain repeatable performance even after being subjected to extreme compression. This work is a pathway toward the design, manufacturing, and simulation of future 3D woven robotic systems where load bearing, high stiffness, active functional deformation, locomotion, and system-level resilience are all needed.

</details>


### [227] [Fiber-Normalized Manipulability and Determinant Proxies: Intrinsic Redundancy Optimization Across and Within Task Fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32079)
*Antonio Franchi,Mirko Mizzoni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明基于行列式的可操作度是固定任务冗余优化的精确目标，并提出纤维归一化可操作度，以实现跨任务纤维的内在、度量无关轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可操作度依赖任务坐标与任务空间度量，导致固定任务优化中的目标等价性不明确，且跨任务纤维比较与轨迹优化缺乏内在客观指标。

Method: 在每条规则任务纤维上，比较行列式代理、任意任务图表示及度量补全可操作度；提出纤维归一化可操作度，即内部状态能力除以同一纤维上最优能力，并构造相应损失用于跨纤维轨迹优化，包括任务演化给定或自由及时间耦合问题。

Result: 同一纤维上各可操作度仅差正常数，因而诱导相同的完全排序、约束极值、梯度方向、临界点与局部最优分类；纤维归一化量无量纲，对内部状态与任务流形坐标变换不变，且独立于任务空间度量。平面机械臂与冗余气动分配示例验证了固定纤维等价性、跨纤维任务依赖差异及不变轨迹优化。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了行列式可操作度在固定任务冗余优化中的精确性，并提供纤维归一化可操作度作为跨纤维轨迹优化的内在、度量无关目标。

Abstract: This work establishes that determinant-based manipulability is an exact objective for fixed-task redundancy optimization, despite its dependence on task coordinates and the choice of task-space metric used for volume measurement. On every regular task fiber, the determinant proxy, its representation in any task chart, and every metric-completed manipulability differ only by positive constants. They consequently induce the same complete ordering, constrained extrema, gradient directions, critical points, and local optimality classifications. For comparisons and trajectory optimization across task fibers, this work introduces fiber-normalized manipulability: the capability attained at an internal state divided by the best capability available on the same fiber. The resulting dimensionless scalar is invariant under coordinate changes on the internal-state and task manifolds and independent of the task-space metric. Its associated loss provides an intrinsic objective for physically admissible cross-fiber trajectories, including problems with prescribed or free task evolution and temporal coupling. Planar-manipulator and redundant aerodynamic-allocation examples demonstrate fixed-fiber equivalence, task-dependent cross-fiber differences, and invariant fiber-normalized trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [228] [Find Something You Can't Do: Agentic Real-World Reinforcement Learning for Self-Improving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32069)
*Yuan Fang,Zechu Li,Haolei Tong,Puze Liu,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIND 是一个面向真实世界机器人操作的自主强化学习框架，通过视觉-语言代理进行场景理解、弱点感知任务选择与自评估，使冻结的 VLA 策略能在持续工作空间中自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型为机器人操作提供了强先验，但通常以冻结策略部署，无法从自身失败中改进；真实世界强化学习虽可继续提升，却受限于人工重置环境和任务成功监督，难以实现自主学习。

Method: FIND 将自主练习重构为场景条件化、性能感知的任务选择问题：每次 rollout 后不恢复预定义场景，而是基于当前场景决定下一步练习任务。视觉-语言代理从预定义任务库中识别可行任务，优先选择近期成功率较低的任务，并利用执行前后成对观测评估结果。系统以冻结的 π_{0.5} VLA 与残差离策略 RL 实例化。

Result: 在八项真实世界操作任务中，独立人工评估的成功率从 55% 提升至 71.9%。一次代表性运行在 6 小时交互内完成 456 个自主回合，仅需 30 次场景恢复干预，且在线学习期间无需人工奖励标签。消融与系统评估进一步考察了关键设计选择、代理评估准确性和人工干预需求。

Conclusion: FIND 表明，结合视觉-语言代理的任务选择、自评估与残差离策略强化学习，可让冻结 VLA 策略在真实世界中持续自主改进，并显著减少人工重置与奖励标注需求。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models provide strong priors for robotic manipulation but are typically deployed as frozen policies, unable to improve from their own failures. Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) offers a path to continued improvement, yet manual environment resets and task-success supervision hinder autonomous learning. We introduce \textbf{FIND}, an agentic real-world RL framework that closes the loop between scene understanding, weakness-aware practice, self-evaluation, and policy improvement in a persistent workspace. FIND reframes autonomous practice as a scene-conditioned, performance-aware task-selection problem: instead of restoring a predefined scene after each rollout, it uses the resulting scene to determine what to practice next. A vision--language agent identifies feasible tasks from a predefined library, prioritizes those with lower recent success rates, and evaluates outcomes using paired pre- and post-execution observations. We instantiate FIND with a frozen $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA and residual off-policy RL. Across eight real-world manipulation tasks, the independent human-assessed success rate improves from $55\%$ to $71.9\%$. A representative run completes 456 autonomous episodes within 6 hours of interaction, requiring 30 scene-recovery interventions and no human-provided reward labels during online learning. Ablations and systematic evaluations further examine key design choices, agent evaluation accuracy, and human intervention requirements. Our website is made publicly available at: this http URL .

</details>


### [229] [Grasp2Twist: Learning Bimanual Dexterous Jar Opening by Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32064)
*Mo Xu,Yunfu Deng,Jianuo Wang,Josiah Hanna,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Grasp2Twist——一个基于强化学习的双臂灵巧操作系统，可学习抓取并拧开罐盖；通过几何包围度引导抓取与过渡，并用三阶段课程促进手指接触重构，实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，在六种家用容器上达到 88% 任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该任务面临三个挑战：为多阶段任务学习统一策略、维持罐盖持续旋转，以及仿真到现实迁移。固定接触下手指运动学限制会阻碍持续拧转，因此需要手指接触重构。

Method: 引入连续包围度测度引导抓取形成，二值包围指示器引导抓取到拧转的过渡；二者由物体中心与手掌及指尖构成的凸包之间的几何关系推导。采用三阶段课程促进接触变化探索，并提升 sim-to-real 鲁棒性。

Result: 学习到的策略展现手指步态（finger gaiting），通过重配置手-物接触维持罐盖旋转；零样本迁移到物理系统，在六种家用容器（包括花生酱、维生素和速溶咖啡罐）上取得 88% 成功率。消融实验验证了几何包围度在抓取形成中的作用，以及课程在接触重配置探索中的作用。

Conclusion: Grasp2Twist 通过几何引导与课程学习有效统一多阶段策略、实现持续拧转并完成零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，表明该方法可用于双臂灵巧开罐任务，且几何包围度与课程设计是关键因素。

Abstract: This paper presents Grasp2Twist, a bimanual dexterous manipulation system that learns to grasp and twist open jar lids using reinforcement learning. Learning this task raises three challenges: learning a unified policy for a multi-stage task, sustaining lid twisting, and sim-to-real transfer. To address the first challenge, we introduce a continuous enclosure measure to guide grasp formation and a binary enclosure indicator to guide the grasp-to-twist transition for unified policy learning. We derive both from the geometric relationship between the object center and the convex hull formed by the hand's palm and fingertips. Kinematic constraints limit how far the hand can rotate the lid with fixed contacts, so sustained twisting requires finger contact reconfiguration. We use a three-stage curriculum to facilitate exploration of these contact changes and also improve robustness for sim-to-real transfer. With our approach, the learned policy demonstrates finger gaiting, reconfiguring hand-object contacts to sustain lid rotation. It transfers zero-shot to the physical system and achieves an 88% task success rate across six household containers, including peanut-butter, vitamin, and instant-coffee jars. Ablations further validate the roles of the geometric enclosure in grasp formation and the curriculum in contact-reconfiguration exploration.

</details>


### [230] [Low Cost Eye Tracking for Vision Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31941)
*Maissa Abir Smaili,Yaman Ismaiel,Zafer Iscan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工程研究比较了网络摄像头与GC308近红外眼动管线在视觉筛查中的注视辅助数据采集；结果显示GC308数据仅反映采集稳定性，且因显示参数缺失，logMAR值不能作为视力指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估在视觉筛查中利用注视辅助数据采集的工程可行性，并比较两种眼动追踪实现方式的数据采集表现。

Method: 10名被试在两种采集条件下完成Tumbling E方向判断。行为指标（Tumbling E方向准确率、应用logMAR值、反应潜伏期）由键盘反应确定。GC308采用Orlosky眼动管线，有效采样率约8 FPS，诊断指标为相邻归一化注视向量间夹角的帧间变化（28.2°），未进行屏幕目标校准或注视到屏幕映射；网络摄像头管线使用25点屏幕校准。

Result: GC308的帧间注视向量夹角变化为28.2°，有效采样率约8 FPS。由于未进行屏幕目标校准与注视—屏幕映射，GC308数据仅代表采集稳定性，而非相对目标的注视准确度。因显示分辨率和像素间距未保留，应用logMAR值不能视为视力测量。

Conclusion: 该研究目前不能用于视觉筛查中的视力评估；若要将注视辅助采集用于视力筛查，需进行屏幕目标校准、注视到屏幕映射并保留显示参数。

Abstract: This engineering study assesses gaze-assisted data acquisition during visual screening with a Gaze Quest implementation via webcam and GC308 near infrared camera with an Orlosky eye tracking pipeline. Ten subjects performed under both acquisition conditions. Accuracy of Tumbling E orientation, reported application logMAR value,and response latency were determined from keyboard responses and hence are behavioral outputs as opposed toeye tracker screening results. Frame-to-frame change in theangle between successive normalized gaze vectors (28.2degrees) served as the GC308 eye tracker derived diagnostic with an effective sampling rate of roughly 8 FPS. No screen target calibration or gaze to screen translation was done on the GC308 eye tracker output data for the current analysis and hence, these data represent acquisition stability and not gaze accuracy in relation to the target. The webcam pipeline relied on 25-point screen calibration. Because the display resolution and pixel pitch could not be retained in the records of the study, the reported application logMAR values cannot be viewed as measures of visual acuity in any way.

</details>


### [231] [Inertia-Corrected Newton Method For Generalized Nash Equilibria in Dynamic Games with Optimality Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31929)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于约束动态博弈的快速牛顿求解器，通过KKT矩阵惯性高效验证局部广义纳什均衡（GNE）的二阶充分条件，并用惯性校正更新提升收敛性，在数值基准与微型自主赛车实验中验证了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有牛顿法通过求解KKT必要条件快速获得动态博弈中的GNE，可支持高动态机器人多智能体模型预测控制（MPC）；但KKT残差小并不能保证解满足局部GNE的二阶充分条件（SOSC），因此需要高效的最优性验证方法。

Method: 建立智能体KKT矩阵的惯性与代价函数约化Hessian在约束零空间上正定性之间的联系，提出验证局部GNE的SOSC的高效数值方法；进一步引入惯性校正更新步骤，通过 destabilizing 弱跨智能体耦合下的严格鞍点来改善向局部GNE的收敛。

Result: 数值基准展示了所提求解器在多智能体规划问题中的运行时间与收敛性能；物理实验使用微型自主赛车平台验证了求解器的实时能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为约束动态博弈提供了一种兼具快速求解与最优性检查的牛顿求解器，能够支持实时多智能体MPC与高动态机器人规划，并提升对局部GNE的收敛可靠性。

Abstract: Newton methods efficiently find Generalized Nash Equilibria (GNE) in dynamic games by solving for the KKT necessary conditions. These methods are fast and can support multi-agent Model Predictive Control (MPC) for highly dynamic robots. However, a small KKT residual alone does not certify that the returned solution satisfies the second-order sufficient conditions for a local GNE. In this paper, we propose an efficient numerical method to verify the second-order sufficient conditions (SOSC) for a local GNE. We connect the inertia of the agent KKT matrix with the positive definiteness of the reduced Hessian of the cost function, projected onto the null space of the constraints. Furthermore, we introduce an inertia-corrected update step that improves convergence to local GNEs by destabilizing strict saddle points with weak cross-agent coupling. Our main contribution is a fast Newton solver for Constrained Dynamic Games that provides efficient optimality checking. Through numerical benchmarks, we demonstrate the proposed solver's runtime and convergence performance in practical multi-agent planning problems. We also validate the solver's real-time capabilities in physical experiments using a platform of miniature autonomous race cars.

</details>


### [232] [HapticWorld: an Interactive World Simulator with Real-time Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31924)
*Shaoting Peng,Litian Liang,Yixuan Wang,Ming Yang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HapticWorld：一种交互式世界模拟器，能同时预测观测与关节力矩，并将力矩实时反馈给操作者，从而在学习型世界模型中闭合人类触觉回路，用于接触丰富操作的数据采集、策略训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作依赖力传感，而力信号难以仅从视觉推断，无论是采集示范还是训练策略都受限。带力标注的数据难以规模化获取：真机采集依赖物理硬件，物理模拟器的接触力与真实测量存在系统性偏差，学习型世界模拟器虽可扩展且真实但仅支持视觉，导致采集时操作者无触觉反馈且数据缺少力/力矩（F/T）标签。

Method: HapticWorld 作为交互式世界模拟器，联合预测观测与关节力矩，并将预测力矩实时渲染反馈给操作者，形成人类与学习世界模型之间的触觉闭环。该闭环用于采集带 F/T 标签的示范数据，并支持基于 F/T 条件的策略训练与独立评估。

Result: 在三个接触丰富任务中，力矩反馈使数据采集吞吐平均提升 1.6 倍。使用 HapticWorld 生成示范训练的策略在真实世界 60 次试验中成功 54 次，接近真实数据上界 56/60，并远超仅视觉基线的 19/60。此外，HapticWorld 内部评估的成功率与真实世界评估高度一致。

Conclusion: HapticWorld 能有效规模化获取力标注示范并训练接触丰富操作策略，且其内部成功率与真实评估匹配，可作为独立的 F/T 条件策略评估平台。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation depends on force sensing that is hard to infer from visual signals alone, both for collecting demonstrations and for training policies. Force-annotated data, however, remains hard to obtain at scale: real-robot collection ties every demonstration to physical hardware, physics simulators report contact forces that deviate systematically from real measurements, and learned world simulators, though scalable and realistic, are vision-only, so operators feel nothing during data collection and the data carries no force/torque (F/T) labels. We present HapticWorld, an interactive world simulator that predicts joint torque together with observations and renders it back to the operator in real time, closing the haptic loop between a human and a learned world model. Across three contact-rich tasks, torque feedback raises data collection throughput by 1.6 times on average. Policies trained on HapticWorld-generated demonstrations succeed in 54/60 real-world trials, approaching the 56/60 upper bound of real-world data, and far exceeding the 19/60 success rate of the vision-only baseline. Moreover, the success rates measured inside HapticWorld closely match real-world evaluation, demonstrating that HapticWorld can serve as a stand-alone F/T-conditioned policy evaluation platform.

</details>


### [233] [End-to-end QP-based policies: A unified perspective on robust control and robot learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31905)
*Fausto Vega,Priyanka Supraja Balaji,Chase Dunaway,Joe Koszut,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于二次规划(QP)的端到端策略框架，以最少领域设计实现系统化策略构建，同时保持基于模型控制的透明性与可解释性，并支持鲁棒控制、自动调参和未知动力学控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型控制可解释但依赖领域建模，而端到端学习策略灵活却常为黑箱且需大量设计。作者希望统一二者，提供可解释、少依赖领域知识且系统化的策略表示与构建方法。

Method: 提出QP式策略表示，将策略参数化为二次规划问题，支持两类构建：一是域随机化的基于模型自动调优，在干扰和模型变化的分布上优化策略参数；二是黑盒策略构建，针对可能未知的模型进行形式化，无需显式状态、输入或系统动力学。建立其与鲁棒控制、多层感知机(MLP)及机器人学习的联系，并将其视为这些方法的共同抽象。

Result: 在仿真和硬件上验证该框架，展示其在鲁棒性、自动策略调优以及未知系统动力学下控制等广泛应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为现有策略表示与控制范式的统一抽象，在保持可解释性的同时实现端到端QP策略构建，并具备跨应用的通用性。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end QP-based policy framework that enables systematic policy construction with minimal domain-specific design, while preserving the transparency and interpretability of model-based control. The proposed policy representation supports both domain-randomized model-based auto-tuning, where policy parameters are optimized over distributions of disturbances and model variations, and black-box policy construction, where the problem is formulated in terms of a (possibly) unknown model without requiring explicit notions of states, inputs, or the underlying system dynamics. We establish connections to existing policy representations and control paradigms, including robust control, multilayer perceptrons, and robot learning, and interpret our end-to-end QP policies as a common abstraction of these approaches. We validate the resulting framework in both simulation and hardware, demonstrating a broad range of applications spanning robustness, automatic policy tuning, and control under unknown system dynamics.

</details>


### [234] [GT-VLA: Target-Conditioned Trace Guidance for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31904)
*Ninghan Zhong,Jing-Chen Peng,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GT-VLA，一种可引导框架：利用外部通用 VLM 生成 2D 视觉轨迹，并将其作为动作策略的条件输入，以提升机器人操作的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在未见任务、配置和长时程场景中泛化能力不足，容易过拟合训练场景且无法遵循新语言指令；而通用 VLM 虽具备更强常识与泛化能力，却不能直接控制机器人动作。

Method: GT-VLA 通过外部通用 VLM 识别当前技能的语义引导，将其转换为 2D 视觉轨迹，并以轨迹渲染后的观察作为动作策略的条件。该设计分离语义目标获取、轨迹生成和底层动作执行，使高层引导可传递至机器人动作；同时采用 Mixture-of-Experts 架构，包含技能特定的轨迹模块和动作模块以增强执行鲁棒性。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准和真实机器人平台上评估，GT-VLA 相较近期 VLA 基线均取得更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: GT-VLA 将通用 VLM 的常识推理与 VLA 的动作控制相结合，通过轨迹条件生成实现可引导的机器人操作，并验证了其泛化优势；代码和补充材料已公开于项目网站。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong performance on robotic manipulation, but they often struggle to generalize to unseen tasks, configurations, and long-horizon settings. A key challenge is that VLAs overfit to training scenes and fail to follow novel language instructions. Off-the-shelf vision-language models (VLMs) often provide stronger generalization, but cannot directly control robot actions. To combine the common sense of VLMs with VLA control, we propose Guided Trace VLA (GT-VLA), a steerable framework that accepts guidance from an external generalist VLM through trace-conditioned action generation. GT-VLA uses a generalist model to identify semantic guidance for the current skill, converts this guidance into a 2D visual trace, and conditions its action policy on the resulting trace-rendered observation. This design separates semantic target acquisition, trace generation, and low-level action execution, allowing high-level guidance to propagate to robot actions. GT-VLA uses a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with skill-specific trace and action modules for robust execution. We evaluate GT-VLA on LIBERO and a physical robot platform, showing improved generalization over recent VLA baselines in both settings. The code and additional supplemental materials are available on our project website at this https URL .

</details>


### [235] [Game-Theoretic Control with Constrained Potential Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31901)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种快速通用的约束动态博弈内点法求解器，并加入计算高效二阶校正，以提高收敛到局部广义纳什均衡（GNE）的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNE求解器虽在小规模动态博弈中达到实时性，但速度仍是瓶颈，难以控制超过四个智能体；同时，仅关注一阶条件的牛顿求解器易收敛到非Nash鞍点，限制了解的有效性。

Method: 针对约束动态博弈设计内点法求解器，并引入计算高效二阶校正，以提升在约束动态博弈中收敛到局部GNE解的概率。

Result: 在数值基准测试和缩比赛车物理实验中评估了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有望提升约束动态博弈的求解速度与通用性，并改善收敛到局部GNE的可靠性，且通过数值与物理实验得到验证。

Abstract: Constrained general-sum dynamic games are a popular formulation for highly interactive multi-agent planning problems. In recent years, Generalized Nash Equilibrium (GNE) solvers have achieved real-time performance for small dynamic games. However, solution speed still remains a bottleneck, and controlling even a small number of agents (e.g., more than four) in a dynamic task remains elusive. In addition, Newton solvers that focus on the first-order conditions are vulnerable to non-Nash saddle points, limiting the usefulness of the provided solution. In this work, we propose a fast and versatile interior point solver for constrained dynamic games, along with a computationally efficient second-order correction that increases the probability of converging to a local GNE solution in constrained dynamic games. The performance of the proposed method is evaluated on numerical benchmarks and a physical experiment involving scaled race cars.

</details>


### [236] [Differentiable Dynamics for Autonomous Micro-Mobility Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31885)
*Grace Cai,Joey Lee,Nithin Parepally,Laura Zheng,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向自主微出行车辆（MMV）的可微分动力学模型 DiffKBM 与 DiffGM3，并在开环轨迹拟合和闭环导航中验证其优于简化运动学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮椅、滑板车、自行车等 MMV 有望提升共享行人空间中的低速交通可达性与安全性，但现有自动驾驶栈多采用简化运动学模型，难以刻画轮胎滑移、摩擦和轮系布局等关键特性，限制真实性与基于梯度的优化。

Method: 先构建 DiffKBM，即可微分运动学自行车模型；再提出 DiffGM3，即可微分的通用微出行模型（GM3），统一基于轮胎的动力学形式，支持多种 MMV 构型。二者支持端到端可微分优化，可嵌入可微分自动驾驶栈。评估包括：开环轨迹匹配，将模型作为 DiffStack 的动力学层优化以复现真实 MMV 轨迹；闭环自主导航，将模型与可微分 MPC 控制器结合并在 CrowdNav 行人场景中测试。

Result: 开环中，DiffGM3 在自行车、滑板车和摩托车模式上复现轨迹的 ADE 与 NLL 优于 DiffKBM，并降低自行车和摩托车轨迹的规划损失。闭环中，在自行车模式下，DiffGM3 相较 DiffKBM 将 CrowdNav 碰撞减少 55%，不适频率降低 75%。

Conclusion: 可微分、基于轮胎的 MMV 动力学建模能提升轨迹拟合与闭环导航性能，DiffGM3 比 DiffKBM 更适合集成到可微分自主微出行系统中。

Abstract: Autonomous micro-mobility vehicles (MMVs) such as wheelchairs, scooters, and bicycles have the potential to improve mobility access and support safe low-speed transportation in pedestrian-shared spaces. Achieving MMV autonomy will require realistic, predictable MMV motion. However, many existing autonomous vehicle stacks rely on simplified kinematic models that fail to capture key MMV characteristics such as tire slip, friction, and wheel layouts, limiting realism and gradient-based optimization. In this paper, we explore differentiable formulations of dynamics models for autonomous micro-mobility systems. We first construct DiffKBM, a differentiable version of the kinematic bicycle model (KBM). Then, we introduce DiffGM3, a differentiable formulation of the General Micro-Mobility Model (GM3), a unified tire-based dynamics formulation for micro-mobility vehicles that supports a wide range of MMV configurations. DiffKBM and DiffGM3 enable end-to-end differentiable optimization through MMV dynamics, making them suitable for integration into differentiable autonomy stacks. We evaluate these dynamics models in both open-loop and closed-loop settings: (1) open-loop trajectory matching, where DiffKBM and DiffGM3 are integrated as a dynamics layer within DiffStack and optimized to reproduce real-world MMV trajectories, and (2) closed-loop autonomous navigation, where DiffKBM and DiffGM3 are paired with a differentiable MPC controller in CrowdNav pedestrian scenarios. In the open-loop setting, DiffGM3 outperforms DiffKBM in reproducing trajectories with improvements in ADE and NLL across bicycle, scooter, and motorcycle modes, and reductions in planning loss for bicycle and motorcycle trajectories. We also find that, in closed-loop settings, DiffGM3 improves on DiffKBM's CrowdNav performance by producing 55\% fewer collisions and a 75\% lower discomfort frequency for the bicycle mode.

</details>


### [237] [Efficient Bezier Velocity Optimization for Free-Floating Space Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31880)
*Duo Zhang,Zhizhuo Zhang,Xiaoli Bai,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAVOR规划器，用于无驱动航天器上机械臂的无碰撞到达、跟踪与指定时间预抓取拦截；通过优化Bezier关节速度曲线并结合解析递归灵敏度，实现高成功率与低计算耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无驱动航天器上的机械臂规划受到基座-机械臂耦合运动、碰撞避免和时间约束影响，传统IK初始化位置样条或单步QP基线成功率低且计算慢，因此需要高效统一的到达、跟踪和指定时间拦截规划方法。

Method: 优化满足线速度、加速度和连续性约束的Bezier关节速度曲线；决策维度独立于rollout分辨率；利用解析递归灵敏度计算任务与避障间隙梯度，贯穿耦合的基座-机械臂运动；并通过并行评估、缓存和增量碰撞发现降低计算量。

Result: 在7-DoF机械臂和5个仿真航天器模型上，FAVOR点到点成功率达99.8%，平均计算时间1.779 s，而IK初始化位置样条基线为62.9%和50.270 s；跟踪任务完成30/36，单步QP为15；拦截任务完成36/36，样条基线为22；消融实验表明有限差分使平均规划时间增加4.7倍。

Conclusion: FAVOR在无驱动航天器机械臂的到达、跟踪和指定时间预抓取拦截中显著优于IK初始化样条与单步QP基线，兼顾高成功率与计算效率；解析递归灵敏度是关键，有限差分替代会带来显著时间代价。

Abstract: We present FAVOR (Free-floating Arm Velocity Optimization with Recursive Sensitivities), a planner for collision-free reaching, tracking, and prescribed-time pre-grasp interception on an unactuated spacecraft. It optimizes Bezier joint-velocity curves with linear velocity, acceleration, and continuity constraints. Decision dimension is independent of rollout resolution. Analytical recursive sensitivities provide task and clearance gradients through the coupled base-arm motion. Parallel evaluation, caching, and incremental collision discovery reduce computation. With a seven-DoF arm and five simulated spacecraft models, FAVOR achieves 99.8% point-to-point success with 1.779 s mean computation, versus 62.9% and 50.270 s for an IK-initialized position-spline baseline. Means include failures and timeouts. FAVOR completes 30 of 36 tracking cases, versus 15 for single-step QP, and all 36 interception instances, versus 22 for the spline baseline. A controlled ablation shows that finite differences increase mean planning time 4.7-fold.

</details>


### [238] [PHIRL: Aligning Learned Rewards with Task Progress for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31855)
*Hang Yu,James Staley,Cheng Xi Tsou,Xiujin Liu,Wenchang Gao,Jindan Huang,Shijie Fang,Zhegong Shangguan,Angelo Cangelosi,Reuben Aronson,Elaine Short*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHIRL 通过联合利用人类演示与进度反馈，迭代进行逆强化学习并沿四个维度对齐奖励，从而在仅标注20%演示的情况下实现更高任务回报与成功率，并具备抗奖励黑客能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示提供密集的策略级信息，但局部精度不足；人类反馈能给出准确的局部评价，却稀疏且不直接提供策略指导。因此需要一种数据高效方法，融合二者优势以学习稳健奖励函数。

Method: 提出 Progress-Heuristicized Inverse Reinforcement Learning（PHIRL）。使用“进度”这一描述累计任务完成度的反馈模态；迭代地通过逆强化学习从演示中推断奖励函数，在带进度标注的演示上计算学习到的奖励，并将奖励与进度标注在四个维度上进行对齐。在真实与仿真机器人任务上评估，并额外探索使用微调视觉语言模型提供进度反馈。

Result: PHIRL 显著优于基线，在仅20%演示被标注的情况下取得更高的环境回报奖励和任务成功率。对奖励黑客场景的分析表明，PHIRL 学到的奖励函数可靠，不易被利用。

Conclusion: PHIRL 是一种数据高效且稳健的奖励学习框架，通过联合演示与进度反馈，有效提升机器人任务性能并增强奖励函数对奖励黑客的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide dense policy-level information but sometimes lack local precision. Human feedback presents accurate local critiques, but offers sparse evaluations rather than direct policy guidance.
