We propose Progress-Heuristicized Inverse Reinforcement Learning (PHIRL), a data-efficient framework that learns robust reward functions by jointly leveraging demonstrations and feedback. Specifically, we use progress, a feedback modality that describes cumulative task completion. PHIRL iteratively infers a reward function from demonstrations via inverse reinforcement learning, calculates the learned rewards over the progress-annotated demonstrations, and aligns the rewards with progress annotations over four dimensions. We evaluate PHIRL on real and simulated robot tasks, with additional exploration using a fine-tuned vision-language model to provide progress feedback. Results demonstrate that PHIRL significantly outperforms the baselines, achieving substantially higher environmental return rewards and task success with only twenty percent of demonstrations annotated. Analysis of reward-hacking scenarios demonstrates that PHIRL learned reward functions are reliable against exploitation.

</details>


### [239] [Humanoid Badminton: Learning Dynamic Racket Skills from Limited Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31840)
*Jingzhi Cui,Zhexiong Wang,Bangjie Xu,Pengyu Zhao,Youyuan Li,Zhi Su,Peng Ren,Mengdi Xu,Chao Yu,Yi Wu,Luyang Wang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段分层强化学习框架，使真实人形机器人实现羽毛球多技能对打，包括正手、反手与高动态跳跃回球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速持拍运动对人形机器人要求极高：需在极短时间内决策、精确击球并协调全身动态。羽毛球轨迹快速变化，要求短暂接触窗口内精确控制拍面位姿与速度，并覆盖广阔三维击球空间。人类动作数据可提供先验，但可用羽毛球参考有限且不完美；直接跟踪难以生成适应多样来球的可执行变化，而纯任务驱动优化又可能导致动作不自然。

Method: 提出三阶段分层强化学习框架。第一阶段，通过任务随机化动作增强，将稀疏标注击球事件扩展为目标条件可执行的击球变体，构建连续潜技能空间。第二阶段，高层规划器根据观测到的羽毛球状态，在线输出连续潜技能编码以组合技能。第三阶段，引入上下文条件对抗正则化，在保持回球性能的同时鼓励规划器更自然地使用技能。

Result: 在真实人形机器人上部署后，系统可与人类选手进行持续多技能对打，涵盖正手、反手以及高动态跳跃回球。

Conclusion: 这是首个在真实世界中展示多技能人-机器人持拍运动对打的系统，并包含高动态跳跃回球。

Abstract: High-speed racket sports provide a demanding testbed for humanoid robots, requiring time-critical decisions, precise striking, and dynamic whole-body coordination. In badminton, fast-changing shuttle trajectories require timely contact decisions, while successful returns demand precise racket pose and velocity within a brief contact window and across a broad three-dimensional striking workspace. Human motion data provide valuable priors for such athletic skills, but usable badminton references are limited and imperfect. Direct tracking provides insufficient executable variation for diverse shuttle conditions, while purely task-driven optimization may produce unnatural motion. To address these challenges, we present a three-stage hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic humanoid badminton. First, task-randomized motion augmentation expands sparse annotated hitting events into executable target-conditioned stroke variations, forming a continuous latent skill space. Second, a high-level planner outputs continuous latent skill codes to compose these skills online according to the observed shuttle state. Third, a context-conditioned adversarial regularizer encourages more natural planner-level skill usage while preserving return performance. When deployed on a real humanoid robot, our system achieves sustained multi-skill rallies with human players, including forehand, backhand, and highly dynamic jump returns. This is the first real-world humanoid racket-sport system to demonstrate multi-skill human--robot rallies including highly dynamic jump returns.

</details>


### [240] [Path Planning with Motion Primitives in Dynamic Environments: SIPP on Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31803)
*Marat Agranovskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于状态格与预计算运动原语的SIPP适配方法，用于动态环境中差分约束机器人的自主导航；通过将移动障碍物的时空扫掠体素化到栅格上，方法在牺牲一定计算开销的情况下，生成比2^k连通栅格规划器更平滑、连续且物理可执行的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态共享空间中的自主导航要求规划器既能避障，又能满足差分约束机器人的运动学与动力学限制。传统2^k连通栅格规划器虽然能高效搜索无碰撞路径，但依赖停转式运动，产生分段线性轨迹，对差分约束机器人而言运动学上高度次优。

Method: 在状态格上适配Safe Interval Path Planning（SIPP），使用预计算的、运动学平滑的运动原语进行搜索；为处理动态环境，将移动障碍物的时空扫掠体积直接栅格化到网格上，把网格单元视为原子空间单位，其分辨率通常由定位噪声决定；并在多种拓扑环境中与2^k连通栅格规划器进行对比。

Result: 实验考虑规划时间、路径角度性、累积航向变化（角度/长度）和弯曲能量等指标。结果表明，状态格搜索扩展了状态空间并增加计算开销，但轨迹在运动动力学性质上显著更优；在可达性上可与高连通度栅格相当，同时保证路径平滑、连续并物理可执行。

Conclusion: 基于状态格和运动原语的SIPP适配方法适合动态环境中的差分约束机器人导航；尽管计算代价更高，它能在保持较高可达性的同时生成面向真实部署的平滑且可执行轨迹。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in dynamic environments is a critical challenge, particularly when spaces are shared with other mobile agents whose future trajectories are known. While traditional grid-based planners efficiently find collision-free paths, their reliance on stop-and-turn mechanics over $2^k$-connected grids produces piecewise-linear trajectories that are kinodynamically highly sub-optimal for differentially constrained robots. In this paper, we present an adaptation of Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) that operates on state lattices, utilizing precomputed, kinodynamically smooth motion primitives. To efficiently handle dynamic environments, we rasterize the spatiotemporal swept volumes of moving obstacles directly onto the grid, treating grid cells as atomic units of space, whose resolution is typically dictated by inherent localization noise. We perform a comprehensive comparative analysis between our lattice-based approach and $2^k$-connected grid planners across diverse topological environments. Our evaluation considers a broad spectrum of performance metrics, including planning time, path angularity, cumulative heading change (angle-over-length), and bending energy. The results demonstrate that while the expanded state space of lattice-based search increases computational overhead, it yields trajectories with significantly superior kinodynamic properties. Specifically, our method achieves a reachability comparable to highly connected grids while ensuring smooth, continuous, and physically executable paths ready for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [241] [Timed Rule-Based Supervision of an End-to-End Autonomous Parking Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31773)
*Kejia Gao,Liguo Zhou,Lei Yu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定 CARLA 停车场中，为已有端到端泊车策略增加手工规则运行时监督器 PSS，可将目标成功率从 85.16% 提升到 97.66%，但结果局限于该仿真设置，不构成泛化或形式化安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究手工设定的运行时监督器能否纠正现有端到端泊车策略在固定 CARLA 停车场中的反复失败。

Method: 视觉 Transformer 架构继承自 Yang 等；本文贡献是将其控制输出接入基于定时规则的参数化安全盾（PSS）。PSS 使用人工校准的速度、位置和持续时间阈值，对边界驶出、制动延迟、停滞或振荡控制等已观察失败模式进行干预。

Result: 闭环评估中，16 个留出目标车位和 6 个初始位姿各评估 4 轮，每个配置 384 次尝试。目标成功率从重训练策略的 327/384（85.16%）提升至加入 PSS 后的 375/384（97.66%）；成功尝试的平均位置和朝向误差分别为 0.21 m 和 0.33 度。

Conclusion: 结果表明在该仿真器设置内有所改善。重复尝试共享同一地图、车辆和传感器配置，且 PSS 使用仿真器世界坐标；因此结果不能证明对其他停车场或真实车辆的泛化能力，也不提供形式化安全保证。

Abstract: We study whether a manually specified runtime supervisor can correct recurring failures of an existing end-to-end parking policy in a fixed CARLA parking lot. The vision-based Transformer architecture is inherited from Yang et al.; our contribution is a timed, rule-based Parametric Safety Shield (PSS) applied to its control outputs. The PSS uses hand-calibrated speed, position, and duration thresholds to intervene in observed failure modes, including boundary exits, delayed braking, and stalled or oscillatory control. In the reported closed-loop evaluation, 16 held-out target slots and six initial poses are each evaluated in four rounds (384 attempts per configuration). Target success increases from 327/384 (85.16%) for the retrained policy to 375/384 (97.66%) with the PSS; mean position and orientation errors among successful attempts are 0.21m and 0.33 degrees. These results show an improvement within this simulator setup. The repeated attempts share one map, vehicle, and sensor configuration, and the PSS uses simulator world coordinates; thus the results do not establish generalization to other lots or real vehicles, or a formal safety guarantee.

</details>


### [242] [Reduced Cartesian Kinetostatics for Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots: Residual-Stabilized Full-Shape Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31771)
*Ke Wu,Fangju Yang,Xiaohui Zhang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jingang Yi,Jian S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于平面、可轴向压缩腱驱动连续体机器人（TDCR）的降阶笛卡尔框架，可沿给定腱力/腱位移轨迹高效传播完整 backbone 平衡构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多 TDCR 的规划与控制任务需要完整的笛卡尔 backbone 几何，而现有逐点求解（如 GVS）计算成本高，因此需要一种高效且准确的整形预测方法。

Method: 将 backbone 用两个全局位置场表示；通过精确变分和 Taylor-Galerkin 降阶，将给定空间属性与分布载荷离线凝聚为矩向量，得到解析降阶残差与 Jacobian，避免在线空间积分和数值微分；结合解析微分与残差校正，构建一阶速率系统，在规则分支上完成初始平衡对齐后，每次速率评估仅需一次固定维线性求解。

Result: 四个仿真案例（变腱路由、非均匀几何、轴向压缩及其组合）中，传播的笛卡尔形状与分布应变均与逐点 GVS 平衡解高度一致；残差校正可抑制传播漂移，相比未校正 Euler 仅增加约 0.98% 平均更新时间；平均每次更新 0.508 ms，比逐点 GVS 求解快约 11 倍；位移驱动实验中最大归一化平均 backbone 位置误差为 1.02%，最大末端执行器位置误差为 0.28%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该方法能够在给定驱动路径上实现高效、准确的笛卡尔完整形状预测，适用于 TDCR 的规划与控制。

Abstract: Many planning and control tasks for tendon-driven continuum robots (TDCRs) require the complete Cartesian backbone geometry. We present a reduced Cartesian framework for planar, axially compressible TDCRs that propagates equilibrium configurations along prescribed tendon-force and tendon-displacement trajectories. The backbone is represented by two global position fields. Following exact variation, a Taylor-Galerkin reduction condenses prescribed spatial properties and distributed loads into offline moment vectors, yielding analytic reduced residuals and Jacobians without online spatial quadrature or numerical differentiation. Analytical differentiation and residual correction yield first-order rate systems requiring one fixed-dimensional linear solve per rate evaluation after initial equilibrium alignment on a regular branch. Across four simulated cases covering variable tendon routing, nonuniform geometry, axial compression, and their combined effects, the propagated Cartesian shapes and distributed strains closely match pointwise geometrically variable-strain (GVS) equilibrium solutions. Residual correction suppresses propagation drift across the tested step sizes while adding only about 0.98% to the mean update time of uncorrected Euler. The proposed method requires 0.508 ms per update on average, approximately 11 times faster than pointwise GVS solves. Displacement-driven experiments yield a maximum normalized mean backbone position error of 1.02% and a maximum end-effector position error of 0.28%. These results support efficient and accurate Cartesian full-shape prediction along prescribed actuation paths.

</details>


### [243] [Robot Manipulation with GPT-6-Astra: Body Knowledge, Experience Reuse, Emergent Skills, and Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31770)
*Sida He,Lingxi Xie,Yunning Cao,Pengfei Chen,Kaiwen Duan,Jiannan Ge,Xinyue Huo,Jiacheng Shao,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究验证：向 GPT-6-Astra 控制的 XLeRobot 提供机器可读的本体描述、同步经验/演示与可复用技能，可显著缩短仿真和真实电梯按钮任务的完成时间，并实现 sim2real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态智能体虽能生成机器人控制程序，但反复探索和模型中介的动作选择会导致执行缓慢；作者探究外部本体知识、成功经验和可执行技能能否提升 XLeRobot 的任务效率、泛化与迁移能力。

Method: 在仿真和物理电梯按钮任务中，以仅有通用控制接口且无先验经验的基线为对照，考察三类信息：完整机器人几何与相机信息；与动作/状态同步的图像记录；研究者重构的智能体自发视觉反馈程序。实验包括固定起点仿真试验、位移起点配对比较以及真实机器人 sim2real 试验。

Result: 30 次固定起点仿真试验中，完整几何与相机信息使平均完成时间较基线降低 57.4%，而仅用同步动作/状态图像记录且无额外本体资产降低 68.6%。9 组配对比较（18 次试验）在起点偏移 10–100 cm 时，原起点记录经验较无经验降低 58–63%，显示对新起点的泛化。经验实验中 GPT-6-Astra 自发产生简短视觉反馈程序，研究者重构版本在 27 次仿真试验中使平均局部任务时间降低 29–31%。12 次真实机器人试验在操作员确认按钮接触条件下实现 sim2real 复用：共享名义起点下，仿真 XML 资产和仿真经验分别降低平均时间 53.0% 和 49.9%；真实经验也可迁移到两个新起点。

Conclusion: 结果表明一种实用范式：为 GPT-6-Astra 提供机器可读本体描述与同步演示，并将智能体生成的有效反馈例程转化为可复用技能，同时让智能体基于当前图像自适应动作；作者公开任务提示、试验级数据和技能实现。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal agents can write robot-control programs, but repeated exploration and model-mediated action selection can make execution slow. We study how external body knowledge, successful experience, and executable skills improve an XLeRobot controlled by GPT-6-Astra in a simulated and a physical elevator-button task. In 30 fixed-start simulation trials, complete robot geometry and camera information reduce mean completion time by 57.4% relative to a baseline with only the common control interface and no prior experience; images with synchronized action and state records reduce it by 68.6% without additional body assets. In nine paired comparisons (18 trials) at starts displaced by 10-100 cm, experience recorded at the original start reduces mean time by 58-63% relative to no experience, demonstrating generalization to the tested new starting positions. During experience experiments, GPT-6-Astra spontaneously generates a short visual-feedback program. Researcher-refactored versions reduce mean local-task time by 29-31% in 27 simulation trials. Finally, 12 real-robot trials using operator-confirmed button contact demonstrate sim2real reuse: at a shared nominal start, simulation XML assets and simulation experience reduce mean time by 53.0% and 49.9%, respectively; real experience also transfers to two new starts. These results suggest a practical way to build general-purpose manipulation experiments around GPT-6-Astra: supply machine-readable body descriptions and synchronized demonstrations, and turn useful agent-generated feedback routines into reusable skills, while the agent adapts actions from current images. We release all task prompts, trial-level experimental data, and acquired skill implementations at this https URL .

</details>
